# 中华人民共和国生态环境法典

**分类**：法律  
**法律部门**：社会法  
**公布日期**：2026-03-12  
**生效日期**：2026-08-15

> （2026年3月12日第十四届全国人民代表大会第四次会议通过）

---

## 第一编　总则

### 第一章　基本规定

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条　为了保护生态环境，防治污染和其他公害，保障公众健康和生态环境权益，维护生态安全，推动绿色低碳发展，建设生态文明，全面推进美丽中国建设，加快推进人与自然和谐共生的现代化，实现中华民族永续发展，根据宪法，制定本法。

**Article 1** This Law is formulated in accordance with the Constitution to protect the ecological environment, prevent and control pollution and other public hazards, safeguard public health and ecological environment rights and interests, maintain ecological security, promote green and low-carbon development, build an ecological civilization, comprehensively advance the building of a Beautiful China, accelerate the modernization of harmony between humanity and nature, and realize the sustainable development of the Chinese nation.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条　本法所称生态环境，是指影响人类生存和发展以及生态系统功能的各种天然的和经过人工改造的自然空间、自然因素及其相互联系与作用的总体，包括大气、水、海洋、土地、矿藏、森林、山岭、草原、湿地、冰川、高原、荒漠、野生生物、自然遗迹、人文遗迹、自然保护地、城市和乡村等。

**Article 2** For the purposes of this Law, "ecological environment" means the totality of various natural and artificially modified natural spaces, natural factors, and their interconnections and interactions that affect human survival and development as well as ecosystem functions, including the atmosphere, water, oceans, land, mineral deposits, forests, mountains, grasslands, wetlands, glaciers, plateaus, deserts, wildlife, natural relics, cultural relics, nature reserves, cities, and villages.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条　中华人民共和国领域和中华人民共和国管辖的其他海域内的污染防治、生态保护、绿色低碳发展等生态环境保护相关活动，适用本法。  
在中华人民共和国领域和中华人民共和国管辖的其他海域以外，造成或者可能造成中华人民共和国领域和中华人民共和国管辖的其他海域环境污染、生态破坏的，适用本法相关规定。

**Article 3** This Law shall apply to activities related to ecological environment protection, such as pollution prevention and control, ecological protection, and green and low-carbon development, within the territory of the People's Republic of China and other sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China.  
Where, outside the territory of the People's Republic of China and other sea areas under its jurisdiction, any conduct causes or may cause environmental pollution or ecological damage within the territory of the People's Republic of China or other sea areas under its jurisdiction, the relevant provisions of this Law shall apply.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条　生态环境保护坚持中国共产党的领导，贯彻习近平生态文明思想，树立尊重自然、顺应自然、保护自然的生态文明理念，坚持良好生态环境是最普惠的民生福祉，完善落实绿水青山就是金山银山理念的体制机制，坚持用最严格制度最严密法治保护生态环境。

**Article 4** Ecological environment protection shall adhere to the leadership of the Communist Party of China, implement Xi Jinping Thought on Ecological Civilization, establish an ecological civilization concept of respecting, conforming to, and protecting nature, uphold that a sound ecological environment is the most inclusive public welfare, improve the systems and mechanisms for implementing the concept that lucid waters and lush mountains are invaluable assets, and adhere to protecting the ecological environment with the strictest systems and the most rigorous rule of law.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条　节约资源和保护生态环境是国家的基本国策。  
国家采取有利于保护生态环境、促进人与自然和谐共生的经济、技术等政策措施，统筹产业结构调整、污染防治、生态保护、应对气候变化，协同推进降碳、减污、扩绿、增长，推进生态优先、节约集约和绿色低碳发展。

**Article 5** Conserving resources and protecting the ecological environment are fundamental state policies.  
The State shall adopt economic, technological, and other policies and measures that are conducive to protecting the ecological environment and promoting harmony between humanity and nature, coordinate industrial restructuring, pollution prevention and control, ecological protection, and response to climate change, synergistically advance carbon reduction, pollution reduction, green expansion, and economic growth, and promote ecological priority, conservation and intensification, and green and low-carbon development.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条　生态环境保护坚持预防为主、系统治理、生态优先、绿色发展、公众参与、损害担责的原则。

**Article 6** Ecological environment protection shall adhere to the principles of prevention first, systematic governance, ecological priority, green development, public participation, and liability for damage.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条　任何单位和个人都有保护生态环境的义务。

**Article 7** All units and individuals shall have the obligation to protect the ecological environment.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条　各级人民政府应当加强生态环境保护工作，正确处理高质量发展和高水平保护的关系，根据生态环境保护目标和治理任务，采取有效措施，改善生态环境质量。  
地方各级人民政府应当对本行政区域及其管理的海域的生态环境保护工作和生态环境质量负责。

**Article 8** People's governments at all levels shall strengthen ecological environment protection work, properly handle the relationship between high-quality development and high-level protection, adopt effective measures based on ecological environment protection objectives and governance tasks, and improve ecological environment quality.  
Local people's governments at all levels shall be responsible for the ecological environment protection work and the ecological environment quality of their respective administrative regions and the sea areas under their management.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条　企业事业单位和其他生产经营者应当采取有效措施，防止、减少环境污染和生态破坏，节约集约利用资源，控制温室气体排放，履行绿色低碳发展义务，并对所造成的损害依法承担责任。

**Article 9** Enterprises, public institutions, and other producers and business operators shall adopt effective measures to prevent and reduce environmental pollution and ecological damage, conserve and intensively utilize resources, control greenhouse gas emissions, fulfill their obligations for green and low-carbon development, and bear liability according to law for any damage caused.

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条　公民应当增强生态环境保护意识，采取简约适度、绿色低碳的生活方式，自觉履行生态环境保护义务，遵守生态环境法律法规，配合实施生态环境保护措施。

**Article 10** Citizens shall enhance their awareness of ecological environment protection, adopt a simple, moderate, green, and low-carbon lifestyle, consciously fulfill their obligations for ecological environment protection, abide by ecological environment laws and regulations, and cooperate with the implementation of ecological environment protection measures.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条　国家支持生态环境科学技术研究、开发和应用，加强生态环境保护工作的科技支撑和人才培养，在确保安全前提下促进信息技术、数字技术、人工智能技术等的应用，提高生态环境保护的科学技术水平。

**Article 11** The State shall support scientific and technological research, development, and application in the ecological environment, strengthen scientific and technological support and talent cultivation for ecological environment protection work, promote the application of information technology, digital technology, artificial intelligence technology, etc., under the premise of ensuring safety, and improve the scientific and technological level of ecological environment protection.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条　各级人民政府及其有关部门应当加强生态环境保护宣传和普及工作，开展生态环境法治宣传教育，弘扬生态文化，增强公众的生态环境保护意识和法治素养。  
教育行政部门、学校应当将生态环境法律法规和生态环境保护知识纳入学校教育内容，培养学生的生态环境保护意识和法治素养。  
新闻媒体应当开展生态环境法律法规和生态环境保护知识的宣传，对生态环境违法行为进行舆论监督。  
国家鼓励基层群众性自治组织、社会组织、生态环境保护志愿者等开展生态环境法律法规和生态环境保护知识的宣传，营造保护生态环境、建设生态文明的良好风气。

**Article 12** People's governments at all levels and their relevant departments shall strengthen the publicity and popularization of ecological environment protection, carry out publicity and education on the rule of law in the ecological environment, promote ecological culture, and enhance the public's awareness of ecological environment protection and legal literacy.  
Education administrative departments and schools shall incorporate ecological environment laws, regulations, and knowledge into school education content, and cultivate students' awareness of ecological environment protection and legal literacy.  
News media shall conduct publicity on ecological environment laws, regulations, and knowledge, and exercise public opinion supervision over illegal acts in the ecological environment.  
The State encourages grassroots self-governing organizations, social organizations, ecological environment protection volunteers, etc., to carry out publicity on ecological environment laws, regulations, and knowledge, and to create a good atmosphere for protecting the ecological environment and building an ecological civilization.

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条　对在生态环境保护工作中做出显著成绩的单位和个人，按照国家规定给予表彰和奖励。

**Article 13** Units and individuals that have made outstanding achievements in ecological environment protection work shall be commended and rewarded in accordance with state regulations.

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条　国家加强生态环境领域的国际合作，履行中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约规定的义务，支持生态环境保护国际交流与合作，积极参与生态环境国际规则的研究与制定，积极阐释中国特色生态环境法治理念、主张和成功实践，推动构建公平合理、合作共赢的全球环境治理体系。

**Article 14** The State shall strengthen international cooperation in the field of ecological environment, fulfill the obligations stipulated in international treaties concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China, support international exchanges and cooperation in ecological environment protection, actively participate in the research and formulation of international rules on ecological environment, actively explain the Chinese characteristics of ecological environment legal concepts, propositions, and successful practices, and promote the building of a fair, reasonable, and win-win global environmental governance system.

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条　每年8月15日为全国生态日。国家通过多种形式开展生态文明宣传教育活动。

**Article 15** August 15 of each year shall be National Ecology Day. The State shall carry out publicity and education activities on ecological civilization through various forms.

### 第二章　监督管理

#### 第一节　监督管理体制和工作机制

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条　县级以上人民政府应当组织、协调、督促有关部门依法履行生态环境保护监督管理职责。

**Article 16** People's governments at or above the county level shall organize, coordinate, and supervise the relevant departments to perform their duties of supervision and administration of ecological environment protection according to law.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条　国务院生态环境主管部门在其职责范围内对全国生态环境保护工作实施统一监督管理，统一政策规划标准制定，统一监测评估，统一监督执法，统一督察问责。地方人民政府生态环境主管部门在其职责范围内对本行政区域生态环境保护工作实施统一监督管理。  
国务院自然资源主管部门在其职责范围内对全国自然资源开发利用和保护工作实施监督管理，根据国务院授权统一履行全民所有自然资源资产所有者职责，统一履行所有国土空间用途管制和生态的保护与修复职责。地方人民政府自然资源主管部门在其职责范围内对本行政区域自然资源开发利用和保护，以及国土空间开发保护工作实施监督管理。  
县级以上人民政府发展改革、工业和信息化、住房城乡建设、交通运输、水行政、农业农村、林业草原等有关部门和军队生态环境保护部门在各自职责范围内对生态环境保护工作实施监督管理。

**Article 17** The competent department of ecological environment under the State Council shall, within its scope of duties, exercise unified supervision and administration over the national ecological environment protection work, unify the formulation of policies, plans, and standards, unify monitoring and evaluation, unify supervision and law enforcement, and unify inspection and accountability. The competent departments of ecological environment under local people's governments shall, within their respective scopes of duties, exercise unified supervision and administration over the ecological environment protection work in their respective administrative regions.  
The competent department of natural resources under the State Council shall, within its scope of duties, exercise supervision and administration over the development, utilization, and protection of national natural resources, uniformly perform the duties of the owner of all natural resources assets owned by the whole people as authorized by the State Council, and uniformly perform the duties of the regulation of the use of all territorial space and the protection and restoration of ecology. The competent departments of natural resources under local people's governments shall, within their respective scopes of duties, exercise supervision and administration over the development, utilization, and protection of natural resources, as well as the development and protection of territorial space, in their respective administrative regions.  
The relevant departments of the people's governments at or above the county level, such as development and reform, industry and information technology, housing and urban-rural construction, transportation, water administration, agriculture and rural affairs, forestry and grassland, and the ecological environment protection department of the military shall, within their respective scopes of duties, exercise supervision and administration over ecological environment protection work.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条　国家建立健全生态环境保护责任清单制度，明确国务院有关部门和地方人民政府有关部门的生态环境保护监督管理职责。

**Article 18** The State shall establish and improve a responsibility list system for ecological environment protection, clarifying the ecological environment protection supervision and administration responsibilities of the relevant departments of the State Council and the relevant departments of local people's governments.

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条　省、市、县、乡依法建立健全河湖长制、林长制。各级河湖长负责本行政区域江河湖泊管理和保护相关工作；各级林长负责本行政区域森林草原资源保护发展相关工作。

**Article 19** Provinces, cities, counties, and townships shall establish and improve the river and lake chief system and the forest chief system according to law. River and lake chiefs at all levels shall be responsible for the management and protection of rivers and lakes in their respective administrative regions; forest chiefs at all levels shall be responsible for the protection and development of forest and grassland resources in their respective administrative regions.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条　县级以上地方人民政府根据实际需要建立生态环境保护工作协调联动机制，加强部门协同配合，推进本行政区域生态环境保护工作。

**Article 20** Local people's governments at or above the county level shall, based on actual needs, establish a coordination and linkage mechanism for ecological environment protection work, strengthen departmental coordination and cooperation, and advance the ecological environment protection work in their respective administrative regions.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条　国家建立健全跨行政区域的重点区域、流域、海域生态环境联合保护协调机制，实行统一规划、统一标准、统一监测、统一保护措施。  
前款规定以外的跨行政区域的生态环境保护，由上级人民政府协调解决，或者由有关地方人民政府协商解决。

**Article 21** The State shall establish and improve a coordination mechanism for joint ecological and environmental protection across administrative regions, river basins, and sea areas, and shall implement unified planning, unified standards, unified monitoring, and unified protection measures.  
For ecological and environmental protection across administrative regions other than those specified in the preceding paragraph, the matter shall be resolved through coordination by the people's government at a higher level, or through consultation by the relevant local people's governments.

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条　国家完善生态安全工作协调机制，采取有效措施提升生态安全风险研判评估、监测预警、应急应对和处置能力，形成全域联动、立体高效的生态安全防护体系，统筹推进生态安全工作。

**Article 22** The State shall improve the coordination mechanism for ecological security work, take effective measures to enhance the capabilities of risk assessment, monitoring and early warning, emergency response, and disposal of ecological security risks, form an all-dimensional, interconnected, and efficient ecological security protection system, and advance ecological security work in a coordinated manner.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条　国家建立健全生态环境信息共享机制，提升信息共享能力，加强生态环境信息共享。  
县级以上人民政府应当推进生态环境信息共享平台建设，有关部门应当及时组织上传生态环境信息并动态更新。

**Article 23** The State shall establish and improve a mechanism for sharing ecological and environmental information, enhance information sharing capabilities, and strengthen the sharing of ecological and environmental information.  
People's governments at or above the county level shall promote the construction of platforms for sharing ecological and environmental information, and relevant departments shall promptly organize the uploading of ecological and environmental information and update it on a dynamic basis.

#### 第二节　监督管理制度

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条　国家坚持分区域、差异化、精准管控，实施地上地下、陆海统筹、区域联动的生态环境监督管理制度，加强规划、标准、监测等监督管理制度的衔接协调。

**Article 24** The State shall adhere to differentiated, precise, and region-specific management and control, implement an ecological and environmental supervision and management system that coordinates above-ground and underground, land and sea, and regional linkages, and strengthen the connection and coordination of planning, standards, monitoring, and other supervision and management systems.

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条　各级人民政府及其有关部门应当加强生态环境保护监督管理能力建设，提高生态环境保护监督管理信息化、数字化、智能化水平。

**Article 25** People's governments at all levels and their relevant departments shall strengthen capacity building for ecological and environmental protection supervision and management, and improve the level of informatization, digitalization, and intelligentization of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management.

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条　国务院有关部门和县级以上地方人民政府组织制定经济、技术政策和措施，应当充分考虑对生态环境的影响，听取有关方面和专家的意见。

**Article 26** When organizing the formulation of economic and technical policies and measures, the relevant departments of the State Council and local people's governments at or above the county level shall fully consider the impact on the ecological environment and solicit opinions from relevant parties and experts.

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条　国家实行生态环境保护目标责任制和考核评价制度，将生态环境保护目标完成情况纳入考核评价内容。考核评价工作应当增强针对性、实效性，力戒形式主义。考核结果应当向社会公开。

**Article 27** The State shall implement a target responsibility system and an assessment and evaluation system for ecological and environmental protection, and shall incorporate the completion of ecological and environmental protection targets into the assessment and evaluation content. The assessment and evaluation work shall be targeted and effective, and formalism shall be strictly avoided. The assessment results shall be disclosed to the public.

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条　国家建立健全生态环境保护督察制度，对有关方面履行生态文明建设和生态环境保护责任组织开展全面督察。生态环境保护督察实行中央和省、自治区、直辖市两级督察体制。

**Article 28** The State shall establish and improve an inspection system for ecological and environmental protection, and shall organize comprehensive inspections on the performance of responsibilities by relevant parties in ecological civilization construction and ecological and environmental protection. Ecological and environmental protection inspections shall implement a two-level inspection system at the central level and the provincial, autonomous regional, and municipal level.

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条　被督察对象应当自觉接受生态环境保护督察，积极配合督察工作。  
被督察对象收到生态环境保护督察反馈意见后，应当组织编制督察整改方案，针对督察反馈问题逐项明确整改实施主体、整改目标、整改时限、重点措施和验收单位。  
对生态环境保护督察及整改中发现的重要生态环境问题及其失职失责情况，按照国家规定追责问责。

**Article 29** The inspected entities shall willingly accept ecological and environmental protection inspections and actively cooperate with the inspection work.  
After receiving the feedback opinions from ecological and environmental protection inspections, the inspected entities shall organize and prepare inspection rectification plans, specifying for each issue identified in the inspection feedback the implementing entity, rectification target, rectification deadline, key measures, and acceptance unit.  
For significant ecological and environmental issues and cases of dereliction of duty discovered during ecological and environmental protection inspections and rectifications, liability shall be investigated and accountability shall be pursued in accordance with national regulations.

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条　县级以上人民政府应当每年向本级人民代表大会或者人民代表大会常务委员会报告生态环境状况和生态环境保护目标完成情况，对发生的重大生态环境事件应当及时向本级人民代表大会常务委员会报告，依法接受监督。

**Article 30** People's governments at or above the county level shall annually report to the people's congress at the corresponding level or its standing committee on the ecological and environmental conditions and the completion of ecological and environmental protection targets, and shall promptly report to the standing committee of the people's congress at the corresponding level on major ecological and environmental incidents that occur, and shall accept supervision according to law.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条　国家加强生态环境司法保障建设。  
人民法院应当加强生态环境审判工作，推进生态环境专业化审判机制建设。  
人民检察院应当加强生态环境检察工作，强化检察监督。

**Article 31** The State shall strengthen judicial guarantee construction for ecological and environmental protection.  
People's courts shall strengthen ecological and environmental adjudication work and promote the construction of specialized adjudication mechanisms for ecological and environmental matters.  
People's procuratorates shall strengthen ecological and environmental procuratorial work and strengthen procuratorial supervision.

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条　国家完善生态环境损害赔偿和生态环境公益诉讼制度，维护国家利益、社会公共利益。

**Article 32** The State shall improve the systems for ecological and environmental damage compensation and ecological and environmental public interest litigation to safeguard national interests and social public interests.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条　行政机关、监察机关、审判机关和检察机关应当加强协同配合，建立健全案件移送、信息共享等机制，对污染环境、破坏生态等违法行为依法追究法律责任。

**Article 33** Administrative organs, supervisory organs, adjudicatory organs, and procuratorial organs shall strengthen coordination and cooperation, and establish and improve mechanisms such as case transfer and information sharing, to investigate legal liability according to law for illegal acts such as environmental pollution and ecological damage.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条　国家实行自然资源资产离任审计，对领导干部履行自然资源资产管理和生态环境保护责任情况进行审计。

**Article 34** The State shall implement an audit of natural resources assets upon departure, and shall conduct audits on the performance of leading cadres' responsibilities for natural resources asset management and ecological and environmental protection.

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条　国家加强生态的保护与修复，坚持山水林田湖草沙一体化保护与修复，实行自然恢复为主、自然恢复与人工修复相结合的系统治理。

**Article 35** The State shall strengthen ecological protection and restoration, adhere to the integrated protection and restoration of mountains, waters, forests, farmlands, grasslands, deserts, and implement systematic governance that prioritizes natural recovery and combines natural recovery with artificial restoration.

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条　国家健全主体功能区制度体系，根据城市化地区、农产品主产区、重点生态功能区等的不同定位，优化国土空间发展格局。

**Article 36** The State shall improve the functional zoning system, and optimize the development pattern of national land space according to the different orientations of urbanization areas, main agricultural production areas, key ecological function areas, and others.

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条　国家完善资源总量管理和全面节约制度，避免资源浪费，提高资源利用效率。

**Article 37** The State shall improve the systems for total resource management and comprehensive conservation, avoid resource waste, and improve resource utilization efficiency.

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条　国家健全自然资源资产产权制度和管理制度体系，完善全民所有自然资源资产所有权委托代理机制，推进自然资源有偿使用。

**Article 38** The State shall improve the property rights system and management system for natural resources assets, improve the principal-agent mechanism for the ownership of natural resources assets owned by the whole people, and promote the compensated use of natural resources.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条　国家健全生态产品价值实现机制，形成多元化的生态产品价值实现路径，推进生态产业化和产业生态化。

**Article 39** The State shall improve the mechanism for realizing the value of ecological products, form diversified paths for realizing the value of ecological products, and promote ecological industrialization and industrial ecology.

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条　开发利用自然资源，应当合理开发，节约集约，高效利用，防止污染环境、破坏生态，减少温室气体排放，依法制定有关生态环境保护的方案并予以实施。

**Article 40** The exploitation and utilization of natural resources shall be carried out rationally, economically, intensively, and efficiently, prevent environmental pollution and ecological damage, reduce greenhouse gas emissions, and formulate plans concerning ecological and environmental protection according to law and implement them.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条　国家统筹水资源、水环境、水生态治理，加强水生态环境保护。  
国家实行水资源刚性约束制度，坚持以水定城、以水定地、以水定人、以水定产，促进人口和城市科学合理布局，构建与水资源承载能力相适应的现代产业体系，保障国家水安全。

**Article 41** The State shall coordinate the management of water resources, water environment, and water ecology, and strengthen the protection of the water ecological environment.  
The State shall implement a rigid restraint system for water resources, adhere to the principle of determining cities, land, population, and industries based on water availability, promote a scientific and rational layout of population and cities, establish a modern industrial system compatible with the carrying capacity of water resources, and ensure national water security.

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条　国家加强生物多样性保护，建立健全生物多样性保护工作协调机制和生物多样性调查、监测、评估和保护制度，合理布局建设生物多样性保护空间体系。  
引进外来物种以及研究、开发和利用生物技术，应当采取有效措施，防止对生物多样性的破坏。

**Article 42** The State shall strengthen biodiversity protection, establish and improve a coordination mechanism for biodiversity protection work and a system for biodiversity survey, monitoring, assessment, and protection, and rationally layout and construct a spatial system for biodiversity protection.  
The introduction of alien species and the research, development, and use of biotechnology shall adopt effective measures to prevent damage to biodiversity.

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条　国家建设以国家公园为主体、以自然保护区为基础、以各类自然公园为补充的自然保护地体系，确保重要生态系统、自然遗迹、自然景观和生物多样性得到系统性保护。

**Article 43** The State shall establish a system of protected natural areas with national parks as the mainstay, nature reserves as the foundation, and various types of natural parks as supplements, ensuring that important ecosystems, natural relics, natural landscapes, and biodiversity receive systematic protection.

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条　国家推进青藏高原生态屏障区、长江重点生态区、黄河重点生态区和东北森林带、北方防沙带、南方丘陵山地带、海岸带等区域生态屏障建设，加强长江、黄河、淮河、海河、珠江、松花江、辽河、洞庭湖、鄱阳湖、太湖、洪泽湖、巢湖等重要江河湖泊生态环境保护。

**Article 44** The State shall promote the construction of ecological barriers in regions such as the Qinghai-Xizang Plateau Ecological Barrier Zone, the key ecological zones of the Yangtze River and the Yellow River, the northeast forest belt, the northern sand prevention belt, the southern hilly and mountainous belt, and the coastal zone, and strengthen the ecological environmental protection of important rivers and lakes including the Yangtze River, Yellow River, Huaihe River, Haihe River, Pearl River, Songhua River, Liaohe River, Dongting Lake, Poyang Lake, Taihu Lake, Hongze Lake, and Chaohu Lake.

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条　国务院生态环境主管部门根据海洋生态环境状况和质量改善要求，会同国务院发展改革、自然资源、住房城乡建设、交通运输、水行政、渔业渔政、林业草原等有关部门和海警机构，划定国家生态环境治理重点海域及其控制区域，拟订综合治理行动方案，报国务院批准后实施。  
沿海设区的市级以上地方人民政府应当根据综合治理行动方案，制定其管理海域的实施方案，因地制宜采取特别管控措施，开展综合治理，协同推进重点海域治理与美丽海湾建设。

**Article 45** The competent department of ecological environment under the State Council shall, based on the status of the marine ecological environment and the requirements for quality improvement, consult with the relevant departments under the State Council for development and reform, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, transport, water administration, fisheries administration, forestry and grassland, and the maritime law enforcement agency, to designate key sea areas for national ecological environment governance and their control areas, formulate comprehensive governance action plans, and submit them to the State Council for approval before implementation.  
The local people's governments at or above the level of a city with districts in coastal areas shall, according to the comprehensive governance action plans, formulate implementation plans for the sea areas under their administration, adopt special control measures tailored to local conditions, carry out comprehensive governance, and coordinate the advancement of governance of key sea areas and the construction of beautiful bays.

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条　各级人民政府应当加强对农业生态环境的保护，促进农业生态环境保护新技术的应用，加强对农业污染源的监测预警，统筹有关部门采取有效措施，防治土壤污染和土地沙化、酸化、盐渍化、贫瘠化、石漠化、地面沉降以及防治植被破坏、水土流失、水体富营养化、水源枯竭、种源灭绝、外来物种入侵等生态失调现象，推广植物病虫害的综合防治。  
各级人民政府应当提高农村生态环境保护公共服务水平，推动农村生态环境综合整治，持续改善农村人居环境。

**Article 46** People's governments at all levels shall strengthen the protection of the agricultural ecological environment, promote the application of new technologies for agricultural ecological environmental protection, strengthen monitoring and early warning of agricultural pollution sources, coordinate relevant departments to adopt effective measures to prevent and control soil pollution and land desertification, acidification, salinization, impoverishment, rocky desertification, and land subsidence, and to prevent and control ecological imbalance phenomena such as vegetation destruction, soil erosion, eutrophication of water bodies, depletion of water sources, extinction of germplasm resources, and invasion of alien species, and promote integrated pest management of plant diseases and insect pests.  
People's governments at all levels shall improve the level of public services for rural ecological environmental protection, promote comprehensive improvement of the rural ecological environment, and continuously improve the rural living environment.

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条　城乡建设应当结合当地自然的生态环境特点，保护植被、水域和自然景观、人文遗迹、古树名木等，加强城市园林、绿地的建设与管理。

**Article 47** Urban and rural construction shall take into account the characteristics of the local natural ecological environment, protect vegetation, water bodies, natural landscapes, cultural relics, ancient and famous trees, etc., and strengthen the construction and management of urban gardens and green spaces.

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条　国家建立健全生态环境与健康监测、调查和风险评估制度；鼓励和组织开展生态环境质量对公众健康影响的研究，采取有效措施预防和控制与环境污染、生态破坏等有关的疾病。

**Article 48** The State shall establish and improve a system for monitoring, investigating, and assessing the risks of the ecological environment and health; encourage and organize research on the impact of ecological environmental quality on public health, and adopt effective measures to prevent and control diseases related to environmental pollution and ecological damage.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条　国家对严重污染环境、破坏生态等的技术、工艺、设备、材料、产品实行淘汰制度。  
国务院发展改革部门会同有关部门确定严重污染环境、破坏生态等的技术、工艺、设备、材料、产品淘汰期限，并纳入国家综合性产业政策目录。  
生产者、进口者、销售者、使用者应当在规定期限内停止生产、进口、销售、使用列入前款规定目录的设备、材料、产品。技术、工艺的采用者应当在规定期限内停止采用列入前款规定目录的技术、工艺。  
被淘汰的设备、材料、产品，不得转让给他人使用。

**Article 49** The State shall implement a phase-out system for technologies, processes, equipment, materials, and products that seriously pollute the environment or damage ecology.  
The development and reform department under the State Council shall, in conjunction with relevant departments, determine the phase-out period for technologies, processes, equipment, materials, and products that seriously pollute the environment or damage ecology, and incorporate them into the national comprehensive industrial policy catalog.  
Producers, importers, sellers, and users shall cease the production, import, sale, or use of equipment, materials, and products listed in the catalog as provided in the preceding paragraph within the prescribed period. Adopters of technologies and processes shall cease the adoption of technologies and processes listed in the catalog as provided in the preceding paragraph within the prescribed period.  
Phased-out equipment, materials, and products shall not be transferred to others for use.

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条　国务院负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门以及重点区域、流域、海域有关省、自治区、直辖市人民政府可以组织有关部门开展联合执法，跨行政区域、流域、海域执法，交叉执法。

**Article 50** The departments of the State Council responsible for ecological environmental protection supervision and management, as well as the people's governments of relevant provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government in key regions, river basins, and sea areas, may organize relevant departments to carry out joint law enforcement, cross-administrative region, river basin, and sea area law enforcement, and cross law enforcement.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条　负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门有权依法对污染环境、破坏生态等的企业事业单位和其他生产经营者以及生态环境技术服务机构等进行现场检查。被检查者应当如实反映情况，提供必要的资料。实施现场检查的部门及其工作人员应当为被检查者保守商业秘密、个人隐私和个人信息。

**Article 51** The departments with ecological environmental protection supervision and management responsibilities shall have the authority to conduct on-site inspections according to law of enterprises, public institutions, and other producers and operators that pollute the environment or damage ecology, as well as ecological environmental technology service institutions. The inspected party shall truthfully report the situation and provide necessary information. The department conducting the on-site inspection and its staff shall keep confidential the trade secrets, personal privacy, and personal information of the inspected party.

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条　企业事业单位和其他生产经营者违反法律法规规定，造成或者可能造成严重污染环境、破坏生态等后果，或者有关证据可能灭失、被隐匿的，负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门可以依法查封、扣押有关场所、船舶、设施、设备、工具、物品。

**Article 52** Where an enterprise, public institution, or other producer or operator violates laws or regulations, causing or likely to cause serious environmental pollution, ecological damage, or other consequences, or where relevant evidence may be destroyed or concealed, the department with ecological environmental protection supervision and management responsibilities may, according to law, seal up or seize the relevant premises, vessels, facilities, equipment, tools, and articles.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条　设区的市级人民政府生态环境主管部门设立的派出机构，可以以自己的名义依法实施现场检查、查封、扣押、代履行和行政处罚。

**Article 53** The dispatched agencies established by the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government of a city with districts may, in their own name, carry out on-site inspections, sealing up, seizure, substitute performance, and administrative penalties according to law.

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条　国家构建生态环境信用监管体系。  
负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门应当依法将企业事业单位和其他生产经营者有关生态环境违法信息记入信用记录。  
企业事业单位和其他生产经营者纠正失信行为、消除不良影响、符合信用修复条件的，可以提出信用修复申请，有关国家机关应当按照规定开展信用修复。

**Article 54** The State shall build an ecological environmental credit supervision system.  
The departments with ecological environmental protection supervision and management responsibilities shall, according to law, record the ecological environmental illegal information of enterprises, public institutions, and other producers and operators in their credit records.  
Where an enterprise, public institution, or other producer or operator corrects its dishonest conduct, eliminates adverse effects, and meets the conditions for credit repair, it may apply for credit repair, and the relevant state organs shall carry out credit repair in accordance with the regulations.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条　在中华人民共和国领域和中华人民共和国管辖的其他海域以外，造成或者可能造成中华人民共和国领域和中华人民共和国管辖的其他海域环境污染、生态破坏的，有关部门和机构有权采取必要的措施。

**Article 55** Where, beyond the territory of the People's Republic of China and other sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China, an act causes or may cause environmental pollution or ecological damage within the territory of the People's Republic of China or other sea areas under its jurisdiction, the relevant departments and institutions shall have the authority to take necessary measures.

### 第三章　规划和生态环境分区管控

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条　国家建立健全以国家发展规划为统领，以国土空间规划为基础，以专项规划、区域规划为支撑，由国家和地方规划共同组成的国家规划体系，加强规划之间的衔接协调，发挥规划对生态环境保护的引领、指导和约束作用。

**Article 56** The State shall establish and improve a national planning system with national development plans as the guide, territorial space plans as the foundation, and special plans and regional plans as support, composed of national and local plans, strengthen the connection and coordination between plans, and give play to the leading, guiding, and constraining role of plans in ecological environmental protection.

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条　县级以上人民政府应当将生态环境保护工作纳入国民经济和社会发展规划。

**Article 57** People's governments at or above the county level shall incorporate ecological environmental protection work into national economic and social development plans.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条　国家建立健全覆盖全域全类型、统一衔接的国土空间用途管制和规划许可制度，依据国土空间规划对国土空间实行分区、分类用途管制，国土空间开发利用活动应当符合国土空间用途管制要求，并依法取得规划许可。

**Article 58** The State shall establish and improve a system for territorial space use control and planning permission that covers all areas and all types, is unified and coherent, implement zonal and categorical use control of territorial space based on territorial space plans, and the development and utilization activities of territorial space shall comply with the requirements of territorial space use control and obtain planning permission according to law.

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条　国土空间规划应当科学有序统筹安排农业、生态、城镇等功能空间，划定耕地和永久基本农田、生态保护红线、城镇开发边界，优化国土空间结构和布局。  
涉及国土空间利用的专项规划应当与国土空间规划相衔接。

**Article 59** Territorial space plans shall scientifically and orderly coordinate the arrangement of functional spaces such as agriculture, ecology, and urban areas, designate farmland and permanent basic farmland, ecological protection redlines, and urban development boundaries, and optimize the structure and layout of territorial space.  
Special plans involving the use of territorial space shall be aligned with the territorial space plans.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条　国家建立健全生态保护红线管理制度，优先将生态功能极重要区域、生态极敏感脆弱区域等区域划入生态保护红线，实行严格保护。  
开发利用自然资源或者从事影响生态环境的建设活动，应当严守生态保护红线，不得造成生态环境的损害。  
省、自治区、直辖市人民政府应当加强对生态保护红线内人为活动的监督管理，定期评估保护成效。

**Article 60** The State shall establish and improve a management system for ecological protection redlines, giving priority to designating areas with extremely important ecological functions and ecologically extremely sensitive and fragile areas as ecological protection redlines, and implementing strict protection.  
The exploitation of natural resources or construction activities affecting the ecological environment shall strictly observe the ecological protection redlines and shall not cause damage to the ecological environment.  
The people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government shall strengthen the supervision and management of human activities within the ecological protection redlines and regularly assess the effectiveness of protection.

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门，根据国家发展规划编制国家生态环境保护规划，报国务院批准并公布实施。  
设区的市级以上地方人民政府生态环境主管部门会同有关部门，根据国家生态环境保护规划的要求，编制本行政区域的生态环境保护规划，报本级人民政府批准并公布实施。  
生态环境保护规划的内容应当包括污染防治、生态保护、应对气候变化的目标、任务、保障措施等。

**Article 61** The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, formulate a national ecological and environmental protection plan in accordance with the national development plan, and submit it to the State Council for approval and promulgation for implementation.  
The competent department of ecology and environment of the local people's government at or above the city with districts shall, together with the relevant departments, formulate an ecological and environmental protection plan for its administrative region in accordance with the requirements of the national ecological and environmental protection plan, and submit it to the people's government at the same level for approval and promulgation for implementation.  
The content of an ecological and environmental protection plan shall include objectives, tasks, and safeguard measures for pollution prevention, ecological protection, and response to climate change.

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条　未达到国家生态环境质量标准的重点区域、流域、海域的有关地方人民政府，应当依法编制限期达标规划、改善规划及其实施方案，并采取有效措施按期达标、改善生态环境质量。限期达标规划、改善规划及其实施方案应当及时公布。

**Article 62** The relevant local people's governments of key areas, river basins, and sea areas that fail to meet the national ecological and environmental quality standards shall, according to law, formulate time-bound compliance plans, improvement plans, and implementation schemes therefor, and take effective measures to achieve compliance and improve ecological and environmental quality within the prescribed time limit. The time-bound compliance plans, improvement plans, and implementation schemes shall be published in a timely manner.

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条　生态环境、发展改革、自然资源、住房城乡建设、水行政、农业农村、林业草原等有关部门，可以在各自职责范围内根据实际需要编制有关生态环境领域的规划。

**Article 63** The relevant departments such as ecology and environment, development and reform, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, water administration, agriculture and rural affairs, and forestry and grassland may, within their respective functions and duties, formulate plans concerning the field of ecology and environment based on actual needs.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条　国家可以根据实际需要，对国家发展规划确定的重点地区、跨行政区域且经济社会活动联系紧密的连片区域和承担重大战略任务的特定区域编制区域规划，指导区域生态环境保护和协调协同发展。

**Article 64** The State may, based on actual needs, formulate regional plans for key areas determined in the national development plan, contiguous trans-administrative regions with close economic and social ties, and specific areas undertaking major strategic tasks, to guide regional ecological and environmental protection and coordinated and collaborative development.

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条　编制生态环境领域的规划，应当征求有关部门、专家等方面的意见；规划实施后，规划编制机关应当按照国家规定组织开展监测分析和评估。

**Article 65** The formulation of plans in the field of ecology and environment shall solicit opinions from relevant departments, experts, and other parties; after the implementation of a plan, the plan formulation authority shall organize and carry out monitoring, analysis, and assessment in accordance with the provisions of the State.

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条　国家建立健全生态环境分区管控制度。  
设区的市级以上地方人民政府根据本行政区域的生态环境状况，制定和调整生态环境分区管控方案，报上一级人民政府生态环境主管部门备案后公布实施。  
生态环境分区管控方案应当与国土空间规划相衔接。

**Article 66** The State shall establish and improve a system for regional management and control of ecology and environment.  
The local people's government at or above the city with districts shall, based on the ecological and environmental conditions of its administrative region, formulate and adjust a regional management and control scheme for ecology and environment, and publish it for implementation after reporting it to the competent department of ecology and environment of the people's government at the next higher level for recordation.  
The regional management and control scheme for ecology and environment shall be aligned with the territorial spatial plan.

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条　生态环境分区管控方案应当落实生态保护红线、环境质量底线、资源利用上线目标，划定优先保护、重点管控、一般管控单元，明确相应的生态环境准入清单。禁止违反生态环境准入清单规定进行生产建设活动。

**Article 67** The regional management and control scheme for ecology and environment shall implement the objectives of ecological protection redlines, environmental quality bottom lines, and resource utilization upper limits, delimit priority protection, key management and control, and general management and control units, and specify the corresponding ecological and environmental access list. Production and construction activities in violation of the provisions of the ecological and environmental access list shall be prohibited.

### 第四章　标准和监测

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条　国家推进生态环境领域标准体系建设，加强标准之间的衔接协调，发挥标准对生态环境保护的支撑作用。

**Article 68** The State shall promote the development of a standard system in the field of ecology and environment, strengthen the coordination and alignment among standards, and leverage the supporting role of standards in ecological and environmental protection.

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条　国务院生态环境主管部门制定国家生态环境质量标准。  
省、自治区、直辖市人民政府对国家生态环境质量标准中未作规定的项目，可以制定地方生态环境质量标准；对国家生态环境质量标准中已作规定的项目，可以制定严于国家生态环境质量标准的地方生态环境质量标准。地方生态环境质量标准应当报国务院生态环境主管部门备案。

**Article 69** The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall formulate national ecological and environmental quality standards.  
The people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government may formulate local ecological and environmental quality standards for items not specified in the national ecological and environmental quality standards; for items already specified in the national ecological and environmental quality standards, they may formulate local ecological and environmental quality standards that are stricter than the national ones. The local ecological and environmental quality standards shall be reported to the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council for recordation.

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条　国务院生态环境、发展改革、工业和信息化、自然资源、住房城乡建设、交通运输、水行政、农业农村、林业草原、标准化等有关部门，在各自职责范围内依法制定有关污染防治、生态保护、绿色低碳发展等方面的标准。

**Article 70** The relevant departments of the State Council, such as ecology and environment, development and reform, industry and information technology, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, transport, water administration, agriculture and rural affairs, forestry and grassland, and standardization, shall, within their respective functions and duties, formulate standards concerning pollution prevention, ecological protection, green and low-carbon development, and other aspects according to law.

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条　制定生态环境领域的标准，应当以保障公众健康、保护生态环境为宗旨，与经济社会发展相适应，做到科学合理。

**Article 71** The formulation of standards in the field of ecology and environment shall aim at protecting public health and the ecological environment, be compatible with economic and social development, and be scientific and reasonable.

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条　制定生态环境领域的标准，应当组织专家进行审查和论证，并征求有关部门、行业协会、企业事业单位和公众等方面的意见，提高标准的科学性。

**Article 72** The formulation of standards in the field of ecology and environment shall organize experts for review and demonstration, and solicit opinions from relevant departments, industry associations, enterprises, institutions, and the public, so as to improve the scientific nature of the standards.

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条　生态环境领域标准的制定机关，应当在其网站上及时公布标准全文，供公众免费查阅、下载。

**Article 73** The authority formulating standards in the field of ecology and environment shall publish the full text of the standards on its website in a timely manner for the public to view and download free of charge.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条　生态环境领域标准的执行情况应当定期进行评估，根据评估结果对标准适时进行修订、废止。

**Article 74** The implementation of standards in the field of ecology and environment shall be assessed periodically, and the standards shall be revised or abolished in due course based on the assessment results.

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条　国家鼓励、支持开展生态环境基准研究。  
国务院生态环境主管部门根据保障公众健康、保护生态环境的需要，制定生态环境基准。

**Article 75** The State shall encourage and support the conduct of research on ecological and environmental baselines.  
The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall formulate ecological and environmental baselines based on the need to protect public health and the ecological environment.

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条　国家建立健全生态环境监测制度。  
国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门制定生态环境监测规范，构建陆海统筹、天地一体、上下协同、信息共享的生态环境监测网络，统一规划国家生态环境质量监测站点的设置，推进综合监测、协同监测和常态化监测，建立健全监测数据共享机制，加强对生态环境监测的管理。  
生态环境、自然资源、住房城乡建设、交通运输、水行政、农业农村、气象、林业草原、疾病预防控制等有关部门，在各自职责范围内做好生态环境监测相关工作。

**Article 76** The State shall establish and improve the ecological and environmental monitoring system.  
The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, formulate ecological and environmental monitoring specifications, build an ecological and environmental monitoring network that coordinates land and sea, integrates space and ground, ensures synergy between different levels, and shares information, uniformly plan the setting of national ecological and environmental quality monitoring stations, promote comprehensive monitoring, collaborative monitoring, and routine monitoring, establish and improve a monitoring data sharing mechanism, and strengthen the management of ecological and environmental monitoring.  
The relevant departments such as ecology and environment, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, transport, water administration, agriculture and rural affairs, meteorology, forestry and grassland, and disease prevention and control shall, within their respective functions and duties, perform the relevant work concerning ecological and environmental monitoring.

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条　各类生态环境监测站点的设置应当符合法律法规规定和有关监测规范的要求。  
生态环境监测应当使用符合法律、行政法规规定和国家标准的监测设施、设备，遵守有关监测规范。  
禁止生产、进口、销售或者使用不符合法律、行政法规规定和国家标准的监测设施、设备。

**Article 77** The setting of various types of ecological and environmental monitoring stations shall comply with the provisions of laws and regulations and the requirements of relevant monitoring specifications.  
Ecological and environmental monitoring shall use monitoring facilities and equipment that comply with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations and national standards, and abide by the relevant monitoring specifications.  
It shall be prohibited to produce, import, sell, or use monitoring facilities or equipment that do not comply with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations or national standards.

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条　生态环境监测机构、实行排污许可管理和其他负有法定监测义务的企业事业单位应当依法建立健全监测数据质量管理制度。  
前款规定的监测机构和企业事业单位及其负责人应当对监测数据的真实性、准确性和完整性负责。

**Article 78** Ecological and environmental monitoring institutions, enterprises and institutions that implement pollutant discharge permit management, and other enterprises and institutions that have statutory monitoring obligations shall, according to law, establish and improve a monitoring data quality management system.  
The monitoring institutions and enterprises and institutions specified in the preceding paragraph, as well as their responsible persons, shall be responsible for the authenticity, accuracy, and completeness of the monitoring data.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条　生态环境监测机构应当具备相应的设施、设备、技术人员、技术能力和管理能力，并依法报有关部门备案。

**Article 79** Ecological and environmental monitoring institutions shall have the corresponding facilities, equipment, technical personnel, technical capability, and management capability, and shall report to the relevant departments for recordation according to law.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条　禁止通过干扰采样、调换样品、改变监测条件、虚假监测、篡改或者伪造记录等方式对生态环境监测数据弄虚作假或者指使对生态环境监测数据弄虚作假。  
禁止通过侵占、损毁或者擅自移动、改变等方式干扰、破坏生态环境监测设施、设备或者指使干扰、破坏生态环境监测设施、设备。

**Article 80** It shall be prohibited to falsify ecological and environmental monitoring data or instruct others to falsify such data by means such as interfering with sampling, replacing samples, altering monitoring conditions, conducting false monitoring, tampering with or forging records.  
It shall be prohibited to interfere with or damage ecological and environmental monitoring facilities or equipment, or instruct others to do so, by means such as occupying, damaging, or unauthorized moving or altering.

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条　省级以上人民政府应当组织有关部门或者委托专业机构，对生态环境状况进行调查、评价，建立健全生态环境的承载能力监测预警机制。

**Article 81** The people's government at or above the provincial level shall organize relevant departments or entrust professional institutions to investigate and evaluate the ecological and environmental conditions, and establish and improve a monitoring and early warning mechanism for the carrying capacity of the ecological environment.

### 第五章　生态环境影响评价

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条　本法所称生态环境影响评价，是指对规划和建设项目实施后可能造成的生态环境影响进行分析、预测和评估，提出预防或者减轻不良生态环境影响的对策和措施，进行跟踪监测的方法与制度。  
国家加强温室气体排放的生态环境影响评价，具体办法和实施步骤由国务院生态环境主管部门规定。

**Article 82** For the purposes of this Law, ecological and environmental impact assessment refers to the methods and systems for analyzing, predicting, and assessing the possible ecological and environmental impacts after the implementation of plans and construction projects, proposing countermeasures and measures to prevent or mitigate adverse ecological and environmental impacts, and conducting follow-up monitoring.  
The State strengthens the ecological and environmental impact assessment of greenhouse gas emissions, and the specific measures and implementation steps shall be prescribed by the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条　编制有关开发利用规划，建设对生态环境有影响的项目，应当依法进行生态环境影响评价。  
未依法进行生态环境影响评价的开发利用规划，不得组织实施；未依法进行生态环境影响评价的建设项目，不得开工建设。

**Article 83** The formulation of relevant development and utilization plans and the construction of projects that may have impacts on the ecological environment shall undergo an ecological and environmental impact assessment according to law.  
No development and utilization plan that has not undergone an ecological and environmental impact assessment according to law shall be organized for implementation; no construction project that has not undergone an ecological and environmental impact assessment according to law shall commence construction.

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条　生态环境影响评价应当客观、公开、公正，综合考虑规划或者建设项目实施后对各种生态环境因素及其所构成的生态系统可能造成的影响，为决策提供科学依据。  
国家鼓励有关单位、专家和公众依法参与生态环境影响评价。

**Article 84** The ecological and environmental impact assessment shall be objective, open, and fair, comprehensively considering the possible impacts on various ecological and environmental factors and the ecosystems composed thereof after the implementation of a plan or construction project, and shall provide a scientific basis for decision-making.  
The State encourages relevant entities, experts, and the public to participate in ecological and environmental impact assessment according to law.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条　国家加强生态环境影响评价的基础数据库和评价指标体系建设，鼓励、支持对生态环境影响评价的方法、技术规范进行科学研究，建立必要的生态环境影响评价信息共享制度，提高生态环境影响评价的科学性。  
国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门组织建立健全生态环境影响评价的基础数据库和评价指标体系。

**Article 85** The State shall strengthen the construction of basic databases and evaluation indicator systems for ecological and environmental impact assessment, encourage and support scientific research on the methods and technical specifications of ecological and environmental impact assessment, establish necessary information sharing systems for ecological and environmental impact assessment, and improve the scientific nature of ecological and environmental impact assessment.  
The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall, together with relevant departments, organize the establishment and improvement of basic databases and evaluation indicator systems for ecological and environmental impact assessment.

#### 第二节　规划的生态环境影响评价

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条　国务院有关部门、设区的市级以上地方人民政府及其有关部门，对其组织编制的国土空间规划，区域、流域、海域的建设、开发利用规划，应当在规划编制过程中组织进行生态环境影响评价，编写该规划有关生态环境影响的篇章或者说明。  
规划有关生态环境影响的篇章或者说明，应当对规划实施后可能造成的生态环境影响作出分析、预测和评估，提出预防或者减轻不良生态环境影响的对策和措施，作为规划草案的组成部分一并报送规划审批机关。  
未编写有关生态环境影响的篇章或者说明的规划草案，审批机关不予审批。

**Article 86** The relevant departments of the State Council, the local people's governments at or above the city with districts level and their relevant departments shall, in the process of formulating the national territory spatial planning, and the construction and development plans for regions, river basins, and sea areas, organize an ecological and environmental impact assessment and prepare a chapter or description on the ecological and environmental impacts of such plan.  
The chapter or description on the ecological and environmental impacts of the plan shall analyze, predict, and assess the possible ecological and environmental impacts after the implementation of the plan, propose countermeasures and measures to prevent or mitigate adverse ecological and environmental impacts, and shall be submitted together with the plan draft to the plan approving authority as an integral part thereof.  
The plan approving authority shall not approve a plan draft that does not contain a chapter or description on the ecological and environmental impacts.

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条　国务院有关部门、设区的市级以上地方人民政府及其有关部门，对其组织编制的工业、农业、林业、能源、水利、交通、城市和产业园区建设、旅游、自然资源开发的有关专项规划，应当在规划编制过程中组织进行生态环境影响评价，并向审批该专项规划的机关提出生态环境影响报告书。  
前款所列专项规划中的指导性规划，依照[本法第八十六条](#art-86)规定进行生态环境影响评价。

**Article 87** The relevant departments of the State Council, the local people's governments at or above the city with districts level and their relevant departments shall, in the process of formulating specific plans concerning industry, agriculture, forestry, energy, water conservancy, transportation, urban and industrial park construction, tourism, and natural resources development, organize an ecological and environmental impact assessment and submit a report on the ecological and environmental impact to the authority which shall approve such specific plan.  
The guiding plans among the specific plans listed in the preceding paragraph shall be subject to ecological and environmental impact assessment in accordance with Article 86 of this Law.

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条　依照[本法第八十六条](#art-86)、第八十七条规定进行生态环境影响评价的规划的具体范围，由国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门规定，报国务院批准。  
省、自治区、直辖市人民政府可以根据本地实际情况，要求对本辖区的县级人民政府编制的规划进行生态环境影响评价。具体办法由省、自治区、直辖市参照本节规定制定。

**Article 88** The specific scope of plans subject to ecological and environmental impact assessment in accordance with Articles 86 and 87 of this Law shall be prescribed by the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council together with relevant departments and shall be submitted to the State Council for approval.  
The people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government may, based on actual local conditions, require that plans formulated by the people's governments at the county level within their respective jurisdictions undergo ecological and environmental impact assessment. The specific measures shall be formulated by the provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government by reference to the provisions of this Section.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条　专项规划的生态环境影响报告书应当包括下列内容：  
（一）实施该规划对生态环境可能造成影响的分析、预测和评估；  
（二）预防或者减轻不良生态环境影响的对策和措施；  
（三）生态环境影响评价的结论。

**Article 89** The report on the ecological and environmental impact of a specific plan shall include the following:  
(1) Analysis, prediction, and assessment of the possible impacts of implementing the plan on the ecological environment;  
(2) Countermeasures and measures to prevent or mitigate adverse ecological and environmental impacts;  
(3) Conclusion of the ecological and environmental impact assessment.

<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条　专项规划的编制机关对可能造成不良生态环境影响并直接涉及公众生态环境权益的规划，应当在该规划草案报送审批前，举行论证会、听证会，或者采取其他形式，征求有关单位、专家和公众对生态环境影响报告书草案的意见。但是，国家规定不予公开的情形除外。  
编制机关应当认真考虑有关单位、专家和公众对生态环境影响报告书草案的意见，并在报送审查的生态环境影响报告书中附具对意见采纳或者不采纳的说明。

**Article 90** The organ formulating a specific plan which may cause adverse ecological and environmental impacts and directly involve the ecological and environmental rights and interests of the public shall, before submitting the plan draft for approval, hold demonstration meetings, hearings, or adopt other forms to solicit opinions from relevant entities, experts, and the public on the draft ecological and environmental impact report, except for circumstances that the State provides shall not be disclosed.  
The formulating organ shall carefully consider the opinions of relevant entities, experts, and the public on the draft ecological and environmental impact report and shall attach an explanation of whether such opinions have been adopted or not in the ecological and environmental impact report submitted for review.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条　专项规划的编制机关在报批规划草案时，应当将生态环境影响报告书一并附送审批机关审查；未附送生态环境影响报告书的，审批机关不予审批。

**Article 91** When submitting a plan draft for approval, the organ formulating a specific plan shall submit the ecological and environmental impact report together with it to the approving authority for review; if no ecological and environmental impact report is submitted, the approving authority shall not approve the plan.

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条　设区的市级以上人民政府在审批专项规划草案、作出决策前，应当先由其生态环境主管部门召集有关部门代表和专家组成审查小组，对生态环境影响报告书进行审查，并提出书面审查意见。  
参加前款规定的审查小组的专家，应当从按照国务院生态环境主管部门规定设立的专家库内相关专业的专家名单中，以随机抽取的方式确定。  
由省级以上人民政府有关部门负责审批的专项规划，其生态环境影响报告书的审查办法，由国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门制定。

**Article 92** Before approving a draft specific plan and making a decision, the people's government at or above the city with districts level shall first have its competent department of ecology and environment convene a review group composed of representatives from relevant departments and experts to review the ecological and environmental impact report and issue a written review opinion.  
The experts participating in the review group as prescribed in the preceding paragraph shall be determined by random selection from the list of experts in relevant specialties within the expert database established in accordance with the provisions of the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council.  
For specific plans to be approved by the relevant departments of the people's government at or above the provincial level, the review measures for their ecological and environmental impact reports shall be formulated by the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council together with relevant departments.

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条　审查小组提出修改意见的，专项规划的编制机关应当根据生态环境影响报告书结论和审查意见对规划草案进行修改完善，并对生态环境影响报告书结论和审查意见的采纳情况作出说明；不采纳的，应当说明理由。  
设区的市级以上人民政府或者省级以上人民政府有关部门在审批专项规划草案时，应当将生态环境影响报告书结论以及审查意见作为决策的重要依据。  
在审批中未采纳生态环境影响报告书结论以及审查意见的，应当作出说明，并存档备查。

**Article 93** If the review group proposes modification opinions, the organ formulating a specific plan shall revise and improve the plan draft based on the conclusions of the ecological and environmental impact report and the review opinions, and shall explain whether the conclusions and review opinions have been adopted; if not adopted, it shall state the reasons.  
The people's government at or above the city with districts level or the relevant departments of the people's government at or above the provincial level, when approving a draft specific plan, shall take the conclusions of the ecological and environmental impact report and the review opinions as important bases for decision-making.  
If the conclusions of the ecological and environmental impact report and the review opinions are not adopted in the approval, an explanation shall be provided and kept on file for future reference.

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条　对生态环境有重大影响的规划实施后，编制机关应当及时组织生态环境影响的跟踪评价，将评价结果报告审批机关，并通报生态环境等有关部门；发现有明显不良生态环境影响的，应当及时提出改进措施。

**Article 94** After the implementation of a plan that has a significant impact on the ecological environment, the formulating organ shall promptly organize a follow-up evaluation of the ecological and environmental impacts, report the evaluation results to the approving authority, and notify the ecology and environment department and other relevant departments; if any obvious adverse ecological and environmental impacts are found, it shall promptly propose improvement measures.

#### 第三节　建设项目的生态环境影响评价

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条　国家根据建设项目对生态环境的影响程度，对建设项目的生态环境影响评价实行分类管理。  
建设单位应当按照下列规定组织编制生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表或者填报生态环境影响登记表：  
（一）可能造成重大生态环境影响的，应当编制生态环境影响报告书，对产生的生态环境影响进行全面评价；  
（二）可能造成轻度生态环境影响的，应当编制生态环境影响报告表，对产生的生态环境影响进行分析或者专项评价；  
（三）对生态环境影响很小，不需要编制生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的，应当填报生态环境影响登记表。  
建设项目的生态环境影响评价分类管理名录，由国务院生态环境主管部门制定并公布。

**Article 95** The State shall implement classified management of ecological and environmental impact assessment of construction projects based on the degree of impact of the construction projects on the ecological environment.  
The construction unit shall, in accordance with the following provisions, organize the preparation of an ecological and environmental impact report, an ecological and environmental impact report form, or fill out an ecological and environmental impact registration form:  
(1) For projects that may cause significant ecological and environmental impacts, an ecological and environmental impact report shall be prepared to comprehensively evaluate the resulting ecological and environmental impacts;  
(2) For projects that may cause minor ecological and environmental impacts, an ecological and environmental impact report form shall be prepared to analyze or conduct a specific evaluation of the resulting ecological and environmental impacts;  
(3) For projects with minimal ecological and environmental impacts that do not require the preparation of an ecological and environmental impact report or report form, an ecological and environmental impact registration form shall be filled out.  
The classified management catalogue for ecological and environmental impact assessment of construction projects shall be formulated and published by the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条　建设项目的生态环境影响报告书应当包括下列内容：  
（一）建设项目概况；  
（二）建设项目周围生态环境现状；  
（三）建设项目对生态环境可能造成影响的分析、预测和评估；  
（四）建设项目生态环境保护措施及其技术、经济论证；  
（五）建设项目对生态环境影响的经济损益分析；  
（六）对建设项目实施生态环境监测和排放管理的建议；  
（七）生态环境影响评价的结论；  
（八）法律规定的其他内容。  
生态环境影响报告表和生态环境影响登记表的内容和格式，由国务院生态环境主管部门制定。

**Article 96** An ecological and environmental impact report of a construction project shall include the following:  
(1) Overview of the construction project;  
(2) Current status of the ecological environment around the construction project;  
(3) Analysis, prediction, and assessment of the possible impacts of the construction project on the ecological environment;  
(4) Ecological and environmental protection measures for the construction project and their technical and economic feasibility;  
(5) Economic cost-benefit analysis of the ecological and environmental impacts of the construction project;  
(6) Recommendations for implementing ecological and environmental monitoring and discharge management for the construction project;  
(7) Conclusion of the ecological and environmental impact assessment;  
(8) Other contents as prescribed by law.  
The content and format of the ecological and environmental impact report form and the ecological and environmental impact registration form shall be prescribed by the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条　建设项目的生态环境影响评价，应当避免与规划的生态环境影响评价相重复。  
作为一项整体建设项目的规划，按照建设项目进行生态环境影响评价，不进行规划的生态环境影响评价。  
已经进行了生态环境影响评价的规划包含具体建设项目的，规划的生态环境影响评价结论应当作为建设项目生态环境影响评价的重要依据，建设项目生态环境影响评价的内容应当根据规划的生态环境影响评价审查意见予以简化。

**Article 97** The ecological and environmental impact assessment of a construction project shall avoid duplication with the ecological and environmental impact assessment of a plan.  
A plan that serves as an overall construction project shall be subject to ecological and environmental impact assessment as a construction project and shall not be subject to ecological and environmental impact assessment as a plan.  
If a plan that has already undergone an ecological and environmental impact assessment contains specific construction projects, the conclusions of the ecological and environmental impact assessment of the plan shall serve as an important basis for the ecological and environmental impact assessment of the construction projects, and the content of the ecological and environmental impact assessment of the construction projects shall be simplified based on the review opinions of the ecological and environmental impact assessment of the plan.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条　建设单位可以委托技术单位对其建设项目开展生态环境影响评价，编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表；建设单位具备生态环境影响评价技术能力的，可以自行对其建设项目开展生态环境影响评价，编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表。  
编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表应当遵守国家有关生态环境影响评价标准、技术规范等规定。  
建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表编制的管理办法由国务院生态环境主管部门制定。

**Article 98** A construction unit may entrust a technical unit to conduct an ecological and environmental impact assessment for its construction project and prepare an ecological and environmental impact report or report form; if the construction unit has the technical capability for ecological and environmental impact assessment, it may conduct the ecological and environmental impact assessment for its construction project on its own and prepare the ecological and environmental impact report or report form.  
The preparation of an ecological and environmental impact report or report form of a construction project shall comply with the national standards, technical specifications, and other provisions related to ecological and environmental impact assessment.  
The administrative measures for the preparation of ecological and environmental impact reports and report forms of construction projects shall be formulated by the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条　接受委托为建设单位编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的技术单位应当依法独立客观公正开展业务，建立健全质量控制制度，确保所出具的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表客观、真实、准确。  
接受委托为建设单位编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的技术单位和人员，应当具有良好的信用记录，不得与负责审批建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的生态环境主管部门或者其他有关审批部门存在任何利益关系，编制、审核等关键岗位人员应当具有相关专业技术水平和从业经历。  
接受委托为建设单位编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的技术单位，应当报生态环境主管部门备案，生态环境主管部门应当加强对技术单位开展生态环境影响评价业务的监督管理。具体办法由国务院生态环境主管部门制定。

**Article 99** A technical unit that is entrusted to prepare an ecological and environmental impact report or report form of a construction project for a construction unit shall conduct business independently, objectively, and fairly according to law, establish and improve a quality control system, and ensure that the ecological and environmental impact report or report form issued is objective, truthful, and accurate.  
Technical units and personnel entrusted to prepare ecological and environmental impact reports or report forms of construction projects for construction units shall have a good credit record, and shall not have any interest relationship with the competent department of ecology and environment or other relevant approving authorities responsible for approving the ecological and environmental impact reports or report forms of construction projects; key personnel involved in preparation, review, and other critical positions shall have the relevant professional technical level and work experience.  
A technical unit entrusted to prepare an ecological and environmental impact report or report form of a construction project for a construction unit shall report to the competent department of ecology and environment for recordation, and the competent department of ecology and environment shall strengthen supervision and management over the technical unit's conduct of ecological and environmental impact assessment business. The specific measures shall be formulated by the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条　建设单位应当对建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的内容和结论负责，接受委托编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的技术单位对其编制的建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表承担相应责任。  
设区的市级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门应当加强对建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表编制单位和人员的监督管理和质量考核。  
负责审批建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的生态环境主管部门应当对编制单位、编制主持人和主要编制人员有关违法行为，依照法律规定进行信用监管。  
任何单位和个人不得为建设单位指定编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的技术单位。

**Article 100** A construction unit shall be responsible for the content and conclusions of the ecological and environmental impact report or report form of the construction project; the technical unit entrusted to prepare the ecological and environmental impact report or report form of the construction project shall bear corresponding liability for the ecological and environmental impact report or report form it has prepared.  
The competent department of ecology and environment of the people's government at or above the city with districts level shall strengthen supervision, management, and quality assessment of the units and personnel that prepare ecological and environmental impact reports or report forms of construction projects.  
The competent department of ecology and environment responsible for approving the ecological and environmental impact report or report form of a construction project shall, with respect to the illegal acts of the preparing unit, the principal preparing person, and the main preparing personnel, conduct credit supervision in accordance with legal provisions.  
No entity or individual may designate a technical unit for a construction unit to prepare an ecological and environmental impact report or report form of a construction project.

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条　除国家规定不予公开的情形外，对生态环境可能造成重大影响、应当编制生态环境影响报告书的建设项目，建设单位应当在报批建设项目生态环境影响报告书前，举行论证会、听证会，或者采取其他形式，向可能受影响的公众说明情况，充分征求有关单位、专家和公众的意见。  
建设单位报批的生态环境影响报告书应当附具对有关单位、专家和公众的意见采纳或者不采纳的说明。

**Article 101** Except for circumstances where disclosure is not permitted by state regulations, for a construction project that may have a significant impact on the ecological environment and for which an ecological environmental impact report shall be prepared, the construction entity shall, prior to submitting the ecological environmental impact report for approval, hold feasibility study meetings, public hearings, or adopt other forms to explain the situation to the public that may be affected, and fully solicit opinions from relevant entities, experts, and the public.  
The ecological environmental impact report submitted by the construction entity for approval shall include an explanation of whether the opinions of relevant entities, experts, and the public have been adopted or not.

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条　建设项目的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表，由建设单位按照规定报有审批权的生态环境主管部门审批。  
生态环境主管部门应当自收到生态环境影响报告书之日起六十日内，收到生态环境影响报告表之日起三十日内，分别作出审批决定并书面通知建设单位。  
国家对生态环境影响登记表实行备案管理。  
审批、审核建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表以及备案生态环境影响登记表，不得收取任何费用。

**Article 102** The ecological environmental impact report or the ecological environmental impact statement of a construction project shall be submitted by the construction entity to the competent ecological environmental department with approval authority for examination and approval in accordance with regulations.  
The competent ecological environmental department shall, within sixty days from the date of receiving the ecological environmental impact report and within thirty days from the date of receiving the ecological environmental impact statement, make a decision on the approval and notify the construction entity in writing.  
The state implements filing management for ecological environmental impact registration forms.  
No fees shall be charged for the examination and approval of ecological environmental impact reports and ecological environmental impact statements of construction projects, or for the filing of ecological environmental impact registration forms.

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条　生态环境主管部门在收到建设项目生态环境影响报告书后，除国家规定不予公开的情形外，应当全文公开；发现建设项目未充分征求公众意见的，应当责成建设单位征求公众意见。

**Article 103** After receiving the ecological environmental impact report of a construction project, the competent ecological environmental department shall disclose the full text thereof, except for circumstances where disclosure is not permitted by state regulations; if it finds that the construction project has not adequately solicited public opinions, it shall instruct the construction entity to solicit public opinions.

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条　国务院生态环境主管部门负责审批下列建设项目的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表：  
（一）核设施等特殊性质的建设项目；  
（二）跨省、自治区、直辖市行政区域的建设项目；  
（三）由国务院审批的或者由国务院授权有关部门审批的建设项目；  
（四）法律、行政法规规定的其他建设项目。  
前款规定以外的建设项目的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的审批权限，由省、自治区、直辖市人民政府规定，报国务院生态环境主管部门备案。  
建设项目可能造成跨行政区域的不良生态环境影响，有关生态环境主管部门对该项目的生态环境影响评价结论有争议的，其生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表由共同的上一级生态环境主管部门审批。

**Article 104** The competent ecological environmental department of the State Council shall be responsible for examining and approving the ecological environmental impact reports and ecological environmental impact statements of the following construction projects:  
(1) Construction projects of special nature such as nuclear facilities;  
(2) Construction projects that span the administrative regions of provinces, autonomous regions, or municipalities directly under the Central Government;  
(3) Construction projects approved by the State Council or authorized by the State Council for approval by relevant departments;  
(4) Other construction projects as provided by laws and administrative regulations.  
The authority for examining and approving ecological environmental impact reports and ecological environmental impact statements of construction projects other than those specified in the preceding paragraph shall be prescribed by the people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, or municipalities directly under the Central Government and shall be reported to the competent ecological environmental department of the State Council for filing.  
If a construction project may cause adverse ecological environmental impacts across administrative regions, and the relevant competent ecological environmental departments have disputes over the conclusion of the ecological environmental impact assessment of the project, the ecological environmental impact report or the ecological environmental impact statement thereof shall be examined and approved by the common competent ecological environmental department at the next higher level.

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条　建设项目有下列情形之一的，生态环境主管部门应当对生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表作出不予批准的决定：  
（一）建设项目类型及其选址、布局、规模等不符合生态环境法律法规规定和相关法定规划；  
（二）所在区域、流域、海域生态环境质量未达到生态环境质量标准，且建设项目拟采取的措施不能满足生态环境质量改善目标管理要求；  
（三）建设项目采取的污染防治措施无法确保污染物排放达到排放标准，或者未采取必要措施预防和控制生态破坏；  
（四）改建、扩建和技术改造项目，未针对项目原有环境污染和生态破坏提出有效防治措施；  
（五）建设项目的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表存在基础资料明显不实，内容存在重大缺陷、遗漏或者虚假，生态环境影响评价结论不正确或者不合理等严重质量问题。

**Article 105** Where a construction project falls under any of the following circumstances, the competent ecological environmental department shall make a decision not to approve the ecological environmental impact report or the ecological environmental impact statement:  
(1) The type, site selection, layout, and scale of the construction project do not comply with ecological environmental laws and regulations and relevant statutory plans;  
(2) The ecological environmental quality of the area, river basin, or sea area where the project is located does not meet the ecological environmental quality standards, and the measures proposed by the construction project cannot meet the management requirements for ecological environmental quality improvement targets;  
(3) The pollution prevention and control measures adopted by the construction project cannot ensure that pollutant emissions meet discharge standards, or necessary measures have not been taken to prevent and control ecological damage;  
(4) For reconstruction, expansion, and technological transformation projects, effective prevention and control measures have not been proposed for the original environmental pollution and ecological damage of the project;  
(5) The ecological environmental impact report or ecological environmental impact statement of the construction project has serious quality issues such as obviously false basic data, major defects, omissions, or falsehoods in content, or incorrect or unreasonable conclusions of ecological environmental impact assessment.

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条　建设项目的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表经批准后，建设项目的性质、规模、地点、采用的生产工艺或者防治污染、防止生态破坏的措施发生重大变动的，建设单位应当重新报批建设项目的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表。  
建设项目的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表自批准之日起超过五年，该项目方开工建设的，其生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表应当报原审批部门重新审核；原审批部门应当自收到建设项目生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表之日起十日内，将审核意见书面通知建设单位。

**Article 106** After the ecological environmental impact report or the ecological environmental impact statement of a construction project has been approved, if major changes occur in the nature, scale, location, production process adopted, or measures for pollution prevention and ecological damage prevention of the construction project, the construction entity shall re-submit the ecological environmental impact report or the ecological environmental impact statement of the construction project for approval.  
If more than five years have elapsed since the approval date of the ecological environmental impact report or the ecological environmental impact statement of the construction project before the project commences construction, the ecological environmental impact report or the ecological environmental impact statement thereof shall be submitted to the original approval department for re-examination; the original approval department shall, within ten days from the date of receiving the ecological environmental impact report or the ecological environmental impact statement of the construction project, notify the construction entity in writing of the re-examination opinion.

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条　建设项目的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表未依法经审批部门审查或者审查后未予批准的，或者未经原审批部门重新审核同意的，建设单位不得开工建设。

**Article 107** If the ecological environmental impact report or the ecological environmental impact statement of a construction project has not been examined by the approval department according to law, or has been examined but not approved, or has not been re-examined and agreed upon by the original approval department, the construction entity shall not commence construction.

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条　建设项目建设、运行过程中，建设单位应当实施生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表及其审批部门审批意见中提出的生态环境保护对策措施。

**Article 108** During the construction and operation of a construction project, the construction entity shall implement the ecological environmental protection countermeasures proposed in the ecological environmental impact report, the ecological environmental impact statement, and the approval opinions of the approval department thereof.

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条　建设项目建设、运行过程中产生不符合经审批的生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的情形的，建设单位应当组织生态环境影响的后评价，采取改进措施，并报原生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表审批部门和建设项目审批部门备案；原生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表审批部门也可以责成建设单位进行生态环境影响的后评价，采取改进措施。
