<a id="art-324"></a>第三百二十四条　单一水源供水城市的人民政府应当建设应急水源或者备用水源，有条件的地区可以开展区域联网供水。  
县级以上地方人民政府应当合理安排、布局农村饮用水水源，有条件的地区可以采取城镇供水管网延伸或者建设跨村、跨乡镇联片集中供水工程等方式，发展规模集中供水。

**Article 324** : The people's government of a city supplied by a single water source shall construct emergency water sources or backup water sources; conditions permitting, regional networked water supply may be carried out.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall reasonably arrange and distribute rural drinking water sources; conditions permitting, measures such as extending urban water supply pipe networks or constructing cross-village, cross-township joint centralized water supply projects may be adopted to develop large-scale centralized water supply.

<a id="art-325"></a>第三百二十五条　饮用水供水单位应当做好取水口和出水口的水质检测工作。发现取水口水质不符合饮用水水源水质标准或者出水口水质不符合饮用水卫生标准的，应当及时采取相应措施，并向所在地设区的市、县级人民政府供水主管部门报告。供水主管部门接到报告后，应当通报生态环境、卫生健康、水行政等有关部门。  
饮用水供水单位应当对供水水质负责，确保供水设施安全可靠运行，保证供水水质符合国家相关标准。

**Article 325** : A drinking water supply unit shall properly conduct water quality testing at the water intake and the water outlet. If it is found that the water quality at the intake does not meet the water quality standards for drinking water sources or the water quality at the outlet does not meet the hygienic standards for drinking water, it shall promptly take corresponding measures and report to the competent department of water supply of the local people's government at the city with districts or county level. Upon receipt of the report, the competent department of water supply shall notify the relevant departments of ecology and environment, health, and water administration.  
A drinking water supply unit shall be responsible for the quality of the supplied water, ensure the safe and reliable operation of water supply facilities, and guarantee that the quality of the supplied water meets the relevant national standards.

<a id="art-326"></a>第三百二十六条　县级以上地方人民政府应当组织有关部门监测、评估本行政区域饮用水水源、供水单位供水和用户水龙头出水的水质等饮用水安全状况。  
县级以上地方人民政府应当组织有关部门至少每季度向社会公开一次饮用水安全状况信息。

**Article 326** : Local people's governments at or above the county level shall organize relevant departments to monitor and assess the drinking water safety status within their administrative regions, including the water quality of drinking water sources, water supplied by water supply units, and water from users' taps.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall organize relevant departments to disclose to the public information on drinking water safety status at least once every quarter.

<a id="art-327"></a>第三百二十七条　省级以上人民政府根据水生态环境保护的需要，可以规定在饮用水水源保护区内，采取禁止或者限制使用含磷洗涤剂、农药、化肥以及限制种植养殖等措施。

**Article 327** : The people's governments at or above the provincial level may, in accordance with the needs of water ecological environment protection, prescribe measures such as prohibiting or restricting the use of phosphorus-containing detergents, pesticides, and chemical fertilizers, as well as restricting cultivation and breeding, in drinking water source protection zones.

<a id="art-328"></a>第三百二十八条　县级以上人民政府可以对自然保护地水体、重要渔业水体和其他具有特殊经济文化价值的水体划定保护区，并采取有效措施，保证保护区的水质符合规定用途的水生态环境质量标准。

**Article 328** : People's governments at or above the county level may delineate protection zones for water bodies in nature reserves, important fishery water bodies, and other water bodies with special economic or cultural value, and take effective measures to ensure that the water quality in the protection zones meets the water ecological environment quality standards for the designated uses.

<a id="art-329"></a>第三百二十九条　在自然保护地水体、重要渔业水体和其他具有特殊经济文化价值的水体的保护区内，禁止新设排污口；法律、行政法规另有规定的除外。在保护区附近新设排污口，应当科学论证，制定水污染防治方案，采取有效措施保证保护区水体不受污染。

**Article 329** : It is prohibited to set up new sewage outlets within the protection zones of water bodies in nature reserves, important fishery water bodies, and other water bodies with special economic or cultural value, unless otherwise provided by laws and administrative regulations. Where a new sewage outlet is to be set up near a protection zone, a scientific demonstration shall be conducted, a water pollution prevention plan shall be formulated, and effective measures shall be taken to ensure that the water body within the protection zone is not polluted.

### 第十章　重点流域水污染防治

<a id="art-330"></a>第三百三十条　国家加强重点流域水污染的综合治理、系统治理、源头治理，推进重点河湖综合整治。  
国务院生态环境等有关部门和重点流域地方各级人民政府应当采取有效措施，加大对重点流域的水污染防治力度，预防、控制和减少水环境污染。

**Article 330** : The state shall strengthen comprehensive, systematic, and source-oriented treatment of water pollution in key river basins, and promote integrated remediation of key rivers and lakes.  
The relevant departments of the State Council, such as the department of ecology and environment, and the local people's governments at all levels in key river basins shall take effective measures to intensify water pollution prevention and control in key river basins, and to prevent, control, and reduce water environmental pollution.

<a id="art-331"></a>第三百三十一条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同国务院水行政等有关部门和有关省、自治区、直辖市人民政府，建立健全重要江河湖泊的流域水生态环境联合保护协调机制。

**Article 331** : The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments of the State Council such as the department of water administration and the relevant people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government, establish and improve a joint protection and coordination mechanism for the water ecological environment of the river basins of important rivers and lakes.

<a id="art-332"></a>第三百三十二条　对没有国家水污染物排放标准的特色产业、特有污染物，以及国家有明确要求的特定水污染源或者水污染物，重点流域省、自治区、直辖市人民政府应当补充制定地方水污染物排放标准，报国务院生态环境主管部门备案。  
有下列情形之一的，重点流域省、自治区、直辖市人民政府应当制定严于国家水污染物排放标准的地方水污染物排放标准，报国务院生态环境主管部门备案：  
（一）产业密集、水环境污染问题突出；  
（二）现有水污染物排放标准不能满足重点流域水生态环境质量要求；  
（三）流域或者区域水生态环境形势复杂，无法适用统一的水污染物排放标准。

**Article 332** : For characteristic industries and unique pollutants for which there are no national water pollutant discharge standards, as well as specific water pollution sources or water pollutants for which the state has clear requirements, the people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government in key river basins shall supplement local water pollutant discharge standards and report them to the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council for recordation.  
In any of the following circumstances, the people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government in key river basins shall formulate local water pollutant discharge standards that are stricter than the national water pollutant discharge standards, and report them to the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council for recordation:  
(1) The industry is intensive and the problem of water environmental pollution is prominent;  
(2) The existing water pollutant discharge standards cannot meet the water ecological environment quality requirements of the key river basin;  
(3) The water ecological environment situation of the river basin or region is complex, making it impossible to apply uniform water pollutant discharge standards.

<a id="art-333"></a>第三百三十三条　重点流域产业结构和布局应当与其生态环境的承载能力相适应。禁止重污染企业和项目向重点流域中上游转移。  
第四分编　海洋污染防治

**Article 333** : The industrial structure and layout in key river basins shall be compatible with the carrying capacity of their ecological environment. The transfer of heavily polluting enterprises and projects to the middle and upper reaches of key river basins is prohibited.  
Part 4: Prevention and Control of Marine Pollution

### 第十一章　一般规定

<a id="art-334"></a>第三百三十四条　本分编适用于海洋污染的防治。

**Article 334** : This Part applies to the prevention and control of marine pollution.

<a id="art-335"></a>第三百三十五条　本法所称海洋污染，是指直接或者间接地把物质或者能量引入海洋，导致损害海洋生物资源、危害人体健康、妨害渔业和海上其他合法活动、损害海水使用素质和减损生态环境质量的现象。

**Article 335** : For the purposes of this Law, "marine pollution" means the phenomenon of introducing substances or energy directly or indirectly into the sea, resulting in harm to marine living resources, hazards to human health, hindrance to fisheries and other lawful activities at sea, impairment of the quality of seawater for use, and degradation of the quality of the ecological environment.

<a id="art-336"></a>第三百三十六条　国务院生态环境主管部门负责全国防治陆源污染物、海岸工程和海洋工程建设项目、海洋倾倒废弃物对海洋环境污染损害的工作。  
国务院交通运输主管部门负责所辖港区水域内非军事船舶和港区水域外非渔业、非军事船舶污染海洋环境的监督管理，组织、协调、指挥重大海上溢油应急处置。海事管理机构具体负责上述水域内相关船舶污染海洋环境的监督管理，并负责污染事故的调查处理；对在中华人民共和国管辖海域航行、停泊和作业的外国籍船舶造成的污染事故登轮检查处理。船舶污染事故给渔业造成损害的，应当吸收渔业渔政主管部门参与调查处理。  
国务院渔业渔政主管部门负责渔港水域内非军事船舶和渔港水域外渔业船舶污染海洋环境的监督管理，负责调查处理前款规定的污染事故以外的渔业污染事故。  
军队生态环境保护部门负责军事船舶污染海洋环境的监督管理及污染事故的调查处理。

**Article 336** : The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall be responsible for the work of preventing and controlling pollution damage to the marine environment caused by land-sourced pollutants, coastal engineering and marine engineering construction projects, and marine dumping of wastes nationwide.  
The competent department of transport of the State Council shall be responsible for the supervision and management of pollution of the marine environment by non-military vessels within the port areas under its jurisdiction and by non-fishing and non-military vessels outside port areas, and shall organize, coordinate, and direct the emergency response to major marine oil spills. The maritime administration agency shall be specifically responsible for the supervision and management of pollution of the marine environment by relevant vessels in the above-mentioned waters, and shall be responsible for the investigation and handling of pollution accidents; for pollution accidents caused by foreign vessels navigating, berthing, or operating in the sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China, it shall board and inspect and handle the cases. Where a vessel pollution accident causes damage to fisheries, the competent fisheries and fishery administration department shall be involved in the investigation and handling.  
The competent fisheries and fishery administration department of the State Council shall be responsible for the supervision and management of pollution of the marine environment by non-military vessels within fishing port waters and by fishing vessels outside fishing port waters, and shall be responsible for investigating and handling fishery pollution accidents other than those provided for in the preceding paragraph.  
The ecological and environmental protection department of the military shall be responsible for the supervision and management of pollution of the marine environment by military vessels and for the investigation and handling of pollution accidents.

<a id="art-337"></a>第三百三十七条　跨部门的重大海洋污染防治工作，由国务院生态环境主管部门协调；协调未能解决的，由国务院作出决定。

**Article 337** : Cross-departmental major marine pollution prevention and control work shall be coordinated by the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council; if coordination fails to resolve the matter, the State Council shall make a decision.

<a id="art-338"></a>第三百三十八条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门、机构和沿海省、自治区、直辖市人民政府编制全国海洋生态环境保护规划，报国务院批准后实施。  
沿海地方各级人民政府应当根据全国海洋生态环境保护规划，组织实施其管理海域的海洋生态环境保护工作。

**Article 338** : The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, agencies, and the people's governments of coastal provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government, formulate a national marine ecological environment protection plan, which shall be implemented after approval by the State Council.  
Local people's governments at all levels in coastal areas shall, in accordance with the national marine ecological environment protection plan, organize and implement marine ecological environment protection work in the sea areas under their administration.

<a id="art-339"></a>第三百三十九条　国务院生态环境主管部门负责海洋生态环境监测工作，组织实施海洋生态环境质量监测，统一发布国家海洋生态环境状况公报，定期组织对海洋生态环境质量状况进行调查评价。

**Article 339** : The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall be responsible for marine ecological environment monitoring work, organize and implement monitoring of marine ecological environment quality, uniformly release the national bulletin on the status of the marine ecological environment, and regularly organize investigations and assessments of the status of marine ecological environment quality.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条　国务院有关部门和海警机构应当向国务院生态环境主管部门提供编制国家海洋生态环境状况公报所必需的入海河口和海洋生态环境监测、调查、监视等方面的资料。  
生态环境主管部门应当向有关部门和海警机构提供与海洋生态环境监督管理有关的资料。  
本法所称入海河口，是指河流终端与受水体（海）相结合的地段。

**Article 340** : The relevant departments of the State Council and the coast guard agencies shall provide the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council with data necessary for compiling the national bulletin on the status of the marine ecological environment, including data on monitoring, investigation, and surveillance of estuaries and the marine ecological environment.  
The competent department of ecology and environment shall provide the relevant departments and coast guard agencies with data related to the supervision and management of the marine ecological environment.  
For the purposes of this Law, "estuary" means the section where the terminal of a river joins the receiving water body (the sea).

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条　国家加强海洋辐射环境监测，国务院生态环境主管部门负责制定海洋辐射环境应急监测方案并组织实施。

**Article 341** The State shall strengthen marine radiation environment monitoring. The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall be responsible for formulating marine radiation environment emergency monitoring plans and organizing their implementation.

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条　企业事业单位和其他生产经营者应当优先使用清洁低碳能源，采用资源利用率高、污染物排放量少的清洁生产工艺，防止对海洋环境的污染。

**Article 342** Enterprises, public institutions, and other producers and business operators shall give priority to the use of clean and low-carbon energy, adopt clean production processes with high resource utilization rates and low pollutant emissions, and prevent pollution to the marine environment.

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条　沿海县级以上地方人民政府因地制宜依法编制并组织实施养殖水域滩涂规划，确定可以用于养殖业的水域和滩涂，科学划定海水养殖限养区和养殖区，建立禁养区内海水养殖的清理和退出机制。  
从事海水养殖活动应当保护海域生态环境，及时规范收集处理固体废物，防止造成海洋生态环境的损害。  
向海洋排放养殖尾水污染物等应当符合污染物排放标准。沿海省、自治区、直辖市人民政府应当制定海水养殖污染物排放相关地方标准，加强养殖尾水污染防治的监督管理。工厂化养殖和设置统一排污口的集中连片养殖的单位和其他生产经营者，应当按照规定对养殖尾水自行监测。  
禁止在氮磷浓度严重超标的近岸海域新增或者扩大投饵、投肥、投饲海水养殖规模。

**Article 343** The local people's governments at or above the county level in coastal areas shall, according to law, formulate and organize the implementation of plans for aquaculture waters and tidal flats according to local conditions, determine the waters and tidal flats that can be used for aquaculture, scientifically designate restricted aquaculture areas and aquaculture areas for mariculture, and establish mechanisms for the cleanup and withdrawal of mariculture in prohibited aquaculture areas.  
Persons engaged in mariculture activities shall protect the ecological environment of the sea area, collect and dispose of solid waste in a timely and standardized manner, and prevent damage to the marine ecological environment.  
The discharge of aquaculture tailwater pollutants and the like into the sea shall comply with the pollutant discharge standards. The people's governments of coastal provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government shall formulate relevant local standards for the discharge of mariculture pollutants and strengthen the supervision and management of the prevention and control of aquaculture tailwater pollution. Units and other producers and business operators engaged in factory farming and concentrated contiguous aquaculture with a unified sewage outlet shall conduct self-monitoring of aquaculture tailwater in accordance with the provisions.  
It is prohibited to increase or expand the scale of mariculture involving baiting, fertilization, or feeding in the coastal sea areas where the concentrations of nitrogen and phosphorus seriously exceed the standards.

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条　因发生事故或者其他突发性事件，造成或者可能造成海洋环境污染、生态破坏事件的单位和个人，应当立即采取有效措施解除或者减轻危害，及时通报可能受到危害者，并向负有海洋生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构报告，接受调查处理。  
沿海县级以上地方人民政府在本行政区域近岸海域的生态环境受到严重损害时，应当采取有效措施，解除或者减轻危害。

**Article 344** Units and individuals that cause or may cause marine environmental pollution or ecological damage incidents due to accidents or other emergencies shall immediately take effective measures to eliminate or mitigate the harm, promptly notify those who may be endangered, and report to the departments and institutions responsible for the supervision and management of marine ecological environment protection, and accept investigation and handling.  
When the ecological environment of the coastal sea areas within their administrative regions suffers serious damage, the local people's governments at or above the county level in coastal areas shall take effective measures to eliminate or mitigate the harm.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条　国家根据防止海洋环境污染的需要，制定国家重大海上污染事件应急预案，建立健全海上溢油污染等应急机制，保障应对工作的必要经费。  
国家建立健全重大海上溢油应急处置部际联席会议制度。国务院交通运输主管部门牵头组织制定国家重大海上溢油应急处置预案并组织实施。  
国务院生态环境主管部门负责制定全国海洋石油勘探开发海上溢油污染事件应急预案并组织实施。  
国家海事管理机构负责制定全国船舶重大海上溢油污染事件应急预案，报国务院生态环境主管部门、应急管理部门备案。  
沿海县级以上地方人民政府及其有关部门应当制定有关应急预案，在发生海洋污染事件时，及时启动应急预案，采取有效措施，解除或者减轻危害。  
可能发生海洋污染事件的单位，应当按照规定制定本单位的应急预案，配备应急设备和器材，定期组织开展应急演练；应急预案应当报负有海洋生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构备案。

**Article 345** The State shall, based on the need to prevent marine environmental pollution, formulate national emergency plans for major marine pollution incidents, establish and improve emergency mechanisms such as for offshore oil spill pollution, and ensure the necessary funds for response work.  
The State shall establish and improve a ministerial joint conference system for major offshore oil spill emergency response. The competent department of transport of the State Council shall take the lead in organizing the formulation of the national emergency response plan for major offshore oil spills and organizing its implementation.  
The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall be responsible for formulating the national emergency plan for offshore oil spill pollution incidents from marine petroleum exploration and development and organizing its implementation.  
The national maritime safety administration shall be responsible for formulating the national emergency plan for major offshore oil spill pollution incidents from ships and reporting it to the competent department of ecological environment and the emergency management department of the State Council for recordation.  
The local people's governments at or above the county level in coastal areas and their relevant departments shall formulate relevant emergency plans, and when a marine pollution incident occurs, promptly activate the emergency plan, take effective measures, and eliminate or mitigate the harm.  
Units that may cause marine pollution incidents shall formulate their own emergency plans in accordance with the provisions, equip themselves with emergency equipment and devices, and regularly organize emergency drills; the emergency plans shall be reported to the departments and institutions responsible for the supervision and management of marine ecological environment protection for recordation.

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条　负有海洋生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构，有权对从事影响海洋生态环境活动的单位和个人进行监督检查，可以在海上开展联合执法；在巡航监视中发现违法行为时，应当依法予以制止并调查取证，必要时有权采取有效措施，防止事态扩大，并报告有关部门、机构处理。

**Article 346** The departments and institutions responsible for the supervision and management of marine ecological environment protection shall have the authority to supervise and inspect units and individuals engaged in activities affecting the marine ecological environment, and may conduct joint law enforcement at sea; when discovering illegal acts during cruise surveillance, they shall stop them according to law and collect evidence, and where necessary, have the authority to take effective measures to prevent the situation from escalating, and report to the relevant departments and institutions for handling.

### 第十二章　陆源污染物海洋污染防治

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条　向海域排放陆源污染物，应当严格执行有关规定和标准。  
本法所称陆源，是陆地污染源的简称，是指从陆地向海域排放污染物，造成或者可能造成海洋环境污染的场所、设施等。  
本法所称陆源污染物，是指由陆地污染源排放的污染物。

**Article 347** The discharge of land-sourced pollutants into the sea shall strictly comply with the relevant provisions and standards.  
For the purposes of this Law, 'land source' is the abbreviation of land-based pollution source, referring to the places, facilities, etc. that discharge pollutants from land to the sea, causing or likely to cause marine environmental pollution.  
For the purposes of this Law, 'land-sourced pollutants' means pollutants discharged from land-based pollution sources.

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条　入海排污口位置的选择，应当符合国土空间用途管制要求，根据海水动力条件和有关规定，经科学论证后，报设区的市级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门备案。入海排污口的责任主体应当加强入海排污口监测，按照规定开展监控、自动监测。  
生态环境主管部门应当在完成备案后十五个工作日内将入海排污口设置情况通报自然资源、海事、渔业渔政等有关部门和海警机构、军队生态环境保护部门。  
沿海县级以上地方人民政府应当根据入海排污口类别、责任主体，组织有关部门对本行政区域的各类入海排污口进行排查整治和日常监督管理，建立健全近岸海域、入海排污口、排污管线、污染源全链条治理体系。  
国务院生态环境主管部门负责制定入海排污口设置和管理的具体办法，制定入海排污口技术规范，组织建设统一的入海排污口信息平台，加强动态更新、信息共享和公开。  
本法所称入海排污口，是指海岸线向海一侧，直接或者通过管道、沟、渠等排污通道向海洋排放污水的口门，包括工业排污口、城镇污水集中处理设施排污口、农业排口及其他排口等类型。

**Article 348** The selection of the location of a sea outfall shall comply with the requirements for territorial space use control, and after scientific demonstration based on seawater dynamic conditions and relevant provisions, it shall be reported to the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government at or above the city with districts level for recordation. The responsible entity for the sea outfall shall strengthen the monitoring of the sea outfall, and carry out monitoring and automatic monitoring in accordance with the provisions.  
The competent department of ecological environment shall, within fifteen working days after completing the recordation, notify the relevant departments such as natural resources, maritime, fisheries and fishing administration, as well as the coast guard and the military ecological environment protection department of the establishment of the sea outfall.  
The local people's governments at or above the county level in coastal areas shall, based on the categories and responsible entities of sea outfalls, organize relevant departments to conduct investigation, rectification, and daily supervision and management of various types of sea outfalls within their administrative regions, and establish a comprehensive chain governance system for coastal sea areas, sea outfalls, discharge pipelines, and pollution sources.  
The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall be responsible for formulating specific measures for the establishment and management of sea outfalls, formulating technical specifications for sea outfalls, organizing the construction of a unified information platform for sea outfalls, and strengthening dynamic updates, information sharing, and disclosure.  
For the purposes of this Law, 'sea outfall' means the opening on the seaward side of the coastline that directly or through pipes, ditches, canals, and other discharge channels discharges sewage into the sea, including types such as industrial discharge outlets, discharge outlets of centralized urban sewage treatment facilities, agricultural outlets, and other outlets.

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条　在自然保护地、重要渔业水域、海水浴场、生态保护红线区域及其他需要特别保护的区域，禁止新设工业排污口和城镇污水集中处理设施排污口；法律、行政法规另有规定的除外。  
在有条件的地区，应当将入海排污口深水设置，实行离岸排放。

**Article 349** In natural protected areas, important fishery waters, seawater bathing beaches, areas within the ecological protection red line, and other areas requiring special protection, it is prohibited to newly set up industrial discharge outlets and discharge outlets of centralized urban sewage treatment facilities, unless otherwise provided by laws and administrative regulations.  
In areas where conditions permit, sea outfalls shall be set in deep water and offshore discharge shall be implemented.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条　经开放式沟、渠向海洋排放污染物的，对开放式沟、渠按照有关规定和标准实施水生态环境质量管理。

**Article 350** Where pollutants are discharged into the sea through open ditches or canals, the water ecological environment quality management of the open ditches or canals shall be implemented in accordance with the relevant provisions and standards.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条　国务院有关部门、县级以上地方人民政府及其有关部门应当按照河海联动的原则，依照水污染防治有关法律、行政法规的规定，加强入海河流管理，协同推进入海河流污染防治，使入海河口的水质符合入海河口生态环境质量要求。  
入海河流流域省、自治区、直辖市人民政府应当按照国家规定，加强入海总氮、总磷排放的管控，制定控制方案并组织实施。

**Article 351** The relevant departments of the State Council, the local people's governments at or above the county level and their relevant departments shall, in accordance with the principle of river-sea coordination and pursuant to the relevant laws and administrative regulations on water pollution prevention and control, strengthen the management of rivers entering the sea, collaboratively promote the prevention and control of pollution in rivers entering the sea, so that the water quality of the river estuaries entering the sea meets the ecological environment quality requirements of the river estuaries entering the sea.  
The people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government in the basins of rivers entering the sea shall, in accordance with the state provisions, strengthen the control of the discharge of total nitrogen and total phosphorus into the sea, formulate control plans and organize their implementation.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条　禁止向海域排放油类、酸液、碱液、剧毒废液。  
禁止向海域排放污染海洋环境、破坏海洋生态的放射性废水。  
严格控制向海域排放含有不易降解的有机物和重金属的废水。

**Article 352** It is prohibited to discharge oils, acid liquids, alkali liquids, or highly toxic waste liquids into the sea.  
It is prohibited to discharge radioactive wastewater that pollutes the marine environment or damages the marine ecology into the sea.  
The discharge of wastewater containing non-degradable organic matters and heavy metals into the sea shall be strictly controlled.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条　含病原体的医疗污水、生活污水和工业废水应当经过处理，符合有关排放标准后，方可排入海域。

**Article 353** Medical sewage, domestic sewage, and industrial wastewater containing pathogens shall be treated and, after meeting the relevant discharge standards, may be discharged into the sea.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条　含有机物和营养物质的工业废水、生活污水，应当严格控制向海湾、半封闭海及其他自净能力较差的海域排放。

**Article 354** The discharge of industrial wastewater and domestic sewage containing organic matters and nutrients into bays, semi-enclosed seas, and other sea areas with poor self-purification capacity shall be strictly controlled.

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条　向海域排放含热废水，应当采取有效措施，保证邻近自然保护地、渔业水域的水温符合海洋生态环境质量标准，避免对珍稀濒危海洋生物、海洋水产资源造成危害。

**Article 355** Where thermal wastewater is discharged into the sea, effective measures shall be taken to ensure that the water temperature of adjacent natural protected areas and fishery waters complies with the marine ecological environment quality standards, and to avoid harm to rare and endangered marine organisms and marine fishery resources.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条　沿海地方各级人民政府应当加强农业面源污染防治。沿海农田、林场施用化学农药，应当执行国家农药安全使用的规定和标准。沿海农田、林场应当合理使用化肥和植物生长调节剂。

**Article 356** The local people's governments at all levels in coastal areas shall strengthen the prevention and control of agricultural non-point source pollution. The application of chemical pesticides in coastal farmlands and forest farms shall comply with the national provisions and standards for safe use of pesticides. Coastal farmlands and forest farms shall use chemical fertilizers and plant growth regulators rationally.

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条　在沿海陆域弃置、堆放和处理尾矿、矿渣、煤灰渣、垃圾和其他固体废物的，依照法律规定执行，并采取有效措施防止固体废物进入海洋。  
禁止在岸滩弃置、堆放或者处理固体废物；法律、行政法规另有规定的除外。  
本法所称沿海陆域，是指与海岸相连，或者通过管道、沟、渠、设施，直接或者间接向海洋排放污染物及其相关活动的一带区域。

**Article 357** The abandonment, stacking, and treatment of tailings, slag, coal ash, garbage, and other solid waste in coastal land areas shall be carried out in accordance with the provisions of the law, and effective measures shall be taken to prevent solid waste from entering the sea.  
It is prohibited to abandon, stack, or treat solid waste on the shore and beach, unless otherwise provided by laws and administrative regulations.  
For the purposes of this Law, 'coastal land area' means the area adjacent to the coast, or the area where pollutants are directly or indirectly discharged into the sea through pipes, ditches, canals, facilities, and related activities.

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条　沿海县级以上地方人民政府负责其管理海域的海洋垃圾污染防治，建立健全海洋垃圾监测、清理制度，统筹规划、建设陆域接收、转运、处理海洋垃圾的设施，明确有关部门、乡镇、街道、企业事业单位等的海洋垃圾管控区域，建立海洋垃圾监测、拦截、收集、打捞、运输、处理体系并组织实施，采取有效措施鼓励、支持公众参与上述活动。  
国务院生态环境、住房城乡建设、发展改革等有关部门应当在各自职责范围内加强海洋垃圾污染防治的监督指导和保障。

**Article 358** The local people's governments at or above the county level in coastal areas shall be responsible for the prevention and control of marine garbage pollution in the sea areas under their management, establish and improve a system for monitoring and cleaning up marine garbage, make overall plans and construct facilities for receiving, transferring, and treating marine garbage on land, designate the marine garbage control areas for relevant departments, townships, sub-districts, enterprises, public institutions, etc., establish and organize the implementation of a system for monitoring, intercepting, collecting, salvaging, transporting, and treating marine garbage, and take effective measures to encourage and support public participation in the above activities.  
The relevant departments of the State Council, such as ecological environment, housing and urban-rural construction, and development and reform, shall, within their respective responsibilities, strengthen the supervision, guidance, and guarantee for the prevention and control of marine garbage pollution.

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条　禁止经中华人民共和国内水、领海过境转移危险废物。  
经中华人民共和国管辖的其他海域转移危险废物的，应当事先取得国务院生态环境主管部门的书面同意。  
本法所称内水，是指我国领海基线向内陆一侧的所有海域。

**Article 359** It is prohibited to transit and transfer hazardous waste through the internal waters and territorial sea of the People's Republic of China.  
To transfer hazardous waste through other sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China, prior written consent from the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall be obtained.  
For the purposes of this Law, 'internal waters' means all sea areas on the landward side of the baseline of the territorial sea of China.

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条　沿海县级以上地方人民政府应当建设和完善排水管网，根据改善海洋生态环境质量的需要建设污水处理设施，加强城乡污水处理。  
建设污水海洋处置工程，应当符合国家规定。

**Article 360** The local people's governments at or above the county level in coastal areas shall construct and improve drainage pipe networks, construct sewage treatment facilities based on the need to improve the quality of the marine ecological environment, and strengthen urban and rural sewage treatment.  
The construction of marine sewage disposal projects shall comply with the state provisions.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条　国家采取必要措施，防止、减少和控制来自大气层或者通过大气层造成的海洋污染。

**Article 361** The State shall take necessary measures to prevent, reduce and control marine pollution from or through the atmosphere.

### 第十三章　工程建设项目海洋污染防治

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条　新建、改建、扩建海岸工程和海洋工程建设项目，应当遵守国家有关建设项目生态环境保护的规定，并把污染防治和生态保护所需资金纳入建设项目投资计划。  
禁止在依法划定的自然保护地、重要渔业水域及其他需要特别保护的区域，违法建设污染环境、破坏生态的海岸工程和海洋工程建设项目或者从事其他活动。

**Article 362** The new construction, reconstruction, or expansion of coastal engineering and marine engineering construction projects shall comply with the state provisions on ecological and environmental protection of construction projects, and shall incorporate the funds required for pollution prevention and ecological protection into the investment plan of the construction project.  
It is prohibited to illegally construct coastal engineering and marine engineering construction projects that pollute the environment and damage the ecology, or engage in other activities, within the nature reserves, important fishing waters and other areas requiring special protection as delineated according to law.

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条　禁止在沿海陆域建设不符合国家产业政策的化学制浆造纸、化工、印染、制革、电镀、酿造、炼油、岸边冲滩拆船及其他严重污染海洋环境的生产项目。

**Article 363** It is prohibited to construct, in the coastal land areas, production projects of chemical pulping and papermaking, chemical industry, printing and dyeing, tanning, electroplating, brewing, oil refining, beach beaching and ship breaking, and other production projects that seriously pollute the marine environment, which do not conform to the state industrial policies.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条　从岸上打井开采海底矿产资源，应当采取有效措施，防止污染海洋环境。

**Article 364** Where drilling wells from shore to exploit seabed mineral resources, effective measures shall be taken to prevent pollution of the marine environment.

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条　海岸工程和海洋工程建设项目不得使用含超标放射性物质或者易溶出有毒有害物质的材料；不得造成领海基点及其周边环境的侵蚀、淤积、损害，不得危及领海基点的稳定。

**Article 365** Coastal engineering and marine engineering construction projects shall not use materials containing radioactive substances exceeding the standard or toxic and hazardous substances that are easily leached; shall not cause erosion, siltation, or damage to the base points of the territorial sea and their surrounding environment, and shall not endanger the stability of the base points of the territorial sea.

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条　海岸工程和海洋工程建设项目需要爆破作业时，应当采取有效措施，保护海洋生态环境。  
海洋石油勘探开发及输油过程中，应当采取有效措施，避免溢油事故的发生。

**Article 366** Where blasting operations are required for coastal engineering and marine engineering construction projects, effective measures shall be taken to protect the marine ecological environment.  
In the process of offshore oil exploration, development and oil transportation, effective measures shall be taken to avoid the occurrence of oil spill accidents.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条　海岸工程和海洋工程建设项目不得违法向海洋排放污染物、废弃物及其他有害物质。  
海洋油气钻井平台（船）、生产生活平台、生产储卸装置等海洋油气装备的含油污水和油性混合物，应当经过处理达标后排放；残油、废油应当予以回收，不得排放入海。  
钻井所使用的油基泥浆和其他有毒复合泥浆不得排放入海。水基泥浆和无毒复合泥浆及钻屑的排放，应当符合国家规定。

**Article 367** Coastal engineering and marine engineering construction projects shall not illegally discharge pollutants, wastes and other harmful substances into the sea.  
The oily wastewater and oily mixtures from marine oil and gas equipment such as offshore oil and gas drilling platforms (vessels), production and living platforms, and production storage and offloading units shall be discharged after treatment to meet the standards; residual oil and waste oil shall be recovered and shall not be discharged into the sea.  
Oil-based mud and other toxic composite muds used for drilling shall not be discharged into the sea. The discharge of water-based mud, non-toxic composite mud and drill cuttings shall comply with the state provisions.

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条　海洋油气钻井平台（船）、生产生活平台、生产储卸装置等海洋油气装备及其有关海上设施，不得向海域处置含油的工业固体废物。处置其他固体废物，不得造成海洋环境污染。

**Article 368** Marine oil and gas equipment such as offshore oil and gas drilling platforms (vessels), production and living platforms, and production storage and offloading units, and related offshore facilities, shall not dispose of oily industrial solid wastes into the sea areas. The disposal of other solid wastes shall not cause marine environmental pollution.

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条　海上试油时，应当确保油气充分燃烧，油和油性混合物不得排放入海。

**Article 369** During offshore well testing, it shall be ensured that the oil and gas are fully burned, and oil and oily mixtures shall not be discharged into the sea.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条　勘探开发海洋油气资源，应当按照规定制定油气污染应急预案，报国务院生态环境主管部门海域派出机构备案。

**Article 370** When exploring and exploiting offshore oil and gas resources, an emergency response plan for oil and gas pollution shall be formulated according to the provisions and submitted to the sea area agency of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council for recordation.

### 第十四章　废弃物倾倒海洋污染防治

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条　任何个人和未经批准的单位，不得向中华人民共和国管辖海域倾倒任何废弃物。  
需要倾倒废弃物的，产生废弃物的单位应当向国务院生态环境主管部门海域派出机构提出书面申请，并出具废弃物特性和成分检验报告，取得倾倒许可证后，方可倾倒。  
国家鼓励疏浚物等废弃物的综合利用，避免或者减少海洋倾倒。  
禁止中华人民共和国境外的废弃物在中华人民共和国管辖海域倾倒。

**Article 371** No individual or unit without approval may dump any waste into the sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China.  
Where waste needs to be dumped, the unit generating the waste shall submit a written application to the sea area agency of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council, and provide a test report on the characteristics and composition of the waste, and may dump only after obtaining a dumping permit.  
The State encourages the comprehensive utilization of waste such as dredged materials to avoid or reduce marine dumping.  
It is prohibited to dump wastes from outside the territory of the People's Republic of China into the sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条　国务院生态环境主管部门根据废弃物的毒性、有毒物质含量和对海洋生态环境的影响程度，制定海洋倾倒废弃物评价程序和标准。  
可以向海洋倾倒的废弃物名录，由国务院生态环境主管部门制定。

**Article 372** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, based on the toxicity, content of toxic substances and the degree of impact on the marine ecological environment of the wastes, formulate the evaluation procedures and standards for marine dumping of wastes.  
The list of wastes that may be dumped into the sea shall be formulated by the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同国务院自然资源主管部门编制全国海洋倾倒区规划，并征求国务院交通运输、渔业渔政等有关部门和海警机构的意见，报国务院批准。  
国务院生态环境主管部门根据全国海洋倾倒区规划，按照科学、合理、经济、安全的原则及时选划海洋倾倒区，征求国务院交通运输、渔业渔政等有关部门和海警机构的意见，并向社会公告。

**Article 373** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, jointly with the competent department of natural resources of the State Council, formulate the national plan for marine dumping areas, and solicit opinions from the relevant departments of the State Council such as transport, fisheries and fishery administration, and the coast guard agency, and submit it to the State Council for approval.  
The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, in accordance with the national plan for marine dumping areas and in adherence to the principles of science, rationality, economy and safety, promptly delineate marine dumping areas, solicit opinions from the relevant departments of the State Council such as transport, fisheries and fishery administration, and the coast guard agency, and announce them to the public.

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条　国务院生态环境主管部门组织开展海洋倾倒区使用状况评估，根据评估结果予以调整、暂停使用或者封闭海洋倾倒区。  
海洋倾倒区的调整、暂停使用和封闭情况，应当通报国务院有关部门、海警机构并向社会公开。

**Article 374** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall organize the assessment of the use status of marine dumping areas, and adjust, suspend the use of, or close the marine dumping areas based on the assessment results.  
The adjustment, suspension of use and closure of marine dumping areas shall be notified to the relevant departments of the State Council and the coast guard agency, and made public to the society.

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条　获准和实施倾倒废弃物的单位，应当按照许可证注明的期限及条件，到指定的区域进行倾倒。倾倒作业船舶等载运工具应当安装使用符合要求的海洋倾倒在线监控设备，并与国务院生态环境主管部门监管系统联网。

**Article 375** The unit that has been approved to dump wastes and actually carries out the dumping shall, within the time limit and under the conditions specified in the permit, dump at the designated area. The vessels and other means of transport engaged in dumping operations shall install and use online monitoring equipment for marine dumping that meets the requirements, and connect to the supervision system of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条　获准和实施倾倒废弃物的单位，应当按照规定向颁发许可证的国务院生态环境主管部门海域派出机构报告倾倒情况。倾倒废弃物的船舶应当向驶出港的海事管理机构、海警机构报告。

**Article 376** The unit that has been approved to dump wastes and actually carries out the dumping shall, according to the provisions, report the dumping situation to the sea area agency of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council that issued the permit. Vessels dumping wastes shall report to the maritime administrative agency and the coast guard agency of the port of departure.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条　获准倾倒废弃物的单位委托实施废弃物海洋倾倒作业的，应当对受托单位的主体资格、技术能力和信用状况进行核实，依法签订书面合同，在合同中约定污染防治和生态保护要求，并监督实施。  
受托单位实施废弃物海洋倾倒作业，应当依照法律法规规定和合同约定，履行污染防治和生态保护要求。

**Article 377** Where a unit that has been approved to dump wastes commissions another unit to carry out the marine dumping operation of wastes, it shall verify the subject qualification, technical capacity and credit status of the commissioned unit, sign a written contract according to law, stipulate the requirements for pollution prevention and ecological protection in the contract, and supervise the implementation thereof.  
The commissioned unit, when carrying out the marine dumping operation of wastes, shall fulfill the requirements for pollution prevention and ecological protection in accordance with the provisions of laws, regulations and the contract.

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条　向海洋倾倒废弃物，应当按照国家规定缴纳倾倒费。具体办法由国务院发展改革部门、财政部门会同国务院生态环境主管部门制定。

**Article 378** For dumping wastes into the sea, a dumping fee shall be paid according to the state provisions. The specific measures shall be formulated by the development and reform department and the finance department of the State Council jointly with the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条　禁止海上焚烧废弃物。  
禁止在海上处置污染海洋环境、破坏海洋生态的放射性废物或者其他放射性物质。  
本法所称海上焚烧，是指以热摧毁为目的，在海上焚烧设施上，故意焚烧废弃物或者其他物质的行为，但是船舶、平台或者其他人工构造物正常操作中所附带发生的行为除外。

**Article 379** It is prohibited to incinerate wastes at sea.  
It is prohibited to dispose of radioactive wastes or other radioactive substances that pollute the marine environment and damage the marine ecology at sea.  
For the purposes of this Law, "incineration at sea" means the deliberate combustion of wastes or other substances on an offshore incineration facility for the purpose of thermal destruction, except for behaviors incidental to the normal operation of ships, platforms or other man-made structures.

### 第十五章　船舶海洋污染防治

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条　在中华人民共和国管辖海域，任何船舶及有关作业不得违法向海洋排放船舶垃圾、生活污水、含油污水、含有毒有害物质污水、废气等污染物，废弃物，压载水和沉积物及其他有害物质。  
船舶应当按照国家规定采取有效措施，对压载水和沉积物进行处理处置，严格防控引入外来有害生物。  
从事船舶污染物、废弃物接收和船舶清舱、洗舱作业活动的，应当具备相应的接收处理能力。

**Article 380** In the sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China, no vessel or related operation shall illegally discharge into the sea pollutants such as ship garbage, domestic sewage, oily wastewater, wastewater containing toxic and hazardous substances, exhaust gas, wastes, ballast water and sediment, and other harmful substances.  
Vessels shall, according to the state provisions, take effective measures to treat and dispose of ballast water and sediment, and strictly prevent and control the introduction of alien harmful organisms.  
Entities engaged in the reception of ship pollutants and wastes, and ship tank cleaning and cargo hold cleaning operations shall have the corresponding reception and treatment capabilities.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条　船舶应当配备相应的防污设备和器材。  
船舶的材料、结构、配备的防污设备和器材应当符合国家防治船舶污染海洋环境的规定，并经检验合格。  
船舶应当取得并持有防治海洋环境污染的证书与文书，进行涉及船舶污染物、压载水和沉积物等排放及操作，应当按照规定开展监测、监控，如实记录并保存。

**Article 381** A ship shall be equipped with corresponding anti-pollution equipment and apparatus.  
The materials, structure, and anti-pollution equipment and apparatus of a ship shall comply with the state provisions on preventing pollution of the marine environment by ships, and shall pass inspection.  
A ship shall obtain and hold certificates and documents for preventing and controlling marine environmental pollution, and when conducting operations involving the discharge of ship pollutants, ballast water, and sediments, it shall carry out monitoring and surveillance in accordance with the provisions, and truthfully record and retain such data.

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条　船舶应当遵守海上交通安全法律法规的规定，防止因碰撞、触礁、搁浅、火灾或者爆炸等引起的海难事故，造成海洋环境污染。

**Article 382** A ship shall comply with the provisions of laws and regulations on maritime traffic safety to prevent marine environmental pollution caused by maritime perils such as collision, stranding, grounding, fire, or explosion.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条　国家完善并实施船舶油污损害民事赔偿责任制度；按照船舶油污损害赔偿责任由船东和货主共同承担风险的原则，完善并实施船舶油污保险、油污损害赔偿基金制度，具体办法由国务院制定。

**Article 383** The State shall improve and implement the system of civil liability for compensation for oil pollution damage from ships; in accordance with the principle that the risk of liability for compensation for oil pollution damage from ships shall be jointly borne by shipowners and cargo owners, the State shall improve and implement the systems of ship oil pollution insurance and oil pollution damage compensation fund, with specific measures to be formulated by the State Council.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条　载运具有污染危害性货物进出港口的船舶，其承运人、货物所有人或者代理人，应当事先向海事管理机构申报。经批准后，方可进出港口或者装卸作业。

**Article 384** Where a ship carries polluting goods into or out of a port, the carrier, cargo owner, or its agent shall declare to the maritime administrative agency in advance. Only after obtaining approval may the ship enter or leave the port or conduct loading and unloading operations.

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条　交付船舶载运污染危害性货物的，托运人应当将货物的正式名称、污染危害性以及应当采取的防护措施如实告知承运人。污染危害性货物的单证、包装、标志、数量限制等，应当符合对所交付货物的有关规定。  
需要船舶载运污染危害性不明的货物，应当按照规定事先进行评估。  
装卸油类、有毒有害货物的作业，船岸双方应当遵守安全防污操作规程。

**Article 385** Where goods with polluting properties are delivered for carriage by ship, the shipper shall truthfully inform the carrier of the formal name, polluting properties, and protective measures to be taken for the goods. The documents, packaging, labels, quantity limitations, etc., of goods with polluting properties shall comply with the relevant provisions for the delivered goods.  
If goods with unknown polluting properties need to be carried by ship, an assessment shall be conducted in advance in accordance with the provisions.  
For operations involving the loading and unloading of oils and toxic and harmful goods, both the ship and shore parties shall comply with safety and anti-pollution operating procedures.

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条　港口、码头、装卸站和船舶修造拆解单位所在地县级以上地方人民政府应当统筹规划、建设船舶污染物等的接收、转运、处理处置设施，建立健全相应的接收、转运、处理处置多部门联合监督管理制度。  
沿海县级以上地方人民政府负责对其管理海域的渔港和渔业船舶停泊点及周边区域污染防治的监督管理，规范生产生活污水和渔业垃圾回收处置，推进污染防治设备建设和环境清理整治。  
港口、码头、装卸站和船舶修造拆解单位应当按照规定配备足够的船舶污染物、废弃物的接收设施，使该设施处于良好状态并有效运行。  
装卸污染危害性货物的港口、码头、装卸站和船舶应当制定污染应急预案，并配备相应的污染应急设备和器材。

**Article 386** The local people's government at or above the county level of the place where a port, wharf, loading and unloading station, or shipbuilding, repair, or dismantling unit is located shall make overall plans to construct facilities for the reception, transfer, and treatment and disposal of ship pollutants, and shall establish and improve a multi-department joint supervision and management system for the corresponding reception, transfer, and treatment and disposal.  
The local people's government at or above the county level in coastal areas shall be responsible for the supervision and management of pollution prevention and control in fishing ports, berthing points for fishing vessels, and surrounding areas within its management sea area, regulate the recovery and disposal of domestic and production wastewater and fishery waste, and promote the construction of pollution prevention and control equipment and environmental clean-up and remediation.  
A port, wharf, loading and unloading station, or shipbuilding, repair, or dismantling unit shall be equipped with sufficient reception facilities for ship pollutants and wastes in accordance with the provisions, and shall keep such facilities in good condition and ensure their effective operation.  
A port, wharf, loading and unloading station, or ship that loads and unloads goods with polluting properties shall formulate a pollution emergency response plan and be equipped with corresponding pollution emergency equipment and apparatus.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条　国家海事管理机构组织制定中国籍船舶禁止或者限制安装和使用的有害材料名录。  
船舶修造单位和船舶所有人、经营人、管理人应当在船上备有有害材料清单，在船舶建造、营运和维修过程中持续更新，并在船舶拆解前提供给从事船舶拆解的单位。

**Article 387** The national maritime administrative agency shall organize the formulation of a list of hazardous materials prohibited or restricted from being installed or used on Chinese-flagged ships.  
A shipbuilding or repair unit and the shipowner, operator, or manager shall keep a list of hazardous materials on board, continuously update it during the ship's construction, operation, and maintenance, and provide it to the unit engaged in ship dismantling prior to ship dismantling.

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条　从事船舶拆解的单位，应当采取有效的污染防治措施，在船舶拆解前将船舶污染物减至最小量，对拆解产生的船舶污染物、废弃物和其他有害物质进行安全与环境无害化处置，防止污染海洋环境。拆解的船舶部件不得入水。  
禁止采取冲滩方式在海岸进行船舶拆解作业。

**Article 388** A unit engaged in ship dismantling shall take effective pollution prevention and control measures, minimize ship pollutants before dismantling, and conduct safe and environmentally sound disposal of ship pollutants, wastes, and other hazardous substances generated from dismantling to prevent pollution of the marine environment. No dismantled ship parts shall be allowed to enter the water.  
It is prohibited to conduct ship dismantling operations on the coast by beaching.

<a id="art-389"></a>第三百八十九条　船舶应当按照国家规定采取有效措施提高能效水平。倡导绿色低碳智能航运，鼓励船舶使用清洁低碳能源，淘汰高耗能高排放老旧船舶，减少温室气体和大气污染物的排放。沿海县级以上地方人民政府应当制定港口岸电、船舶受电等设施建设和改造计划，并组织实施。港口岸电设施的供电能力应当与靠港船舶的用电需求相适应。  
国务院和沿海县级以上地方人民政府对港口岸电设施、船舶受电设施的改造和使用，以及清洁低碳能源动力船舶的建造等按照规定给予支持。

**Article 389** A ship shall take effective measures to improve energy efficiency in accordance with the state provisions. Green, low-carbon, and intelligent shipping shall be promoted, ships shall be encouraged to use clean and low-carbon energy, old ships with high energy consumption and high emissions shall be phased out, and emissions of greenhouse gases and atmospheric pollutants shall be reduced. The local people's government at or above the county level in coastal areas shall formulate and organize the implementation of plans for the construction and renovation of shore power facilities at ports and ship-side power receiving facilities. The power supply capacity of shore power facilities at ports shall match the power demand of ships calling at the port.  
The State Council and the local people's government at or above the county level in coastal areas shall provide support in accordance with the provisions for the renovation and use of shore power facilities at ports and ship-side power receiving facilities, as well as the construction of clean and low-carbon energy-powered ships.

<a id="art-390"></a>第三百九十条　国务院交通运输主管部门可以划定船舶污染物排放控制区。进入控制区的船舶应当符合船舶污染物排放相关控制要求。

**Article 390** The competent transport department of the State Council may designate ship pollutant discharge control areas. Ships entering the control areas shall comply with the relevant control requirements for ship pollutant discharge.

<a id="art-391"></a>第三百九十一条　船舶及有关作业活动应当遵守法律法规、标准和国务院有关部门的规定，采取有效措施，防止造成海洋环境污染。海事管理机构等应当加强对船舶及有关作业活动的监督管理。  
船舶进行散装液体污染危害性货物的过驳作业，应当编制作业方案，采取有效的安全和污染防治措施，并事先按照规定报经批准。

**Article 391** Ships and related operations and activities shall comply with laws, regulations, standards, and provisions of relevant departments under the State Council, and take effective measures to prevent marine environmental pollution. The maritime administrative agency and others shall strengthen the supervision and management of ships and related operations and activities.  
Where a ship conducts lightering operations of bulk liquid goods with polluting properties, it shall work out an operational plan, take effective safety and pollution prevention and control measures, and submit the plan for approval in advance in accordance with the provisions.

<a id="art-392"></a>第三百九十二条　船舶发生海难事故，造成或者可能造成海洋环境重大污染的，海事管理机构有权强制采取避免或者减少污染的措施。  
对在公海上因发生海难事故，造成中华人民共和国管辖海域重大污染后果或者具有污染威胁的船舶、海上设施，海事管理机构有权采取与实际的或者可能发生的损害相称的必要措施。

**Article 392** Where a ship experiences a maritime peril that causes or is likely to cause major pollution to the marine environment, the maritime administrative agency shall have the right to compulsorily take measures to avoid or reduce pollution.  
With respect to a ship or offshore installation on the high seas that, due to a maritime peril, has caused major pollution consequences or poses a pollution threat to the sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China, the maritime administrative agency shall have the right to take necessary measures commensurate with the actual or potential damage.

<a id="art-393"></a>第三百九十三条　所有船舶均有监视海上污染的义务，在发现海上污染事件或者违反本法规定的行为时，应当立即向就近的负有海洋生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构报告。  
民用航空器发现海上排污或者污染事件，应当及时向就近的民用航空空中交通管制单位报告。接到报告的单位，应当立即向负有海洋生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构通报。  
第五分编　土壤污染防治

**Article 393** All ships have the obligation to monitor marine pollution. Upon discovering a marine pollution incident or any act violating the provisions of this Law, they shall immediately report to the nearest department or agency responsible for the supervision and administration of marine ecological and environmental protection.  
Upon discovering a marine discharge or pollution incident, a civil aircraft shall promptly report to the nearest civil aviation air traffic control unit. The unit that receives the report shall immediately notify the department or agency responsible for the supervision and administration of marine ecological and environmental protection.  
Division 5 Soil Pollution Prevention and Control

### 第十六章　一般规定

<a id="art-394"></a>第三百九十四条　本分编适用于土壤污染的防治。

**Article 394** This Division shall apply to the prevention and control of soil pollution.

<a id="art-395"></a>第三百九十五条　本法所称土壤污染，是指因人为因素导致某种物质进入陆地表层土壤，引起土壤化学、物理、生物等方面特性的改变，影响土壤功能和有效利用，危害公众健康或者破坏生态环境的现象。

**Article 395** For the purposes of this Law, "soil pollution" means a phenomenon caused by human factors, whereby a substance enters the surface soil on land, resulting in changes in the chemical, physical, and biological characteristics of the soil, affecting soil functions and effective utilization, endangering public health, or damaging the ecological environment.

<a id="art-396"></a>第三百九十六条　土壤污染防治应当坚持风险管控，保障农产品质量安全、人居环境安全，推动土壤资源永续利用。

**Article 396** The prevention and control of soil pollution shall adhere to risk control, ensure the quality and safety of agricultural products and the safety of the human living environment, and promote the sustainable use of soil resources.

<a id="art-397"></a>第三百九十七条　土地使用权人从事土地开发利用活动，企业事业单位和其他生产经营者从事生产经营活动，应当采取有效措施，防止、减少土壤污染，对所造成的土壤污染依法承担责任。

**Article 397** Land use right holders engaging in land development and utilization activities, and enterprises, institutions, and other producers and business operators engaging in production and business operations, shall take effective measures to prevent and reduce soil pollution and bear liability for soil pollution caused by them according to law.

<a id="art-398"></a>第三百九十八条　国务院生态环境主管部门对全国土壤污染防治工作实施统一监督管理。地方人民政府生态环境主管部门对本行政区域土壤污染防治工作实施统一监督管理。  
县级以上人民政府农业农村、自然资源、住房城乡建设、林业草原等有关部门在各自职责范围内对土壤污染防治工作实施监督管理。

**Article 398** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall exercise unified supervision and administration over the prevention and control of soil pollution nationwide. The competent department of ecological environment of the local people's government shall exercise unified supervision and administration over the prevention and control of soil pollution within its administrative region.  
The relevant departments of the people's government at or above the county level, such as agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, and forestry and grassland, shall exercise supervision and administration over the prevention and control of soil pollution within their respective duties.

<a id="art-399"></a>第三百九十九条　设区的市级以上地方人民政府生态环境主管部门会同发展改革、农业农村、自然资源、住房城乡建设、林业草原等有关部门，根据生态环境保护规划要求、土地用途、土壤污染状况普查和监测结果等，编制土壤污染防治规划，报本级人民政府批准后公布实施。

**Article 399** The competent department of ecological environment of the local people's government at or above the level of a city divided into districts shall, together with the relevant departments such as development and reform, agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, and forestry and grassland, formulate a plan for the prevention and control of soil pollution based on the requirements of ecological and environmental protection planning, land use, and the results of general surveys and monitoring of soil pollution conditions, and submit it to the people's government at the same level for approval before it is promulgated and implemented.

<a id="art-400"></a>第四百条　国务院生态环境主管部门根据土壤污染状况、公众健康风险、生态环境风险和科学技术水平，并按照土地用途，制定国家土壤污染风险管控标准，加强土壤污染防治标准体系建设。  
省、自治区、直辖市人民政府对国家土壤污染风险管控标准中未作规定的项目，可以制定地方土壤污染风险管控标准；对国家土壤污染风险管控标准中已作规定的项目，可以制定严于国家土壤污染风险管控标准的地方土壤污染风险管控标准。地方土壤污染风险管控标准应当报国务院生态环境主管部门备案。  
土壤污染风险管控标准是强制性标准。  
国家支持对土壤环境背景值的研究。

**Article 400** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, based on soil pollution conditions, public health risks, ecological and environmental risks, and the level of science and technology, and in accordance with land use, formulate national standards for soil pollution risk control and strengthen the development of a standards system for soil pollution prevention and control.  
The people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government may formulate local standards for soil pollution risk control for items not specified in the national standards; for items already specified in the national standards, they may formulate local standards for soil pollution risk control that are stricter than the national standards. Local standards for soil pollution risk control shall be filed with the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council for recordation.  
The standards for soil pollution risk control are mandatory standards.  
The State supports research on soil environmental background values.

<a id="art-401"></a>第四百零一条　国务院统一领导全国土壤污染状况普查。国务院生态环境主管部门会同国务院农业农村、自然资源、住房城乡建设、林业草原等有关部门，每十年至少组织开展一次全国土壤污染状况普查。  
国务院有关部门和设区的市级以上地方人民政府可以根据本行业、本行政区域实际情况组织开展土壤污染状况详查。

**Article 401** The State Council shall provide unified leadership for the national soil pollution survey. The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, jointly with the competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, forestry and grassland, and other relevant departments of the State Council, organize and conduct a national soil pollution survey at least once every ten years.  
Relevant departments of the State Council and local people's governments at or above the level of a city divided into districts may, based on the actual conditions of their respective industries and administrative regions, organize and conduct detailed soil pollution surveys.

<a id="art-402"></a>第四百零二条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同国务院农业农村、自然资源、住房城乡建设、水行政、卫生健康、林业草原等有关部门组织监测网络，统一规划国家土壤环境监测站点的设置。

**Article 402** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, jointly with the competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, water administration, health, forestry and grassland, and other relevant departments of the State Council, organize a monitoring network and uniformly plan the layout of national soil environment monitoring stations.

<a id="art-403"></a>第四百零三条　地方人民政府农业农村主管部门、林业草原主管部门会同生态环境主管部门、自然资源主管部门对下列农用地地块进行重点监测：  
（一）产出的农产品污染物含量超标的；  
（二）作为或者曾作为污水灌溉区的；  
（三）用于或者曾用于规模化养殖，固体废物堆放、填埋的；  
（四）曾作为工矿用地或者发生过重大、特别重大污染事故的；  
（五）有毒有害物质生产、贮存、利用、处置设施周边的；  
（六）国务院农业农村主管部门、林业草原主管部门、生态环境主管部门、自然资源主管部门规定的其他情形。

**Article 403** The competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs and forestry and grassland of local people's governments shall, jointly with the competent departments of ecological environment and natural resources, conduct focused monitoring on the following agricultural land plots:  
(1) Where the pollutant content in the agricultural products produced exceeds the standard;  
(2) Which serve or have served as sewage irrigation areas;  
(3) Which are or have been used for large-scale farming, or for the stacking or landfill of solid waste;  
(4) Which have been used as industrial or mining land, or where major or extraordinarily major pollution accidents have occurred;  
(5) Which are in the vicinity of facilities for the production, storage, utilization, or disposal of toxic and hazardous substances; and  
(6) Other circumstances specified by the competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs, forestry and grassland, ecological environment, and natural resources of the State Council.

<a id="art-404"></a>第四百零四条　地方人民政府生态环境主管部门会同自然资源主管部门对下列建设用地地块进行重点监测：  
（一）曾用于生产、贮存、使用、回收、处置有毒有害物质的；  
（二）曾用于固体废物堆放、填埋的；  
（三）曾发生过重大、特别重大污染事故的；  
（四）国务院生态环境主管部门、自然资源主管部门规定的其他情形。

**Article 404** The competent department of ecological environment of a local people's government shall, jointly with the competent department of natural resources, conduct focused monitoring on the following construction land plots:  
(1) Which have been used for the production, storage, use, recovery, or disposal of toxic and hazardous substances;  
(2) Which have been used for the stacking or landfill of solid waste;  
(3) Where major or extraordinarily major pollution accidents have occurred; and  
(4) Other circumstances specified by the competent departments of ecological environment and natural resources of the State Council.

<a id="art-405"></a>第四百零五条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同国务院农业农村、自然资源、住房城乡建设、水行政、卫生健康、林业草原等有关部门建立健全土壤环境基础数据库，构建全国土壤环境信息平台，实行数据动态更新和信息共享。

**Article 405** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, jointly with the competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, water administration, health, forestry and grassland, and other relevant departments of the State Council, establish and improve a basic soil environment database, build a national soil environment information platform, and implement dynamic data updating and information sharing.

<a id="art-406"></a>第四百零六条　土壤污染状况普查报告、监测数据、调查报告和土壤污染风险评估报告、风险管控效果评估报告、修复效果评估报告等，应当及时上传全国土壤环境信息平台。

**Article 406** The reports on soil pollution surveys, monitoring data, investigation reports, soil pollution risk assessment reports, risk control effectiveness assessment reports, remediation effectiveness assessment reports, and the like shall be promptly uploaded to the national soil environment information platform.

<a id="art-407"></a>第四百零七条　生态环境主管部门应当将涉及主要食用农产品生产区域的重大土壤环境信息，及时通报农业农村主管部门、卫生健康主管部门和市场监督管理部门。

**Article 407** The competent department of ecological environment shall promptly notify the competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs, health, and market supervision and administration of major soil environment information involving the areas producing major edible agricultural products.

<a id="art-408"></a>第四百零八条　省级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门和其他负有土壤污染防治监督管理职责的部门，应当在各自职责范围内对从事土壤污染状况调查和土壤污染风险评估、风险管控、修复、风险管控效果评估、修复效果评估、后期管理等活动的单位和个人的执业情况，依照法律规定进行信用监管。

**Article 408** The competent departments of ecological environment of people's governments at or above the provincial level and other departments responsible for the supervision and administration of soil pollution prevention and control shall, within their respective functions and duties, conduct credit supervision on the practicing conditions of entities and individuals engaged in soil pollution investigation, soil pollution risk assessment, risk control, remediation, risk control effectiveness assessment, remediation effectiveness assessment, post-management, and other activities, in accordance with the provisions of laws.

<a id="art-409"></a>第四百零九条　各级人民政府应当加强土壤污染防治，安排必要的资金用于下列事项：  
（一）土壤污染防治的科学技术研究开发、示范工程和项目；  
（二）各级人民政府及其有关部门组织实施的土壤污染状况普查、监测、调查和土壤污染责任人认定、风险评估、风险管控、修复等活动；  
（三）各级人民政府及其有关部门对涉及土壤污染的突发事件的应急处置；  
（四）各级人民政府规定的涉及土壤污染防治的其他事项。

**Article 409** People's governments at all levels shall strengthen soil pollution prevention and control and allocate necessary funds for the following matters:  
(1) Scientific and technological research and development, demonstration projects, and programs on soil pollution prevention and control;  
(2) Activities organized and implemented by people's governments at all levels and their relevant departments, including soil pollution surveys, monitoring, investigations, identification of persons responsible for soil pollution, risk assessment, risk control, remediation, and the like;  
(3) Emergency response by people's governments at all levels and their relevant departments to sudden incidents involving soil pollution; and  
(4) Other matters concerning soil pollution prevention and control as prescribed by people's governments at all levels.

<a id="art-410"></a>第四百一十条　国家加大土壤污染防治资金投入力度，建立健全土壤污染防治基金制度。设立中央土壤污染防治专项资金和省级土壤污染防治基金，主要用于农用地土壤污染防治和土壤污染责任人或者土地使用权人无法认定的土壤污染风险管控和修复以及人民政府规定的其他事项。  
土壤污染责任人无法认定的污染地块，土地使用权人实际承担土壤污染风险管控和修复的，可以按照规定申请土壤污染防治基金，集中用于土壤污染风险管控和修复。  
土壤污染防治基金的具体管理办法，由国务院财政部门会同国务院生态环境、农业农村、自然资源、住房城乡建设、林业草原等有关部门制定。

**Article 410** The State shall increase financial investment in soil pollution prevention and control and establish and improve the fund system for soil pollution prevention and control. Special funds for soil pollution prevention and control at the central level and soil pollution prevention and control funds at the provincial level shall be established, mainly for soil pollution prevention and control of agricultural land and for risk control and remediation of soil pollution where the person responsible for soil pollution or the land-use right holder cannot be identified, as well as other matters prescribed by the people's government.  
Where the person responsible for soil pollution on a contaminated plot cannot be identified and the land-use right holder actually undertakes the risk control and remediation of soil pollution, the land-use right holder may apply for the soil pollution prevention and control fund in accordance with the provisions, to be used centrally for soil pollution risk control and remediation.  
The specific measures for the management of the soil pollution prevention and control fund shall be formulated by the competent department of finance of the State Council jointly with the competent departments of ecological environment, agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, housing and urban-rural construction, forestry and grassland, and other relevant departments of the State Council.

<a id="art-411"></a>第四百一十一条　国家鼓励金融机构加大对土壤污染风险管控和修复项目的信贷投放。  
国家鼓励金融机构在办理土地权利抵押业务时开展土壤污染状况调查。

**Article 411** The State encourages financial institutions to increase credit support for soil pollution risk control and remediation projects.  
The State encourages financial institutions to conduct soil pollution investigations when handling land right mortgage businesses.

### 第十七章　土壤污染预防

<a id="art-412"></a>第四百一十二条　生产、贮存、运输、使用、回收、处置、排放有毒有害物质的单位和个人，应当采取有效措施，防止有毒有害物质渗漏、流失、扬散，避免土壤受到污染。

**Article 412** Entities and individuals that produce, store, transport, use, recover, dispose of, or discharge toxic and hazardous substances shall take effective measures to prevent the leakage, loss, or scattering of toxic and hazardous substances and avoid soil contamination.

<a id="art-413"></a>第四百一十三条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同国务院卫生健康、农业农村等有关部门，根据对公众健康、生态环境的危害和影响程度，对土壤中有毒有害物质进行筛查评估，制定公布重点控制的土壤有毒有害物质名录并适时更新。

**Article 413** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, jointly with the competent departments of health, agriculture and rural affairs, and other relevant departments of the State Council, screen and assess toxic and hazardous substances in soil based on the degree of harm and impact on public health and the ecological environment, and formulate and publish a list of key controlled toxic and hazardous substances in soil, which shall be updated in a timely manner.

<a id="art-414"></a>第四百一十四条　设区的市级以上地方人民政府生态环境主管部门应当按照国务院生态环境主管部门的规定，根据有毒有害物质排放等情况，制定公布本行政区域的土壤污染重点监管单位名录并适时更新。  
土壤污染重点监管单位应当履行下列义务：  
（一）严格控制有毒有害物质排放，并按年度向生态环境主管部门报告排放情况；  
（二）建立土壤、地下水污染隐患排查整治制度，保证持续有效防止有毒有害物质渗漏、流失、扬散；  
（三）制定、实施自行监测方案，并将监测数据报送生态环境主管部门；  
（四）将贮存有毒有害物质的地下储罐信息报送生态环境主管部门。  
前款规定的义务应当在排污许可证中记载。  
生态环境主管部门发现土壤污染重点监管单位监测数据异常，应当及时进行调查。设区的市级以上地方人民政府生态环境主管部门应当定期对土壤污染重点监管单位周边土壤、地下水进行监测。

**Article 414** The competent department of ecological environment of a local people's government at or above the level of a city divided into districts shall, in accordance with the provisions of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council and based on the discharge of toxic and hazardous substances and other conditions, formulate and publish a list of key entities under supervision for soil pollution within its administrative region, which shall be updated in a timely manner.  
Key entities under supervision for soil pollution shall perform the following obligations:  
(1) Strictly control the discharge of toxic and hazardous substances and report the discharge situation to the competent department of ecological environment on an annual basis;  
(2) Establish a system for investigating and rectifying hidden dangers of soil and groundwater pollution, ensuring the continuous and effective prevention of leakage, loss, or scattering of toxic and hazardous substances;  
(3) Formulate and implement a self-monitoring plan and submit monitoring data to the competent department of ecological environment; and  
(4) Submit information on underground storage tanks storing toxic and hazardous substances to the competent department of ecological environment.  
The obligations specified in the preceding paragraph shall be recorded in the pollutant discharge permit.  
Where the competent department of ecological environment discovers that the monitoring data of a key entity under supervision for soil pollution are abnormal, it shall promptly conduct an investigation. The competent department of ecological environment of a local people's government at or above the level of a city divided into districts shall regularly monitor the soil and groundwater surrounding the key entities under supervision for soil pollution.

<a id="art-415"></a>第四百一十五条　企业事业单位和其他生产经营者拆除设施、设备或者建筑物、构筑物的，应当采取相应的土壤污染防治措施。  
土壤污染重点监管单位拆除设施、设备或者建筑物、构筑物的，应当制定包括应急措施在内的土壤污染防治工作方案，报地方人民政府生态环境主管部门、工业和信息化主管部门备案并实施。

**Article 415** Where enterprises, public institutions, and other producers and business operators dismantle facilities, equipment, or buildings or structures, they shall take corresponding soil pollution prevention and control measures.  
Where a key entity under supervision for soil pollution dismantles facilities, equipment, or buildings or structures, it shall formulate a soil pollution prevention and control work plan including emergency measures, and submit it to the competent departments of ecological environment and industry and information technology of the local people's government for recordation and implementation.

<a id="art-416"></a>第四百一十六条　生态环境主管部门、自然资源主管部门应当在各自职责范围内依法加强对矿产资源开发区域土壤污染防治的监督管理，按照相关标准和总量控制的要求，严格控制可能造成土壤污染的重点污染物排放。  
尾矿库运营、管理单位应当按照规定，加强尾矿库的安全管理，采取有效措施防止土壤污染。危库、险库、病库及其他需要重点监管的尾矿库的运营、管理单位应当按照规定，进行土壤污染状况监测和定期评估。

**Article 416** The competent departments of ecological environment and natural resources shall, within their respective functions and duties, strengthen supervision and administration over soil pollution prevention and control in mineral resource development areas according to law, and strictly control the discharge of key pollutants that may cause soil pollution in accordance with relevant standards and total quantity control requirements.  
The operating and management entities of tailings ponds shall, in accordance with the provisions, strengthen the safety management of tailings ponds and take effective measures to prevent soil pollution. The operating and management entities of hazardous, dangerous, defective, or other tailings ponds requiring key supervision shall, in accordance with the provisions, conduct soil pollution monitoring and regular assessment.

<a id="art-417"></a>第四百一十七条　地方人民政府应急管理部门应当监督尾矿库运营、管理单位履行防治土壤污染的法定义务，防止其发生可能污染土壤的事故；地方人民政府生态环境主管部门应当加强对尾矿库土壤污染防治情况的监督检查和定期评估，发现风险隐患的，及时督促尾矿库运营、管理单位采取相应措施。

**Article 417** The emergency management department of a local people's government shall supervise the operating and management entities of tailings ponds in performing their statutory obligations to prevent soil pollution and prevent the occurrence of accidents that may pollute the soil; the competent department of ecological environment of a local people's government shall strengthen supervision, inspection, and regular assessment of soil pollution prevention and control in tailings ponds, and where potential risks are discovered, shall promptly urge the operating and management entities of tailings ponds to take corresponding measures.

<a id="art-418"></a>第四百一十八条　国家鼓励在建筑、通信、电力、交通、水利等领域的信息、网络、防雷、接地等建设工程中采用新技术、新材料，防止土壤污染。  
禁止在土壤中使用重金属含量超标的降阻产品。

**Article 418** The State encourages the use of new technologies and new materials in construction projects such as information, network, lightning protection, and grounding systems in the fields of construction, communications, electric power, transportation, and water conservancy to prevent soil pollution.  
The use of resistance-reducing products with heavy metal content exceeding the standard in soil is prohibited.

<a id="art-419"></a>第四百一十九条　建设和运行污水集中处理设施、固体废物处置设施，应当依照法律法规规定和相关标准的要求，采取有效措施防止土壤污染。  
地方人民政府生态环境主管部门应当定期对污水集中处理设施、固体废物处置设施周边土壤进行监测；对不符合法律法规规定和相关标准要求的，应当根据监测结果，要求污水集中处理设施、固体废物处置设施运营单位采取相应改进措施。

**Article 419** The construction and operation of centralized sewage treatment facilities and solid waste disposal facilities shall, in accordance with the provisions of laws and regulations and the requirements of relevant standards, take effective measures to prevent soil pollution.  
The competent department of ecological environment of a local people's government shall regularly monitor the soil surrounding centralized sewage treatment facilities and solid waste disposal facilities; where such facilities do not comply with the provisions of laws and regulations and the requirements of relevant standards, the competent department shall, based on the monitoring results, require the operating entities of the centralized sewage treatment facilities and solid waste disposal facilities to take corresponding improvement measures.

<a id="art-420"></a>第四百二十条　国务院农业农村主管部门、林业草原主管部门在各自职责范围内编制规划，完善相关标准和措施，加强农用地农药、化肥使用指导和使用总量控制，加强农用薄膜使用控制。  
国务院农业农村主管部门应当加强农药、肥料登记，组织开展农药、肥料对土壤环境影响的安全性评价。  
制定农药、兽药、饲料和饲料添加剂、肥料、农用薄膜等农业投入品及其包装物标准和农田灌溉用水水质标准，应当适应土壤污染防治的要求。

**Article 420** The competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs and forestry and grassland of the State Council shall, within their respective functions and duties, formulate plans, improve relevant standards and measures, strengthen guidance on the use of pesticides and chemical fertilizers on agricultural land and the control of their total usage, and strengthen control over the use of agricultural films.  
The competent department of agriculture and rural affairs of the State Council shall strengthen the registration of pesticides and fertilizers and organize the safety assessment of the impact of pesticides and fertilizers on the soil environment.  
The formulation of standards for agricultural inputs such as pesticides, veterinary drugs, feed and feed additives, fertilizers, agricultural films, and their packaging, as well as standards for water quality for farmland irrigation, shall meet the requirements of soil pollution prevention and control.

<a id="art-421"></a>第四百二十一条　地方人民政府农业农村主管部门、林业草原主管部门应当在各自职责范围内开展农用地土壤污染防治宣传和技术培训活动，扶持农业生产专业化服务，指导农业生产经营者科学合理安全使用农药、兽药、饲料和饲料添加剂、肥料、农用薄膜等农业投入品，控制农药、兽药、化肥等的使用量。  
地方人民政府农业农村主管部门应当鼓励农业生产经营者采取有利于防止土壤污染的种养结合、轮作休耕等农业耕作措施；支持采取土壤改良、土壤肥力提升等有利于土壤养护和培育的措施；支持畜禽粪污处理、利用设施的建设。

**Article 421** The competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs and the competent departments of forestry and grassland of the local people's governments shall, within their respective functions and duties, carry out publicity and technical training activities on the prevention and control of soil pollution on agricultural land, support specialized services for agricultural production, and guide agricultural producers and operators in the scientific, rational and safe use of pesticides, veterinary drugs, feed and feed additives, fertilizers, agricultural films and other agricultural inputs, and control the amount of pesticides, veterinary drugs, fertilizers, etc. used.  
The competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs of the local people's governments shall encourage agricultural producers and operators to adopt agricultural farming measures favorable to preventing soil pollution, such as combination of planting and breeding, crop rotation and fallow; support measures favorable to soil conservation and cultivation, such as soil improvement and soil fertility enhancement; and support the construction of facilities for the treatment and utilization of livestock and poultry manure.

<a id="art-422"></a>第四百二十二条　禁止向耕地、林地、草地等农用地排放重金属或者其他有毒有害物质含量超标的污水、污泥，以及可能造成土壤污染的清淤底泥、尾矿、矿渣等。  
县级以上人民政府有关部门应当在各自职责范围内加强对畜禽粪污、沼渣、沼液等收集、贮存、利用、处置的监督管理，防止土壤污染。  
地方人民政府生态环境主管部门会同农业农村主管部门、水行政主管部门加强对农田灌溉用水水质的管理，对农田灌溉用水水质进行监测和监督检查。

**Article 422** It is prohibited to discharge sewage, sludge or any other substance containing heavy metals or other toxic and harmful substances exceeding the standards, or any dredged sediment, tailings, slag or the like that may cause soil pollution, into agricultural land such as cultivated land, forest land and grassland.  
The relevant departments of the people's governments at or above the county level shall, within their respective functions and duties, strengthen supervision and management of the collection, storage, utilization and disposal of livestock and poultry manure, biogas residue, biogas slurry, etc., to prevent soil pollution.  
The competent department of ecology and environment of the local people's government shall, together with the competent department of agriculture and rural affairs and the competent department of water administration, strengthen the management of the quality of water used for farmland irrigation, and monitor, supervise and inspect the quality of such water.

<a id="art-423"></a>第四百二十三条　国家鼓励、支持农业生产经营者采取下列措施：  
（一）使用高效低毒低残留农药以及先进喷施技术；  
（二）使用符合标准的有机肥、高效肥；  
（三）采用测土配方施肥技术、生物防治等病虫害绿色防控技术；  
（四）使用生物可降解农用薄膜；  
（五）综合利用秸秆、移出高富集污染物秸秆；  
（六）按照规定对退化土壤等进行改良。

**Article 423** The State encourages and supports agricultural producers and operators to adopt the following measures:  
(1) use high-efficiency, low-toxicity and low-residue pesticides and advanced spraying technologies;  
(2) use organic fertilizers and high-efficiency fertilizers that meet the standards;  
(3) adopt soil testing and formulated fertilization technologies, and green prevention and control technologies for diseases, pests and weeds such as biological control;  
(4) use biodegradable agricultural films;  
(5) comprehensively utilize straw and remove straw with high enrichment of pollutants; and  
(6) improve degraded soil, etc. according to the provisions.

<a id="art-424"></a>第四百二十四条　禁止生产、销售或者使用国家明令禁止的农业投入品。  
农业投入品生产者、销售者和使用者应当及时回收农药、肥料等农业投入品的包装废弃物和农用薄膜，并将农药包装废弃物交由专门的机构或者组织进行无害化处理。具体办法由国务院农业农村主管部门会同国务院生态环境等有关部门制定。  
国家采取有效措施，鼓励研究开发、生产、销售、使用在生态环境中可降解且无害的农用薄膜，鼓励、支持单位和个人回收农业投入品包装废弃物和农用薄膜。

**Article 424** It is prohibited to produce, sell or use agricultural inputs explicitly prohibited by the State.
