Storage of hazardous waste shall adopt protective measures that conform to national ecological and environmental protection standards. It shall be prohibited to mix hazardous waste with non-hazardous waste for storage.  
An entity engaged in business activities of collecting, storing, utilizing, or disposing of hazardous waste shall not store hazardous waste for more than one year; where extension of the period is truly necessary, approval shall be obtained from the competent department of ecological environment that issued the license, unless otherwise provided by laws or administrative regulations.

<a id="art-536"></a>第五百三十六条　转移危险废物的，应当按照国家规定填写、运行危险废物电子或者纸质转移联单。  
跨省、自治区、直辖市转移危险废物的，应当向危险废物移出地省级人民政府生态环境主管部门申请。移出地省级人民政府生态环境主管部门应当及时商经接受地省级人民政府生态环境主管部门同意后，在规定期限内批准转移该危险废物，并将批准信息通报相关省级人民政府生态环境主管部门和交通运输主管部门。未经批准的，不得转移。  
危险废物转移管理应当全程管控、提高效率，具体办法由国务院生态环境主管部门会同国务院交通运输主管部门、公安部门制定。

**Article 536** Where hazardous waste is transferred, the electronic or paper-based transfer manifest for hazardous waste shall be filled in and operated in accordance with state regulations.  
Where hazardous waste is transferred across provinces, autonomous regions, or municipalities directly under the Central Government, an application shall be made to the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government of the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government where the hazardous waste is moved out. The competent department of ecological environment of the province where the waste is moved out shall, after promptly consulting with and obtaining consent from the competent department of ecological environment of the receiving province, approve the transfer of the hazardous waste within a specified period, and notify the relevant provincial competent departments of ecological environment and the competent department of transportation of the approval information. Without approval, no transfer shall be made.  
The management of hazardous waste transfer shall ensure whole-process control and improve efficiency, and specific measures shall be formulated by the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council together with the competent department of transportation of the State Council and the public security department.

<a id="art-537"></a>第五百三十七条　运输危险废物，应当采取防止污染环境的措施，并遵守国家有关危险货物运输管理的规定。  
禁止将危险废物与旅客在同一运输工具上载运。

**Article 537** When transporting hazardous waste, measures to prevent environmental pollution shall be taken, and the provisions of the state concerning the management of dangerous goods transport shall be complied with.  
It shall be prohibited to carry hazardous waste together with passengers on the same means of transport.

<a id="art-538"></a>第五百三十八条　收集、贮存、运输、利用、处置危险废物的场所、设施、设备和容器、包装物及其他物品转作他用时，应当按照国家规定经过消除污染处理，方可使用。

**Article 538** When places, facilities, equipment, containers, packaging, and other items used for collecting, storing, transporting, utilizing, or disposing of hazardous waste are converted to other uses, they shall undergo decontamination treatment in accordance with state regulations before they may be used.

<a id="art-539"></a>第五百三十九条　产生、收集、贮存、运输、利用、处置危险废物的单位，应当依法制定意外事故的防范措施和应急预案，并报所在地生态环境主管部门和其他负有固体废物污染防治监督管理职责的部门备案；生态环境主管部门和其他负有固体废物污染防治监督管理职责的部门应当在各自职责范围内进行监督检查。

**Article 539** An entity that generates, collects, stores, transports, utilizes, or disposes of hazardous waste shall formulate preventive measures and emergency response plans for accidents according to law, and shall file them with the competent department of ecological environment of the locality and other departments responsible for supervision and management of solid waste pollution prevention and control; the competent department of ecological environment and other departments responsible for supervision and management of solid waste pollution prevention and control shall conduct supervision and inspection within their respective functions and duties.

<a id="art-540"></a>第五百四十条　因发生事故或者其他突发性事件，造成危险废物严重污染的单位，应当立即采取有效措施消除或者减轻污染危害，及时通报可能受到污染危害者，并向所在地生态环境主管部门和有关部门报告，接受调查处理。

**Article 540** An entity that causes severe pollution from hazardous waste due to an accident or other emergency shall immediately take effective measures to eliminate or mitigate the pollution hazard, promptly notify those who may be harmed by the pollution, and report to the competent department of ecological environment of the locality and relevant departments, and accept investigation and handling.

<a id="art-541"></a>第五百四十一条　在发生或者有证据证明可能发生危险废物严重污染、威胁居民生命财产安全时，生态环境主管部门和其他负有固体废物污染防治监督管理职责的部门应当立即向本级人民政府和上一级人民政府有关部门报告，由人民政府采取防止或者减轻危害的有效措施。有关人民政府可以根据实际需要责令停止导致或者可能导致环境污染事故的作业。

**Article 541** When a serious pollution or a threat to the life and property safety of residents caused by hazardous waste occurs or there is evidence proving that it may occur, the competent department of ecological environment and other departments responsible for the supervision and administration of the prevention and control of solid waste pollution shall immediately report to the people's government at the same level and the relevant departments of the people's government at the next higher level, and the people's government shall take effective measures to prevent or mitigate the harm. The relevant people's government may, based on actual needs, order the suspension of operations that cause or may cause environmental pollution accidents.

<a id="art-542"></a>第五百四十二条　重点危险废物集中处置设施、场所退役前，运营单位应当按照国家规定对设施、场所采取污染防治措施。退役的费用应当预提，列入投资概算或者生产成本，专门用于重点危险废物集中处置设施、场所的退役。具体提取和管理办法，由国务院财政部门、价格主管部门会同国务院生态环境主管部门制定。

**Article 542** Before the decommissioning of key centralized hazardous waste disposal facilities and sites, the operating units shall take pollution prevention and control measures for the facilities and sites in accordance with state regulations. The costs of decommissioning shall be accrued in advance, included in the investment estimate or production costs, and used exclusively for the decommissioning of key centralized hazardous waste disposal facilities and sites. The specific measures for accrual and management shall be formulated by the finance department and the price department of the State Council jointly with the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-543"></a>第五百四十三条　禁止经中华人民共和国过境转移危险废物。

**Article 543** The transfer of hazardous waste through the territory of the People's Republic of China is prohibited.

<a id="art-544"></a>第五百四十四条　医疗废物按照国家危险废物名录管理。县级以上地方人民政府应当加强医疗废物集中处置能力建设。  
卫生健康、生态环境等有关部门应当在各自职责范围内加强对医疗废物收集、贮存、运输、处置的监督管理，防止危害公众健康、造成环境污染。  
医疗卫生机构应当依法分类收集本单位产生的医疗废物，交由医疗废物集中处置单位处置。医疗废物集中处置单位应当及时收集、运输和处置医疗废物。  
医疗卫生机构和医疗废物集中处置单位，应当采取有效措施，防止医疗废物流失、泄漏、渗漏、扩散。

**Article 544** Medical waste shall be managed in accordance with the national list of hazardous wastes. Local people's governments at or above the county level shall strengthen the capacity building for centralized disposal of medical waste.  
The health and ecological environment departments and other relevant departments shall, within their respective functions and duties, strengthen the supervision and management of the collection, storage, transportation, and disposal of medical waste, to prevent harm to public health and environmental pollution.  
Medical and health institutions shall classify and collect the medical waste generated by their units according to law, and deliver it to the centralized medical waste disposal unit for disposal. The centralized medical waste disposal unit shall collect, transport, and dispose of medical waste in a timely manner.  
Medical and health institutions and centralized medical waste disposal units shall take effective measures to prevent the loss, leakage, seepage, and spread of medical waste.

<a id="art-545"></a>第五百四十五条　重大传染病疫情等突发事件发生时，县级以上人民政府应当统筹协调医疗废物等危险废物收集、贮存、运输、处置等工作，保障所需的车辆、场地、处置设施和防护物资。卫生健康、生态环境、环境卫生、交通运输等有关部门应当协同配合，依法履行应急处置职责。  
第七分编　噪声污染防治

**Article 545** When major infectious disease epidemics or other emergencies occur, the people's government at or above the county level shall coordinate the collection, storage, transportation, disposal, and other work of hazardous waste such as medical waste, and ensure the necessary vehicles, sites, disposal facilities, and protective materials. The health, ecological environment, environmental sanitation, transportation, and other relevant departments shall cooperate and perform emergency response duties according to law.  
Division 7 Prevention and Control of Noise Pollution

### 第二十四章　一般规定

<a id="art-546"></a>第五百四十六条　本分编适用于噪声污染的防治。

**Article 546** This Division applies to the prevention and control of noise pollution.

<a id="art-547"></a>第五百四十七条　本法所称噪声污染，是指在工业生产、建筑施工、交通运输和社会生活中超过噪声排放标准或者未依法采取防控措施产生噪声，并干扰他人正常生活、工作和学习的现象。

**Article 547** For the purposes of this Law, "noise pollution" means a phenomenon in which noise exceeding the noise emission standards or generated without taking prevention and control measures according to law in industrial production, construction, transportation, and social life interferes with the normal life, work, and study of others.

<a id="art-548"></a>第五百四十八条　国务院生态环境主管部门对全国噪声污染防治工作实施统一监督管理。地方人民政府生态环境主管部门对本行政区域噪声污染防治工作实施统一监督管理。  
县级以上人民政府住房城乡建设、公安、交通运输、铁路监督管理、民用航空、海事等有关部门在各自职责范围内对建筑施工、交通运输和社会生活噪声污染防治工作实施监督管理。

**Article 548** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall exercise unified supervision and administration over the prevention and control of noise pollution nationwide. The competent department of ecological environment of the local people's government shall exercise unified supervision and administration over the prevention and control of noise pollution within its administrative region.  
The relevant departments of the people's governments at or above the county level, such as housing and urban-rural construction, public security, transportation, railway supervision, civil aviation, and maritime affairs, shall, within their respective functions and duties, exercise supervision and administration over the prevention and control of noise pollution from construction, transportation, and social life.

<a id="art-549"></a>第五百四十九条　基层群众性自治组织协助地方人民政府及其有关部门做好噪声污染防治工作。

**Article 549** The grassroots self-governing mass organizations shall assist the local people's governments and their relevant departments in carrying out the work of prevention and control of noise pollution.

<a id="art-550"></a>第五百五十条　排放噪声的单位和公共场所管理者，应当建立噪声污染防治责任制度，明确负责人和相关人员的责任。

**Article 550** Units that discharge noise and managers of public places shall establish a responsibility system for the prevention and control of noise pollution, and specify the responsibilities of the persons in charge and relevant personnel.

<a id="art-551"></a>第五百五十一条　各级人民政府及其有关部门编制国土空间规划和相关规划，应当充分考虑城乡区域开发、改造和建设项目产生的噪声对周围生活环境的影响，统筹规划，合理安排土地用途和建设布局，防止、减轻噪声污染。

**Article 551** When preparing territorial space plans and relevant plans, people's governments at all levels and their relevant departments shall fully consider the impact of noise generated by urban and rural regional development, renovation, and construction projects on the surrounding living environment, make overall plans, reasonably arrange land use and construction layout, and prevent and mitigate noise pollution.

<a id="art-552"></a>第五百五十二条　未达到国家声环境质量标准的地区所在设区的市、县级人民政府，应当及时编制声环境质量改善规划及其实施方案，采取有效措施，改善声环境质量。

**Article 552** The people's governments of cities divided into districts and counties where areas fail to meet the national acoustic environment quality standards shall, in a timely manner, formulate plans for improving acoustic environment quality and implementation plans thereof, and take effective measures to improve the acoustic environment quality.

<a id="art-553"></a>第五百五十三条　县级以上地方人民政府根据国家声环境质量标准和国土空间规划以及用地现状，划定本行政区域各类声环境质量标准的适用区域；将以用于居住、科学研究、医疗卫生、文化教育、机关团体办公、社会福利等的建筑物为主的区域，划定为噪声敏感建筑物集中区域，加强噪声污染防治。  
声环境质量标准适用区域范围和噪声敏感建筑物集中区域范围应当及时公布。  
本法所称噪声敏感建筑物，是指用于居住、科学研究、医疗卫生、文化教育、机关团体办公、社会福利等需要保持安静的建筑物。

**Article 553** The local people's governments at or above the county level shall, in accordance with the national acoustic environment quality standards, territorial space plans, and current land use, delimit the applicable areas for various types of acoustic environment quality standards within their administrative regions; and shall designate areas mainly consisting of buildings used for residence, scientific research, medical and health care, culture and education, government offices, social welfare, etc., as concentrated areas of noise-sensitive buildings, and strengthen the prevention and control of noise pollution.  
The scope of applicable areas for acoustic environment quality standards and the scope of concentrated areas of noise-sensitive buildings shall be published in a timely manner.  
For the purposes of this Law, "noise-sensitive buildings" means buildings used for residence, scientific research, medical and health care, culture and education, government offices, social welfare, etc., that require maintaining quietness.

<a id="art-554"></a>第五百五十四条　国务院生态环境主管部门根据国家声环境质量标准和国家经济、技术条件，制定国家环境振动控制标准。  
产生振动，应当符合环境振动控制标准和有关法律、法规、规章的要求。

**Article 554** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, in accordance with the national acoustic environment quality standards and the national economic and technological conditions, formulate national environmental vibration control standards.  
Vibrations generated shall comply with the environmental vibration control standards and the requirements of relevant laws, regulations, and rules.

<a id="art-555"></a>第五百五十五条　国务院标准化主管部门会同国务院发展改革、生态环境、工业和信息化、住房城乡建设、交通运输、铁路监督管理、民用航空、海事等有关部门，对可能产生噪声污染的工业设备、施工机械、机动车、铁路机车车辆、城市轨道交通车辆、民用航空器、机动船舶、电气电子产品、建筑附属设备等产品，根据噪声污染防治的要求和国家经济、技术条件，在其技术规范或者产品质量标准中规定噪声限值。  
前款规定的产品使用时产生噪声的限值，应当在有关技术文件中注明。禁止生产、进口或者销售不符合噪声限值的产品。  
县级以上人民政府市场监督管理等有关部门应当在各自职责范围内对生产、销售的有噪声限值的产品进行监督抽查，对电梯等特种设备使用时发出的噪声进行监督抽测，生态环境主管部门予以配合。

**Article 555** The competent department of standardization of the State Council shall, jointly with the relevant departments such as development and reform, ecological environment, industry and information technology, housing and urban-rural construction, transportation, railway supervision, civil aviation, and maritime affairs of the State Council, stipulate noise limits in the technical specifications or product quality standards for products that may cause noise pollution, such as industrial equipment, construction machinery, motor vehicles, railway rolling stock, urban rail transit vehicles, civil aircraft, motor vessels, electrical and electronic products, and building ancillary equipment, in accordance with the requirements of noise pollution prevention and control and the national economic and technological conditions.  
The noise limits for the products specified in the preceding paragraph when in use shall be indicated in the relevant technical documents. The production, import, or sale of products that do not meet the noise limits is prohibited.  
The market supervision and administration departments and other relevant departments of the people's governments at or above the county level shall, within their respective functions and duties, conduct supervisory spot checks on products with noise limits that are produced and sold, and conduct supervisory sampling tests on noise emitted by special equipment such as elevators when in use, and the competent department of ecological environment shall cooperate.

<a id="art-556"></a>第五百五十六条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门组织开展声环境质量监测，推进监测自动化。地方人民政府生态环境主管部门会同有关部门按照规定设置本行政区域的声环境质量监测站点，组织开展本行政区域的声环境质量监测。  
地方人民政府生态环境等有关部门应当在各自职责范围内加强对噪声敏感建筑物周边等重点区域噪声排放情况的调查、监测。

**Article 556** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, jointly with relevant departments, organize and carry out acoustic environment quality monitoring and promote the automation of monitoring. The competent department of ecological environment of local people's governments shall, jointly with relevant departments, set up acoustic environment quality monitoring stations in their administrative regions in accordance with regulations, and organize and carry out acoustic environment quality monitoring in their administrative regions.  
The ecological environment and other relevant departments of local people's governments shall, within their respective functions and duties, strengthen the investigation and monitoring of noise emissions in key areas such as the vicinity of noise-sensitive buildings.

<a id="art-557"></a>第五百五十七条　确定建设布局，应当根据国家声环境质量标准和民用建筑隔声设计相关标准，合理划定建筑物与交通干线等的防噪声距离，并提出相应的规划设计要求。  
前款所称交通干线，包括铁路、高速公路、一级公路、二级公路、城市快速路、城市主干路、城市次干路、城市轨道交通线路、内河高等级航道。

**Article 557** When determining the construction layout, the noise prevention distance between buildings and traffic arteries, etc., shall be reasonably delimited in accordance with the national acoustic environment quality standards and relevant standards for sound insulation design of civil buildings, and corresponding planning and design requirements shall be put forward.  
For the purposes of the preceding paragraph, "traffic arteries" include railways, expressways, Class I highways, Class II highways, urban expressways, urban trunk roads, urban secondary trunk roads, urban rail transit lines, and high-grade inland waterways.

<a id="art-558"></a>第五百五十八条　建设噪声敏感建筑物，应当符合民用建筑隔声设计相关标准的要求，不符合标准要求的，不得通过验收、交付使用；在交通干线两侧、工业企业周边等地方建设噪声敏感建筑物，还应当按照规定间隔一定距离，并采取减少振动、降低噪声的措施。

**Article 558** The construction of noise-sensitive buildings shall meet the requirements of relevant standards for sound insulation design of civil buildings; those that do not meet the standard requirements shall not pass acceptance inspection or be put into use. In addition, when constructing noise-sensitive buildings on both sides of traffic arteries, around industrial enterprises, or in other places, a certain distance shall be left in accordance with regulations, and measures to reduce vibration and noise shall be taken.

<a id="art-559"></a>第五百五十九条　国家鼓励、支持低噪声工艺和设备的研究开发和推广应用。

**Article 559** The State encourages and supports the research, development, and promotion of low-noise processes and equipment.

<a id="art-560"></a>第五百六十条　国家鼓励开展宁静小区、静音车厢等宁静区域创建活动，共同维护生活环境和谐安宁。

**Article 560** The State encourages the creation of quiet areas such as quiet residential communities and quiet train compartments, to jointly maintain a harmonious and peaceful living environment.

<a id="art-561"></a>第五百六十一条　在举行中等学校招生考试、高等学校招生统一考试等特殊活动期间，地方人民政府或者其指定的部门可以对可能产生噪声影响的活动，作出时间和区域的限制性规定，并提前向社会公告。

**Article 561** During special activities such as secondary school entrance examinations and unified college entrance examinations, the local people's government or the department designated by it may impose restrictions on the time and area for activities that may produce noise impact, and shall announce such restrictions to the public in advance.

<a id="art-562"></a>第五百六十二条　排放噪声造成严重污染，被责令改正拒不改正的，生态环境主管部门和其他负有噪声污染防治监督管理职责的部门，可以依法查封、扣押排放噪声的场所、设施、设备、工具、物品。

**Article 562** If a person emits noise causing severe pollution and refuses to correct it after being ordered to do so, the competent department of ecological environment and other departments responsible for the supervision and administration of noise pollution prevention may, according to law, seal up or detain the premises, facilities, equipment, tools, and articles that emit noise.

<a id="art-563"></a>第五百六十三条　因从事本职生产经营工作受到噪声危害的防治，适用职业病防治法律的有关规定。

**Article 563** The prevention and control of noise hazards suffered from engaging in one's own production and operation work shall be governed by the relevant provisions of the occupational disease prevention and control law.

### 第二十五章　工业噪声污染防治

<a id="art-564"></a>第五百六十四条　本法所称工业噪声，是指在工业生产活动中产生的干扰周围生活环境的声音。

**Article 564** Industrial noise as used in this Law means sounds generated during industrial production activities that interfere with the surrounding living environment.

<a id="art-565"></a>第五百六十五条　工业企业选址应当符合国土空间规划以及相关规划要求，县级以上地方人民政府应当按照规划要求优化工业企业布局，防止工业噪声污染。

**Article 565** The site selection of industrial enterprises shall comply with the requirements of land and space planning and relevant planning. The local people's government at or above the county level shall optimize the layout of industrial enterprises in accordance with the planning requirements to prevent industrial noise pollution.

<a id="art-566"></a>第五百六十六条　在噪声敏感建筑物集中区域，禁止新建排放噪声的工业企业，改建、扩建工业企业的，应当采取有效措施防止工业噪声污染。

**Article 566** In areas where noise-sensitive buildings are concentrated, it is prohibited to construct new industrial enterprises that emit noise. Those that reconstruct or expand industrial enterprises shall take effective measures to prevent industrial noise pollution.

<a id="art-567"></a>第五百六十七条　排放工业噪声的企业事业单位和其他生产经营者，应当采取有效措施，减少振动、降低噪声。

**Article 567** Enterprises, institutions, and other producers and business operators that emit industrial noise shall take effective measures to reduce vibration and lower noise.

### 第二十六章　建筑施工噪声污染防治

<a id="art-568"></a>第五百六十八条　本法所称建筑施工噪声，是指在建筑施工过程中产生的干扰周围生活环境的声音。

**Article 568** Construction noise as used in this Law means sounds generated during construction activities that interfere with the surrounding living environment.

<a id="art-569"></a>第五百六十九条　建设单位应当按照规定将噪声污染防治费用列入工程造价，在施工合同中明确施工单位的噪声污染防治责任。  
施工单位应当按照规定制定噪声污染防治实施方案，采取有效措施，减少振动、降低噪声。建设单位应当监督施工单位落实噪声污染防治实施方案。

**Article 569** The construction unit shall, in accordance with the regulations, include the cost of noise pollution prevention and control in the project cost, and specify the noise pollution prevention and control responsibilities of the construction contractor in the construction contract.  
The construction contractor shall, in accordance with the regulations, develop an implementation plan for noise pollution prevention and control, and take effective measures to reduce vibration and lower noise. The construction unit shall supervise the construction contractor in implementing the noise pollution prevention and control implementation plan.

<a id="art-570"></a>第五百七十条　在噪声敏感建筑物集中区域施工作业或者因施工作业可能造成严重污染的，应当优先使用低噪声施工工艺和设备。  
国务院工业和信息化主管部门会同国务院生态环境、住房城乡建设、市场监督管理等有关部门，制定公布低噪声施工设备指导名录并适时更新。

**Article 570** If construction operations are carried out in areas where noise-sensitive buildings are concentrated, or construction operations may cause severe pollution, low-noise construction techniques and equipment shall be used as a priority.  
The competent department of industry and information technology of the State Council shall, together with the competent departments of ecological environment, housing and urban-rural construction, market supervision and administration, and other relevant departments of the State Council, formulate and publish a guidance list of low-noise construction equipment and update it in a timely manner.

<a id="art-571"></a>第五百七十一条　在噪声敏感建筑物集中区域施工作业或者因施工作业可能造成严重污染的，建设单位应当按照国家规定，设置噪声自动监测系统，与监督管理部门联网，保存原始监测记录。

**Article 571** If construction operations are carried out in areas where noise-sensitive buildings are concentrated, or construction operations may cause severe pollution, the construction unit shall, in accordance with state regulations, set up an automatic noise monitoring system, connect it to the supervision and management department, and preserve original monitoring records.

<a id="art-572"></a>第五百七十二条　在噪声敏感建筑物集中区域禁止夜间进行产生噪声的施工作业，但是抢修、抢险施工作业，因生产工艺要求或者其他特殊需要必须连续施工作业的除外。  
因特殊需要必须连续施工作业的，应当取得地方人民政府住房城乡建设主管部门、生态环境主管部门或者地方人民政府指定的部门的证明，并在施工现场显著位置公示或者以其他方式公告附近居民。  
本条第一款所称夜间，是指晚上十点至次日早晨六点之间的期间，设区的市级以上人民政府可以另行规定本行政区域夜间的起止时间，夜间时段长度为八小时。

**Article 572** In areas where noise-sensitive buildings are concentrated, construction operations that generate noise are prohibited at night, except for emergency repair and rescue construction operations, or where construction operations must be continuous due to production process requirements or other special needs.  
If continuous construction operations are necessary due to special needs, the construction unit shall obtain a certificate from the competent department of housing and urban-rural construction, the competent department of ecological environment, or the department designated by the local people's government, and shall post it at a conspicuous place at the construction site or otherwise notify nearby residents.  
'Night' as used in the first paragraph of this Article means the period from 10:00 p.m. to 6:00 a.m. of the following day. The local people's government at or above the city divided into districts may separately prescribe the starting and ending times of night in its administrative area, provided that the duration of the night period is eight hours.

### 第二十七章　交通运输噪声污染防治

<a id="art-573"></a>第五百七十三条　本法所称交通运输噪声，是指机动车、铁路机车车辆、城市轨道交通车辆、机动船舶、航空器等交通运输工具在运行时产生的干扰周围生活环境的声音。

**Article 573** Transportation noise as used in this Law means sounds generated during the operation of transportation vehicles such as motor vehicles, railway locomotives and carriages, urban rail transit vehicles, motor vessels, and aircraft that interfere with the surrounding living environment.

<a id="art-574"></a>第五百七十四条　各级人民政府及其有关部门编制国土空间规划和交通运输等相关规划，应当综合考虑公路、城市道路、铁路、城市轨道交通线路、水路、港口和民用机场及其起降航线对周围声环境的影响。  
新建公路、铁路线路选线设计，应当尽量避让噪声敏感建筑物集中区域。  
新建民用机场选址与噪声敏感建筑物集中区域的距离应当符合标准的要求。

**Article 574** When drawing up land and space planning and transportation and other relevant plans, the people's governments at all levels and their relevant departments shall comprehensively consider the impact of highways, urban roads, railways, urban rail transit lines, waterways, ports, and civil airports and their takeoff and landing routes on the surrounding acoustic environment.  
The route selection and design of newly built highways and railway lines shall, to the extent possible, avoid areas where noise-sensitive buildings are concentrated.  
The distance between the site selection of a newly built civil airport and the areas where noise-sensitive buildings are concentrated shall comply with the requirements of the standards.

<a id="art-575"></a>第五百七十五条　制定交通基础设施工程技术规范，应当明确噪声污染防治要求。  
新建、改建、扩建经过噪声敏感建筑物集中区域的高速公路、城市高架、铁路和城市轨道交通线路等的，建设单位应当在可能造成噪声污染的重点路段设置声屏障或者采取其他减少振动、降低噪声的措施，符合相关交通基础设施工程技术规范和标准的要求。  
建设单位违反前款规定的，由县级以上人民政府指定的部门责令制定、实施治理方案。

**Article 575** When formulating engineering technical specifications for transportation infrastructure, the requirements for noise pollution prevention and control shall be specified.  
If newly built, reconstructed, or expanded highways, urban viaducts, railway lines, and urban rail transit lines pass through areas where noise-sensitive buildings are concentrated, the construction unit shall install sound barriers on key sections that may cause noise pollution or take other measures to reduce vibration and lower noise, and shall comply with the requirements of relevant engineering technical specifications and standards for transportation infrastructure.  
If the construction unit violates the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the department designated by the local people's government at or above the county level shall order it to formulate and implement a remediation plan.

<a id="art-576"></a>第五百七十六条　机动车的消声器和喇叭应当符合国家规定。禁止驾驶拆除或者损坏消声器、加装排气管等擅自改装的机动车以轰鸣、疾驶等方式造成噪声污染。  
使用机动车音响器材，应当控制音量，防止噪声污染。  
机动车应当加强维修和保养，保持性能良好，防止噪声污染。

**Article 576** The mufflers and horns of motor vehicles shall comply with state regulations. It is prohibited to drive a motor vehicle that has been modified without authorization, such as by removing or damaging the muffler or adding an exhaust pipe, to cause noise pollution by roaring or speeding.  
When using audio equipment on a motor vehicle, the volume shall be controlled to prevent noise pollution.  
Motor vehicles shall undergo strengthened repair and maintenance to maintain good performance and prevent noise pollution.

<a id="art-577"></a>第五百七十七条　机动车、铁路机车车辆、城市轨道交通车辆、机动船舶等交通运输工具运行时，应当按照规定使用喇叭等声响装置。  
警车、消防车、工程救险车、救护车等机动车安装、使用警报器，应当符合国务院公安等有关部门的规定；非执行紧急任务，不得使用警报器。

**Article 577** When motor vehicles, railway locomotives and carriages, urban rail transit vehicles, motor vessels, and other transportation vehicles are in operation, they shall use horns and other sound devices in accordance with regulations.  
The installation and use of sirens on motor vehicles such as police cars, fire engines, emergency rescue vehicles, and ambulances shall comply with the provisions of the public security department of the State Council and other relevant departments; they shall not use sirens when not on emergency duties.

<a id="art-578"></a>第五百七十八条　地方人民政府生态环境主管部门会同公安机关根据噪声污染防治的需要，可以划定禁止机动车行驶和使用喇叭等声响装置的路段和时间，向社会公告，并由公安机关交通管理部门依法设置相关标志、标线。

**Article 578** The competent department of ecological environment of the local people's government together with the public security organ may, based on the needs of noise pollution prevention and control, designate sections of road and time periods where motor vehicle driving and use of horns and other sound devices are prohibited, announce such designations to the public, and the traffic management department of the public security organ shall set up relevant signs and markings according to law.

<a id="art-579"></a>第五百七十九条　在车站、铁路站场、港口等地方指挥作业时使用广播喇叭的，应当控制音量，减轻噪声污染。

**Article 579** When using loudspeakers to direct operations at stations, railway yards, ports, and other places, the volume shall be controlled to reduce noise pollution.

<a id="art-580"></a>第五百八十条　公路养护管理单位、城市道路养护维修单位应当加强对公路、城市道路的维护和保养，保证减少振动、降低噪声设施正常运行。  
城市轨道交通运营单位、铁路运输企业应当加强对城市轨道交通线路和城市轨道交通车辆、铁路线路和铁路机车车辆的维护和保养，保证减少振动、降低噪声设施正常运行，并按照国家规定进行监测，保存原始监测记录。

**Article 580** The highway maintenance and management unit and the urban road maintenance and repair unit shall strengthen the maintenance and upkeep of highways and urban roads to ensure the normal operation of facilities for reducing vibration and lowering noise.  
The urban rail transit operation unit and the railway transport enterprise shall strengthen the maintenance and upkeep of urban rail transit lines and urban rail transit vehicles, railway lines and railway locomotives and carriages, to ensure the normal operation of facilities for reducing vibration and lowering noise, and shall conduct monitoring in accordance with state regulations and preserve original monitoring records.

<a id="art-581"></a>第五百八十一条　民用机场所在地人民政府，应当根据生态环境影响评价以及监测结果确定的民用航空器噪声对机场周围生活环境产生影响的范围和程度，划定噪声敏感建筑物禁止建设区域和限制建设区域，并实施控制。  
在禁止建设区域禁止新建与航空无关的噪声敏感建筑物。  
在限制建设区域确需建设噪声敏感建筑物的，建设单位应当对噪声敏感建筑物进行建筑隔声设计，符合民用建筑隔声设计相关标准的要求。

**Article 581** The people's government of the place where a civil airport is located shall, based on the scope and degree of impact of civil aircraft noise on the living environment around the airport as determined by the ecological environment impact assessment and monitoring results, delineate areas where construction of noise-sensitive buildings is prohibited and areas where such construction is restricted, and shall implement controls.  
Construction of noise-sensitive buildings unrelated to aviation is prohibited in the prohibited area.  
Where it is truly necessary to construct noise-sensitive buildings in the restricted area, the construction entity shall carry out architectural sound insulation design for the noise-sensitive buildings, meeting the requirements of relevant standards for sound insulation design of civil buildings.

<a id="art-582"></a>第五百八十二条　民用航空器应当符合国务院民用航空主管部门规定的适航标准中的有关噪声要求。

**Article 582** Civil aircraft shall comply with the noise requirements in the airworthiness standards prescribed by the civil aviation authority under the State Council.

<a id="art-583"></a>第五百八十三条　民用机场管理机构负责机场起降航空器噪声的管理，会同航空运输企业、通用航空企业、空中交通管理部门等单位，采取低噪声飞行程序、起降跑道优化、运行架次和时段控制、高噪声航空器运行限制或者周围噪声敏感建筑物隔声降噪等措施，防止、减轻民用航空器噪声污染。  
民用机场管理机构应当按照国家规定，对机场周围民用航空器噪声进行监测，保存原始监测记录，监测结果定期向民用航空主管部门、生态环境主管部门报告。

**Article 583** The civil airport management authority shall be responsible for the management of noise from aircraft taking off and landing at the airport, and shall, together with air transport enterprises, general aviation enterprises, air traffic control departments and other entities, adopt measures such as low-noise flight procedures, optimization of take-off and landing runways, control of flight sorties and time periods, restrictions on operation of high-noise aircraft, or sound insulation and noise reduction for surrounding noise-sensitive buildings, to prevent and mitigate civil aircraft noise pollution.  
The civil airport management authority shall, in accordance with state regulations, monitor civil aircraft noise around the airport, retain original monitoring records, and regularly report the monitoring results to the civil aviation authority and the ecological environment authority.

<a id="art-584"></a>第五百八十四条　因公路、城市道路和城市轨道交通运行排放噪声造成严重污染的，设区的市、县级人民政府应当组织有关部门和其他有关单位对噪声污染情况进行调查评估和责任认定，制定噪声污染综合治理方案。  
噪声污染责任主体应当按照噪声污染综合治理方案的要求采取管理或者工程措施，减轻噪声污染。

**Article 584** Where serious pollution is caused by noise emitted from the operation of highways, urban roads and urban rail transit, the people's government of a city with districts or at the county level shall organize relevant departments and other relevant entities to investigate and assess the noise pollution situation and determine liability, and shall formulate a comprehensive noise pollution control plan.  
The subject liable for noise pollution shall take management or engineering measures in accordance with the requirements of the comprehensive noise pollution control plan to mitigate the noise pollution.

<a id="art-585"></a>第五百八十五条　因铁路运行排放噪声造成严重污染的，铁路运输企业和设区的市、县级人民政府应当对噪声污染情况进行调查，制定噪声污染综合治理方案。  
铁路运输企业和设区的市、县级人民政府有关部门和其他有关单位应当按照噪声污染综合治理方案的要求采取有效措施，减轻噪声污染。

**Article 585** Where serious pollution is caused by noise emitted from railway operations, the railway transport enterprise and the people's government of a city with districts or at the county level shall investigate the noise pollution situation and formulate a comprehensive noise pollution control plan.  
The railway transport enterprise and the relevant departments of the people's government of a city with districts or at the county level, as well as other relevant entities, shall take effective measures in accordance with the requirements of the comprehensive noise pollution control plan to mitigate the noise pollution.

<a id="art-586"></a>第五百八十六条　因民用航空器起降排放噪声造成严重污染的，民用机场所在地人民政府应当组织有关部门和其他有关单位对噪声污染情况进行调查，综合考虑经济、技术和管理措施，制定噪声污染综合治理方案。  
民用机场管理机构、地方各级人民政府和其他有关单位应当按照噪声污染综合治理方案的要求采取有效措施，减轻噪声污染。

**Article 586** Where serious pollution is caused by noise emitted from the take-off and landing of civil aircraft, the people's government of the place where the civil airport is located shall organize relevant departments and other relevant entities to investigate the noise pollution situation, comprehensively consider economic, technical and management measures, and formulate a comprehensive noise pollution control plan.  
The civil airport management authority, local people's governments at all levels and other relevant entities shall take effective measures in accordance with the requirements of the comprehensive noise pollution control plan to mitigate the noise pollution.

<a id="art-587"></a>第五百八十七条　制定噪声污染综合治理方案，应当征求有关专家、公众等方面的意见。

**Article 587** When formulating a comprehensive noise pollution control plan, opinions of relevant experts, the public and other parties shall be solicited.

### 第二十八章　社会生活噪声污染防治

<a id="art-588"></a>第五百八十八条　本法所称社会生活噪声，是指人为活动产生的除工业噪声、建筑施工噪声和交通运输噪声外的干扰周围生活环境的声音。

**Article 588** For the purposes of this Law, social life noise means sounds generated by human activities, other than industrial noise, construction noise and traffic noise, that interfere with the surrounding living environment.

<a id="art-589"></a>第五百八十九条　全社会应当增强噪声污染防治意识，自觉减少社会生活噪声排放，积极开展噪声污染防治活动，形成人人有责、人人参与、人人受益的良好噪声污染防治氛围，共同维护生活环境和谐安宁。

**Article 589** The whole society shall enhance awareness of noise pollution prevention and control, consciously reduce social life noise emissions, actively carry out noise pollution prevention and control activities, create a good atmosphere of noise pollution prevention and control in which everyone has responsibility, everyone participates, and everyone benefits, and jointly maintain a harmonious and tranquil living environment.

<a id="art-590"></a>第五百九十条　文化娱乐、体育、餐饮等场所的经营管理者应当采取有效措施，防止、减轻噪声污染。

**Article 590** The operators and managers of venues such as cultural entertainment, sports, and catering shall take effective measures to prevent and mitigate noise pollution.

<a id="art-591"></a>第五百九十一条　使用空调器、冷却塔、水泵、油烟净化器、风机、发电机、变压器、锅炉、装卸设备等可能产生社会生活噪声污染的设备、设施的企业事业单位和其他经营管理者等，应当采取优化布局、集中排放等措施，防止、减轻噪声污染。

**Article 591** Enterprises, institutions, and other operators and managers that use equipment and facilities such as air conditioners, cooling towers, water pumps, fume purifiers, fans, generators, transformers, boilers, loading and unloading equipment, which may generate social life noise pollution, shall take measures such as optimizing layout and centralized discharge to prevent and mitigate noise pollution.

<a id="art-592"></a>第五百九十二条　禁止在商业经营活动中使用高音广播喇叭或者采用其他持续反复发出高噪声的方法进行广告宣传。  
对商业经营活动中产生的其他噪声，经营者应当采取有效措施，防止噪声污染。

**Article 592** The use of high-pitched loudspeakers or other methods of continuously and repeatedly emitting high noise for advertising in commercial business activities is prohibited.  
For other noise generated in commercial business activities, the operator shall take effective measures to prevent noise pollution.

<a id="art-593"></a>第五百九十三条　禁止在噪声敏感建筑物集中区域使用高音广播喇叭，但是紧急情况以及地方人民政府规定的特殊情形除外。  
在街道、广场、公园等公共场所组织或者开展娱乐、健身等活动，应当遵守公共场所管理者有关活动区域、时段、音量等规定，采取有效措施，防止噪声污染；不得违反规定使用音响器材产生过大音量。  
公共场所管理者应当合理规定娱乐、健身等活动的区域、时段、音量，可以采取设置噪声自动监测和显示设施等措施加强管理。

**Article 593** The use of high-pitched loudspeakers in areas concentrated with noise-sensitive buildings is prohibited, except in emergency situations and special circumstances as prescribed by the local people's government.  
When organizing or carrying out entertainment, fitness, or other activities in public places such as streets, squares, and parks, the provisions of the public place manager regarding activity areas, time periods, and volume shall be complied with, and effective measures shall be taken to prevent noise pollution; sound equipment shall not be used in violation of regulations to produce excessive volume.  
The manager of a public place shall reasonably stipulate the areas, time periods, and volume for entertainment, fitness, and other activities, and may take measures such as installing noise automatic monitoring and display facilities to strengthen management.

<a id="art-594"></a>第五百九十四条　家庭及其成员应当培养形成减少噪声产生的良好习惯，乘坐公共交通工具、饲养宠物和其他日常活动尽量避免产生噪声对周围人员造成干扰，互谅互让解决噪声纠纷，共同维护声环境质量。  
使用家用电器、乐器或者进行其他家庭场所活动，应当控制音量或者采取其他有效措施，防止噪声污染。

**Article 594** Families and their members shall cultivate good habits of reducing noise generation, and in activities such as using public transportation, keeping pets, and other daily activities, shall try to avoid generating noise that causes disturbance to surrounding persons, resolve noise disputes through mutual understanding and accommodation, and jointly maintain the quality of the acoustic environment.  
When using household appliances, musical instruments, or engaging in other activities in family premises, the volume shall be controlled or other effective measures shall be taken to prevent noise pollution.

<a id="art-595"></a>第五百九十五条　对已竣工交付使用的住宅楼、商铺、办公楼等建筑物进行室内装修活动，应当按照规定限定作业时间，采取有效措施，防止、减轻噪声污染。

**Article 595** When carrying out interior decoration activities on buildings such as residential buildings, shops, and office buildings that have been completed and delivered for use, the operation time shall be limited in accordance with regulations, and effective measures shall be taken to prevent and mitigate noise pollution.

<a id="art-596"></a>第五百九十六条　新建居民住房的房地产开发经营者应当在销售场所公示住房可能受到噪声影响的情况以及采取或者拟采取的防治措施，并纳入买卖合同。  
新建居民住房的房地产开发经营者应当在买卖合同中明确住房的共用设施、设备位置和建筑隔声情况。

**Article 596** The real estate development operator of newly-built residential housing shall, at the sales site, disclose the circumstances under which the housing may be affected by noise and the prevention and control measures taken or to be taken, and shall incorporate these into the sales contract.  
The real estate development operator of newly-built residential housing shall specify in the sales contract the locations of common facilities and equipment of the housing and the building sound insulation conditions.

<a id="art-597"></a>第五百九十七条　居民住宅区安装电梯、水泵、变压器等共用设施、设备的，建设单位应当合理设置，采取减少振动、降低噪声的措施，符合民用建筑隔声设计相关标准的要求。  
已建成使用的居民住宅区电梯、水泵、变压器等共用设施、设备由专业运营单位负责维护管理，符合民用建筑隔声设计相关标准的要求。

**Article 597** Where common facilities and equipment such as elevators, water pumps, and transformers are installed in a residential area, the construction entity shall install them reasonably, take measures to reduce vibration and lower noise, and meet the requirements of relevant standards for sound insulation design of civil buildings.  
The common facilities and equipment such as elevators, water pumps, and transformers in a residential area that has been completed and put into use shall be maintained and managed by a professional operating entity, and shall meet the requirements of relevant standards for sound insulation design of civil buildings.

<a id="art-598"></a>第五百九十八条　基层群众性自治组织指导业主委员会、物业服务人、业主通过制定管理规约或者其他形式，约定本物业管理区域噪声污染防治要求，由业主共同遵守。

**Article 598** A grassroots self-governing mass organization shall guide the owners' committee, property service provider, and owners to stipulate the requirements for noise pollution prevention and control in the property management area through the formulation of management covenants or other forms, to be jointly observed by the owners.

<a id="art-599"></a>第五百九十九条　对噪声敏感建筑物集中区域的社会生活噪声扰民行为，基层群众性自治组织、业主委员会、物业服务人应当及时劝阻、调解；劝阻、调解无效的，可以向负有社会生活噪声污染防治监督管理职责的部门或者地方人民政府指定的部门报告或者投诉，接到报告或者投诉的部门应当依法处理。  
第八分编　放射性污染防治

**Article 599** In the event of social life noise nuisance in areas concentrated with noise-sensitive buildings, the grassroots self-governing mass organization, owners' committee, and property service provider shall promptly dissuade and mediate; if dissuasion and mediation are ineffective, they may report or complain to the department responsible for supervision and administration of social life noise pollution prevention and control or the department designated by the local people's government, and the department receiving the report or complaint shall handle it according to law.  
Division 8 Prevention and Control of Radioactive Pollution

### 第二十九章　一般规定

<a id="art-600"></a>第六百条　本分编适用于在核设施选址、建造、运行、退役和核技术、铀（钍）矿、伴生放射性矿开发利用过程中发生的放射性污染的防治活动。

**Article 600** This Division applies to the prevention and control of radioactive pollution occurring in the processes of siting, construction, operation, and decommissioning of nuclear facilities, and the development and utilization of nuclear technology, uranium (thorium) mines, and associated radioactive mines.

<a id="art-601"></a>第六百零一条　本法所称放射性污染，是指由于人类活动造成物料、人体、场所、环境介质表面或者内部出现超过国家标准的放射性物质或者射线。

**Article 601** For the purposes of this Law, "radioactive pollution" means the presence of radioactive substances or radiation exceeding national standards in materials, human bodies, places, environmental media surfaces or interiors due to human activities.

<a id="art-602"></a>第六百零二条　放射性污染防治应当坚持严格管理、安全第一，促进核能、核技术的开发与和平利用。

**Article 602** The prevention and control of radioactive pollution shall adhere to strict management, safety first, and promote the development and peaceful use of nuclear energy and nuclear technology.

<a id="art-603"></a>第六百零三条　核设施营运单位，核技术利用单位和其他生产经营者，铀（钍）矿和伴生放射性矿开发利用单位，专门从事放射性废物贮存、处理、处置的单位应当采取有效措施，防止、减少放射性污染，依法对其造成的放射性污染承担责任。  
生产、贮存、运输、销售、使用、处置含有放射性物质的物品，应当遵守国家规定，防止污染环境。

**Article 603** Nuclear facility operating entities, nuclear technology utilization entities and other producers and operators, uranium (thorium) mines and associated radioactive mine development and utilization entities, and entities specialized in the storage, treatment and disposal of radioactive waste shall take effective measures to prevent and reduce radioactive pollution, and bear liability for radioactive pollution caused by them according to law.  
Production, storage, transportation, sale, use and disposal of articles containing radioactive substances shall comply with state regulations to prevent environmental pollution.

<a id="art-604"></a>第六百零四条　国务院生态环境主管部门对全国放射性污染防治工作实施统一监督管理。  
国务院卫生健康等有关部门在各自职责范围内对放射性污染防治工作实施监督管理。

**Article 604** The competent department of ecological environment under the State Council shall implement unified supervision and administration over the national prevention and control of radioactive pollution.  
The competent departments of health and other relevant departments under the State Council shall implement supervision and administration over the prevention and control of radioactive pollution within their respective responsibilities.

<a id="art-605"></a>第六百零五条　国务院生态环境主管部门根据环境安全要求和国家经济、技术条件制定国家放射性污染防治标准。

**Article 605** The competent department of ecological environment under the State Council shall formulate national standards for the prevention and control of radioactive pollution in accordance with environmental safety requirements and national economic and technological conditions.

<a id="art-606"></a>第六百零六条　含有放射性物质的产品和射线装置，应当符合国家放射性污染防治标准；不符合国家放射性污染防治标准的，不得出厂、销售。  
使用伴生放射性矿渣和含有天然放射性物质的石材等，应当符合国家建筑材料放射性核素控制等标准。

**Article 606** Products containing radioactive substances and radiation devices shall conform to national standards for the prevention and control of radioactive pollution; those that do not conform to national standards for the prevention and control of radioactive pollution shall not be delivered from factory or sold.  
Use of associated radioactive mine slag and stone materials containing natural radioactive substances shall conform to national standards for control of radionuclides in building materials, etc.

<a id="art-607"></a>第六百零七条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门组织监测网络，对放射性污染实施监测管理。

**Article 607** The competent department of ecological environment under the State Council shall, together with relevant departments, organize a monitoring network to implement monitoring and management of radioactive pollution.

<a id="art-608"></a>第六百零八条　核设施营运单位，核技术利用单位和其他生产经营者，铀（钍）矿和伴生放射性矿开发利用单位，专门从事放射性废物贮存、处理、处置的单位应当采取安全与防护措施，预防发生可能导致放射性污染的各类事故，避免放射性污染危害。  
核设施营运单位，核技术利用单位和其他生产经营者，铀（钍）矿和伴生放射性矿开发利用单位，专门从事放射性废物贮存、处理、处置的单位应当对其工作人员进行放射性安全教育、培训，采取有效的防护安全措施。

**Article 608** Nuclear facility operating entities, nuclear technology utilization entities and other producers and operators, uranium (thorium) mines and associated radioactive mine development and utilization entities, and entities specialized in the storage, treatment and disposal of radioactive waste shall take safety and protection measures to prevent various accidents that may cause radioactive pollution and avoid radioactive pollution hazards.  
Nuclear facility operating entities, nuclear technology utilization entities and other producers and operators, uranium (thorium) mines and associated radioactive mine development and utilization entities, and entities specialized in the storage, treatment and disposal of radioactive waste shall provide radioactive safety education and training to their staff and take effective protection safety measures.

<a id="art-609"></a>第六百零九条　运输放射性物质应当采取有效措施，防止放射性污染。具体办法由国务院制定。

**Article 609** Transportation of radioactive substances shall take effective measures to prevent radioactive pollution. Specific measures shall be formulated by the State Council.

<a id="art-610"></a>第六百一十条　放射性物质和射线装置应当设置明显的放射性标识和中文警示说明。生产、贮存、销售、使用、处置放射性物质和射线装置的场所，以及运输放射性物质的工具，应当设置明显的放射性标志。

**Article 610** Radioactive substances and radiation devices shall be provided with clear radioactive identification and Chinese warning instructions. Places where radioactive substances and radiation devices are produced, stored, sold, used or disposed of, and tools for transporting radioactive substances, shall be provided with clear radioactive signs.

<a id="art-611"></a>第六百一十一条　国家对从事放射性污染防治的专业人员实行资格管理制度，对从事放射性污染监测工作的机构实行资质管理制度。  
从事放射性污染监测工作的机构，应当具备下列条件，经国务院生态环境主管部门依法审查合格，取得资质证书后，方可在资质许可范围内从事放射性污染监测工作：  
（一）有法人资格；  
（二）有与所开展监测工作相匹配的专业技术人员；  
（三）有满足相关标准规范要求的工作场所、实验条件、设施、设备；  
（四）有健全的质量管理体系。

**Article 611** The State implements a qualification management system for professionals engaged in the prevention and control of radioactive pollution, and a qualification management system for institutions engaged in the monitoring of radioactive pollution.  
Institutions engaged in the monitoring of radioactive pollution shall meet the following conditions and, after being lawfully examined and certified qualified by the competent department of ecological environment under the State Council and obtaining a qualification certificate, may engage in the monitoring of radioactive pollution within the scope of the qualification license:  
(1) have legal person status;  
(2) have professional and technical personnel matching the monitoring work;  
(3) have workplaces, experimental conditions, facilities and equipment meeting the requirements of relevant standards and specifications;  
(4) have a sound quality management system.

<a id="art-612"></a>第六百一十二条　国务院生态环境等有关部门应当互通信息、密切配合，在各自职责范围内对核设施、核技术利用、铀（钍）矿开发利用中的放射性污染防治进行监督检查。  
地方人民政府生态环境等有关部门应当互通信息、密切配合，在各自职责范围内对本行政区域核技术利用、伴生放射性矿开发利用中的放射性污染防治进行监督检查。

**Article 612** The competent departments of ecological environment and other relevant departments under the State Council shall exchange information and cooperate closely, and shall supervise and inspect the prevention and control of radioactive pollution in nuclear facilities, nuclear technology utilization, and development and utilization of uranium (thorium) mines within their respective responsibilities.  
The competent departments of ecological environment and other relevant departments of local people's governments shall exchange information and cooperate closely, and shall supervise and inspect the prevention and control of radioactive pollution in nuclear technology utilization and development and utilization of associated radioactive mines within their respective responsibilities in their administrative regions.

<a id="art-613"></a>第六百一十三条　国家建立符合受控热核聚变特点、促进核聚变应用的放射性污染防治制度，对聚变燃料、聚变装置（设施）放射性污染防治实行分级分类管理。聚变装置（设施）建造等应当依法进行生态环境影响评价。

**Article 613** The State shall establish a radioactive pollution prevention and control system that conforms to the characteristics of controlled thermonuclear fusion and promotes the application of nuclear fusion, and implement graded and classified management for the prevention and control of radioactive pollution from fusion fuels and fusion devices (facilities). The construction of fusion devices (facilities) shall undergo ecological environment impact assessment according to law.

<a id="art-614"></a>第六百一十四条　在职业活动中接触放射性物质造成的职业病的防治，适用职业病防治法律的有关规定。

**Article 614** The prevention and treatment of occupational diseases caused by exposure to radioactive substances in occupational activities shall be governed by the relevant provisions of the Law on the Prevention and Control of Occupational Diseases.

### 第三十章　核设施放射性污染防治

<a id="art-615"></a>第六百一十五条　核设施营运单位进行核设施选址、建造、运行、退役等活动，应当依法申请核设施安全许可。  
本法所称核设施，是指核电厂、核热电厂、核供汽供热厂等核动力厂及装置，核动力厂以外的研究堆、实验堆、临界装置等其他反应堆，核燃料生产、加工、贮存和后处理设施等核燃料循环设施，以及上述设施产生的放射性废物的贮存、处理、处置设施。

**Article 615** Nuclear facility operating entities engaging in activities such as siting, construction, operation, and decommissioning of nuclear facilities shall apply for nuclear facility safety licenses according to law.  
For the purposes of this Law, "nuclear facilities" means nuclear power plants, nuclear heating plants, nuclear steam and heat supply plants and other nuclear power installations and devices, other reactors such as research reactors, experimental reactors, and critical assemblies other than nuclear power installations, nuclear fuel cycle facilities such as nuclear fuel production, processing, storage and reprocessing facilities, and facilities for the storage, treatment and disposal of radioactive waste generated by the above facilities.

<a id="art-616"></a>第六百一十六条　核设施营运单位应当按照国务院生态环境主管部门的规定在核设施建造、退役，以及选址、运行等环节开展生态环境影响评价。

**Article 616** Nuclear facility operating entities shall carry out ecological environment impact assessments in the construction, decommissioning, siting, and operation stages of nuclear facilities in accordance with the provisions of the competent department of ecological environment under the State Council.

<a id="art-617"></a>第六百一十七条　进口核设施，应当符合国家放射性污染防治标准；没有相应的国家放射性污染防治标准的，采用国务院生态环境主管部门指定的相关标准。

**Article 617** The import of nuclear facilities shall conform to national standards for the prevention and control of radioactive pollution; where there are no corresponding national standards for the prevention and control of radioactive pollution, the relevant standards designated by the competent department of ecological environment under the State Council shall be adopted.

<a id="art-618"></a>第六百一十八条　省、自治区、直辖市人民政府应当依法在核动力厂等重要核设施周围划定规划限制区。禁止在规划限制区内建设可能威胁核设施安全的易燃、易爆、腐蚀性物品的生产、贮存设施以及人口密集场所。规划限制区的划定和管理办法，由国务院制定。

**Article 618** The people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government shall, according to law, delineate planned restricted zones around important nuclear facilities such as nuclear power plants. Construction of facilities for the production and storage of flammable, explosive, or corrosive articles that may threaten the safety of nuclear facilities, and densely populated places, shall be prohibited within the planned restricted zones. The measures for the delineation and administration of planned restricted zones shall be formulated by the State Council.

<a id="art-619"></a>第六百一十九条　核设施营运单位应当对核设施周边环境中所含的放射性核素的种类、浓度以及核设施流出物中的放射性核素总量实施监测，并定期向国务院生态环境主管部门和所在地省级人民政府生态环境主管部门报告监测结果。  
国务院生态环境主管部门负责对核动力厂等重要核设施实施监督性监测，并根据实际需要对其他核设施的流出物实施监测。监督性监测系统的建设、运行、维护费用由财政预算安排。

**Article 619** Nuclear facility operating entities shall monitor the types and concentrations of radionuclides contained in the surrounding environment of nuclear facilities and the total amount of radionuclides in the effluents of nuclear facilities, and shall regularly report the monitoring results to the competent department of ecological environment under the State Council and the competent department of ecological environment of the provincial people's government where the facility is located.  
The competent department of ecological environment under the State Council shall be responsible for implementing supervised monitoring of important nuclear facilities such as nuclear power plants, and shall monitor the effluents of other nuclear facilities as necessary. The costs of construction, operation and maintenance of the supervised monitoring system shall be arranged by the financial budget.

<a id="art-620"></a>第六百二十条　国家建立健全核事故应急制度。  
国家设立核事故应急协调委员会，组织、协调全国的核事故应急管理工作。国家核事故应急协调委员会成员单位，省、自治区、直辖市人民政府，核设施营运单位，应当依法做好核事故应急工作。

**Article 620** The State shall establish and improve the nuclear accident emergency response system.  
The State shall establish a nuclear accident emergency coordination committee to organize and coordinate the national nuclear accident emergency management work. The member units of the National Nuclear Accident Emergency Coordination Committee, the people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government, and nuclear facility operating entities shall well perform the nuclear accident emergency work according to law.

<a id="art-621"></a>第六百二十一条　核设施营运单位应当制定核设施退役计划。  
核设施的退役费用和放射性废物处置费用应当预提，列入投资概算或者生产成本，专门用于核设施退役、放射性废物处置。核设施的退役费用和放射性废物处置费用的提取和管理办法，由国务院财政部门、价格主管部门会同国务院生态环境主管部门、核工业主管部门和能源主管部门制定。

**Article 621** The operator of a nuclear facility shall formulate a decommissioning plan for the nuclear facility.  
The decommissioning costs of the nuclear facility and the disposal costs of radioactive waste shall be pre-appropriated, included in the investment estimate or production cost, and used exclusively for the decommissioning of the nuclear facility and the disposal of radioactive waste. The measures for the appropriation and management of the decommissioning costs of the nuclear facility and the disposal costs of radioactive waste shall be formulated by the financial department and the price administration department of the State Council jointly with the competent department of ecological environment, the competent department of nuclear industry, and the competent department of energy of the State Council.

### 第三十一章　核技术利用放射性污染防治

<a id="art-622"></a>第六百二十二条　国家加强核技术利用放射性污染防治。国务院生态环境主管部门对可能造成污染的放射性同位素和射线装置实施分级分类管理。  
本法所称核技术利用，是指放射性同位素和射线装置在医疗、工业、农业、地质调查、科学研究和教学等领域中的使用。

**Article 622** The State shall strengthen the prevention and control of radioactive pollution from the use of nuclear technology. The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall implement graded and classified management of radioactive isotopes and radiation devices that may cause pollution.  
For the purposes of this Law, “use of nuclear technology” means the use of radioactive isotopes and radiation devices in such fields as medical treatment, industry, agriculture, geological survey, scientific research, and teaching.

<a id="art-623"></a>第六百二十三条　生产、销售、使用放射性同位素和射线装置的单位和其他生产经营者，应当按照国务院有关放射性同位素与射线装置放射防护的规定取得许可证。未取得许可证的，不得从事放射性同位素和射线装置生产、销售、使用活动。禁止在网络上销售放射性同位素和射线装置。  
转让、进出口放射性同位素和射线装置的单位和其他生产经营者以及装备有放射性同位素的仪表的单位和其他生产经营者，应当按照国务院有关放射性同位素与射线装置放射防护的规定办理有关手续。

**Article 623** Units and other producers and business operators that produce, sell, or use radioactive isotopes and radiation devices shall obtain licenses in accordance with the provisions of the State Council on radiation protection of radioactive isotopes and radiation devices. Without obtaining a license, no one may engage in the production, sale, or use of radioactive isotopes and radiation devices. It is prohibited to sell radioactive isotopes and radiation devices on the Internet.  
Units and other producers and business operators that transfer, import, or export radioactive isotopes and radiation devices, and units and other producers and business operators that are equipped with instruments containing radioactive isotopes, shall handle relevant procedures in accordance with the provisions of the State Council on radiation protection of radioactive isotopes and radiation devices.

<a id="art-624"></a>第六百二十四条　生产、销售、使用放射性同位素和射线装置的单位和其他生产经营者，应当在申请许可证前依法取得生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表批准文件或者已经办理生态环境影响登记表备案手续。  
国家建立健全放射性同位素备案制度，具体办法由国务院制定。

**Article 624** Units and other producers and business operators that produce, sell, or use radioactive isotopes and radiation devices shall, before applying for a license, obtain approval documents for the ecological environment impact report or the ecological environment impact form, or have completed the filing procedures for the ecological environment impact registration form in accordance with law.  
The State shall establish and improve a filing system for radioactive isotopes, and the specific measures shall be formulated by the State Council.

<a id="art-625"></a>第六百二十五条　放射性同位素应当单独存放，不得与易燃、易爆、腐蚀性物品等一起存放，其贮存场所应当采取有效的防火、防盗、防射线泄漏的安全防护措施，并指定专人负责保管。贮存、领取、使用、归还放射性同位素时，应当进行登记、检查，做到账物相符。

**Article 625** Radioactive isotopes shall be stored separately and shall not be stored together with flammable, explosive, corrosive, or other articles. Effective fire-proof, theft-proof, and radiation leakage-proof safety protection measures shall be taken for their storage premises, and special personnel shall be designated to be responsible for custody. When storing, collecting, using, or returning radioactive isotopes, registration and inspection shall be carried out to ensure that the accounts and physical objects are consistent.

<a id="art-626"></a>第六百二十六条　生产、使用放射性同位素和射线装置的单位和其他生产经营者，应当按照国务院生态环境主管部门的规定对其产生的放射性废物进行收集、包装、贮存。  
生产放射源的单位和其他生产经营者，应当按照国务院生态环境主管部门的规定回收和利用废旧放射源；使用放射源的单位和其他生产经营者，应当按照国务院生态环境主管部门的规定将废旧放射源交回生产放射源的单位和其他生产经营者或者送交专门从事放射性废物贮存、处理、处置的单位。

**Article 626** Units and other producers and business operators that produce or use radioactive isotopes and radiation devices shall collect, package, and store the radioactive waste generated by them in accordance with the provisions of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council.  
Units and other producers and business operators that produce radioactive sources shall recover and utilize spent radioactive sources in accordance with the provisions of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council; units and other producers and business operators that use radioactive sources shall return spent radioactive sources to the units and other producers and business operators that produce radioactive sources, or deliver them to units specialized in the storage, treatment, or disposal of radioactive waste, in accordance with the provisions of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-627"></a>第六百二十七条　使用对人体健康、生态环境潜在危害程度较高的放射源的场所和生产放射性同位素的场所，以及终结运行后产生放射性污染的射线装置，应当依法实施退役。具体办法由国务院制定。

**Article 627** Sites using radioactive sources with a higher degree of potential harm to human health and the ecological environment, sites producing radioactive isotopes, and radiation devices that generate radioactive pollution after termination of operation shall be decommissioned in accordance with law. The specific measures shall be formulated by the State Council.

<a id="art-628"></a>第六百二十八条　生产、贮存、销售、使用放射源的单位和其他生产经营者，应当建立健全安全保卫制度，指定专人负责，落实安全责任制，制定必要的事故应急措施。发生放射源丢失、被盗和放射性污染事故时，有关单位和个人应当立即采取应急措施，并向公安机关、卫生健康主管部门和生态环境主管部门报告。  
公安机关、卫生健康主管部门和生态环境主管部门接到放射源丢失、被盗和放射性污染事故报告后，应当报告本级人民政府，并在各自职责范围内立即组织采取有效措施，防止放射性污染蔓延，减少事故损失。当地人民政府应当及时将有关情况告知公众，并做好事故的调查、处理工作。

**Article 628** Units and other producers and business operators that produce, store, sell, or use radioactive sources shall establish and improve a security system, designate special personnel to be responsible, implement a safety responsibility system, and formulate necessary accident emergency measures. When a radioactive source is lost or stolen or a radioactive pollution accident occurs, the relevant unit or individual shall immediately take emergency measures and report to the public security organ, the competent department of health, and the competent department of ecological environment.  
After receiving a report on the loss or theft of a radioactive source or a radioactive pollution accident, the public security organ, the competent department of health, and the competent department of ecological environment shall report to the people's government at the same level, and immediately organize the adoption of effective measures within their respective functions and duties to prevent the spread of radioactive pollution and reduce accident losses. The local people's government shall promptly inform the public of the relevant situation and handle the investigation and treatment of the accident properly.

### 第三十二章　铀（钍）矿和伴生放射性矿开发利用放射性污染防治

<a id="art-629"></a>第六百二十九条　开发利用或者关闭铀（钍）矿的单位，应当在开工建设或者办理退役审批手续前编制生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表，报国务院生态环境主管部门审查批准。  
开发利用伴生放射性矿的单位，应当在开工建设或者退役前编制生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表，报省级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门审查批准。  
本法所称伴生放射性矿，是指列入伴生放射性矿开发利用辐射环境管理名录，含有较高水平天然放射性核素浓度的稀土矿、磷酸盐矿等非铀矿。

**Article 629** Units that exploit, develop, or close uranium (thorium) mines shall, before commencing construction or handling decommissioning approval procedures, prepare an ecological environment impact report or an ecological environment impact form, and submit it to the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council for review and approval.  
Units that exploit and develop associated radioactive mines shall, before commencing construction or decommissioning, prepare an ecological environment impact report or an ecological environment impact form, and submit it to the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government at or above the provincial level for review and approval.  
For the purposes of this Law, “associated radioactive mines” means non-uranium mines such as rare earth mines and phosphate mines that contain a relatively high concentration of natural radionuclides and are included in the list for radiation environment management of the exploitation and development of associated radioactive mines.

<a id="art-630"></a>第六百三十条　国务院生态环境主管部门根据矿产资源开发利用中放射性核素对人体健康、生态环境的潜在危害程度，制定伴生放射性矿开发利用辐射环境管理名录，统一规定纳入伴生放射性矿开发利用辐射环境管理的判定方法及标准、矿产类别和工业活动。伴生放射性矿开发利用辐射环境管理名录应当动态调整。

**Article 630** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, based on the degree of potential harm of radionuclides in the exploitation and utilization of mineral resources to human health and the ecological environment, formulate a list for radiation environment management of the exploitation and development of associated radioactive mines, which shall uniformly stipulate the judgment methods and standards, mineral categories, and industrial activities to be included in the radiation environment management of the exploitation and development of associated radioactive mines. The list for radiation environment management of the exploitation and development of associated radioactive mines shall be subject to dynamic adjustment.

<a id="art-631"></a>第六百三十一条　实行排污许可管理的伴生放射性矿开发利用单位的排污许可证中应当记载排放废液中放射性核素的种类、浓度、排放量，伴生放射性固体废物的类别、物理性状、产生环节、去向，伴生放射性固体废物自行贮存、利用、处置及相关设施等信息。

**Article 631** For units exploiting and developing associated radioactive mines that are subject to pollutant discharge permit management, the pollutant discharge permit shall record information such as the types, concentrations, and discharge amounts of radionuclides in discharged waste liquids, the categories, physical properties, generation links, and destinations of associated radioactive solid waste, and the self-storage, utilization, and disposal of associated radioactive solid waste and related facilities.

<a id="art-632"></a>第六百三十二条　铀（钍）矿开发利用单位应当对铀（钍）矿的流出物和周边环境实施监测，并定期向国务院生态环境主管部门和所在地省级人民政府生态环境主管部门报告监测结果。

**Article 632** Units that exploit and develop uranium (thorium) mines shall monitor the effluents from the uranium (thorium) mines and the surrounding environment, and regularly report the monitoring results to the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council and the competent department of ecological environment of the provincial people's government where the mine is located.

<a id="art-633"></a>第六百三十三条　对铀（钍）矿和伴生放射性矿开发利用过程中产生的尾矿等放射性固体废物，应当建造尾矿库等设施进行贮存、处置；建造的尾矿库等设施应当符合放射性污染防治的要求。

**Article 333** Radioactive solid waste such as tailings generated in the process of exploitation and development of uranium (thorium) mines and associated radioactive mines shall be stored and disposed of by constructing tailings ponds and other facilities; the constructed tailings ponds and other facilities shall meet the requirements for the prevention and control of radioactive pollution.

<a id="art-634"></a>第六百三十四条　铀（钍）矿开发利用单位应当制定铀（钍）矿退役计划。铀矿退役和监护费用由国家财政预算安排。

**Article 634** Units that exploit and develop uranium (thorium) mines shall formulate a decommissioning plan for the uranium (thorium) mine. The costs of decommissioning and monitoring of uranium mines shall be arranged from the state financial budget.

### 第三十三章　放射性废物污染防治

<a id="art-635"></a>第六百三十五条　核设施营运单位、核技术利用单位和其他生产经营者、铀（钍）矿和伴生放射性矿开发利用单位，应当合理选择和利用原材料，采用先进的生产工艺和设备，尽量减少放射性废物的产生量。  
放射性废物是含有放射性核素或者被放射性核素污染，其浓度或者比活度大于国家确定的清洁解控水平，预期不再使用的废弃物。清洁解控的具体办法由国务院生态环境主管部门制定。

**Article 635** Operators of nuclear facilities, units using nuclear technology, and other producers and business operators, and units exploiting and developing uranium (thorium) mines and associated radioactive mines shall rationally select and utilize raw materials, adopt advanced production processes and equipment, and minimize the generation of radioactive waste.  
Radioactive waste means waste that contains radionuclides or is contaminated by radionuclides and whose concentration or specific activity is greater than the clearance level determined by the State and that is no longer expected to be used. The specific measures for clearance shall be formulated by the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-636"></a>第六百三十六条　排放放射性废气、废液，应当符合国家放射性污染防治的规定和标准。

**Article 636** The discharge of radioactive waste gas and waste liquid shall comply with the provisions and standards of the State on the prevention and control of radioactive pollution.

<a id="art-637"></a>第六百三十七条　产生放射性废气、废液的单位排放符合国家放射性污染防治的规定和标准的放射性废气、废液，应当向审批生态环境影响报告书、生态环境影响报告表的生态环境主管部门申请放射性核素排放量，并定期报告排放计量结果。

**Article 637** Units that generate radioactive waste gas and waste liquid and discharge radioactive waste gas and waste liquid that comply with the provisions and standards of the State on the prevention and control of radioactive pollution shall apply to the competent department of ecological environment that approved the ecological environment impact report or ecological environment impact form for the discharge amount of radionuclides, and regularly report the discharge measurement results.

<a id="art-638"></a>第六百三十八条　产生放射性废液的单位，应当按照国家放射性污染防治的规定和标准的要求，对不得排放的放射性废液进行处理或者贮存。不能经净化或者贮存衰变达到国家放射性污染防治的规定和标准要求的放射性废液，应当使其转变为符合处置要求的稳定的、标准化的放射性固体废物。  
产生放射性废液的单位，排放符合国家放射性污染防治的规定和标准的放射性废液，应当采用符合国务院生态环境主管部门规定的排放方式。  
禁止通过渗井、渗坑、天然裂隙、溶洞或者国家禁止的其他方式排放放射性废液。

**Article 638** Units that generate radioactive waste liquid shall, in accordance with the requirements of the provisions and standards of the State on the prevention and control of radioactive pollution, treat or store radioactive waste liquid that may not be discharged. Radioactive waste liquid that cannot meet the provisions and standards of the State on the prevention and control of radioactive pollution after purification or storage decay shall be converted into stable, standardized radioactive solid waste that meets the disposal requirements.  
Units that generate radioactive waste liquid and discharge radioactive waste liquid that complies with the provisions and standards of the State on the prevention and control of radioactive pollution shall adopt a discharge method that conforms to the provisions of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council.  
It is prohibited to discharge radioactive waste liquid through seepage wells, seepage pits, natural fissures, karst caves, or other means prohibited by the State.

<a id="art-639"></a>第六百三十九条　低、中水平放射性固体废物应当按照国家规定实行近地表处置或者中等深度处置。  
高水平放射性固体废物实行集中的深地质处置。  
禁止在内河水域和海上处置放射性固体废物。

**Article 639** Low- and intermediate-level radioactive solid waste shall undergo near-surface disposal or intermediate-depth disposal in accordance with State regulations.  
High-level radioactive solid waste shall undergo centralized deep geological disposal.  
It is prohibited to dispose of radioactive solid waste in inland waters and at sea.

<a id="art-640"></a>第六百四十条　国务院核工业主管部门会同国务院生态环境主管部门根据地质条件和放射性固体废物处置的需要，在生态环境影响评价的基础上，依法组织编制放射性固体废物处置场所选址规划，报国务院批准后实施。  
有关地方人民政府应当根据放射性固体废物处置场所选址规划，提供放射性固体废物处置场所的建设用地，并采取有效措施支持放射性固体废物的处置。

**Article 640** The competent department of nuclear industry of the State Council shall, jointly with the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council, based on geological conditions and the needs for the disposal of radioactive solid waste, and on the basis of ecological environment impact assessment, organize the formulation of a siting plan for radioactive solid waste disposal sites in accordance with law, and submit it to the State Council for approval before implementation.  
The relevant local people's government shall, in accordance with the siting plan for radioactive solid waste disposal sites, provide construction land for radioactive solid waste disposal sites and take effective measures to support the disposal of radioactive solid waste.

<a id="art-641"></a>第六百四十一条　产生放射性固体废物的单位，应当按照国务院生态环境主管部门的规定，对其产生的放射性固体废物进行处理后，送交放射性废物处置单位处置，并承担处置费用。  
放射性固体废物处置费用收取和使用管理办法，由国务院财政部门、价格主管部门会同国务院生态环境主管部门制定。

**Article 641** A unit that generates radioactive solid waste shall, in accordance with the provisions of the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council, treat the radioactive solid waste generated by it and then deliver it to a radioactive waste disposal unit for disposal, and shall bear the disposal costs.  
The measures for the collection and use of radioactive solid waste disposal fees shall be formulated by the finance department and the price department of the State Council jointly with the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council.

<a id="art-642"></a>第六百四十二条　设立专门从事放射性废物贮存、处理、处置的单位，应当经国务院生态环境主管部门审查批准，取得许可证；仅贮存、处理本单位产生的放射性废物的，无需取得许可证。具体办法由国务院制定。  
禁止无许可证或者不按照许可证的要求专门从事贮存、处理、处置放射性废物的活动。  
禁止将放射性废物提供或者委托给无许可证的单位和个人贮存、处理、处置。

**Article 642** An entity engaged exclusively in the storage, treatment, or disposal of radioactive waste shall be subject to the review and approval of the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council and shall obtain a license; an entity that only stores or treats radioactive waste generated by itself need not obtain a license. The specific measures shall be formulated by the State Council.  
It is prohibited to engage exclusively in the activities of storage, treatment, or disposal of radioactive waste without a license or not in accordance with the requirements of the license.  
It is prohibited to provide or entrust radioactive waste to any unit or individual without a license for storage, treatment, or disposal.

<a id="art-643"></a>第六百四十三条　禁止违反法律规定将放射性废物和被放射性污染的物品输入中华人民共和国境内或者经中华人民共和国境内转移。  
第九分编　化学物质污染风险管控、电磁辐射和光污染防治

**Article 643** It is prohibited to import radioactive waste or articles contaminated by radioactivity into the territory of the People's Republic of China, or to transfer them through the territory of the People's Republic of China, in violation of the provisions of laws.  
Division 9 Risk Control of Chemical Substance Pollution, Prevention and Control of Electromagnetic Radiation and Light Pollution

### 第三十四章　化学物质污染风险管控

<a id="art-644"></a>第六百四十四条　国家加强化学物质污染风险管控。生产、进口、储存、运输、销售、使用、处置化学物质，应当遵守国家规定，防止污染环境，保障公众健康。具体办法由国务院制定。

**Article 644** The State shall strengthen risk control of chemical substance pollution. The production, import, storage, transportation, sale, use, and disposal of chemical substances shall comply with the provisions of the State to prevent environmental pollution and protect public health. The specific measures shall be formulated by the State Council.

<a id="art-645"></a>第六百四十五条　国家建立持久性有机污染物等新污染物协同治理和环境风险管控体系。国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门，分析研判新污染物环境风险管控形势，制定完善相关标准，组织开展调查监测，有效降低新污染物环境风险。

**Article 645** The State shall establish a coordinated governance and environmental risk control system for new pollutants such as persistent organic pollutants. The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall, jointly with relevant departments, analyze and assess the situation of environmental risk control of new pollutants, formulate and improve relevant standards, organize and conduct investigation and monitoring, and effectively reduce the environmental risks of new pollutants.

<a id="art-646"></a>第六百四十六条　国务院生态环境主管部门对全国化学物质污染风险管控工作实施统一监督管理。国务院发展改革、工业和信息化、农业农村、商务、卫生健康、海关、市场监督管理、疾病预防控制、药品监督管理等有关部门在各自职责范围内对化学物质污染风险管控工作实施监督管理。  
县级以上地方人民政府及其有关部门应当加强化学物质污染风险管控。

**Article 646** The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall exercise unified supervision and administration over the work of risk control of chemical substance pollution nationwide. The relevant departments of the State Council, including those for development and reform, industry and information technology, agriculture and rural affairs, commerce, health, customs, market regulation, disease prevention and control, and drug administration, shall exercise supervision and administration over the work of risk control of chemical substance pollution within their respective functions and duties.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level and their relevant departments shall strengthen risk control of chemical substance pollution.

<a id="art-647"></a>第六百四十七条　国务院生态环境主管部门根据化学物质的危害特性等定期组织开展化学物质污染信息调查，生产、进口、销售化学物质和使用化学物质生产产品的企业事业单位应当按照规定提供调查所需的化学物质的物理化学性质、数量、用途、危害特性、排放等信息。

**Article 647** The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall, on the basis of the hazardous characteristics of chemical substances, organize periodic investigations into chemical substance pollution information. Enterprises and institutions that produce, import, sell chemical substances or use chemical substances to produce products shall, in accordance with the provisions, provide information such as the physical and chemical properties, quantity, use, hazardous characteristics, and emissions of the chemical substances required for the investigation.

<a id="art-648"></a>第六百四十八条　国务院有关部门应当在各自职责范围内按照规定采取有效措施，推动有毒有害化学物质替代。

**Article 648** The relevant departments of the State Council shall, within their respective functions and duties and in accordance with the provisions, take effective measures to promote the substitution of toxic and hazardous chemical substances.

<a id="art-649"></a>第六百四十九条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门，组织开展化学物质污染风险评估，制定公布重点管控新污染物清单并适时调整，明确禁止、限制等环境风险管控措施。生产、进口重点管控新污染物清单中化学物质和使用重点管控新污染物清单中化学物质生产产品的，应当遵守上述环境风险管控措施。

**Article 649** The competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council shall, jointly with relevant departments, organize and conduct risk assessment of chemical substance pollution, formulate and publish a list of new pollutants subject to priority control and adjust it in a timely manner, and specify environmental risk control measures such as prohibition and restriction. Any entity that produces or imports a chemical substance on the list of new pollutants subject to priority control, or uses such a chemical substance to produce a product, shall comply with the aforementioned environmental risk control measures.

<a id="art-650"></a>第六百五十条　国家实行新化学物质环境管理登记制度。  
生产、进口新化学物质的企业事业单位，应当在生产、进口前向国务院生态环境主管部门申请新化学物质环境管理登记。

**Article 650** The State shall implement a registration system for environmental management of new chemical substances.  
An enterprise or institution that produces or imports a new chemical substance shall, before production or import, apply to the competent department of ecology and environment of the State Council for the registration of environmental management of the new chemical substance.

<a id="art-651"></a>第六百五十一条　禁止无新化学物质环境管理登记证或者不按照新化学物质环境管理登记证的要求生产、进口新化学物质。禁止使用无新化学物质环境管理登记证的企业事业单位生产、进口的新化学物质生产产品。

**Article 651** It is prohibited to produce or import a new chemical substance without a registration certificate for environmental management of new chemical substances, or not in accordance with the requirements of the registration certificate. It is prohibited to use a new chemical substance produced or imported by an enterprise or institution without a registration certificate for environmental management of new chemical substances to produce products.

<a id="art-652"></a>第六百五十二条　化学物质污染防治，本章未作规定的，适用本编其他有关规定。

**Article 652** Where this Chapter does not provide for the prevention and control of chemical substance pollution, the relevant provisions of this Division shall apply.

### 第三十五章　电磁辐射污染防治

<a id="art-653"></a>第六百五十三条　本法所称电磁辐射污染，是指人类活动产生的超过国家电磁辐射排放标准的非电离辐射。

**Article 653** For the purposes of this Law, electromagnetic radiation pollution means non-ionizing radiation generated by human activities that exceeds the national standards for electromagnetic radiation emission.

<a id="art-654"></a>第六百五十四条　国务院生态环境主管部门对全国电磁辐射污染防治工作实施统一监督管理。地方人民政府生态环境主管部门对本行政区域电磁辐射污染防治工作实施统一监督管理。
