**Article 767** The State shall implement scientific and effective policies for the protection of black soil, ensure financial investment in black soil protection, and comprehensively adopt engineering, agronomic, agricultural machinery, and biological measures to protect the excellent production capacity of black soil, ensuring that the total amount of black soil does not decrease, its functions do not degrade, its quality is improved, and its production capacity is sustainable.  
The relevant people's governments at or above the county level shall, in accordance with the provisions of the Law of the People's Republic of China on Black Soil Protection, strengthen the ecological protection of black soil and the protection and restoration of forest land, grasslands, and wetlands surrounding black soil, promote the management of barren hills and slopes, enhance the ecological functions of the ecosystem such as water conservation, soil and water conservation, windbreak and sand fixation, and maintenance of biodiversity, and maintain the ecological environment conducive to black soil protection.  
No entity or individual may damage black soil resources or the ecological environment.

<a id="art-768"></a>第七百六十八条　国家实行建设用地总量控制，严格限制农用地转为建设用地。确需占用的，应当依照《中华人民共和国土地管理法》、《中华人民共和国森林法》、《中华人民共和国草原法》，以及有关耕地保护和质量提升的法律等的规定办理相关手续。  
建设项目使用土地的，应当节约集约利用土地，严格遵守生态环境保护要求，防止污染环境、破坏生态。

**Article 768** The State shall implement total quantity control of construction land, strictly limiting the conversion of agricultural land into construction land. Where occupation is genuinely necessary, the relevant procedures shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of the Land Administration Law of the People's Republic of China, the Forest Law of the People's Republic of China, the Grassland Law of the People's Republic of China, and laws concerning arable land protection and quality improvement.  
Where construction projects use land, they shall use land in a resource-conserving and intensive manner, strictly comply with ecological environmental protection requirements, and prevent environmental pollution and ecological damage.

<a id="art-769"></a>第七百六十九条　依照法律、行政法规的规定申请临时用地的，应当尽量不占或者少占耕地、避让永久基本农田，不得修建永久性建筑物。  
临时用地确需占用耕地的，应当按照规定恢复种植条件。

**Article 769** Where an application for temporary land use is made in accordance with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations, the applicant shall, to the extent possible, avoid occupying arable land or occupy as little as possible, steer clear of permanent basic farmland, and shall not construct permanent buildings.  
Where temporary land use genuinely requires occupation of arable land, the planting conditions shall be restored in accordance with the provisions.

<a id="art-770"></a>第七百七十条　具有重要生态功能的未利用地应当依法划入生态保护红线，实行严格保护。开垦未利用的土地，应当经过科学论证和评估，经依法批准后进行。

**Article 770** Unused land with important ecological functions shall be delimited into the ecological protection red line according to law and subject to strict protection. The reclamation of unused land shall be carried out only after scientific demonstration and assessment, and after approval according to law.

#### 第二节　矿产资源

<a id="art-771"></a>第七百七十一条　矿产资源的勘查、开采以及矿区生态修复，应当坚持开发利用与保护并重，实现绿色发展。  
国家鼓励、支持矿业绿色低碳转型发展，加强绿色矿山建设。

**Article 771** The exploration and mining of mineral resources and ecological restoration of mining areas shall adhere to the principle of equal emphasis on development, utilization, and protection, to achieve green development.  
The State shall encourage and support the green and low-carbon transformation and development of the mining industry, and strengthen the construction of green mines.

<a id="art-772"></a>第七百七十二条　国家对矿产资源的勘查、开采实行统一规划、科学布局、综合勘查、合理开采和综合利用的方针。  
各级人民政府应当依照《中华人民共和国矿产资源法》的规定，加强矿产资源保护工作。禁止以任何手段侵占或者破坏矿产资源。  
对国务院确定的特定战略性矿产资源，按照国家规定实行保护性开采。

**Article 772** The State shall implement the policy of unified planning, scientific layout, comprehensive exploration, rational mining, and comprehensive utilization for the exploration and mining of mineral resources.  
People's governments at all levels shall, in accordance with the provisions of the Mineral Resources Law of the People's Republic of China, strengthen the protection of mineral resources. It shall be prohibited to encroach upon or damage mineral resources by any means.  
For specific strategic mineral resources designated by the State Council, protective mining shall be implemented in accordance with the provisions of the State.

<a id="art-773"></a>第七百七十三条　矿业权出让合同应当明确矿区生态修复等有关要求。  
勘查、开采矿产资源，应当遵守本法和有关生态环境法律法规的规定，防止污染环境、破坏生态；应当采用先进适用、符合生态环境保护要求的技术、工艺、设备，不得使用国家明令淘汰的技术、工艺、设备。

**Article 773** The mining rights transfer contract shall specify relevant requirements such as ecological restoration of the mining area.  
The exploration and mining of mineral resources shall comply with the provisions of this Law and relevant laws and regulations on ecological environment, and prevent environmental pollution and ecological damage; advanced and applicable technologies, processes, and equipment that meet ecological environmental protection requirements shall be adopted, and technologies, processes, and equipment that are expressly eliminated by the State shall not be used.

<a id="art-774"></a>第七百七十四条　勘查活动结束后，探矿权人应当及时对勘查区域进行清理；破坏地表植被的，应当及时恢复。

**Article 774** After the completion of exploration activities, the exploration right holder shall promptly clean up the exploration area; where surface vegetation has been damaged, it shall be promptly restored.

<a id="art-775"></a>第七百七十五条　开采矿产资源，应当采取合理的开采顺序、开采方法；采取有效措施保护地下水资源，并优先使用矿井水；避免、减少对矿区森林、草原、耕地、湿地、江河湖泊、海洋等生态系统的破坏；加强对尾矿库建设、运行、闭库等活动的管理，防范生态环境风险。

**Article 775** When mining mineral resources, reasonable mining sequences and methods shall be adopted; effective measures shall be taken to protect groundwater resources, and mine water shall be given priority for use; damage to ecosystems such as forests, grasslands, arable land, wetlands, rivers, lakes, and oceans in the mining area shall be avoided or reduced; management of activities such as the construction, operation, and closure of tailings ponds shall be strengthened to prevent ecological and environmental risks.

<a id="art-776"></a>第七百七十六条　禁止在严格保护岸线范围内开采海砂。依法在其他区域开发利用海砂资源，应当采取严格措施，保护海洋生态。载运海砂资源应当持有合法来源证明；海砂开采者应当为载运海砂的船舶提供合法来源证明。

**Article 776** It shall be prohibited to mine sea sand within the scope of strictly protected shorelines. Where sea sand resources are developed and utilized in other areas according to law, strict measures shall be taken to protect the marine ecology. The transport of sea sand resources shall be accompanied by a certificate of legal origin; the sea sand miner shall provide a certificate of legal origin for the ship transporting the sea sand.

<a id="art-777"></a>第七百七十七条　国家依法保护煤炭资源，禁止任何乱采、滥挖等破坏煤炭资源的行为。  
国家统筹推进采煤沉陷区综合治理，促进采煤沉陷区转型发展。  
煤矿企业应当依法开展矿区生态环境综合治理，履行环境污染治理和生态修复义务。

**Article 777** The State shall protect coal resources according to law, and prohibit any acts such as disorderly mining and indiscriminate excavation that damage coal resources.  
The State shall coordinate and promote the comprehensive management of coal mining subsidence areas, and promote the transformation and development of coal mining subsidence areas.  
Coal mining enterprises shall, according to law, carry out comprehensive management of the ecological environment of the mining area, and fulfill the obligations of environmental pollution control and ecological restoration.

#### 第三节　水资源

<a id="art-778"></a>第七百七十八条　保护、开发、利用、节约水资源和防治水害，应当全面规划、统筹兼顾、标本兼治、综合利用、讲求效益，实现水资源的可持续利用。

**Article 778** In protecting, developing, utilizing, and conserving water resources and preventing and controlling water disasters, comprehensive planning, overall consideration, addressing both the symptoms and root causes, comprehensive utilization, and seeking benefits shall be pursued to achieve sustainable utilization of water resources.

<a id="art-779"></a>第七百七十九条　国家厉行节水，坚持和落实节水优先方针，全面建设节水型社会。  
任何单位和个人都有节约用水的义务。

**Article 779** The State shall strictly practice water conservation, adhere to and implement the policy of giving priority to water conservation, and comprehensively build a water-saving society.  
All entities and individuals shall have the obligation to conserve water.

<a id="art-780"></a>第七百八十条　国家制定水资源综合规划，对水资源合理配置、有效保护和节约集约利用等作出总体安排。

**Article 780** The State shall formulate a comprehensive plan for water resources, making overall arrangements for the rational allocation, effective protection, and conservation and intensive utilization of water resources.

<a id="art-781"></a>第七百八十一条　国家对水资源实行流域管理与行政区域管理相结合的管理体制。  
国务院水行政主管部门负责全国水资源的统一管理和监督工作；国务院其他有关部门在各自职责范围内负责水资源保护、开发、利用、节约的有关工作。  
国务院水行政主管部门在国家确定的重要江河湖泊设立的流域管理机构及其所属管理机构，在所管辖的范围内行使法律、行政法规规定的和国务院水行政主管部门授予的水资源管理和监督职责。  
县级以上地方人民政府水行政主管部门按照规定的权限，负责本行政区域水资源的统一管理和监督工作。

**Article 781** The State shall adopt a management system for water resources that combines river basin management with administrative regional management.  
The water administrative department of the State Council shall be responsible for the unified management and supervision of water resources nationwide; other relevant departments of the State Council shall be responsible for the protection, development, utilization, and conservation of water resources within their respective functions and duties.  
The river basin management bodies established by the water administrative department of the State Council for important rivers and lakes designated by the State, and the management bodies subordinate thereto, shall exercise the functions and duties of water resources management and supervision as provided by laws, administrative regulations, and as delegated by the water administrative department of the State Council within their respective jurisdictions.  
The water administrative departments of the local people's governments at or above the county level shall be responsible for the unified management and supervision of water resources within their respective administrative regions in accordance with the prescribed authorities.

<a id="art-782"></a>第七百八十二条　国家加强水文、水资源信息系统建设。县级以上人民政府水行政主管部门和流域管理机构应当加强对水资源的动态监测，提升水文监测预报预警能力。

**Article 782** The State shall strengthen the construction of hydrological and water resources information systems. The water administrative departments of the local people's governments at or above the county level and the river basin management bodies shall strengthen the dynamic monitoring of water resources and enhance the capacity for hydrological monitoring, forecasting, and early warning.

<a id="art-783"></a>第七百八十三条　国家建立健全水功能区划制度。  
设区的市级以上地方人民政府生态环境主管部门和流域生态环境监督管理机构应当对水功能区的水生态环境质量状况进行监测，发现水生态环境质量未达到要求的，应当及时报告有关人民政府采取治理措施。

**Article 783** The State shall establish and improve the system of water function zoning.  
The competent departments of ecological environment of the local people's governments at or above the level of a city divided into districts and the ecological environment supervision and management bodies of river basins shall monitor the water ecological environment quality status of water function zones. Where it is found that the water ecological environment quality fails to meet the requirements, they shall promptly report to the relevant people's government to take remedial measures.

<a id="art-784"></a>第七百八十四条　制定水资源开发、利用规划和调度水资源时，应当保障维持江河的合理流量和湖泊、水库以及地下水的合理水位，维护水体的自然净化能力。在干旱和半干旱地区开发、利用水资源，应当充分考虑生态用水需要。

**Article 784** When formulating plans for the development and utilization of water resources and when dispatching water resources, the maintenance of reasonable flow in rivers and reasonable water levels in lakes, reservoirs, and groundwater shall be ensured, and the natural purification capacity of water bodies shall be maintained. In the development and utilization of water resources in arid and semi-arid areas, the needs for ecological water use shall be fully considered.

<a id="art-785"></a>第七百八十五条　国家加强饮用水水源地保护，实行饮用水水源地名录管理，将饮用水水源地水量保障作为流域区域水量调度方案和调度计划的优先目标，严格取水管理，防止水源枯竭和水体污染，保证城乡居民饮用水安全。

**Article 785** The State shall strengthen the protection of drinking water source areas, implement a catalog management system for drinking water source areas, prioritize the guarantee of water quantity in drinking water source areas as the primary objective of river basin and regional water quantity dispatching plans and schedules, strictly manage water abstraction, prevent the depletion of water sources and water body pollution, and ensure the safety of drinking water for urban and rural residents.

<a id="art-786"></a>第七百八十六条　国家加强地下水管理和保护，实行地下水取水总量控制和水位控制制度，划定地下水超采区，实施地下水超采综合治理，保障地下水可持续利用。  
省级人民政府水行政主管部门应当会同自然资源等有关部门，统筹考虑地下水超采区划定、地下水利用情况以及地质环境条件等因素，依法组织划定本行政区域地下水禁止开采区、限制开采区。  
国家建立地下水储备制度。除特殊干旱年份以及发生重大突发事件外，不得动用地下水储备。

**Article 786** The State shall strengthen the management and protection of groundwater, implement a system of total groundwater abstraction control and groundwater level control, delimit areas of groundwater overdraft, carry out comprehensive management of groundwater overdraft, and ensure the sustainable utilization of groundwater.  
The water administrative department of the provincial people's government shall, in conjunction with the competent departments of natural resources and other relevant departments, and taking into comprehensive consideration factors such as the delimitation of groundwater overdraft areas, the status of groundwater utilization, and geological environmental conditions, organize the delimitation of areas where groundwater extraction is prohibited and areas where groundwater extraction is restricted within its administrative region according to law.  
The State shall establish a groundwater reserve system. Groundwater reserves shall not be used except in special drought years and in the event of major emergencies.

<a id="art-787"></a>第七百八十七条　开发、利用水资源，应当坚持节约保护优先、兴利与除害相结合，兼顾上下游、左右岸、干支流和有关地区之间的利益，充分发挥水资源的综合效益，并服从防洪的总体安排。  
国家根据水资源承载能力对水资源实行差别化管控，强化水资源节约保护，合理控制水资源开发利用规模。地方人民政府应当结合本地区水资源的实际情况，按照地表水与地下水统一调度开发、开源与节流相结合、节流优先和污水处理再利用的原则，合理组织开发、综合利用水资源。

**Article 787** The development and utilization of water resources shall adhere to the principles of giving priority to conservation and protection, combining the promotion of benefits with the elimination of hazards, taking into account the interests of upstream and downstream, left and right banks, main streams and tributaries, and relevant regions, giving full play to the comprehensive benefits of water resources, and subjecting to the overall arrangements for flood control.  
The State shall implement differentiated management and control of water resources based on the carrying capacity of water resources, strengthen water resource conservation and protection, and reasonably control the scale of water resource development and utilization. Local people's governments shall, in light of the actual situation of water resources in their respective regions, and in accordance with the principles of unified dispatch and development of surface water and groundwater, combining the development of new sources with conservation, giving priority to conservation, and reuse of treated sewage, reasonably organize the development and comprehensive utilization of water resources.

<a id="art-788"></a>第七百八十八条　国民经济和社会发展规划、国土空间总体规划的编制以及重大建设项目的布局，应当与当地水资源条件和防洪要求相适应，并进行科学论证。  
国家建立健全涉及水资源开发利用的建设项目和有关规划水资源论证制度。

**Article 788** The formulation of national economic and social development plans, overall territorial space plans, and the layout of major construction projects shall be compatible with local water resource conditions and flood control requirements, and shall be subject to scientific demonstration.  
The State shall establish and improve a system for water resource demonstration for construction projects and relevant plans involving the development and utilization of water resources.

<a id="art-789"></a>第七百八十九条　国家鼓励、支持污水资源化利用，县级以上地方人民政府应当统筹规划、建设污水资源化利用基础设施。  
县级以上地方人民政府应当根据当地水资源状况，加强污水处理回用，将再生水、集蓄雨水、海水及海水淡化水、矿井（坑）水、微咸水等非常规水纳入水资源统一配置，统筹规划、建设非常规水开发利用设施。

**Article 789** The State shall encourage and support the resource utilization of sewage. Local people's governments at or above the county level shall make overall plans for and construct infrastructure for the resource utilization of sewage.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall, based on local water resource conditions, strengthen the treatment and reuse of sewage, incorporate unconventional water sources such as reclaimed water, harvested rainwater, seawater and desalinated seawater, mine (pit) water, and brackish water into the unified allocation of water resources, and make overall plans for and construct facilities for the development and utilization of unconventional water sources.

<a id="art-790"></a>第七百九十条　县级以上人民政府组织实施调水工程，应当进行全面规划和科学论证，统筹兼顾调出区和调入区的用水安全和生态安全，加强对调入区的节水管理，优化水资源配置，提高水资源承载能力，防止对生态环境造成破坏。

**Article 790** Where a people's government at or above the county level organizes the implementation of a water diversion project, it shall conduct comprehensive planning and scientific demonstration, take into full consideration the water use safety and ecological safety of both the water-exporting area and the water-receiving area, strengthen water conservation management in the water-receiving area, optimize the allocation of water resources, enhance the carrying capacity of water resources, and prevent damage to the ecological environment.

<a id="art-791"></a>第七百九十一条　开发、利用水资源，应当维护水体的生态功能，防止对水生态造成破坏。  
开发、利用水资源，应当优先满足城乡居民生活用水，保障基本生态用水、粮食安全用水，并统筹农业、工业、生态修复用水以及航运等需要。  
水工程应当加强水生生物保护，合理制定调度规程，在确保防洪、供水安全的前提下，开展适宜水生生物生长繁育需求的生态调度。

**Article 791** The development and utilization of water resources shall maintain the ecological functions of water bodies and prevent damage to the aquatic ecology.  
The development and utilization of water resources shall give priority to meeting the domestic water use of urban and rural residents, ensure basic ecological water use and water use for food security, and coordinate the needs for water use in agriculture, industry, ecological restoration, and navigation.  
Water projects shall strengthen the protection of aquatic organisms, reasonably formulate dispatching procedures, and carry out ecological dispatching that meets the needs for the growth and reproduction of aquatic organisms, provided that flood control safety and water supply safety are ensured.

<a id="art-792"></a>第七百九十二条　国家对用水实行总量控制和定额管理相结合的制度，依照《中华人民共和国水法》的规定对水资源实行取水许可制度和有偿使用制度。

**Article 792** The State shall implement a system combining total water use control with quota management, and shall implement a water abstraction permit system and a paid use system for water resources in accordance with the provisions of the Water Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-793"></a>第七百九十三条　国家大力推行节约用水措施，推广节约用水新技术、新工艺，发展节水型工业、农业和服务业，推进生活节水，加强生活废水循环利用。  
各级人民政府应当采取措施，加强对节约用水的管理，建立节约用水技术开发推广体系，培育和发展节约用水产业。  
新建、扩建、改建建设项目应当制定节水措施方案，配套建设节水设施。节水设施应当与主体工程同时设计、同时施工、同时投产使用。  
工业和服务业企业应当采用先进适用节水技术、工艺和设备，增加循环用水次数，提高水的重复利用率。  
县级以上人民政府及其有关部门应当推行节水灌溉方式和节水技术，提高农业用水效率，加强灌区建设和改造，发展节水型农业。  
县级以上地方人民政府应当全面推进节水型城市建设。城市绿化、道路清扫、车辆冲洗、建筑施工以及生态景观等用水，应当优先使用符合标准要求的再生水。

**Article 793** The State shall vigorously promote water-saving measures, disseminate new technologies and processes for water conservation, develop water-saving industry, agriculture, and service sectors, advance domestic water conservation, and strengthen the recycling of domestic wastewater.  
People's governments at all levels shall take measures to strengthen the management of water conservation, establish a system for the development and dissemination of water-saving technologies, and cultivate and develop the water-saving industry.  
New construction, expansion, or renovation projects shall formulate water-saving measures plans and construct supporting water-saving facilities. Water-saving facilities shall be designed, constructed, and put into operation simultaneously with the main project.  
Enterprises in industry and the service sector shall adopt advanced and applicable water-saving technologies, processes, and equipment, increase the number of recycling water uses, and enhance the rate of water reuse.  
People's governments at or above the county level and their relevant departments shall promote water-saving irrigation methods and technologies, improve agricultural water use efficiency, strengthen the construction and renovation of irrigation areas, and develop water-saving agriculture.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall comprehensively advance the construction of water-saving cities. For water use in urban greening, road cleaning, vehicle washing, construction, and ecological landscapes, priority shall be given to the use of reclaimed water that meets the required standards.

#### 第四节　渔业资源

<a id="art-794"></a>第七百九十四条　渔业生产应当坚持养殖为主、适度捕捞，实现渔业资源的保护和可持续利用。  
地方人民政府及其有关部门应当因地制宜采取改良水域条件、人工投放苗种、投放鱼巢、灌江纳苗、营救幼鱼、移植驯化、消除敌害、引种栽植等措施，增殖渔业资源。

**Article 794** Fishery production shall adhere to the principles of prioritizing aquaculture with moderate fishing, so as to achieve the protection and sustainable utilization of fishery resources.  
Local people's governments and their relevant departments shall, based on local conditions, adopt measures such as improving water area conditions, artificially releasing seedlings and fingerlings, placing fish nests, diverting river water to recruit fry, rescuing juvenile fish, transplanting and domesticating species, eliminating predators, and introducing and planting species to enhance fishery resources.

<a id="art-795"></a>第七百九十五条　国务院渔业渔政主管部门主管全国的渔业工作。县级以上地方人民政府渔业渔政主管部门主管本行政区域渔业工作。

**Article 795** The competent department of fisheries and fishery administration under the State Council shall be in charge of fishery work nationwide. The competent departments of fisheries and fishery administration of local people's governments at or above the county level shall be in charge of fishery work within their respective administrative regions.

<a id="art-796"></a>第七百九十六条　国家保护水产种质资源及其生存环境，并在具有较高经济价值和遗传育种价值的水产种质资源的主要生长繁育区域建立水产种质资源保护区。  
未经省级以上人民政府渔业渔政主管部门批准，任何单位和个人不得在水产种质资源保护区内从事捕捞活动。  
禁止非法占用或者破坏水产种质资源保护区。

**Article 796** The State shall protect aquatic germplasm resources and their living environment, and shall establish aquatic germplasm resource reserves in the main growth and breeding areas of aquatic germplasm resources with high economic value and genetic breeding value.  
Without approval from the competent department of fisheries and fishery administration of the people's government at or above the provincial level, no unit or individual may engage in fishing activities within aquatic germplasm resource reserves.  
It is prohibited to illegally occupy or damage aquatic germplasm resource reserves.

<a id="art-797"></a>第七百九十七条　国家鼓励、支持因地制宜采取增殖放流、建设海洋牧场、投放人工鱼礁、种植海藻场或者海草床等措施，养护水生生物资源，修复、改善渔业水域生态环境。  
国家对具有较高经济价值的水生动植物资源实行重点保护。国家重点保护经济水生动植物资源名录，由国务院渔业渔政主管部门组织科学论证评估后制定并公布。  
禁止向开放水域投放不符合生态要求的水生外来种、杂交种等水生生物。  
在重要渔业水域开展建闸、筑坝、航道建设、港口建设等工程建设，对渔业资源有严重影响的，建设单位应当依法建造过鱼设施或者采取其他渔业资源保护措施。

**Article 797** The State shall encourage and support the adoption of measures such as stock enhancement and release, construction of marine ranches, deployment of artificial reefs, and planting of seaweed fields or seagrass beds, based on local conditions, to conserve aquatic living resources and to restore and improve the ecological environment of fishery waters.  
The State shall provide key protection to aquatic animal and plant resources with high economic value. The catalog of nationally protected economic aquatic animal and plant resources shall be formulated and published by the competent department of fisheries and fishery administration under the State Council after organizing scientific demonstration and evaluation.  
It is prohibited to release aquatic organisms such as alien species and hybrid species that do not meet ecological requirements into open waters.  
Where construction projects such as sluice gate construction, dam construction, waterway construction, and port construction are carried out in important fishery waters and have a serious impact on fishery resources, the construction entity shall, according to law, build fish passage facilities or take other measures for the protection of fishery resources.

<a id="art-798"></a>第七百九十八条　国家对水域、滩涂利用进行统一规划，确定可以用于养殖业的水域和滩涂，建立养殖水域、滩涂保护制度；单位和个人使用国家所有的水域、滩涂从事养殖生产的，应当依照《中华人民共和国渔业法》的规定取得养殖证。  
县级以上地方人民政府应当采取有效措施，加强对水产品生产基地和重要养殖水域、滩涂的保护。  
从事养殖生产应当保护水域、滩涂生态环境，科学确定养殖密度和养殖规模，合理投饵、投饲、投药，养殖排放尾水应当符合有关污染物排放标准，不得破坏水域、滩涂的生态环境。

**Article 798** The State shall make unified plans for the utilization of waters and tidal flats, determine the waters and tidal flats that may be used for aquaculture, and establish a protection system for aquaculture waters and tidal flats; where units or individuals use State-owned waters or tidal flats for aquaculture production, they shall obtain aquaculture permits in accordance with the provisions of the Fisheries Law of the People's Republic of China.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall take effective measures to strengthen the protection of aquatic product production bases and important aquaculture waters and tidal flats.  
Those engaged in aquaculture production shall protect the ecological environment of waters and tidal flats, scientifically determine stocking density and cultivation scale, reasonably apply bait, feed, and veterinary drugs, and the tail water discharged from aquaculture shall comply with the relevant pollutant discharge standards, and shall not damage the ecological environment of waters and tidal flats.

<a id="art-799"></a>第七百九十九条　国家根据捕捞量低于渔业资源增长量的原则，确定渔业资源的总可捕捞量，实行捕捞限额制度。  
国家根据捕捞能力与渔业资源可捕捞量相适应的原则，确定渔业船网工具控制指标。  
国家对捕捞业实行捕捞许可证制度。从事捕捞作业的单位和个人，应当按照捕捞许可证规定的作业类型、场所、时限、渔具数量和捕捞限额等进行作业。  
禁止捕捞有重要经济价值的水生动物苗种或者禁捕的怀卵亲本；捕捞作业时误捕的，应当及时放生；因科学研究、养殖、捕捞过坝、增殖放流或者其他特殊需要确需捕捞的，应当经省级以上人民政府渔业渔政主管部门批准，在指定的区域和时间内，按照限额捕捞。  
捕捞活动中误捕珍贵、濒危水生野生动物的，应当及时采取救护、处置措施，并报告渔业渔政主管部门。

**Article 799** The State shall, in accordance with the principle that the catch shall be lower than the growth of fishery resources, determine the total allowable catch of fishery resources and implement a catch quota system.  
The State shall, in accordance with the principle that fishing capacity shall match the allowable catch of fishery resources, determine the control targets for fishing vessels and fishing gear.  
The State shall implement a fishing license system for the fishing industry. Units and individuals engaged in fishing operations shall conduct operations in accordance with the operation type, location, time limit, quantity of fishing gear, and catch quota specified in the fishing license.  
It is prohibited to catch juvenile aquatic animals of important economic value or prohibited broodstock carrying eggs; those accidentally caught during fishing operations shall be released promptly. Where catching is truly necessary for scientific research, aquaculture, fish passage over dams, stock enhancement, or other special needs, approval shall be obtained from the competent department of fisheries and fishery administration of the people's government at or above the provincial level, and catching shall be carried out in designated areas and within designated time periods and in accordance with quotas.  
Where rare or endangered aquatic wild animals are accidentally caught during fishing activities, rescue and disposal measures shall be taken promptly, and the matter shall be reported to the competent department of fisheries and fishery administration.

<a id="art-800"></a>第八百条　各级人民政府应当采取有效措施，保护和改善渔业水域生态环境。  
对用于渔业并兼有调蓄、灌溉等功能的水体，有关主管部门应当确定渔业资源保护和渔业生产所需的最低水位线。  
进行水下爆破、勘探、采砂、施工等作业，对渔业资源有严重影响的，作业单位应当事先同有关县级以上地方人民政府渔业渔政主管部门协商，采取措施，防止或者减少对渔业资源的损害。

**Article 800** People's governments at all levels shall take effective measures to protect and improve the ecological environment of fishery waters.  
For water bodies used for fishery purposes and also serving functions such as regulation and storage or irrigation, the relevant competent departments shall determine the minimum water level lines necessary for the protection of fishery resources and fishery production.  
Where operations such as underwater blasting, exploration, sand mining, or construction are carried out and have a serious impact on fishery resources, the operating unit shall consult in advance with the competent department of fisheries and fishery administration of the relevant local people's government at or above the county level and take measures to prevent or reduce damage to fishery resources.

<a id="art-801"></a>第八百零一条　禁止违反禁渔区、禁渔期的规定从事捕捞活动。禁止渔业船舶违反禁渔区、禁渔期的规定航行、停泊。  
对违反前款规定的渔业船舶，任何单位和个人不得为其提供供油、供水、供冰等服务，不得代冻、转载、运输、收购、加工、销售其非法捕捞的渔获物。  
禁止使用炸鱼、毒鱼、电鱼等破坏渔业资源和渔业水域生态环境的方法进行捕捞。禁止制造、销售、使用禁用的渔具。禁止使用小于最小网目尺寸的网具进行捕捞。捕捞的渔获物中幼鱼不得超过规定的比例。

**Article 801** It shall be prohibited to engage in fishing activities in violation of the provisions on closed fishing areas and closed fishing seasons. It shall be prohibited for fishing vessels to navigate or berth in violation of the provisions on closed fishing areas and closed fishing seasons.  
With respect to fishing vessels that violate the provisions of the preceding paragraph, no entity or individual may provide them with services such as oil supply, water supply, or ice supply, nor may they process, reload, transport, purchase, process, or sell their illegally caught catches on their behalf.  
It shall be prohibited to use methods that destroy fishery resources and the ecological environment of fishing waters, such as explosive fishing, poison fishing, and electric fishing, for fishing. It shall be prohibited to manufacture, sell, or use prohibited fishing gear. It shall be prohibited to use fishing nets with a mesh size smaller than the minimum mesh size for fishing. The proportion of juvenile fish in the catches shall not exceed the prescribed percentage.

#### 第五节　其他自然资源

<a id="art-802"></a>第八百零二条　国家加强对林木、草原、野生动物和海洋资源等其他自然资源开发利用的管理，实现资源的保护与可持续利用。

**Article 802** The State shall strengthen the management of the development and utilization of forest trees, grasslands, wild animals, marine resources, and other natural resources, so as to achieve the protection and sustainable utilization of resources.

<a id="art-803"></a>第八百零三条　国家实行林木采伐许可制度。采伐林地上的林木应当依法申请采伐许可证，并按照采伐许可证的规定进行采伐。非林地上的农田防护林、防风固沙林、护路林、护岸护堤林和城镇林木等的更新采伐，由有关主管部门按照规定管理。

**Article 803** The State shall implement a forest tree felling licensing system. Felling of forest trees on forestland shall, according to law, be subject to an application for a felling license and shall be carried out in accordance with the provisions of the felling license. The renewed felling of farmland shelterbelts, windbreak and sand-fixing forests, road protection forests, bank and levee protection forests, and urban forest trees on non-forestland shall be managed by the relevant competent departments in accordance with the regulations.

<a id="art-804"></a>第八百零四条　国家鼓励、支持人工草地建设、天然草原改良和饲草饲料基地建设，稳定、提高草原生产能力。  
县级以上人民政府应当按照草原保护、修复、利用规划加强草种基地建设，鼓励选育、引进、推广优良草品种。  
草原承包经营者应当合理利用草原，不得超过林业草原主管部门核定的载畜量，采取有效措施保持草畜平衡。  
牧区的草原承包经营者应当实行划区轮牧，合理配置畜群，均衡利用草原。

**Article 804** The State shall encourage and support the construction of artificial grasslands, the improvement of natural grasslands, and the construction of forage and feed bases, so as to stabilize and enhance the grassland production capacity.  
People's governments at or above the county level shall, in accordance with the grassland protection, restoration, and utilization plan, strengthen the construction of grass seed bases, and encourage the selection, breeding, introduction, and promotion of fine grass varieties.  
Grassland contractual operators shall reasonably utilize grasslands, shall not exceed the livestock carrying capacity verified by the competent department of forestry and grassland, and shall take effective measures to maintain the balance between grass and livestock.  
Grassland contractual operators in pastoral areas shall implement rotational grazing in designated areas, reasonably allocate livestock herds, and achieve balanced utilization of grasslands.

<a id="art-805"></a>第八百零五条　人工繁育野生动物，实行分类分级管理，严格保护和科学利用野生动物资源，并依法办理许可或者备案。  
人工繁育野生动物应当使用人工繁育子代种源，建立物种系谱、繁育档案和个体数据。因物种保护目的确需采用野外种源的，应当遵守本法和《中华人民共和国野生动物保护法》有关猎捕野生动物的规定。

**Article 805** Artificial breeding of wild animals shall be subject to classified and tiered management, with strict protection and scientific utilization of wild animal resources, and shall, according to law, undergo licensing or filing procedures.  
Artificial breeding of wild animals shall use offspring provenance from artificial breeding, and shall establish species pedigrees, breeding archives, and individual data. Where it is truly necessary to adopt wild provenance for the purpose of species protection, the provisions of this Law and the Wildlife Protection Law of the People's Republic of China concerning the hunting of wild animals shall be complied with.

<a id="art-806"></a>第八百零六条　开发利用海洋资源或者从事影响海洋生态环境的建设活动，应当根据国土空间规划科学合理布局，严格遵守国土空间用途管制要求，严守生态保护红线，不得造成海洋生态环境的损害。沿海省、自治区、直辖市人民政府应当加强对生态保护红线内人为活动的监督管理，定期评估保护成效。  
新建、改建、扩建工程建设项目，应当采取有效措施，保护国家和地方重点保护的野生动植物及其生存环境，保护海洋水产资源，避免或者减轻对海洋生物的影响。  
国家严格管理填海、围海等改变海域自然属性的用海活动。沿海地方各级人民政府应当根据国土空间规划，保护和科学合理使用海域。  
使用海域的单位和个人应当依照《中华人民共和国海域使用管理法》的规定，保护和科学合理使用海域，不得擅自改变经批准的海域用途。海域使用权终止后，应当及时拆除可能造成海洋环境污染、生态破坏或者影响其他用海项目的用海设施和构筑物。

**Article 806** The development and utilization of marine resources or construction activities affecting the marine ecological environment shall be scientifically and rationally laid out in accordance with the territorial space plan, strictly comply with the requirements of territorial space use control, strictly adhere to the ecological protection red line, and shall not cause damage to the marine ecological environment. The people's governments of coastal provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government shall strengthen supervision and management of human activities within the ecological protection red line, and regularly assess the effectiveness of protection.  
New construction, renovation, and expansion projects shall take effective measures to protect wildlife and their habitats under national and local key protection, protect marine fishery resources, and avoid or mitigate impacts on marine organisms.  
The State shall strictly manage sea use activities that alter the natural attributes of sea areas, such as land reclamation and enclosure of sea areas. Local people's governments at all levels in coastal areas shall, in accordance with the territorial space plan, protect and rationally utilize sea areas in a scientific manner.  
Entities and individuals using sea areas shall, in accordance with the provisions of the Sea Area Use Management Law of the People's Republic of China, protect and rationally utilize sea areas in a scientific manner, and shall not arbitrarily change the approved sea area use. After the termination of the right to use sea areas, they shall promptly remove sea use facilities and structures that may cause marine environmental pollution, ecological damage, or affect other sea use projects.

<a id="art-807"></a>第八百零七条　国家加强对专属经济区和大陆架自然资源开发利用活动的管理。中华人民共和国主管机关依照《中华人民共和国专属经济区和大陆架法》的规定，有权采取必要的措施，防止、减少和控制对海洋生态环境的破坏。

**Article 807** The State shall strengthen management over the development and utilization activities of natural resources in the exclusive economic zone and the continental shelf. The competent authorities of the People's Republic of China shall, in accordance with the provisions of the Exclusive Economic Zone and Continental Shelf Act of the People's Republic of China, have the right to take necessary measures to prevent, reduce, and control damage to the marine ecological environment.

<a id="art-808"></a>第八百零八条　从事深海海底区域资源勘探、开发活动应当依照《中华人民共和国深海海底区域资源勘探开发法》的规定，采取必要措施，保护和保全稀有或者脆弱的生态系统，以及衰竭、受威胁或者有灭绝危险的物种和其他海洋生物的生存环境，保护海洋生物多样性，维护海洋资源的可持续利用。

**Article 808** Entities engaged in exploration and development activities of resources in the deep seabed area shall, in accordance with the provisions of the Deep Seabed Area Resources Exploration and Development Act of the People's Republic of China, take necessary measures to protect and preserve rare or fragile ecosystems, as well as the living environments of depleted, threatened, or endangered species and other marine organisms, protect marine biodiversity, and maintain the sustainable utilization of marine resources.

### 第四章　物种保护

#### 第一节　野生动物保护

<a id="art-809"></a>第八百零九条　本法规定保护的野生动物，是指珍贵、濒危的陆生、水生野生动物和有重要生态、科学、社会价值的陆生野生动物。

**Article 809** Wild animals protected under this Law refer to precious and endangered terrestrial and aquatic wild animals, and terrestrial wild animals of significant ecological, scientific, or social value.

<a id="art-810"></a>第八百一十条　国家加强野生动物保护，拯救珍贵、濒危野生动物，维护生物多样性和生态平衡。

**Article 810** The State shall strengthen wildlife protection, rescue precious and endangered wild animals, and maintain biodiversity and ecological balance.

<a id="art-811"></a>第八百一十一条　国务院林业草原、渔业渔政主管部门分别主管全国陆生、水生野生动物保护工作。  
县级以上地方人民政府对本行政区域野生动物保护工作负责，其林业草原、渔业渔政主管部门分别主管本行政区域陆生、水生野生动物保护工作。  
县级以上人民政府其他有关部门在各自职责范围内负责野生动物保护相关工作。

**Article 811** The competent departments of forestry and grassland and of fisheries and fishery administration under the State Council shall be respectively responsible for the protection of terrestrial and aquatic wild animals nationwide.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall be responsible for wildlife protection work within their respective administrative regions, and their competent departments of forestry and grassland and of fisheries and fishery administration shall be respectively responsible for the protection of terrestrial and aquatic wild animals within their respective administrative regions.  
Other relevant departments of the people's governments at or above the county level shall be responsible for work related to wildlife protection within their respective functions and duties.

<a id="art-812"></a>第八百一十二条　国家对野生动物实行分类分级保护。  
国家对珍贵、濒危的野生动物实行重点保护。国家重点保护的野生动物分为一级保护野生动物和二级保护野生动物。  
国家重点保护野生动物，有重要生态、科学、社会价值的陆生野生动物，以及地方重点保护野生动物实行名录管理，定期论证评估，及时进行调整。

**Article 812** The State shall implement classified and tiered protection for wild animals.  
The State shall implement key protection for precious and endangered wild animals. State key protected wild animals are divided into Class I protected wild animals and Class II protected wild animals.  
State key protected wild animals, terrestrial wild animals of significant ecological, scientific, or social value, and local key protected wild animals shall be subject to list management, with regular demonstration and assessment, and timely adjustments.

<a id="art-813"></a>第八百一十三条　县级以上人民政府野生动物保护主管部门应当加强信息技术应用，定期组织或者委托有关科学研究机构对野生动物及其栖息地状况进行调查、监测和评估，建立健全野生动物及其栖息地档案。  
各级野生动物保护主管部门应当监测外部因素对野生动物的影响，发现野生动物受到危害时，应当会同有关部门及时进行调查处理。

**Article 813** The competent departments of wildlife protection of the people's governments at or above the county level shall strengthen the application of information technology, regularly organize or entrust relevant scientific research institutions to investigate, monitor, and assess the status of wild animals and their habitats, and establish and improve archives of wild animals and their habitats.  
Wildlife protection competent departments at all levels shall monitor the impact of external factors on wild animals, and when discovering that wild animals are endangered, shall, together with relevant departments, promptly investigate and handle the matter.

<a id="art-814"></a>第八百一十四条　县级以上人民政府野生动物保护主管部门根据野生动物及其栖息地调查、监测和评估情况，对种群数量明显超过生态环境容量的物种，可以采取迁地保护、猎捕等种群调控措施，保障人身财产安全、生态安全和农业生产。

**Article 814** The competent departments of wildlife protection of the people's governments at or above the county level may, based on the investigation, monitoring, and assessment of wild animals and their habitats, take population adjustment measures such as ex situ protection and hunting for species whose population clearly exceeds the ecological environmental capacity, so as to ensure personal and property safety, ecological safety, and agricultural production.

<a id="art-815"></a>第八百一十五条　国家保护野生动物及其栖息地。  
国务院野生动物保护主管部门应当会同有关部门，根据野生动物及其栖息地状况的调查、监测和评估结果，依法确定并发布野生动物重要栖息地名录。  
省级以上人民政府依法将野生动物重要栖息地划入自然保护地，保护、恢复和改善野生动物生存环境。对不具备划定自然保护地条件的，县级以上人民政府可以采取划定禁猎（渔）区、规定禁猎（渔）期等措施予以保护。  
禁止破坏野生动物栖息地，禁止或者限制在自然保护地内引入外来物种、营造单一纯林、过量施洒农药、产生高噪声、过度照明等人为干扰、威胁野生动物生息繁衍的行为。

**Article 815** The State shall protect wild animals and their habitats.  
The competent department of wildlife protection under the State Council shall, together with relevant departments, based on the results of investigation, monitoring, and assessment of the status of wild animals and their habitats, determine and publish a list of important wildlife habitats according to law.  
People's governments at or above the provincial level shall, according to law, incorporate important wildlife habitats into natural protected areas, and protect, restore, and improve the living environment of wild animals. Where conditions for designating natural protected areas are not met, people's governments at or above the county level may take measures such as designating closed hunting (fishing) areas and specifying closed hunting (fishing) seasons for protection.  
It shall be prohibited to destroy wildlife habitats, and to engage in or restrict activities such as introducing alien species, creating single-species pure forests, excessive application of pesticides, generating high noise, excessive lighting, and other human disturbances that threaten the reproduction and survival of wild animals within natural protected areas.

<a id="art-816"></a>第八百一十六条　县级以上人民政府及其有关部门在依法编制有关开发利用规划时，应当充分考虑野生动物及其栖息地保护的需要，分析、预测和评估规划实施可能对野生动物及其栖息地保护产生的整体影响，避免或者减少规划实施可能造成的不利后果。  
机场、铁路、公路、航道、水利水电、风电、光伏发电、围堰、填海、围海等建设项目的选址选线，应当避让野生动物重要栖息地、迁徙洄游通道；确实无法避让的，应当采取修建野生动物通道、过鱼设施等措施，消除或者减少对野生动物的不利影响。

**Article 816** When formulating relevant development and utilization plans according to law, the people's governments at or above the county level and their relevant departments shall fully consider the need for the protection of wild animals and their habitats, analyze, predict, and assess the overall impact that the implementation of the plans may have on the protection of wild animals and their habitats, and avoid or reduce the adverse consequences that may be caused by the implementation of the plans.  
The site selection and route alignment of construction projects such as airports, railways, highways, waterways, water conservancy and hydropower, wind power, photovoltaic power generation, cofferdams, land reclamation, and enclosure of sea areas shall avoid important wildlife habitats and migration and spawning routes; where avoidance is truly impossible, measures such as building wildlife passages and fish passage facilities shall be taken to eliminate or reduce the adverse impact on wild animals.

<a id="art-817"></a>第八百一十七条　在自然保护地和禁猎（渔）区、禁猎（渔）期内，禁止猎捕以及其他妨碍野生动物生息繁衍的活动，法律法规另有规定的除外。  
野生动物迁徙洄游期间，在前款规定区域外的迁徙洄游通道内，禁止猎捕并严格限制其他妨碍野生动物生息繁衍的活动。县级以上人民政府或者其野生动物保护主管部门应当规定并公布迁徙洄游通道的范围以及妨碍野生动物生息繁衍活动的内容。

**Article 817** Within natural protected areas and closed hunting (fishing) areas and during closed hunting (fishing) seasons, hunting and other activities that impede the reproduction and survival of wild animals shall be prohibited, except as otherwise provided by laws and regulations.  
During the period of wildlife migration and spawning, within the migration and spawning routes outside the areas specified in the preceding paragraph, hunting shall be prohibited and other activities that impede the reproduction and survival of wild animals shall be strictly restricted. The people's governments at or above the county level or their wildlife protection competent departments shall specify and publish the scope of the migration and spawning routes and the content of activities that impede the reproduction and survival of wild animals.

<a id="art-818"></a>第八百一十八条　禁止猎捕、杀害国家重点保护野生动物，因科学研究、种群调控、疫源疫病监测或者其他特殊情况需要猎捕的，应当依法申请特许猎捕证。  
猎捕有重要生态、科学、社会价值的陆生野生动物和地方重点保护野生动物的，应当依法取得狩猎证，并服从猎捕量限额管理。

**Article 818** It shall be prohibited to hunt or kill State key protected wild animals. Where hunting is necessary for scientific research, population adjustment, epidemic source and disease monitoring, or other special circumstances, a special hunting permit shall be applied for according to law.  
Hunting of terrestrial wild animals of significant ecological, scientific, or social value and local key protected wild animals shall, according to law, require a hunting license and shall be subject to the management of hunting quantity limits.

<a id="art-819"></a>第八百一十九条　禁止使用毒药、爆炸物、电击或者电子诱捕装置以及猎套、猎夹、捕鸟网、地枪、排铳、地弓等工具进行猎捕，禁止使用夜间照明行猎、歼灭性围猎、捣毁巢穴、设置陷阱、火攻、烟熏、网捕等方法进行猎捕，但因物种保护、科学研究确需网捕、电子诱捕以及植保作业等除外。  
前款规定以外的禁止使用的猎捕工具和方法，由县级以上地方人民政府规定并公布。

**Article 819** It shall be prohibited to use tools such as poisons, explosives, electric shocks or electronic trapping devices, and hunting snares, hunting traps, bird nets, ground guns, volley guns, and ground bows for hunting; it shall be prohibited to use methods such as night lighting hunting, exterminatory encircling hunting, destroying nests, setting traps, fire attacks, smoke fumigation, and net hunting for hunting, except where net hunting, electronic trapping, and plant protection operations are truly necessary for species protection or scientific research.  
The prohibited hunting tools and methods other than those specified in the preceding paragraph shall be prescribed and published by local people's governments at or above the county level.

<a id="art-820"></a>第八百二十条　社会公众应当增强保护野生动物和维护公共卫生安全的意识，防止野生动物源性传染病传播，抵制违法食用野生动物，养成文明健康的生活方式。

**Article 820** The public shall enhance awareness of protecting wild animals and maintaining public health safety, prevent the spread of wildlife-origin infectious diseases, resist the illegal consumption of wild animals, and cultivate a civilized and healthy lifestyle.

<a id="art-821"></a>第八百二十一条　禁止出售、购买、利用国家重点保护野生动物及其制品。  
因科学研究、人工繁育、公众展示展演、文物保护或者其他特殊情况，需要出售、购买、利用国家重点保护野生动物及其制品的，应当经依法批准，并按照规定取得和使用专用标识，保证可追溯。  
出售、利用有重要生态、科学、社会价值的陆生野生动物和地方重点保护野生动物及其制品的，应当提供狩猎、人工繁育、进出口等合法来源证明。  
出售本条第二款、第三款规定的野生动物的，还应当依法附有检疫证明。  
利用野生动物进行公众展示展演应当采取安全管理措施，并保障野生动物健康状态。

**Article 821** The sale, purchase, utilization of nationally key protected wild animals and their products shall be prohibited.  
Where it is necessary to sell, purchase, or utilize nationally key protected wild animals and their products due to scientific research, artificial breeding, public exhibition and performance, cultural relic protection, or other special circumstances, approval shall be obtained according to law, and special identification shall be obtained and used in accordance with the provisions to ensure traceability.  
Where terrestrial wild animals of significant ecological, scientific, or social value and locally key protected wild animals and their products are sold or utilized, a certificate of legal source, such as hunting, artificial breeding, import and export, shall be provided.  
Where wild animals specified in the second and third paragraphs of this Article are sold, a quarantine certificate shall also be attached according to law.  
Where wild animals are used for public exhibition and performance, safety management measures shall be taken, and the healthy condition of the wild animals shall be ensured.

<a id="art-822"></a>第八百二十二条　国家重点保护野生动物和有重要生态、科学、社会价值的陆生野生动物或者地方重点保护野生动物受到突发事件威胁时，当地人民政府应当及时采取应急救助措施。  
国家加强野生动物收容救护能力建设。县级以上人民政府野生动物保护主管部门应当按照国家规定组织开展野生动物收容救护工作，加强对社会组织开展野生动物收容救护工作的规范和指导。  
收容救护机构应当根据野生动物收容救护的实际需要，建立收容救护场所，配备相应的专业技术人员、救护工具、设备和药品等。  
禁止以野生动物收容救护为名买卖野生动物及其制品。

**Article 822** Where nationally key protected wild animals and terrestrial wild animals of significant ecological, scientific, or social value or locally key protected wild animals are threatened by an emergency, the local people's government shall promptly take emergency rescue measures.  
The State shall strengthen the capacity building for the shelter and rescue of wild animals. The wildlife protection competent authorities of the people's governments at or above the county level shall organize the shelter and rescue of wild animals in accordance with the State provisions, and strengthen the regulation and guidance of social organizations in carrying out shelter and rescue work.  
Shelter and rescue institutions shall, based on the actual needs of shelter and rescue of wild animals, establish shelter and rescue sites, and be equipped with corresponding professional and technical personnel, rescue tools, equipment, and medicines, etc.  
It shall be prohibited to buy or sell wild animals and their products under the pretext of shelter and rescue of wild animals.

<a id="art-823"></a>第八百二十三条　国务院野生动物保护主管部门应当会同有关部门加强对放生野生动物活动的规范、引导。任何单位和个人将野生动物放生至野外环境，应当选择适合放生地野外生存的当地物种，不得干扰当地居民的正常生活、生产，避免对生态系统造成危害。

**Article 823** The wildlife protection competent authority of the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, strengthen the regulation and guidance of the release of wild animals into the wild. Any unit or individual releasing wild animals into the wild environment shall select local species suitable for survival in the release area, shall not interfere with the normal life and production of local residents, and shall avoid causing harm to the ecosystem.

<a id="art-824"></a>第八百二十四条　外国人在我国对国家重点保护野生动物进行野外考察或者在野外拍摄电影、录像，应当经依法批准，并遵守有关法律法规的规定。

**Article 824** Where foreigners conduct field surveys of nationally key protected wild animals or film or videotape these animals in the wild within China, approval shall be obtained according to law, and the provisions of relevant laws and regulations shall be complied with.

<a id="art-825"></a>第八百二十五条　国家加强对野生动物遗传资源的保护，对濒危野生动物实施抢救性保护。  
国务院野生动物保护主管部门应当会同有关部门制定有关野生动物遗传资源保护和利用规划，建立国家野生动物遗传资源基因库，对原产我国的珍贵、濒危野生动物遗传资源实行重点保护。  
禁止向境外机构或者人员提供我国特有的野生动物遗传资源。开展国际科学研究合作的，应当依法取得批准，有我国科研机构、高等学校、企业及其研究人员实质性参与研究，按照规定提出国家共享惠益的方案，并遵守我国法律、行政法规的规定。

**Article 825** The State shall strengthen the protection of wildlife genetic resources and implement rescue protection for endangered wild animals.  
The wildlife protection competent authority of the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, formulate a plan for the protection and utilization of wildlife genetic resources, establish a national wildlife genetic resources gene bank, and implement key protection for the genetic resources of precious and endangered wild animals native to China.  
It shall be prohibited to provide the unique wildlife genetic resources of China to foreign institutions or individuals. Where international scientific research cooperation is carried out, approval shall be obtained according to law, there shall be substantive participation by Chinese research institutions, institutions of higher education, enterprises, and their researchers in the research, a plan for national shared benefits shall be proposed in accordance with the provisions, and the laws and administrative regulations of China shall be complied with.

<a id="art-826"></a>第八百二十六条　中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际公约禁止或者限制贸易的野生动物或者其制品名录，由国家濒危物种进出口管理机构制定、调整并公布。  
进出口列入前款名录的野生动物或者其制品，或者出口国家重点保护野生动物或者其制品的，应当经依法批准，并取得允许进出口证明书，凭允许进出口证明书办理海关手续。  
涉及科学技术保密的野生动物物种的出口，按照国务院有关规定办理。

**Article 826** The list of wild animals or their products whose trade is prohibited or restricted by international conventions concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China shall be formulated, adjusted, and published by the national authority for the administration of import and export of endangered species.  
Where wild animals or their products included in the list in the preceding paragraph are imported or exported, or nationally key protected wild animals or their products are exported, approval shall be obtained according to law, and an import/export permit certificate shall be obtained, and customs formalities shall be handled with the import/export permit certificate.  
The export of wild animal species involving scientific and technological confidentiality shall be handled in accordance with the relevant provisions of the State Council.

<a id="art-827"></a>第八百二十七条　国家组织开展野生动物保护及相关执法活动的国际交流与合作，加强与毗邻国家的协作，保护候鸟、洄游鱼类等野生动物的迁徙洄游通道；建立防范、打击野生动物及其制品走私和非法贸易的部门协调机制，开展防范、打击走私和非法贸易行动。

**Article 827** The State shall organize international exchanges and cooperation in wildlife protection and related law enforcement activities, strengthen collaboration with neighboring countries, protect the migration and passage routes of migratory birds, migratory fish, and other wild animals; establish a departmental coordination mechanism for preventing and combating smuggling and illegal trade of wild animals and their products, and carry out actions to prevent and combat smuggling and illegal trade.

<a id="art-828"></a>第八百二十八条　在野生动物致害严重区域，县级以上地方人民政府应当组织有关单位和个人积极开展野生动物致害综合防控，通过建设隔离防护设施、设置安全警示标志、强化监测预警、开展防护知识宣传等，科学预防野生动物可能造成的危害。  
因保护珍贵、濒危的陆生、水生野生动物和有重要生态、科学、社会价值的陆生野生动物，造成人员伤亡、农作物或者其他财产损失的，依法给予补偿。  
在野生动物危及人身安全的紧急情况下，采取措施造成野生动物损害的，依法不承担法律责任。

**Article 828** In areas where wild animals cause serious damage, the local people's governments at or above the county level shall organize relevant units and individuals to actively carry out comprehensive prevention and control of wildlife damage, by constructing isolation and protection facilities, setting up safety warning signs, strengthening monitoring and early warning, carrying out publicity on protection knowledge, etc., to scientifically prevent the harm that wild animals may cause.  
Where damage is caused to persons, crops, or other property due to the protection of precious and endangered terrestrial and aquatic wild animals and terrestrial wild animals of significant ecological, scientific, or social value, compensation shall be given according to law.  
In emergency situations where wild animals endanger personal safety, measures taken that cause damage to wild animals shall not bear legal liability according to law.

#### 第二节　野生植物保护

<a id="art-829"></a>第八百二十九条　本法规定保护的野生植物，是指原生地天然生长的珍贵植物和原生地天然生长并具有重要经济、科学、文化价值的濒危、稀有植物。  
药用野生植物和城市园林、自然保护地内的野生植物的保护，同时适用有关法律法规的规定。

**Article 829** The wild plants protected under this Law refer to the precious plants naturally growing in their native places and the endangered and rare plants naturally growing in their native places and having important economic, scientific, or cultural value.  
The protection of medicinal wild plants and wild plants within urban gardens and natural protected areas shall also be subject to the provisions of relevant laws and regulations.

<a id="art-830"></a>第八百三十条　国家加强野生植物保护，严格管理我国野生植物采集、利用、对外提供等活动。

**Article 830** The State shall strengthen the protection of wild plants, and strictly manage activities such as the collection, utilization, and provision to foreign parties of wild plants in China.

<a id="art-831"></a>第八百三十一条　国务院林业草原、农业农村主管部门在各自职责范围内分别主管全国野生植物保护和监督管理工作。  
国务院住房城乡建设主管部门负责城市园林内野生植物的监督管理工作。国务院生态环境主管部门负责监督全国野生植物保护工作。国务院其他有关部门在各自职责范围内负责野生植物保护相关工作。  
县级以上地方人民政府林业草原、农业农村主管部门在各自职责范围内分别主管本行政区域野生植物保护和监督管理工作。县级以上地方人民政府其他有关部门在各自职责范围内负责野生植物保护相关工作。

**Article 831** The competent authorities of forestry and grassland and of agriculture and rural affairs of the State Council shall, within their respective functions and duties, take charge of the protection and supervision of wild plants nationwide.  
The competent authority of housing and urban-rural development of the State Council shall be responsible for the supervision and management of wild plants within urban gardens. The competent authority of ecological environment of the State Council shall be responsible for supervising the protection of wild plants nationwide. Other relevant departments of the State Council shall be responsible for work related to the protection of wild plants within their respective functions and duties.  
The competent authorities of forestry and grassland and of agriculture and rural affairs of the local people's governments at or above the county level shall, within their respective functions and duties, take charge of the protection and supervision of wild plants within their respective administrative regions. Other relevant departments of the local people's governments at or above the county level shall be responsible for work related to the protection of wild plants within their respective functions and duties.

<a id="art-832"></a>第八百三十二条　国家保护野生植物及其生长环境。禁止非法采集野生植物或者破坏其生长环境。

**Article 832** The State shall protect wild plants and their growing environment. It shall be prohibited to illegally collect wild plants or damage their growing environment.

<a id="art-833"></a>第八百三十三条　国家对野生植物实行分类分级保护。  
野生植物分为国家重点保护野生植物和地方重点保护野生植物。国家重点保护野生植物分为国家一级保护野生植物和国家二级保护野生植物。  
国家重点保护野生植物、地方重点保护野生植物实行名录管理，并及时进行调整。

**Article 833** The State implements categorized and graded protection for wild plants.  
Wild plants are divided into nationally key protected wild plants and locally key protected wild plants. Nationally key protected wild plants are divided into Class I nationally key protected wild plants and Class II nationally key protected wild plants.  
Nationally key protected wild plants and locally key protected wild plants shall be subject to list management and shall be adjusted in a timely manner.

<a id="art-834"></a>第八百三十四条　国家鼓励、支持野生植物的就地保护。在国家重点保护野生植物物种和地方重点保护野生植物物种的天然集中分布区域，应当依法建立自然保护地；在其他区域，县级以上地方人民政府野生植物保护主管部门和其他有关部门可以根据实际情况建立重点保护野生植物的保护点或者设立保护标志。  
禁止擅自移动或者破坏重点保护野生植物的保护设施和保护标志。

**Article 834** The State shall encourage and support in-situ conservation of wild plants. In areas where nationally key protected wild plant species and locally key protected wild plant species are naturally concentrated, natural protected areas shall be established according to law; in other areas, the competent authorities of wild plant protection and other relevant departments of the local people's governments at or above the county level may, based on actual conditions, establish protection points for key protected wild plants or set up protection signs.  
It shall be prohibited to move or damage the protection facilities and protection signs of key protected wild plants without authorization.

<a id="art-835"></a>第八百三十五条　各级野生植物保护主管部门和有关单位对生长受到威胁的国家重点保护野生植物和地方重点保护野生植物应当采取拯救措施，恢复其生长环境，必要时应当采取迁地保护、建立种质资源库和野外回归等措施。

**Article 835** The competent authorities of wild plant protection at all levels and relevant units shall take rescue measures for nationally key protected wild plants and locally key protected wild plants whose growth is threatened, restore their growing environment, and when necessary, take measures such as ex-situ conservation, establishing germplasm resource banks, and reintroduction to the wild.

<a id="art-836"></a>第八百三十六条　国家建立健全野生植物迁地保护网络，逐步建立国家植物园体系。

**Article 836** The State shall establish and improve a network for ex-situ conservation of wild plants, and gradually establish a national botanical garden system.

<a id="art-837"></a>第八百三十七条　国务院野生植物保护主管部门负责组织开展全国野生植物资源调查、监测、评估和信息发布工作。地方人民政府野生植物保护主管部门负责本行政区域相关工作。  
县级以上人民政府野生植物保护主管部门及其他有关部门应当监视、监测外部因素对国家重点保护野生植物和地方重点保护野生植物生长的影响，并采取有效措施，维护和改善国家重点保护野生植物和地方重点保护野生植物的生长条件。国家重点保护野生植物和地方重点保护野生植物的生长受到危害时，野生植物保护主管部门应当会同其他有关部门调查并依法处理。

**Article 837** The competent authority of wild plant protection of the State Council shall be responsible for organizing the nationwide survey, monitoring, assessment, and information release of wild plant resources. The competent authorities of wild plant protection of the local people's governments shall be responsible for the relevant work in their respective administrative regions.  
The competent authorities of wild plant protection and other relevant departments of the people's governments at or above the county level shall monitor and survey the impact of external factors on the growth of nationally key protected wild plants and locally key protected wild plants, and take effective measures to maintain and improve the growth conditions of nationally key protected wild plants and locally key protected wild plants. When the growth of nationally key protected wild plants and locally key protected wild plants is endangered, the competent authorities of wild plant protection shall, together with other relevant departments, investigate and handle the matter according to law.

<a id="art-838"></a>第八百三十八条　除因科学研究、人工培育、文化交流等特殊需要外，禁止采集国家一级保护野生植物。  
采集国家一级、二级保护野生植物的，应当依法申请采集证。  
采集城市园林、自然保护地内的国家重点保护野生植物的，须先征得城市园林、自然保护地管理机构同意，分别依照前两款的规定申请采集证。

**Article 838** Except for special needs such as scientific research, artificial cultivation, or cultural exchange, it shall be prohibited to collect Class I nationally protected wild plants.  
Where Class I or Class II nationally protected wild plants are collected, a collection permit shall be applied for according to law.  
Where nationally key protected wild plants within urban gardens or natural protected areas are collected, the consent of the management authority of the urban garden or natural protected area shall first be obtained, and a collection permit shall be applied for in accordance with the provisions of the preceding two paragraphs respectively.

<a id="art-839"></a>第八百三十九条　国家引导和规范具备条件的单位和个人依法开展野生植物人工培植活动。

**Article 839** The State shall guide and regulate units and individuals that meet the conditions to carry out artificial cultivation of wild plants according to law.

<a id="art-840"></a>第八百四十条　禁止出售、收购国家一级保护野生植物。  
出售、收购国家二级保护野生植物的，应当经依法批准。

**Article 840** It shall be prohibited to sell or purchase Class I nationally protected wild plants.  
Where Class II nationally protected wild plants are sold or purchased, approval shall be obtained according to law.

<a id="art-841"></a>第八百四十一条　禁止外国人在中国境内采集或者收购国家重点保护野生植物及其种质资源。

**Article 841** : Foreigners shall be prohibited from collecting or purchasing wild plants under key national protection and their germplasm resources within the territory of China.

<a id="art-842"></a>第八百四十二条　出口国家重点保护野生植物及其制品或者进出口中华人民共和国参加的国际公约所限制进出口的野生植物及其制品的，应当经依法批准，并取得允许进出口证明书或者标签，凭允许进出口证明书或者标签办理海关手续。国务院野生植物保护主管部门应当将有关野生植物进出口的资料抄送国务院生态环境主管部门。  
禁止出口未定名的或者新发现并有重要价值的野生植物及其制品。

**Article 842** : Where any person exports wild plants under key national protection and their products, or imports or exports wild plants and their products that are restricted from import and export under international conventions to which the People's Republic of China is a party, such person shall obtain approval according to law and acquire a permit certificate or label for import and export, and shall complete customs formalities with such permit certificate or label. The competent department of wild plant protection under the State Council shall serve copies of the relevant materials on the import and export of wild plants to the competent department of ecological environment under the State Council.  
It shall be prohibited to export unnamed or newly discovered wild plants and their products of significant value.

#### 第三节　外来入侵物种防控

<a id="art-843"></a>第八百四十三条　本法所称外来入侵物种，是指境外传入定殖并对生态系统、物种及其栖息生长环境带来威胁或者危害，影响我国生态环境，损害农林牧渔业可持续发展和生物多样性的外来物种。

**Article 843** : For the purposes of this Law, "invasive alien species" means alien species that are introduced from outside the territory, become established, and pose a threat or cause harm to ecosystems, species, and their habitats or growth environments, affecting the ecological environment of China and damaging the sustainable development of agriculture, forestry, animal husbandry, and fishery, as well as biodiversity.

<a id="art-844"></a>第八百四十四条　国家坚持风险预防、源头管控、综合治理、协同配合、公众参与，完善外来物种入侵防控体系，加强外来入侵物种防控管理，保护生物多样性，维护国家生物安全。  
国家严厉打击非法引入外来物种行为。任何单位和个人不得违反《中华人民共和国生物安全法》、《中华人民共和国野生动物保护法》等法律法规的规定，擅自引进、释放或者丢弃外来物种。

**Article 844** : The State shall adhere to risk prevention, source control, comprehensive governance, coordination, and public participation, improve the prevention and control system for invasive alien species, strengthen the management of prevention and control of invasive alien species, protect biodiversity, and safeguard national biosecurity.  
The State shall severely crack down on the illegal introduction of alien species. No unit or individual may, in violation of the provisions of the Biosecurity Law of the People's Republic of China, the Law of the People's Republic of China on the Protection of Wildlife, or other laws and regulations, introduce, release, or discard alien species without authorization.

<a id="art-845"></a>第八百四十五条　国务院农业农村主管部门会同有关部门建立外来入侵物种防控协调机制，统筹全国外来入侵物种防控工作，协调解决重大问题。  
省级人民政府农业农村主管部门会同有关部门建立外来入侵物种防控协调机制，组织开展本行政区域外来入侵物种防控工作。  
海关完善境外风险预警和应急处理机制，强化入境货物、运输工具、寄递物品、旅客行李、跨境电商、边民互市等渠道外来入侵物种的口岸检疫监督管理。  
县级以上地方人民政府依法对本行政区域外来入侵物种防控工作负责，组织、协调、督促有关部门依法履行外来入侵物种防控管理职责。  
县级以上地方人民政府农业农村、林业草原、自然资源、生态环境以及其他有关部门在各自职责范围内负责外来入侵物种的监督管理工作。

**Article 845** : The competent department of agriculture and rural affairs under the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, establish a coordination mechanism for the prevention and control of invasive alien species, coordinate nationwide prevention and control efforts, and coordinate the resolution of major issues.  
The competent department of agriculture and rural affairs of a provincial people's government shall, together with the relevant departments, establish a coordination mechanism for the prevention and control of invasive alien species, and organize and carry out prevention and control efforts within its administrative region.  
Customs shall improve the overseas risk early warning and emergency response mechanisms, and strengthen quarantine supervision and management over invasive alien species in channels such as inbound goods, means of transport, postal items, passenger luggage, cross-border e-commerce, and border trade.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall, according to law, be responsible for the prevention and control of invasive alien species within their administrative regions, and shall organize, coordinate, and supervise the relevant departments to perform their duties in the management of prevention and control of invasive alien species according to law.  
The departments of agriculture and rural affairs, forestry and grassland, natural resources, ecological environment, and other relevant departments of local people's governments at or above the county level shall, within their respective duties, be responsible for the supervision and management of invasive alien species.

<a id="art-846"></a>第八百四十六条　国务院农业农村主管部门会同有关部门制定完善外来入侵物种名录，实行动态调整和分类分级管理，建立外来入侵物种数据库，制定外来入侵物种风险评估、监测预警、防控治理等技术规范。

**Article 846** : The competent department of agriculture and rural affairs under the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, formulate and improve the list of invasive alien species, implement dynamic adjustment and classification and grading management, establish a database of invasive alien species, and formulate technical specifications for risk assessment, monitoring and early warning, and prevention, control, and management of invasive alien species.

<a id="art-847"></a>第八百四十七条　国务院农业农村、自然资源、生态环境、海关、林业草原等有关部门建立健全应急处置机制，组织制定相关领域外来入侵物种突发事件应急预案。

**Article 847** : The relevant departments under the State Council, including agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, ecological environment, customs, and forestry and grassland, shall establish and improve emergency response mechanisms, and organize the formulation of contingency plans for emergencies related to invasive alien species in relevant fields.

<a id="art-848"></a>第八百四十八条　国家加强外来物种引入审批管理。因品种培育等特殊需要从境外引进农作物和林草种子苗木、水产苗种等外来物种的，应当依法办理进口审批与检疫审批。  
属于首次引进的，引进单位应当就引进物种对生态环境的潜在影响进行风险分析，并向审批部门提交风险评估报告。审批部门应当及时组织开展审查评估。

**Article 848** : The State shall strengthen the approval management of the introduction of alien species. Where alien species such as crop seeds, forest and grass seeds and seedlings, and aquatic fry are introduced from outside the territory due to special needs such as variety breeding, procedures for import approval and quarantine approval shall be completed according to law.  
Where the introduction is for the first time, the introducing unit shall conduct a risk analysis of the potential impact of the introduced species on the ecological environment, and submit a risk assessment report to the approving department. The approving department shall promptly organize and carry out review and assessment.

<a id="art-849"></a>第八百四十九条　国务院农业农村主管部门会同有关部门建立外来入侵物种普查制度，掌握我国外来入侵物种的种类数量、分布范围、危害程度等情况。

**Article 849** : The competent department of agriculture and rural affairs under the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, establish a census system for invasive alien species to ascertain the species, quantity, distribution, and degree of harm of invasive alien species in China.

<a id="art-850"></a>第八百五十条　国务院农业农村主管部门会同有关部门建立外来入侵物种监测制度，构建全国外来入侵物种监测网络，在各自职责范围内布设监测站点，组织开展常态化监测和信息共享。  
县级以上地方人民政府农业农村主管部门和有关部门在各自职责范围内开展本行政区域外来入侵物种监测工作。

**Article 850** : The competent department of agriculture and rural affairs under the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, establish a monitoring system for invasive alien species, build a national monitoring network for invasive alien species, deploy monitoring stations within their respective duties, and organize and carry out routine monitoring and information sharing.  
The competent departments of agriculture and rural affairs and the relevant departments of local people's governments at or above the county level shall, within their respective duties, carry out monitoring of invasive alien species within their administrative regions.

<a id="art-851"></a>第八百五十一条　国家加强外来入侵物种口岸防控。  
输入动物、动物产品、植物种子苗木及其他繁殖材料的，应当依照《中华人民共和国进出境动植物检疫法》的规定，事先提出申请，办理检疫审批手续。  
输入动植物、动植物产品和其他检疫物，应当在进境口岸实施检疫。未经海关同意，不得卸离运输工具。输入动植物，需隔离检疫的，在海关指定的隔离场所检疫。  
输入动物，经检疫合格的，准予进境。输入植物、动植物产品和其他检疫物，经检疫合格或者经除害处理合格的，准予进境。

**Article 851** : The State shall strengthen port prevention and control of invasive alien species.  
Where any animals, animal products, plant seeds, seedlings, or other propagation materials are imported, an application shall be made in advance and quarantine approval procedures shall be completed in accordance with the provisions of the Law of the People's Republic of China on the Quarantine of Imported and Exported Animals and Plants.  
Where animals, plants, animal or plant products, or other quarantine objects are imported, quarantine shall be carried out at the port of entry. Without the consent of customs, they shall not be unloaded from the means of transport. Where imported animals or plants require quarantine in isolation, quarantine shall be carried out at a quarantine site designated by customs.  
Where imported animals pass quarantine inspection, they shall be allowed to enter the country. Where imported plants, animal or plant products, or other quarantine objects pass quarantine inspection or pass disinfestation treatment, they shall be allowed to enter the country.

<a id="art-852"></a>第八百五十二条　国务院农业农村、自然资源、生态环境、林业草原等有关部门在各自职责范围内研究制定本领域外来入侵物种防控策略措施，指导地方开展防控。  
县级以上地方人民政府农业农村、自然资源、林业草原等有关部门应当在各自职责范围内综合考虑外来入侵物种种类、危害对象、危害程度、扩散趋势等因素，制定本行政区域外来入侵物种防控治理方案，并组织实施，及时控制或者消除危害。

**Article 852** : The relevant departments under the State Council, including agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, ecological environment, and forestry and grassland, shall, within their respective duties, study and formulate prevention and control strategies and measures for invasive alien species in their respective fields, and guide local authorities in carrying out prevention and control.  
The relevant departments of local people's governments at or above the county level, including agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, and forestry and grassland, shall, within their respective duties, comprehensively consider factors such as the types of invasive alien species, the objects harmed, the degree of harm, and the trend of spread, formulate prevention, control, and management plans for invasive alien species within their administrative regions, and organize and implement them to timely control or eliminate the harm.

### 第五章　重要地理单元保护

#### 第一节　自然保护地

<a id="art-853"></a>第八百五十三条　国家以保护自然、服务人民、永续发展为目标，坚持严格保护、科学管理、政府主导、多方参与、合理利用、社会共享，建立健全自然保护地体系，保护生物多样性与地质地貌景观多样性，维护生态系统健康稳定，提升生态产品供给能力，维护生态安全。

**Article 853** : The State, with the goals of protecting nature, serving the people, and achieving sustainable development, shall adhere to strict protection, scientific management, government leadership, multi-party participation, rational utilization, and social sharing, establish and improve a system of protected natural areas, protect biodiversity and geological and geomorphological landscape diversity, maintain the health and stability of ecosystems, enhance the capacity to supply ecological products, and safeguard ecological security.

<a id="art-854"></a>第八百五十四条　国务院林业草原主管部门负责各类自然保护地的监督管理工作。  
国务院自然资源主管部门根据国务院授权履行自然保护地范围内全民所有自然资源资产所有者职责。  
国务院生态环境主管部门在其职责范围内负责组织制定各类自然保护地生态环境监督管理制度并监督执法。  
国务院住房城乡建设、交通运输、水行政、农业农村、文化和旅游等有关部门和海警机构在各自职责范围内负责自然保护地内相关工作。  
按照规定设立的各自然保护地管理机构依照法律和规定的职责，负责各该自然保护地的保护和管理工作。  
有关县级以上地方人民政府履行本行政区域自然保护地的经济社会发展综合协调、公共服务、社会管理、市场监督管理、防灾减灾等职责；其有关部门在各自职责范围内负责自然保护地的有关监督管理工作。

**Article 854** : The competent department of forestry and grassland under the State Council shall be responsible for the supervision and management of all types of protected natural areas.  
The competent department of natural resources under the State Council shall, as authorized by the State Council, perform the duties of the owner of the natural resources assets owned by the whole people within the protected natural areas.  
The competent department of ecological environment under the State Council shall, within its duties, be responsible for organizing the formulation of ecological environment supervision and management systems for all types of protected natural areas and supervising law enforcement.  
The relevant departments under the State Council, including housing and urban-rural construction, transport, water administration, agriculture and rural affairs, culture and tourism, and the coast guard agency, shall, within their respective duties, be responsible for relevant work within the protected natural areas.  
The management agencies of each protected natural area established in accordance with the regulations shall, in accordance with the law and their prescribed duties, be responsible for the protection and management of the relevant protected natural area.  
The relevant local people's governments at or above the county level shall perform the duties of comprehensive coordination of economic and social development, public services, social management, market supervision and management, and disaster prevention and mitigation for the protected natural areas within their administrative regions; their relevant departments shall, within their respective duties, be responsible for the relevant supervision and management of the protected natural areas.

<a id="art-855"></a>第八百五十五条　国务院林业草原主管部门会同国务院发展改革、财政、自然资源、生态环境、住房城乡建设、交通运输、水行政、农业农村、文化和旅游等有关部门开展保护协作、信息共享等，研究解决自然保护地保护工作中的重大问题。

**Article 855** : The competent department of forestry and grassland under the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments under the State Council, including development and reform, finance, natural resources, ecological environment, housing and urban-rural construction, transport, water administration, agriculture and rural affairs, and culture and tourism, carry out protection coordination and information sharing, and study and resolve major issues in the protection of protected natural areas.

<a id="art-856"></a>第八百五十六条　跨两个以上行政区域的自然保护地，由相关行政区域人民政府加强自然保护地保护工作协商，或者由涉及行政区域联合申请，由上级人民政府协调解决；跨两个以上省级行政区域的，由国务院林业草原主管部门商自然保护地所在地省、自治区、直辖市人民政府解决。

**Article 856** : For a protected natural area spanning two or more administrative regions, the people's governments of the relevant administrative regions shall strengthen consultation on the protection work of the protected natural area, or the involved administrative regions shall jointly apply, and the people's government at the higher level shall coordinate and resolve the matter; for a protected natural area spanning two or more provincial administrative regions, the competent department of forestry and grassland under the State Council shall consult with the people's governments of the provinces, autonomous regions, or municipalities directly under the Central Government where the protected natural area is located to resolve the matter.

<a id="art-857"></a>第八百五十七条　国家加强自然保护地监测网络体系建设，充分发挥各类监测站点的作用，运用信息化手段加强监测数据集成分析、共享和综合应用，全面掌握生态系统构成、分布、动态变化和生物多样性状况，及时评估和预警生态风险。

**Article 857** : The State shall strengthen the construction of a monitoring network system for protected natural areas, give full play to the role of various monitoring stations, use information technology to strengthen the integrated analysis, sharing, and comprehensive application of monitoring data, comprehensively grasp the composition, distribution, dynamic changes, and biodiversity status of ecosystems, and timely assess and provide early warning of ecological risks.

<a id="art-858"></a>第八百五十八条　国家建立健全自然保护地相关标准体系，依法组织制定并适时修订自然保护地相关标准。

**Article 858** : The State shall establish and improve a standard system related to protected natural areas, and organize the formulation and timely revision of relevant standards for protected natural areas according to law.

<a id="art-859"></a>第八百五十九条　国家实行自然保护地分类分级管理。自然保护地按生态价值和保护强度高低依次分为国家公园、自然保护区和自然公园。自然保护区和自然公园分为国家级和省级。  
国家科学规划自然保护地总体布局，严格自然保护地设立条件，合理确定自然保护地数量和规模。  
各类自然保护地范围不得交叉重叠。

**Article 859** : The State shall implement categorized and graded management of protected natural areas. Protected natural areas shall be classified, in descending order of ecological value and protection intensity, into national parks, nature reserves, and natural parks. Nature reserves and natural parks shall be divided into national-level and provincial-level.  
The State shall scientifically plan the overall layout of protected natural areas, strictly set conditions for the establishment of protected natural areas, and reasonably determine the number and scale of protected natural areas.  
The boundaries of various types of protected natural areas shall not overlap.

<a id="art-860"></a>第八百六十条　国家公园的设立、名称变更、区域范围或者管控分区调整等，依照《中华人民共和国国家公园法》的规定办理。  
国家级自然保护区的设立、名称变更、范围调整报国务院批准。省级自然保护区的设立、名称变更、范围调整报省、自治区、直辖市人民政府批准。  
自然公园的设立、范围调整报省级以上人民政府或者其林业草原主管部门批准。

**Article 860** : The establishment, change of name, adjustment of regional scope or control zones of a national park shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of the National Park Law of the People's Republic of China.  
The establishment, change of name, or adjustment of scope of a national-level nature reserve shall be submitted to the State Council for approval. The establishment, change of name, or adjustment of scope of a provincial-level nature reserve shall be submitted to the people's government of the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government for approval.  
The establishment or adjustment of scope of a natural park shall be submitted to the people's government at or above the provincial level or its competent department of forestry and grassland for approval.

<a id="art-861"></a>第八百六十一条　自然保护地实行分区管控。根据生态系统特性、功能定位和管理目标，国家公园和自然保护区划分为核心保护区和一般控制区。  
国家公园区域内生态系统保存完整、代表性强，核心资源集中分布，或者生态脆弱需要休养生息的区域划为核心保护区，核心保护区以外的区域划为一般控制区。  
自然保护区区域内自然生态系统保存完整或者生态脆弱需要休养生息的区域、珍稀濒危野生动植物物种的关键分布区域以及生态廊道重要节点、重要自然遗迹的集中分布区域，以及其他需要重点保护的区域划为核心保护区，核心保护区以外的区域划为一般控制区。  
自然公园按一般控制区管理，法律法规另有规定的，依照其规定管理。
