**Article 861** Natural protected areas shall be subject to zoning and control. Based on the characteristics of ecosystems, functional positioning, and management objectives, national parks and nature reserves shall be divided into core protection zones and general control zones.  
Within the area of a national park, zones where the ecosystem is intact and representative, core resources are concentrated, or where the ecology is fragile and needs restoration shall be designated as core protection zones, and areas outside the core protection zones shall be designated as general control zones.  
Within the area of a nature reserve, zones where the natural ecosystem is intact or the ecology is fragile and needs restoration, key distribution areas of rare and endangered species of wild animals and plants, important nodes of ecological corridors, concentrated distribution areas of significant natural relics, and other areas requiring key protection shall be designated as core protection zones, and areas outside the core protection zones shall be designated as general control zones.  
Nature parks shall be managed as general control zones, unless otherwise provided by laws and regulations, in which case such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-862"></a>第八百六十二条　自然保护地周边社区建设应当与自然保护地保护目标相协调，避免或者减少对自然保护地的不利影响。

**Article 862** The construction of communities around natural protected areas shall be coordinated with the protection objectives of the natural protected areas, and shall avoid or mitigate adverse impacts on the natural protected areas.

<a id="art-863"></a>第八百六十三条　国家对自然保护地实行整体保护，建立健全政府、企业事业单位、社会组织和公众共同参与保护的长效机制，指导、支持自然保护地内原有居民、农村集体经济组织以及自然保护地周边居民、企业等积极参与自然保护地的保护，提供与自然保护地保护目标相一致的生态产品和服务。

**Article 863** The State shall implement overall protection of natural protected areas, establish and improve a long-term mechanism for the joint participation of the government, enterprises and institutions, social organizations, and the public in protection, and guide and support the original residents within natural protected areas, rural collective economic organizations, and residents and enterprises around natural protected areas to actively participate in the protection of natural protected areas and to provide ecological products and services consistent with the protection objectives of the natural protected areas.

<a id="art-864"></a>第八百六十四条　国家完善自然保护地公共服务体系，提升公共服务功能，促进社会共享自然保护地生态价值。  
自然保护地一般控制区可以按照规定划定适当区域，设置必要的辅助设施设备，为开展相关科学研究、科普宣传、生态旅游、教育文化体育等公共服务活动提供支持。  
自然保护地区域内不得开设与保护目标不一致的参观、旅游等项目。

**Article 864** The State shall improve the public service system for natural protected areas, enhance public service functions, and promote the sharing of the ecological value of natural protected areas by the society.  
In the general control zones of natural protected areas, appropriate areas may be designated and necessary auxiliary facilities and equipment may be installed in accordance with the provisions to provide support for public service activities such as relevant scientific research, science popularization, ecotourism, education, culture, and sports.  
No sightseeing, tourism, or other projects inconsistent with the protection objectives shall be established within natural protected areas.

<a id="art-865"></a>第八百六十五条　国家加强自然保护地生态建设，尊重自然规律，建设生态廊道，开展重要栖息地恢复和废弃地修复。

**Article 865** The State shall strengthen ecological construction in natural protected areas, respect natural laws, build ecological corridors, and carry out restoration of important habitats and remediation of abandoned lands.

<a id="art-866"></a>第八百六十六条　国家公园管理机构组织编制所管理国家公园的总体规划，明确保护和管理的目标任务、保护对象、保护措施等事项，并对拟开展的生态修复活动和原有居民生产生活活动等作出安排。

**Article 866** The management authority of a national park shall organize the preparation of the master plan for the national park under its management, specifying the tasks and objectives for protection and management, protection targets, protection measures, and other matters, and shall make arrangements for the ecological restoration activities to be carried out and the production and living activities of the original residents.

<a id="art-867"></a>第八百六十七条　设立国家公园后，国家公园所在地省、自治区、直辖市人民政府应当及时组织完成国家公园勘界；国家公园管理机构应当根据保护和管理需要及时设置界线标志。  
禁止损毁、涂改、遮挡或者擅自拆除、移动国家公园界线标志。

**Article 867** After the establishment of a national park, the people's government of the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government where the national park is located shall promptly organize the completion of the boundary survey of the national park; the management authority of the national park shall, based on the needs of protection and management, promptly set up boundary markers.  
It shall be prohibited to damage, alter, obscure, or without authorization dismantle or move the boundary markers of a national park.

<a id="art-868"></a>第八百六十八条　对国家公园、自然保护区内自然生态过程、保护对象生息繁衍具有明显季节性变化规律的区域，经科学论证，在不损害生态功能的前提下，可以实行季节性差别管控措施。

**Article 868** For areas within national parks and nature reserves where natural ecological processes and the breeding and reproduction of protected objects have obvious seasonal variation patterns, after scientific demonstration and on the premise of not damaging the ecological functions, seasonal differentiated control measures may be implemented.

<a id="art-869"></a>第八百六十九条　在国家公园区域内开展经营性服务的，可以由国家公园管理机构以竞争性方式选择服务提供者。

**Article 869** Where commercial services are to be carried out within the area of a national park, the management authority of the national park may select service providers through a competitive method.

#### 第二节　长江、黄河、青藏高原等重要流域、区域

<a id="art-870"></a>第八百七十条　长江、黄河、青藏高原等重要流域、区域生态环境保护及相关工作，应当坚持生态优先、绿色发展，因地制宜、分类施策，统筹协调、科学规划、创新驱动、系统治理。

**Article 870** The ecological environmental protection and related work of important river basins and regions such as the Yangtze River, the Yellow River, and the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau shall adhere to ecological priority and green development, adopt measures suited to local conditions and implement policies by category, coordinate and plan scientifically, be driven by innovation, and be governed systematically.

<a id="art-871"></a>第八百七十一条　国家根据需要在长江、黄河、青藏高原等重要流域、区域建立协调机制，统筹指导、综合协调长江、黄河、青藏高原等重要流域、区域的生态环境保护等工作。  
国务院有关部门和长江、黄河、青藏高原等重要流域、区域地方各级人民政府在各自职责范围内做好相关工作。

**Article 871** The State shall, as needed, establish coordination mechanisms in important river basins and regions such as the Yangtze River, the Yellow River, and the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau to provide overall guidance and comprehensive coordination for ecological environmental protection and other work in such important river basins and regions.  
The relevant departments of the State Council and the local people's governments at all levels in important river basins and regions such as the Yangtze River, the Yellow River, and the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau shall carry out relevant work within their respective duties.

<a id="art-872"></a>第八百七十二条　国务院生态环境主管部门会同国务院发展改革、自然资源、水行政、农业农村等有关部门编制长江、黄河等重要流域生态环境保护规划。  
国务院水行政主管部门会同有关部门编制流域综合规划。长江、黄河等重要流域的流域综合规划是水资源保护、开发、利用、节约等的重要依据。

**Article 872** The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, jointly with the relevant departments such as development and reform, natural resources, water administration, and agriculture and rural affairs of the State Council, formulate ecological environmental protection plans for important river basins such as the Yangtze River and the Yellow River.  
The competent department of water administration of the State Council shall, jointly with the relevant departments, formulate comprehensive river basin plans. The comprehensive river basin plans for important river basins such as the Yangtze River and the Yellow River shall serve as important bases for water resources protection, development, utilization, and conservation.

<a id="art-873"></a>第八百七十三条　长江、黄河等重要流域产业结构和布局应当与流域生态环境的承载能力相适应。禁止在流域重点生态功能区布局对生态系统有严重影响的产业。

**Article 873** The industrial structure and layout in important river basins such as the Yangtze River and the Yellow River shall be compatible with the bearing capacity of the ecological environment of the river basin. It shall be prohibited to locate industries that have a serious impact on the ecosystem in key ecological function areas of the river basin.

<a id="art-874"></a>第八百七十四条　国家加强长江、黄河等重要流域源头、重点水域、湖泊、库区消落区、河口生态的保护与修复，保护良好生态功能。

**Article 874** The State shall strengthen the protection and restoration of the ecology in the headwaters, key waters, lakes, drawdown areas of reservoir areas, and estuaries of important river basins such as the Yangtze River and the Yellow River, and protect the good ecological functions.

<a id="art-875"></a>第八百七十五条　国家推动建立长江、黄河等重要流域生态系统水生生物完整性指数评价体系，组织开展水生生物完整性评价，并将结果作为评估生态系统总体状况的重要依据。重要流域水生生物完整性指数应当与水生态环境质量标准相衔接。

**Article 875** The State shall promote the establishment of an evaluation system for the aquatic biological integrity index of ecosystems in important river basins such as the Yangtze River and the Yellow River, organize and carry out aquatic biological integrity assessments, and use the results as an important basis for evaluating the overall condition of the ecosystem. The aquatic biological integrity index of important river basins shall be linked to the water ecological environment quality standards.

<a id="art-876"></a>第八百七十六条　国家对长江流域江河湖泊岸线实施特殊管制，依照《中华人民共和国长江保护法》的规定划定江河湖泊岸线保护范围，制定江河湖泊岸线保护规划，严格控制岸线开发建设，促进岸线合理高效利用。

**Article 876** The State shall implement special control over the shorelines of rivers and lakes in the Yangtze River Basin, delineate the protection scope of the shorelines of rivers and lakes in accordance with the provisions of the Yangtze River Protection Law of the People's Republic of China, formulate protection plans for the shorelines of rivers and lakes, strictly control the development and construction of shorelines, and promote the rational and efficient use of shorelines.

<a id="art-877"></a>第八百七十七条　国家对跨长江流域调水实行科学论证，加强控制和管理。实施跨长江流域调水应当优先保障调出区域及其下游区域的用水安全和生态安全，统筹调出区域和调入区域用水需求。  
丹江口库区及其上游所在地县级以上地方人民政府应当加强山水林田湖草沙整体保护，增强水源涵养能力，保障水质稳定达标。

**Article 877** The State shall carry out scientific demonstration for water diversion across the Yangtze River Basin and strengthen control and management. The implementation of water diversion across the Yangtze River Basin shall give priority to ensuring the water safety and ecological safety of the water-exporting area and its downstream areas, and coordinate the water demand of the water-exporting area and the water-receiving area.  
The local people's governments at or above the county level in the Danjiangkou Reservoir area and its upstream shall strengthen the overall protection of mountains, waters, forests, farmlands, lakes, grasslands, and deserts, enhance water conservation capacity, and ensure the stable compliance of water quality with standards.

<a id="art-878"></a>第八百七十八条　黄河流域水资源利用，应当坚持节水优先、统筹兼顾、集约使用、精打细算，优先满足城乡居民生活用水，保障基本生态用水，统筹生产用水。

**Article 878** The utilization of water resources in the Yellow River Basin shall adhere to the principles of prioritizing water conservation, making overall plans, using water intensively, and being frugal, giving priority to meeting the domestic water needs of urban and rural residents, ensuring basic ecological water use, and coordinating production water use.

<a id="art-879"></a>第八百七十九条　国家依照《中华人民共和国黄河保护法》的规定，实行黄河流域水沙统一调度制度。水沙调控应当尽量减少对水生生物及其栖息地的影响。

**Article 879** The State shall, in accordance with the provisions of the Yellow River Protection Law of the People's Republic of China, implement a unified regulation system for water and sediment in the Yellow River Basin. The regulation of water and sediment shall minimize the impact on aquatic organisms and their habitats.

<a id="art-880"></a>第八百八十条　国家加强对黄河流域水土流失重点预防区、治理区、重要支流源头区的水土流失防治和生态脆弱区域保护治理，开展土壤侵蚀和水土流失状况评估，实施重点防治工程。  
禁止在黄河流域水土流失严重、生态脆弱区域开展可能造成水土流失的生产建设活动。确因国家战略和国计民生需要建设的，应当进行科学论证，并依法办理审批手续。

**Article 880** The State shall strengthen the prevention and control of soil erosion in key prevention areas, control areas, and headwater areas of important tributaries in the Yellow River Basin, as well as the protection and management of ecologically fragile areas, carry out assessments of soil erosion and soil and water loss conditions, and implement key prevention and control projects.  
It shall be prohibited to carry out production and construction activities that may cause soil erosion in areas with severe soil erosion or ecologically fragile areas in the Yellow River Basin. Where such activities are truly necessary for national strategies or the national economy and people's livelihood, scientific demonstration shall be conducted, and the approval procedures shall be completed according to law.

<a id="art-881"></a>第八百八十一条　国家加强淮河、海河、珠江、松花江、辽河、洞庭湖、鄱阳湖、太湖、洪泽湖、巢湖等重要江河湖泊治理，巩固提升流域水源涵养、水土保持、生物多样性保护等生态功能，保障流域生态安全。

**Article 881** The State shall strengthen the management of important rivers and lakes, including the Huaihe River, Haihe River, Pearl River, Songhua River, Liaohe River, Dongting Lake, Poyang Lake, Taihu Lake, Hongze Lake, and Chaohu Lake, consolidate and enhance the ecological functions of the basins such as water conservation, soil and water conservation, and biodiversity protection, and ensure the ecological security of the basins.

<a id="art-882"></a>第八百八十二条　国家依照《中华人民共和国青藏高原生态保护法》的规定，加强对青藏高原森林、草原、湿地、江河湖泊、荒漠、雪山冰川、高原冻土、泉域等生态系统的保护，巩固提升青藏高原内国家重点生态功能区的水源涵养、生物多样性保护、水土保持、防风固沙等生态功能。  
国家统筹青藏高原生态安全布局，推进山水林田湖草沙冰综合治理、系统治理、源头治理，实施重要生态系统保护修复重大工程，提升生态系统质量和多样性、稳定性、持续性，增强生态产品供给能力和生态系统服务功能，建设国家生态安全屏障战略地。  
青藏高原产业结构和布局应当与青藏高原生态环境的承载能力相适应。

**Article 882** The State shall, in accordance with the provisions of the Ecological Protection Law of the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau of the People's Republic of China, strengthen the protection of ecosystems on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, including forests, grasslands, wetlands, rivers and lakes, deserts, snowy mountains and glaciers, plateau permafrost, and spring areas, consolidate and enhance the ecological functions of the national key ecological function zones within the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, such as water conservation, biodiversity protection, soil and water conservation, and wind prevention and sand fixation.  
The State shall coordinate the layout of ecological security on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, promote comprehensive, systematic, and source-based management of mountains, waters, forests, farmlands, lakes, grasslands, deserts, and ice, carry out major projects for the protection and restoration of important ecosystems, improve the quality, diversity, stability, and sustainability of ecosystems, enhance the capacity to provide ecological products and ecosystem service functions, and build a strategic area for the national ecological security barrier.  
The industrial structure and layout on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau shall be compatible with the carrying capacity of the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau's ecological environment.

<a id="art-883"></a>第八百八十三条　国家建立健全青藏高原生态风险防控体系，采取有效措施提高防治自然灾害、应对气候变化等生态风险防控能力和水平，保障青藏高原生态安全。  
国务院有关部门和青藏高原县级以上地方人民政府及其有关部门应当加强对青藏高原建设、文化旅游、山地户外运动等活动的监督管理，切实维护青藏高原生态安全。

**Article 883** The State shall establish and improve an ecological risk prevention and control system for the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, take effective measures to improve the capacity and level of ecological risk prevention and control, including preventing and controlling natural disasters and responding to climate change, and ensure the ecological security of the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau.  
The relevant departments of the State Council and the local people's governments at or above the county level on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau and their relevant departments shall strengthen the supervision and management of activities such as construction, cultural tourism, and mountain outdoor sports on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, and effectively maintain the ecological security of the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau.

<a id="art-884"></a>第八百八十四条　国家建立健全青藏高原雪山冰川冻土保护制度，加强对雪山冰川冻土的监测预警和系统保护，对重要雪山冰川实施封禁保护，采取有效措施，严格控制人为扰动。  
国家建立健全青藏高原江河湖泊管理和保护制度，严格保护青藏高原大江大河源头等重要生态区位的天然草原，全面加强青藏高原天然林保护，加强高原湿地生态保护、泥炭资源保护。  
国家对青藏高原珍贵、濒危或者特有野生动植物物种实行重点保护。  
国家加强对青藏高原退化草原、退化湿地、沙化土地治理和水土流失防治的力度，综合整治重度退化土地，加强对重要江河源头区和水土流失重点预防区、治理区以及人口相对密集高原河谷区的水土流失防治。

**Article 884** The State shall establish and improve a protection system for snowy mountains, glaciers, and permafrost on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, strengthen monitoring, early warning, and systematic protection of snowy mountains, glaciers, and permafrost, implement closed protection for important snowy mountains and glaciers, and take effective measures to strictly control man-made disturbances.  
The State shall establish and improve a management and protection system for rivers and lakes on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, strictly protect natural grasslands in important ecological locations such as the sources of major rivers on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, comprehensively strengthen the protection of natural forests on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, and strengthen the ecological protection of plateau wetlands and the protection of peat resources.  
The State shall provide priority protection to rare, endangered, or endemic wild animal and plant species on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau.  
The State shall strengthen efforts in the treatment of degraded grasslands, degraded wetlands, desertified lands, and the prevention and control of soil erosion on the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, comprehensively improve severely degraded lands, and strengthen the prevention and control of soil erosion in important river source areas, key prevention and treatment areas for soil erosion, and relatively densely populated plateau river valley areas.

<a id="art-885"></a>第八百八十五条　海南岛全岛坚持生态优先、绿色发展，创新生态文明体制机制，持续优化生态环境质量和资源利用效率，加强生态环境损害责任追究。

**Article 885** The entire island of Hainan shall adhere to ecological priority and green development, innovate the systems and mechanisms for ecological civilization, continuously optimize the quality of the ecological environment and the efficiency of resource utilization, and strengthen the accountability for ecological and environmental damage.

<a id="art-886"></a>第八百八十六条　国家加强秦岭地区生态的保护与修复，巩固提升秦岭在气候调节、水土保持、水源涵养和生物多样性保护等方面的生态功能。

**Article 886** The State shall strengthen the protection and restoration of the ecology in the Qinling Mountains region, consolidate and enhance the ecological functions of the Qinling Mountains in climate regulation, soil and water conservation, water conservation, and biodiversity protection.

<a id="art-887"></a>第八百八十七条　国务院建立加强荒漠化综合防治和推进“三北”等重点生态工程建设协调机制，统筹协调解决重大问题，推动重点生态工程建设，推进重大政策实施。  
国家加强“三北”工程建设区生态系统保护和修复，实行目标责任制，定期开展建设成效监测评估，保护和巩固工程建设成果。

**Article 887** The State Council shall establish a coordination mechanism for strengthening comprehensive desertification control and promoting the construction of key ecological projects such as the "Three-North" Shelterbelt Forest Program, coordinate and solve major issues, promote the construction of key ecological projects, and advance the implementation of major policies.  
The State shall strengthen the protection and restoration of ecosystems in the "Three-North" project construction areas, implement a target responsibility system, regularly monitor and evaluate the effectiveness of the construction, and protect and consolidate the achievements of the project construction.

### 第六章　生态退化的预防和治理

#### 第一节　水土保持

<a id="art-888"></a>第八百八十八条　本法所称水土保持，是指对自然因素和人为活动造成水土流失所采取的预防和治理措施。

**Article 888** For the purposes of this Law, "soil and water conservation" means the prevention and control measures taken against soil erosion caused by natural factors and human activities.

<a id="art-889"></a>第八百八十九条　水土保持工作实行预防为主、保护优先、全面规划、综合治理、因地制宜、突出重点、科学管理、注重效益的方针。

**Article 889** The policy of soil and water conservation work shall be: prevention first, protection priority, comprehensive planning, integrated management, adaptation to local conditions, highlighting priorities, scientific management, and focusing on benefits.

<a id="art-890"></a>第八百九十条　国务院水行政主管部门主管全国的水土保持工作。  
国务院水行政主管部门在国家确定的重要江河湖泊设立的流域管理机构，在所管辖范围内依法承担水土保持监督管理职责。  
县级以上地方人民政府水行政主管部门主管本行政区域的水土保持工作。  
县级以上人民政府林业草原、农业农村、自然资源、生态环境等有关部门在各自职责范围内做好水土流失预防和治理相关工作。

**Article 890** The water administrative department of the State Council shall be in charge of soil and water conservation work nationwide.  
The river basin management organs established by the water administrative department of the State Council for important rivers and lakes designated by the State shall, within their respective jurisdictions, assume the duties of supervision and management of soil and water conservation according to law.  
The water administrative departments of the local people's governments at or above the county level shall be in charge of soil and water conservation work within their respective administrative regions.  
The relevant departments of forestry and grassland, agriculture and rural affairs, natural resources, and ecological environment under the people's governments at or above the county level shall, within their respective functions, carry out relevant work on the prevention and control of soil erosion.

<a id="art-891"></a>第八百九十一条　国家建立健全水土保持相关标准。国务院水行政主管部门、标准化主管部门组织制定并完善水土流失综合治理、生产建设项目水土保持、水土流失监测等国家标准。国家标准未作规定的，可以依法制定地方标准或者行业标准。

**Article 891** The State shall establish and improve relevant standards for soil and water conservation. The water administrative department and the standardization administrative department of the State Council shall organize the formulation and improvement of national standards for comprehensive treatment of soil erosion, soil and water conservation for production and construction projects, and monitoring of soil erosion. Where national standards are not provided, local standards or industry standards may be formulated according to law.

<a id="art-892"></a>第八百九十二条　国家在水土流失调查结果及水土流失重点预防区和重点治理区划定的基础上，编制水土保持规划。  
有关基础设施建设、矿产资源开发、城镇建设、公共服务设施建设等方面的规划，以及重大产业和工程布局等实施方案，在实施过程中可能造成水土流失的，规划的组织编制机关应当在规划中提出水土流失预防和治理的对策和措施，并在规划报请审批前征求本级人民政府水行政主管部门的意见。

**Article 892** The State shall, on the basis of the results of soil erosion surveys and the delimitation of key prevention areas and key treatment areas for soil erosion, formulate soil and water conservation plans.  
Where any plan concerning infrastructure construction, mineral resources development, urban construction, or public service facility construction, or any implementation plan for major industries and project layouts, may cause soil erosion during implementation, the organ organizing the formulation of the plan shall propose countermeasures and measures for the prevention and control of soil erosion in the plan, and shall seek the opinions of the water administrative department of the people's government at the same level before submitting the plan for approval.

<a id="art-893"></a>第八百九十三条　县级以上人民政府水行政主管部门应当加强水土保持监测工作，完善水土保持监测网络，定期组织水土流失调查并公告调查结果，发挥水土保持监测工作在政府决策、经济社会发展和社会公众服务中的作用。

**Article 893** The water administrative department of the people's government at or above the county level shall strengthen soil and water conservation monitoring work, improve the soil and water conservation monitoring network, regularly organize soil erosion surveys and announce the survey results, and give play to the role of soil and water conservation monitoring work in government decision-making, economic and social development, and public services.

<a id="art-894"></a>第八百九十四条　地方各级人民政府应当按照水土保持规划，采取封育保护、自然修复、重点治理等措施，组织单位和个人植树种草，扩大林草覆盖面积，涵养水源，加强对取土、挖砂、采石等活动的管理，预防和减轻水土流失。

**Article 894** Local people's governments at all levels shall, in accordance with soil and water conservation plans, take measures such as closed protection, natural restoration, and focused treatment, organize units and individuals to plant trees and grass, expand the coverage of forests and grass, conserve water sources, strengthen the management of activities such as soil extraction, sand digging, and quarrying, and prevent and mitigate soil erosion.

<a id="art-895"></a>第八百九十五条　国家按照国土空间规划和用途管控要求，建立水土保持空间管控制度，落实差别化保护治理措施。  
禁止在崩塌、滑坡危险区和泥石流易发区从事取土、挖砂、采石等可能造成水土流失的活动。  
限制或者禁止在水土流失严重、生态脆弱区域开展可能造成水土流失的生产建设活动。确因国家发展战略和国计民生需要建设的，应当进行科学论证，并依照《中华人民共和国水土保持法》的规定办理审批手续。  
在侵蚀沟的沟坡和沟岸、河流的两岸以及湖泊和水库的周边，土地所有权人、使用权人或者有关管理单位应当营造植物保护带。禁止开垦、开发植物保护带。  
禁止在二十五度以上陡坡地开垦种植农作物。在二十五度以上陡坡地种植经济林的，应当科学选择树种，合理确定规模，采取水土保持措施，防止造成水土流失。  
禁止毁林、毁草开垦和采集发菜。禁止在水土流失重点预防区和重点治理区铲草皮、挖树兜或者滥挖虫草、甘草、麻黄等。

**Article 895** The State shall, in accordance with the requirements of territorial space planning and use control, establish a spatial control system for soil and water conservation and implement differentiated protection and treatment measures.  
It shall be prohibited to engage in activities such as soil extraction, sand digging, and quarrying that may cause soil erosion in areas prone to collapse, landslides, and debris flows.  
Production and construction activities that may cause soil erosion in areas with severe soil erosion and fragile ecology shall be restricted or prohibited. Where construction is truly necessary for national development strategies and the national economy and people's livelihood, scientific demonstration shall be carried out, and the approval procedures shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of the Law of the People's Republic of China on Water and Soil Erosion Control.  
On the slopes and banks of erosion gullies, on both banks of rivers, and around lakes and reservoirs, the land owners, land users, or relevant management entities shall create plant protection belts. It shall be prohibited to reclaim or develop plant protection belts.  
It shall be prohibited to reclaim and plant crops on steep slopes with a gradient of more than 25 degrees. Where economic forests are planted on steep slopes with a gradient of more than 25 degrees, tree species shall be scientifically selected, the scale shall be reasonably determined, and soil and water conservation measures shall be taken to prevent soil erosion.  
It shall be prohibited to destroy forests or grasslands for reclamation and to collect facai (a kind of lichen). It shall be prohibited to strip turf, dig tree stumps, or indiscriminately dig for Cordyceps sinensis, licorice, ephedra, etc., in key prevention areas and key treatment areas of soil erosion.

<a id="art-896"></a>第八百九十六条　林木采伐应当采用合理方式，严格控制皆伐；对水源涵养林、水土保持林、防风固沙林等防护林只能进行抚育和更新性质的采伐；对采伐区和集材道应当采取防止水土流失的措施，并在采伐后及时更新造林。

**Article 896** The cutting of forest trees shall be carried out in a reasonable manner, with strict control over clear-cutting; for shelter forests such as water conservation forests, soil conservation forests, and windbreak and sand-fixation forests, only tending and regeneration cutting shall be allowed; measures to prevent soil erosion shall be taken in cutting areas and along skidding roads, and reforestation shall be carried out in a timely manner after cutting.

<a id="art-897"></a>第八百九十七条　县级以上人民政府水行政主管部门应当加强对地表扰动、土石方开挖等人为活动的管理，严格管控因人为活动造成的水土流失。  
在山区、丘陵区、风沙区以及水土保持规划确定的容易发生水土流失的其他区域开办可能造成水土流失的生产建设项目，生产建设单位应当编制水土保持方案，报县级以上人民政府水行政主管部门审批，并按照经批准的水土保持方案，采取水土流失预防和治理措施。  
未纳入水土保持方案管理的生产建设活动，依照生产建设活动水土流失防治标准进行水土流失防治。

**Article 897** The water administrative department of the people's government at or above the county level shall strengthen the management of man-made activities such as surface disturbance and earthwork excavation, and strictly control soil erosion caused by man-made activities.  
For any production and construction project that may cause soil erosion and is to be established in mountainous, hilly, sandy, or other areas prone to soil erosion as determined by soil and water conservation plans, the project entity shall prepare a soil and water conservation plan, submit it to the water administrative department of the people's government at or above the county level for approval, and take measures to prevent and control soil erosion in accordance with the approved soil and water conservation plan.  
For production and construction activities not included in the management of soil and water conservation plans, soil erosion prevention and control shall be carried out in accordance with the standards for prevention and control of soil erosion for production and construction activities.

<a id="art-898"></a>第八百九十八条　依法应当编制水土保持方案的生产建设项目中的水土保持设施，应当与主体工程同时设计、同时施工、同时投产使用；生产建设项目竣工验收，应当验收水土保持设施；水土保持设施未经验收或者验收不合格的，生产建设项目不得投产使用。  
在山区、丘陵区、风沙区以及水土保持规划确定的容易发生水土流失的其他区域开办生产建设项目或者从事其他生产建设活动，损坏水土保持设施、地貌植被，不能恢复原有水土保持功能的，应当缴纳水土保持补偿费，专项用于水土流失预防和治理。

**Article 898** The soil and water conservation facilities in a production and construction project that is required by law to prepare a soil and water conservation plan shall be designed, constructed, and put into operation simultaneously with the main project; upon completion and acceptance of the production and construction project, the soil and water conservation facilities shall be inspected and accepted; if the soil and water conservation facilities have not been inspected and accepted or have failed the inspection, the production and construction project shall not be put into operation.  
Where a production and construction project is established or other production and construction activities are carried out in mountainous, hilly, sandy, or other areas prone to soil erosion as determined by soil and water conservation plans, and soil and water conservation facilities or landforms and vegetation are damaged and the original soil and water conservation functions cannot be restored, the project entity or the party engaged in the activities shall pay a soil and water conservation compensation fee, which shall be used exclusively for the prevention and control of soil erosion.

<a id="art-899"></a>第八百九十九条　国家加强水土流失重点预防区和重点治理区的小流域综合治理提质增效、坡耕地改梯田、淤地坝、侵蚀沟治理、崩岗治理、固沟保塬等水土保持重点工程建设，加大生态修复力度。

**Article 899** The State shall strengthen the construction of key soil and water conservation projects in key prevention areas and key treatment areas of soil erosion, including improving the quality and efficiency of comprehensive small watershed management, converting sloping farmland into terraced fields, building check dams for sediment retention, managing erosion gullies, controlling collapsing hills, and stabilizing gullies and protecting tablelands, and increase the intensity of ecological restoration.

<a id="art-900"></a>第九百条　国家鼓励、支持承包治理荒山、荒沟、荒丘、荒滩，防治水土流失，保护和改善生态环境，促进土地资源的合理开发和可持续利用。  
承包治理荒山、荒沟、荒丘、荒滩和承包水土流失严重地区农村土地的，在依法签订的土地承包合同中应当包括预防和治理水土流失责任的内容。

**Article 900** The State shall encourage and support the contracting for the management of barren hills, barren gullies, barren mounds, and barren beaches, to prevent and control soil erosion, protect and improve the ecological environment, and promote the rational development and sustainable use of land resources.  
Where a person contracts for the management of barren hills, barren gullies, barren mounds, barren beaches, or contracts for rural land in areas with severe soil erosion, the land contract concluded according to law shall include the content of responsibilities for preventing and controlling soil erosion.

<a id="art-901"></a>第九百零一条　在水力侵蚀地区，以流域水系为单元一体化推进小流域综合治理，开展坡耕地和沟道水土流失综合整治，因地制宜采取工程措施和植物措施，加强坡耕地改梯田、淤地坝等工程建设，统筹推进保护性耕作和高标准农田建设，完善田间道路、坡面水系等配套设施；建设生态清洁小流域，提供更多更优蕴含水土保持功能的生态产品。  
在风力侵蚀地区，地方人民政府及其有关部门应当组织单位和个人，因地制宜采取轮封轮牧、植树种草、设置人工沙障和网格林带等措施，建立防风固沙防护体系。  
在重力侵蚀地区，地方人民政府及其有关部门应当组织单位和个人，采取径流排导、削坡减载、支挡固坡、修建拦挡工程等措施，建立监测预报预警体系。

**Article 901** : In areas subject to water erosion, small watershed comprehensive management shall be promoted in an integrated manner with the watershed as a unit, and comprehensive remediation of soil erosion on slope farmland and in gullies shall be carried out. Engineering measures and plant measures shall be adopted according to local conditions, and the construction of projects such as converting slope farmland to terraces and silt-arresting dams shall be strengthened. Conservation tillage and high-standard farmland construction shall be promoted in a coordinated manner, and supporting facilities such as field roads and slope water systems shall be improved. Ecologically clean small watersheds shall be built to provide more and better ecological products containing soil and water conservation functions.  
In areas subject to wind erosion, local people's governments and the relevant departments thereof shall organize entities and individuals to adopt measures such as rotational fencing and grazing, tree planting and grass seeding, setting up artificial sand barriers and grid forest belts according to local conditions, and establish a windbreak and sand-fixing protection system.  
In areas subject to gravity erosion, local people's governments and the relevant departments thereof shall organize entities and individuals to adopt measures such as runoff drainage, cutting slopes to reduce loads, supporting and stabilizing slopes, and building retaining works, and establish a monitoring, forecasting and early warning system.

<a id="art-902"></a>第九百零二条　在饮用水水源保护区，地方人民政府及其有关部门应当组织单位和个人，采取预防保护、自然修复和综合治理措施，配套建设植物过滤带，开展生态清洁小流域建设，严格控制化肥和农药的使用，减少水土流失引起的面源污染，保护饮用水水源。

**Article 902** : In drinking water source protection zones, local people's governments and the relevant departments thereof shall organize entities and individuals to adopt measures for prevention, protection, natural restoration and comprehensive management, build supporting plant filter strips, carry out the construction of ecologically clean small watersheds, strictly control the use of chemical fertilizers and pesticides, reduce non-point source pollution caused by soil erosion, and protect drinking water sources.

<a id="art-903"></a>第九百零三条　已在禁止开垦的陡坡地上开垦种植农作物的，应当按照国家规定进行生态修复；耕地短缺、退耕确有困难的，应当修建梯田或者采取其他水土保持措施。  
在禁止开垦坡度以下的坡耕地上开垦种植农作物的，应当根据不同情况，采取修建梯田、坡面水系整治、蓄水保土耕作或者退耕等水土保持措施。

**Article 903** : Where crops have been cultivated on steep slopes where reclamation is prohibited, ecological restoration shall be carried out in accordance with state regulations; if there is a shortage of arable land and it is truly difficult to return the farmland to forests or grasslands, terraces shall be built or other soil and water conservation measures shall be adopted.  
Where crops are cultivated on sloping farmland with a gradient below the prohibited reclamation gradient, soil and water conservation measures such as building terraces, regulating slope water systems, conservation tillage for water and soil retention, or returning farmland shall be adopted according to different circumstances.

<a id="art-904"></a>第九百零四条　对生产建设活动所占用土地的地表土应当进行分层剥离、保存和利用，做到土石方挖填平衡，减少地表扰动范围。依法应当编制水土保持方案的生产建设项目，应当综合利用其排弃的砂、石、土、矸石、尾矿、废渣等；确需废弃的，应当堆放在水土保持方案确定的存放地，并采取拦挡、坡面防护、防洪排导等措施。生产建设活动结束后，应当及时在取土场、开挖面和存放地的裸露土地上植树种草、恢复植被。  
在干旱缺水地区从事生产建设活动，应当采取防止风力侵蚀措施，设置降水蓄渗设施，充分利用降水资源。

**Article 904** : The topsoil on land occupied by production and construction activities shall be stripped, preserved and utilized in layers, so as to achieve a balance between earth and rock excavation and backfilling, and reduce the scope of surface disturbance. For production and construction projects that are required by law to prepare soil and water conservation plans, the sand, stone, soil, gangue, tailings, waste residues, etc. discharged by them shall be comprehensively utilized; if they indeed need to be discarded, they shall be stockpiled in the sites designated in the soil and water conservation plan, and measures such as retaining, slope protection, flood control and drainage shall be adopted. After the completion of production and construction activities, trees and grass shall be planted in a timely manner on the bare land of borrow pits, excavation faces and stockpile sites to restore vegetation.  
Where production and construction activities are carried out in arid and water-deficient areas, measures shall be taken to prevent wind erosion, rainwater storage and infiltration facilities shall be installed, and rainwater resources shall be fully utilized.

<a id="art-905"></a>第九百零五条　国家鼓励、支持在山区、丘陵区、风沙区以及容易发生水土流失的其他区域，采取下列有利于水土保持的措施：  
（一）免耕、等高耕作、轮耕轮作、草田轮作、间作套种等；  
（二）封禁抚育、轮封轮牧、舍饲圈养；  
（三）发展生物质能，利用太阳能、风能和水能，以煤、电、气代替薪柴等；  
（四）从生态脆弱地区向外移民；  
（五）其他有利于水土保持的措施。

**Article 905** : The State encourages and supports the adoption of the following measures conducive to soil and water conservation in mountainous areas, hilly areas, wind-sand areas and other areas prone to soil erosion:  
(1) no-tillage, contour farming, rotational tillage and cropping, grass-crop rotation, intercropping and interplanting, etc.;  
(2) closure and tending, rotational enclosure and grazing, and stall feeding and pen raising;  
(3) developing biomass energy, utilizing solar energy, wind energy and hydro energy, replacing firewood with coal, electricity and gas, etc.;  
(4) relocating people outward from ecologically fragile areas; and  
(5) other measures conducive to soil and water conservation.

#### 第二节　防沙治沙

<a id="art-906"></a>第九百零六条　本法所称土地沙化，是指主要因人类不合理活动所导致的天然沙漠扩张和沙质土壤上植被及覆盖物被破坏，形成流沙及沙土裸露的过程。  
本法所称沙化土地，包括已经沙化的土地和具有明显沙化趋势的土地。

**Article 906** : For the purposes of this Law, "land desertification" refers to the process of natural desert expansion and the destruction of vegetation and cover on sandy soil, resulting in the formation of shifting sand and exposed sandy soil, which is mainly caused by irrational human activities.  
For the purposes of this Law, "desertified land" includes land that has already become desertified and land with an obvious trend of desertification.

<a id="art-907"></a>第九百零七条　防沙治沙坚持预防为主、防治结合、合理利用、统一规划、因地制宜、因害设防、分类施策的原则。

**Article 907** : Desert prevention and control shall adhere to the principles of giving priority to prevention, combining prevention with control, rational utilization, unified planning, adapting measures to local conditions, taking measures according to the hazards, and implementing categorized policies.

<a id="art-908"></a>第九百零八条　国务院林业草原主管部门负责组织、协调、指导全国防沙治沙工作。  
国务院林业草原、农业农村、水行政、自然资源、生态环境、气象等有关部门在各自职责范围内共同做好防沙治沙工作。  
县级以上地方人民政府组织、领导有关部门密切配合共同做好本行政区域的防沙治沙工作。

**Article 908** : The competent department of forestry and grassland under the State Council shall be responsible for organizing, coordinating and guiding the national desert prevention and control work.  
The relevant departments of the State Council, including those of forestry and grassland, agriculture and rural affairs, water administration, natural resources, ecological environment, and meteorology, shall, within their respective functions and duties, jointly carry out the desert prevention and control work.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall organize and lead the relevant departments to closely coordinate and jointly carry out the desert prevention and control work within their respective administrative regions.

<a id="art-909"></a>第九百零九条　国务院林业草原主管部门会同有关部门依照《中华人民共和国防沙治沙法》的规定编制全国防沙治沙规划。县级以上地方人民政府可以根据实际需要，组织编制本行政区域的防沙治沙规划。

**Article 909** : The competent department of forestry and grassland under the State Council shall, in conjunction with the relevant departments, formulate a national desert prevention and control plan in accordance with the provisions of the Law of the People's Republic of China on Desert Prevention and Control. Local people's governments at or above the county level may, based on actual needs, organize the formulation of desert prevention and control plans for their respective administrative regions.

<a id="art-910"></a>第九百一十条　国家加强荒漠化综合防治，积极推进防沙治沙，重点开展沙漠边缘、沙源区和路径区生态修复，加强自然生态空间管控以及沙生植物保护，推行保护性耕作措施，实施防护林建设等生态工程，在岩溶地区开展石漠化综合治理。

**Article 910** : The State shall strengthen comprehensive desertification prevention and control, actively promote desert prevention and control, and focus on carrying out ecological restoration in desert margins, sand source areas and path areas, strengthen the control of natural ecological spaces and the protection of sand-growing plants, promote conservation tillage measures, implement ecological projects such as shelterbelt construction, and carry out comprehensive stony desertification control in karst areas.

<a id="art-911"></a>第九百一十一条　沙化土地所在地区的县级以上地方人民政府应当按照防沙治沙规划，划出一定比例的土地，因地制宜营造防风固沙林网、林带，种植多年生灌木和草本植物。  
除了抚育更新性质的采伐外，不得批准对防风固沙林网、林带进行采伐。在对防风固沙林网、林带进行抚育更新性质的采伐之前，应当在其附近预先形成接替林网和林带。对林木更新困难地区已有的防风固沙林网、林带，不得批准采伐。

**Article 911** : Local people's governments at or above the county level in areas where desertified land is located shall, in accordance with the desert prevention and control plan, designate a certain proportion of land to plant windbreak and sand-fixing forest networks and belts and perennial shrubs and herbaceous plants according to local conditions.  
Except for felling for the purpose of tending and regeneration, no felling of windbreak and sand-fixing forest networks and belts shall be approved. Before felling for the purpose of tending and regeneration of windbreak and sand-fixing forest networks and belts, replacement forest networks and belts shall be established in advance in the vicinity. For existing windbreak and sand-fixing forest networks and belts in areas where tree regeneration is difficult, no felling shall be approved.

<a id="art-912"></a>第九百一十二条　禁止在沙化土地上砍挖灌木、药材及其他固沙植物，法律、行政法规另有规定的除外。  
沙化土地所在地区的县级人民政府，应当建立植被管护制度，完善管护组织体系，建设管护设施，严格保护植被。

**Article 912** : It is prohibited to cut or dig up shrubs, medicinal herbs and other sand-fixing plants on desertified land, unless otherwise provided by laws and administrative regulations.  
The people's governments at the county level in areas where desertified land is located shall establish a vegetation management and protection system, improve the management and protection organization system, construct management and protection facilities, and strictly protect vegetation.

<a id="art-913"></a>第九百一十三条　沙化土地所在地区的县级以上地方人民政府水行政主管部门，应当加强流域和区域水资源的统一调配和管理，防止因地下水和上游水资源的过度开发利用，导致植被破坏和土地沙化。  
沙化土地所在地区的县级以上地方人民政府，不得批准在沙漠边缘地带和林地、草原开垦耕地；已经开垦并对生态产生不良影响的，应当有计划地组织退耕还林还草。

**Article 913** : The water administrative departments of local people's governments at or above the county level in areas where desertified land is located shall strengthen the unified allocation and management of water resources in river basins and regions to prevent vegetation destruction and land desertification caused by over-exploitation and utilization of groundwater and upstream water resources.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level in areas where desertified land is located shall not approve the reclamation of arable land in desert fringe areas, forest land, or grasslands; where such reclamation has already been carried out and has had adverse effects on the ecology, a planned return of farmland to forests or grasslands shall be organized.

<a id="art-914"></a>第九百一十四条　国家建立沙化土地封禁保护区制度。对暂不具备治理条件的连片沙化土地，应当规划为沙化土地封禁保护区。  
在沙化土地封禁保护区范围内，禁止破坏植被的活动。禁止在沙化土地封禁保护区范围内安置移民。未经批准，不得在沙化土地封禁保护区范围进行修建铁路、公路等建设活动。

**Article 914** : The State shall establish a system of enclosed protection areas for desertified land. For contiguous desertified land that does not yet have the conditions for treatment, it shall be designated as enclosed protection areas for desertified land.  
Within the enclosed protection areas for desertified land, activities that damage vegetation are prohibited. It is prohibited to resettle people within the enclosed protection areas for desertified land. Without approval, construction activities such as building railways and highways shall not be carried out within the enclosed protection areas for desertified land.

<a id="art-915"></a>第九百一十五条　沙化土地所在地区的地方各级人民政府，应当按照防沙治沙规划，组织有关部门、单位和个人，因地制宜采取工程措施、生物措施和合理调配生态用水等措施，恢复和增加植被，防止土地沙化，治理已经沙化的土地。

**Article 915** : Local people's governments at all levels in areas where desertified land is located shall, in accordance with the desert prevention and control plan, organize relevant departments, entities and individuals to adopt measures such as engineering measures, biological measures and rational allocation of ecological water according to local conditions, to restore and increase vegetation, prevent land desertification, and treat already desertified land.

<a id="art-916"></a>第九百一十六条　国家鼓励单位和个人在自愿的前提下，捐资或者以其他形式开展公益性的治沙活动。

**Article 916** : The State encourages entities and individuals, on a voluntary basis, to donate funds or carry out public welfare sand control activities in other forms.

<a id="art-917"></a>第九百一十七条　使用已经沙化的国有土地的使用权人和农民集体所有土地的承包经营权人，应当采取治理措施，改善土地质量。  
采取退耕还林还草、植树种草或者封育措施治沙的土地使用权人和承包经营权人，按照国家规定享受政策优惠。

**Article 917** : The right holders of use of desertified state-owned land and the holders of contractual management rights of land owned by peasant collectives shall take treatment measures to improve the quality of the land.  
The right holders of use of land and the holders of contractual management rights who adopt measures such as returning farmland to forests or grasslands, planting trees and grass, or enclosure for sand control shall enjoy policy preferences in accordance with state regulations.

<a id="art-918"></a>第九百一十八条　在沙化土地范围内从事种植、养殖、旅游、风电、光伏发电等生产经营活动，应当采取措施防止土地沙化。

**Article 918** : Where production and operation activities such as planting, breeding, tourism, wind power generation, and photovoltaic power generation are carried out within the scope of desertified land, measures shall be taken to prevent land desertification.

<a id="art-919"></a>第九百一十九条　沙化土地所在地区的地方各级人民政府，可以组织当地农村集体经济组织及其成员在自愿的前提下，对已经沙化的土地进行集中治理。

**Article 919** : Local people's governments at all levels in areas where desertified land is located may organize local rural collective economic organizations and their members, on a voluntary basis, to conduct centralized treatment of already desertified land.

### 第七章　生态修复

<a id="art-920"></a>第九百二十条　各级人民政府及有关部门开展生态修复活动，以及依法承担生态修复责任的单位和个人开展生态修复活动，应当遵守本章的有关规定。

**Article 920** : Where people's governments at all levels and relevant departments carry out ecological restoration activities, and where entities and individuals that bear the responsibility for ecological restoration according to law carry out ecological restoration activities, they shall comply with the relevant provisions of this Chapter.

<a id="art-921"></a>第九百二十一条　生态修复应当建立健全源头保护和全过程修复治理相结合的工作机制，实现整体保护、系统修复、综合治理。

**Article 921** For ecological restoration, a working mechanism combining source protection with whole-process restoration and management shall be established and improved to achieve overall protection, systematic restoration, and comprehensive management.

<a id="art-922"></a>第九百二十二条　国务院自然资源主管部门应当会同有关部门组织实施重大生态修复项目，统筹重点流域和重要区域生态的保护与修复工作。  
国务院生态环境主管部门在其职责范围内指导协调和监督生态的保护与修复工作。

**Article 922** The competent department of natural resources under the State Council shall, jointly with relevant departments, organize the implementation of major ecological restoration projects and coordinate the protection and restoration of ecosystems in key river basins and important regions.  
The competent department of ecological environment under the State Council shall, within its functions, guide, coordinate and supervise the protection and restoration of ecosystems.

<a id="art-923"></a>第九百二十三条　县级以上人民政府自然资源主管部门会同有关部门依法编制国土空间生态修复规划。  
国务院有关部门在各自职责范围内组织编制生态修复等相关专项规划。县级以上地方人民政府按照国家规定，在本行政区域组织实施生态修复等相关专项规划。

**Article 923** The competent departments of natural resources of the people's governments at or above the county level shall, jointly with relevant departments, compile territorial spatial ecological restoration plans according to law.  
The relevant departments of the State Council shall, within their respective functions, organize the compilation of relevant special plans such as ecological restoration. The local people's governments at or above the county level shall, in accordance with state regulations, organize the implementation of relevant special plans such as ecological restoration within their respective administrative regions.

<a id="art-924"></a>第九百二十四条　编制生态修复规划、设计项目方案，应当充分征求有关方面的意见、科学论证，确定生态修复的目标、内容、实施方案、修复技术措施等。

**Article 924** When compiling ecological restoration plans and designing project proposals, opinions of relevant parties shall be fully solicited and scientific demonstration shall be conducted to determine the objectives, contents, implementation plans, restoration technical measures, etc. of ecological restoration.

<a id="art-925"></a>第九百二十五条　国务院自然资源、生态环境主管部门会同有关部门对重大生态修复项目的实施开展动态监测。  
地方人民政府有关部门在各自职责范围内对各类生态修复项目的实施开展动态监测。

**Article 925** The competent departments of natural resources and ecological environment under the State Council shall, jointly with relevant departments, conduct dynamic monitoring on the implementation of major ecological restoration projects.  
The relevant departments of local people's governments shall, within their respective functions, conduct dynamic monitoring on the implementation of all types of ecological restoration projects.

<a id="art-926"></a>第九百二十六条　国务院有关部门在各自职责范围内建立健全生态修复实施过程的监督机制，对重大生态修复项目进行跟踪检查。  
地方人民政府有关部门加强对各类生态修复项目的监督，及时组织开展跟踪检查。

**Article 926** The relevant departments of the State Council shall, within their respective functions, establish and improve a supervision mechanism for the implementation process of ecological restoration, and conduct follow-up inspections on major ecological restoration projects.  
The relevant departments of local people's governments shall strengthen supervision over all types of ecological restoration projects, and timely organize follow-up inspections.

<a id="art-927"></a>第九百二十七条　生态修复项目竣工后，县级以上人民政府有关部门在各自职责范围内针对各类生态修复项目的不同特点，组织开展生态修复项目的验收。

**Article 927** After the completion of an ecological restoration project, the relevant departments of the people's governments at or above the county level shall, within their respective functions and in light of the different characteristics of various ecological restoration projects, organize and carry out the acceptance of the ecological restoration project.

<a id="art-928"></a>第九百二十八条　国家实施生态修复项目后期管护制度。  
生态修复项目验收合格后，县级以上人民政府及其有关部门应当确定后期管护责任主体，明确管护内容、管护措施、管护周期和资金来源等。

**Article 928** The state implements a post-project management and protection system for ecological restoration projects.  
After an ecological restoration project is accepted as qualified, the people's governments at or above the county level and their relevant departments shall determine the responsible entities for post-project management and protection, and specify the management and protection contents, measures, cycle, and source of funds, etc.

<a id="art-929"></a>第九百二十九条　国家鼓励、支持生态修复的科学技术研究开发和推广应用，提升生态修复领域自主创新能力，提升生态修复科学化、专业化水平。  
国务院自然资源主管部门会同有关部门制定生态修复相关标准、规范并组织实施。

**Article 929** The state encourages and supports scientific and technological research, development and application of ecological restoration, enhances independent innovation capability in the field of ecological restoration, and improves the scientific and professional level of ecological restoration.  
The competent department of natural resources under the State Council shall, jointly with relevant departments, formulate relevant standards and specifications for ecological restoration and organize their implementation.

<a id="art-930"></a>第九百三十条　国家坚持政策支持、企业和社会参与、市场化运作，采取有效措施鼓励社会资本参与生态修复项目投资、设计、修复、管护等全过程活动。  
国家建立生态修复资金保障制度，支持设立绿色基金、生态专项债券，鼓励社会捐赠，拓宽生态修复资金渠道。

**Article 930** The state adheres to policy support, enterprise and social participation, and market-oriented operation, and takes effective measures to encourage social capital to participate in the whole process of ecological restoration projects including investment, design, restoration, and management and protection.  
The state establishes a fund guarantee system for ecological restoration, supports the establishment of green funds and special ecological bonds, encourages social donations, and broadens funding channels for ecological restoration.

<a id="art-931"></a>第九百三十一条　各级人民政府应当科学保护和修复森林生态系统，加强生态脆弱地区森林生态系统的保护与修复。新造幼林地和其他应当封山育林的地方，由当地人民政府组织封山育林。  
各级人民政府应当对自然因素等导致的荒废和受损山体、退化林地以及宜林荒山荒地荒滩，因地制宜实施森林生态修复工程，恢复植被。

**Article 931** People's governments at all levels shall scientifically protect and restore forest ecosystems and strengthen the protection and restoration of forest ecosystems in ecologically fragile areas. For newly created young forest land and other places where mountain closure for afforestation shall be carried out, the local people's governments shall organize mountain closure for afforestation.  
People's governments at all levels shall, for barren and damaged mountain bodies, degraded forest land, and barren mountains, wasteland and beaches suitable for afforestation caused by natural factors, implement forest ecological restoration projects according to local conditions to restore vegetation.

<a id="art-932"></a>第九百三十二条　国家加强生态脆弱草原保护。对退化、沙化、盐碱化、石漠化和水土流失的草原，地方各级人民政府应当按照草原保护修复利用规划，组织生态的保护与修复，提高生态系统稳定性。

**Article 932** The state strengthens the protection of ecologically fragile grasslands. For grasslands that are degraded, desertified, salinized, stony, or subject to soil erosion, local people's governments at all levels shall, in accordance with the grassland protection, restoration and utilization plan, organize the protection and restoration of ecosystems to improve ecosystem stability.

<a id="art-933"></a>第九百三十三条　县级以上人民政府应当坚持系统治理，加强湿地修复工作，恢复湿地面积，提高湿地生态系统质量。  
县级以上人民政府对破碎化严重或者功能退化的自然湿地进行综合整治和修复，优先修复生态功能严重退化的重要湿地。

**Article 933** People's governments at or above the county level shall adhere to systematic management, strengthen wetland restoration, restore wetland area, and improve the quality of wetland ecosystems.  
People's governments at or above the county level shall carry out comprehensive improvement and restoration of natural wetlands that are severely fragmented or functionally degraded, and give priority to restoring important wetlands with severely degraded ecological functions.

<a id="art-934"></a>第九百三十四条　对遭到破坏的具有重要生态、经济、社会价值的海洋生态系统，应当进行修复。海洋生态修复应当以改善动植物栖息生长环境、恢复生物多样性和生态系统基本功能为重点，以自然恢复为主、人工修复为辅，并优先修复具有典型性、代表性的海洋生态系统。  
国务院自然资源主管部门负责统筹海洋生态修复，牵头组织编制海洋生态修复规划并实施有关海洋生态修复重大工程。编制海洋生态修复规划，应当进行科学论证评估。

**Article 934** Marine ecosystems that are damaged and have important ecological, economic and social values shall be restored. Marine ecological restoration shall focus on improving the habitat and growth environment of animals and plants, restoring biodiversity and basic ecosystem functions, giving priority to natural restoration supplemented by artificial restoration, and giving priority to restoring typical and representative marine ecosystems.  
The competent department of natural resources under the State Council shall be responsible for coordinating marine ecological restoration, taking the lead in organizing the compilation of marine ecological restoration plans and implementing major marine ecological restoration projects. Scientific demonstration and evaluation shall be conducted when compiling marine ecological restoration plans.

<a id="art-935"></a>第九百三十五条　县级以上人民政府应当加强对生态恶化江河湖泊的治理和修复，统筹运用生态补水、生物净化、生态清淤、江河湖泊连通等措施，对江河湖泊生态系统进行综合治理，改善和恢复江河湖泊生态系统。  
入海河口所在地县级以上地方人民政府及其有关部门按照河海联动的要求，制定实施河口生态修复和其他保护措施方案，加强对水、沙、盐、潮滩、生物种群、河口形态的综合监测，采取有效措施防止海水入侵和倒灌，维护河口良好生态功能。

**Article 935** People's governments at or above the county level shall strengthen the management and restoration of rivers and lakes with deteriorating ecosystems, comprehensively use measures such as ecological water replenishment, biological purification, ecological dredging, and river-lake connection, and carry out comprehensive treatment of river and lake ecosystems to improve and restore river and lake ecosystems.  
The local people's governments at or above the county level where the estuaries flow into the sea and their relevant departments shall, in accordance with the requirements of river-sea linkage, formulate and implement plans for estuarine ecological restoration and other protection measures, strengthen comprehensive monitoring of water, sediment, salt, tidal flats, biological populations, and estuarine morphology, take effective measures to prevent seawater intrusion and backflow, and maintain good ecological functions of estuaries.

<a id="art-936"></a>第九百三十六条　开采矿产资源前，采矿权人应当依法编制矿区生态修复方案。采矿权人应当按照经批准的矿区生态修复方案进行矿区生态修复。  
采矿权人应当按照规定提取矿区生态修复费用，专门用于矿区生态修复。

**Article 936** Before mining mineral resources, the mining right holder shall, according to law, compile a mining area ecological restoration plan. The mining right holder shall carry out mining area ecological restoration in accordance with the approved mining area ecological restoration plan.  
The mining right holder shall, according to regulations, set aside fees for mining area ecological restoration to be used exclusively for mining area ecological restoration.

<a id="art-937"></a>第九百三十七条　国家建立健全生态修复成效评估机制，制定和完善生态修复效果的评估标准、技术指南等。  
国务院自然资源、生态环境等主管部门应当在各自职责范围内开展生态修复成效评估。  
县级以上人民政府及其有关部门应当加强生态修复成效评估工作。

**Article 937** The state establishes and improves a mechanism for evaluating the effectiveness of ecological restoration, and formulates and improves evaluation standards, technical guidelines, etc. for the effectiveness of ecological restoration.  
The competent departments of natural resources, ecological environment, etc. under the State Council shall, within their respective functions, carry out evaluation of the effectiveness of ecological restoration.  
People's governments at or above the county level and their relevant departments shall strengthen the evaluation of the effectiveness of ecological restoration.

## 第四编　绿色低碳发展

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-938"></a>第九百三十八条　本编适用于发展循环经济、能源节约与绿色低碳转型、应对气候变化等绿色低碳发展相关活动。

**Article 938** This Part applies to activities related to green and low-carbon development such as developing a circular economy, energy conservation and green and low-carbon transformation, and addressing climate change.

<a id="art-939"></a>第九百三十九条　国家建立健全绿色低碳发展机制，完善绿色低碳转型政策体系，加快经济社会发展全面绿色转型，形成有利于节约资源和保护生态环境的空间格局、产业结构、生产方式、生活方式。

**Article 939** The state establishes and improves a mechanism for green and low-carbon development, improves the policy system for green and low-carbon transformation, accelerates the comprehensive green transformation of economic and social development, and forms a spatial pattern, industrial structure, production mode, and lifestyle that are conducive to resource conservation and ecological environmental protection.

<a id="art-940"></a>第九百四十条　国家采取有效措施推动各类资源节约集约循环利用，促进生产、流通、消费过程中减少污染物和温室气体的产生、排放，提高资源利用效率，建设绿色低碳循环发展经济体系。

**Article 940** The state takes effective measures to promote intensive, economical and recycling utilization of various resources, promote the reduction of generation and emission of pollutants and greenhouse gases in the processes of production, circulation and consumption, improve resource utilization efficiency, and build a green, low-carbon and circular development economic system.

<a id="art-941"></a>第九百四十一条　国家推进节能降碳增效，加强化石能源清洁高效利用和安全可靠有序替代，大力发展非化石能源，稳妥推进能源绿色低碳转型，加快建设清洁低碳安全高效的新型能源体系，建设能源强国。

**Article 941** The State shall promote energy conservation, carbon reduction, and efficiency improvement; strengthen the clean, efficient utilization and safe, reliable, orderly replacement of fossil energy; vigorously develop non-fossil energy; prudently advance the green and low-carbon transformation of energy; accelerate the construction of a new, clean, low-carbon, safe, and efficient energy system; and build a strong energy nation.

<a id="art-942"></a>第九百四十二条　国家采取积极应对气候变化的政策和措施，控制和减少温室气体排放，积极稳妥推进碳达峰碳中和，增强适应气候变化能力，提高全社会的应对气候变化意识。

**Article 942** The State shall adopt active policies and measures to address climate change, control and reduce greenhouse gas emissions, actively and prudently promote the achievement of peak carbon and carbon neutrality, enhance the capacity to adapt to climate change, and raise the awareness of the whole society in addressing climate change.

<a id="art-943"></a>第九百四十三条　国家强化区域重大战略实施中的绿色低碳发展导向和任务要求，构建绿色低碳高质量发展空间格局。

**Article 943** The State shall strengthen the green and low-carbon development orientation and task requirements in the implementation of major regional strategies, and build a spatial pattern for green, low-carbon, and high-quality development.

<a id="art-944"></a>第九百四十四条　国家推动钢铁、有色金属、石化、化工、建材、造纸、印染等行业绿色低碳转型，推广节能低碳和清洁生产技术装备，推进工艺流程更新升级，完善能源效率、碳排放等相关约束性标准，促进传统产业优化升级。  
国家促进绿色低碳产业发展，培育壮大新能源、生物制造等新兴产业和未来产业，建设绿色制造和服务体系，逐步提升绿色低碳产业在经济总量中的比重。

**Article 944** The State shall promote the green and low-carbon transformation of industries such as steel, non-ferrous metals, petrochemicals, chemicals, building materials, papermaking, and printing/dyeing; promote energy-saving, low-carbon, and clean production technologies and equipment; advance the renewal and upgrading of process flows; improve binding standards related to energy efficiency, carbon emissions, etc.; and promote the optimization and upgrading of traditional industries.  
The State shall promote the development of green and low-carbon industries; cultivate and strengthen emerging industries and future industries such as new energy and bio-manufacturing; build a green manufacturing and service system; and gradually increase the proportion of green and low-carbon industries in the total economic output.

<a id="art-945"></a>第九百四十五条　国家优化交通运输结构，完善交通运输网络，推动铁路、公路、水运、航空不同运输方式合理分工、有效衔接，构建绿色高效交通运输体系。  
国家建设绿色交通基础设施，优先发展公共交通，推广低碳交通运输工具，推动使用清洁动力，积极引导绿色出行。

**Article 945** The State shall optimize the transport structure, improve the transport network, promote reasonable division of labor and effective connection among different modes of transport such as railway, highway, waterway, and aviation, and build a green and efficient transport system.  
The State shall build green transport infrastructure; give priority to the development of public transport; promote low-carbon transport vehicles; promote the use of clean power; and actively guide green travel.

<a id="art-946"></a>第九百四十六条　国家倡导绿色低碳规划设计理念，推广绿色建造方式，优化建筑用能结构，支持超低能耗、低碳建筑规模化发展，推动绿色低碳和气候适应型城市建设。

**Article 946** The State shall advocate green and low-carbon planning and design concepts, promote green construction methods, optimize building energy consumption structure, support the large-scale development of ultra-low energy consumption and low-carbon buildings, and promote the construction of green, low-carbon, and climate-resilient cities.

<a id="art-947"></a>第九百四十七条　国家推动农业农村绿色低碳发展，加快绿色技术推广应用，引导农业生产经营者科学合理使用生产资料，加强农业废弃物资源化利用，优先发展生态农业。

**Article 947** The State shall promote green and low-carbon development in agriculture and rural areas; accelerate the promotion and application of green technologies; guide agricultural producers and operators to use production materials scientifically and rationally; strengthen the resource utilization of agricultural waste; and give priority to the development of ecological agriculture.

<a id="art-948"></a>第九百四十八条　国家建立健全绿色低碳标准体系。  
国务院有关部门和县级以上地方人民政府应当采取有效措施，促进绿色低碳产品标准实施和认证结果、标识信息的使用与效果评价。

**Article 948** The State shall establish and improve the green and low-carbon standards system.  
The relevant departments of the State Council and local people's governments at or above the county level shall take effective measures to promote the implementation of standards for green and low-carbon products and the use and effect evaluation of certification results and labeling information.

<a id="art-949"></a>第九百四十九条　国家机关和其他使用财政性资金的公共机构应当率先贯彻绿色低碳理念，厉行节约、反对浪费，推进绿色办公、绿色采购，使用绿色低碳产品，发挥绿色低碳转型示范引领作用。  
企业应当加大绿色低碳技术和产品的研究开发、推广应用，推进生产经营活动绿色化，提高资源利用效率，减少污染物和温室气体的产生、排放，自觉履行绿色低碳发展社会责任。  
公民应当增强绿色低碳意识，积极参与绿色消费、绿色出行、“光盘行动”、生活垃圾分类等绿色低碳活动，反对铺张浪费，形成文明健康的生活方式和消费模式。

**Article 949** State organs and other public institutions using fiscal funds shall take the lead in implementing the concept of green and low-carbon, practice strict economy and oppose waste, promote green office and green procurement, use green and low-carbon products, and play a demonstration and leading role in green and low-carbon transformation.  
Enterprises shall increase the research, development, promotion, and application of green and low-carbon technologies and products; promote the greening of production and business activities; improve resource utilization efficiency; reduce the generation and emission of pollutants and greenhouse gases; and conscientiously fulfill social responsibilities for green and low-carbon development.  
Citizens shall enhance their awareness of green and low-carbon; actively participate in green consumption, green travel, the 'Clean Plate Campaign', domestic waste sorting, and other green and low-carbon activities; oppose extravagance and waste; and form a civilized and healthy lifestyle and consumption pattern.

<a id="art-950"></a>第九百五十条　国家优化绿色转型投资机制，加大对绿色低碳先进适用技术示范、重点行业节能降碳、资源高效循环利用等领域重点项目的财政投入，引导和规范社会资本参与绿色低碳项目投资、建设、运营，鼓励社会资本以市场化方式设立绿色低碳产业投资基金。

**Article 950** The State shall optimize the investment mechanism for green transformation; increase financial investment in key projects in areas such as demonstration of advanced and applicable green and low-carbon technologies, energy conservation and carbon reduction in key industries, and efficient recycling of resources; guide and regulate the participation of social capital in the investment, construction, and operation of green and low-carbon projects; and encourage social capital to establish green and low-carbon industry investment funds in a market-based manner.

<a id="art-951"></a>第九百五十一条　国家加强绿色低碳领域应用基础研究，鼓励、支持资源节约与循环利用、能源绿色低碳转型、减缓和适应气候变化相关技术研究开发，加快先进适用技术示范和推广应用。

**Article 951** The State shall strengthen applied basic research in the field of green and low-carbon; encourage and support research and development of technologies related to resource conservation and recycling, green and low-carbon transformation of energy, and climate change mitigation and adaptation; and accelerate the demonstration, promotion, and application of advanced and applicable technologies.

<a id="art-952"></a>第九百五十二条　国家加强绿色经贸、技术、金融、标准等领域国际合作，鼓励绿色低碳技术、产品和服务进出口，积极参与国际规则和标准制定，引领全球绿色转型。

**Article 952** The State shall strengthen international cooperation in areas such as green trade, technology, finance, and standards; encourage the import and export of green and low-carbon technologies, products, and services; actively participate in the formulation of international rules and standards; and lead the global green transformation.

### 第二章　发展循环经济

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-953"></a>第九百五十三条　发展循环经济应当坚持减量化、再利用、资源化，遵循统筹规划、合理布局，因地制宜、注重实效，政府推动、市场引导，企业实施、公众参与的方针。

**Article 953** The development of circular economy shall adhere to the principles of reduction, reuse, and recycling; and follow the policy of integrated planning, rational layout, adaptation to local conditions, emphasis on practical results, government promotion, market guidance, enterprise implementation, and public participation.

<a id="art-954"></a>第九百五十四条　国务院发展改革部门负责组织协调、监督管理全国的循环经济发展工作；国务院生态环境、工业和信息化、住房城乡建设等有关部门在各自职责范围内，负责有关循环经济的监督管理工作。  
县级以上地方人民政府发展改革部门负责组织协调、监督管理本行政区域的循环经济发展工作；县级以上地方人民政府生态环境、工业和信息化、住房城乡建设等有关部门在各自职责范围内，负责有关循环经济的监督管理工作。

**Article 954** The development and reform department of the State Council shall be responsible for organizing, coordinating, supervising, and managing the development of circular economy nationwide; the relevant departments of the State Council such as ecology and environment, industry and information technology, and housing and urban-rural construction shall, within their respective duties, be responsible for the supervision and management of circular economy.  
The development and reform departments of local people's governments at or above the county level shall be responsible for organizing, coordinating, supervising, and managing the development of circular economy in their respective administrative regions; the relevant departments of local people's governments at or above the county level such as ecology and environment, industry and information technology, and housing and urban-rural construction shall, within their respective duties, be responsible for the supervision and management of circular economy.

<a id="art-955"></a>第九百五十五条　国务院发展改革部门会同国务院生态环境等有关部门编制全国循环经济发展规划，报国务院批准后公布实施。  
设区的市级以上地方人民政府发展改革部门会同生态环境等有关部门编制本行政区域循环经济发展规划，报本级人民政府批准后公布实施。  
循环经济发展规划应当包括规划目标、适用范围、主要内容、重点任务和保障措施等。

**Article 955** The development and reform department of the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments of the State Council such as ecology and environment, formulate the national circular economy development plan, which shall be submitted to the State Council for approval before being published and implemented.  
The development and reform departments of local people's governments at or above the level of a city divided into districts shall, together with the relevant departments such as ecology and environment, formulate circular economy development plans for their respective administrative regions, which shall be submitted to the people's government at the same level for approval before being published and implemented.  
The circular economy development plan shall include planning objectives, scope of application, main contents, key tasks, and safeguard measures.

<a id="art-956"></a>第九百五十六条　国家制定产业政策，应当符合发展循环经济的要求。  
国家支持在技术可行、经济合理和有利于保护生态环境的前提下，推广资源循环型生产模式，促进资源循环利用产业集约化、规模化发展。

**Article 956** When formulating industrial policies, the State shall comply with the requirements for developing circular economy.  
The State shall support the promotion of resource recycling production models and promote the intensive and large-scale development of resource recycling industries under the premise of technical feasibility, economic rationality, and favorability to the protection of the ecological environment.

<a id="art-957"></a>第九百五十七条　国务院标准化主管部门会同国务院发展改革、生态环境、工业和信息化等有关部门制定绿色设计、绿色制造、产业废弃物综合利用、生活垃圾回收利用等循环经济标准。

**Article 957** The standardization department of the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments of the State Council such as development and reform, ecology and environment, and industry and information technology, formulate circular economy standards such as green design, green manufacturing, comprehensive utilization of industrial waste, and recycling of domestic waste.

<a id="art-958"></a>第九百五十八条　国家建立健全循环经济统计制度，加强资源消耗、综合利用、废弃物产生等的统计管理，并将主要统计指标定期向社会公布。

**Article 958** The State shall establish and improve the circular economy statistical system; strengthen the statistical management of resource consumption, comprehensive utilization, and waste generation; and regularly publish main statistical indicators to the public.

<a id="art-959"></a>第九百五十九条　国家建立健全生产者责任延伸制度，促进资源循环利用。

**Article 959** The State shall establish and improve the extended producer responsibility system to promote resource recycling.

#### 第二节　清洁生产

<a id="art-960"></a>第九百六十条　国家鼓励通过开展绿色设计、使用清洁的能源和原料、采用先进适用的工艺技术与设备等措施，从源头降低资源消耗，减少污染物和温室气体的产生、排放，推进清洁生产。

**Article 960** The State shall encourage measures such as carrying out green design, using clean energy and raw materials, adopting advanced and applicable process technologies and equipment, to reduce resource consumption at the source, reduce the generation and emission of pollutants and greenhouse gases, and promote cleaner production.

<a id="art-961"></a>第九百六十一条　国务院发展改革部门会同国务院生态环境、工业和信息化等有关部门编制全国清洁生产推行方案，报国务院批准后公布。全国清洁生产推行方案应当包括推进清洁生产的目标、主要任务和保障措施，确定开展清洁生产的重点领域、重点行业和重点工程。  
国务院有关行业主管部门根据全国清洁生产推行方案，确定本行业推进清洁生产的重点任务和重点项目，并组织落实。  
县级以上地方人民政府根据全国清洁生产推行方案，按照本行政区域节约资源、降低能源消耗、减少污染物和温室气体排放的要求，制定推进清洁生产的实施方案，并组织落实。

**Article 961** The development and reform department of the State Council shall, in conjunction with the ecological and environmental, industrial and information technology, and other relevant departments of the State Council, formulate a national plan for the promotion of clean production, which shall be published after being submitted to and approved by the State Council. The national plan for the promotion of clean production shall include the objectives, main tasks, and safeguard measures for promoting clean production, and shall identify the key areas, key industries, and key projects for carrying out clean production.  
The relevant competent departments of industries of the State Council shall, according to the national plan for the promotion of clean production, determine the key tasks and key projects for promoting clean production in their respective industries, and shall organize their implementation.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall, according to the national plan for the promotion of clean production and in compliance with the requirements of saving resources, reducing energy consumption, and reducing pollutant and greenhouse gas emissions within their respective administrative regions, formulate implementation plans for promoting clean production, and shall organize their implementation.

<a id="art-962"></a>第九百六十二条　中央预算应当加强对清洁生产推进工作的资金投入，包括中央财政清洁生产专项资金和中央预算安排的其他清洁生产资金，用于支持全国清洁生产推行方案确定的重点领域、重点行业、重点工程实施清洁生产及其技术推广工作，以及生态脆弱地区实施清洁生产的项目。中央预算用于支持清洁生产推进工作的资金使用的具体办法，由国务院财政、发展改革部门会同有关部门制定。  
县级以上地方人民政府应当统筹地方财政安排的清洁生产推进工作的资金，引导社会资金支持清洁生产重点项目。  
在按照国家规定设立的中小企业发展基金中，应当根据需要安排适当数额用于支持中小企业实施清洁生产。

**Article 962** The central budget shall strengthen financial input for the promotion of clean production, including special funds for clean production from the central finance and other clean production funds arranged in the central budget, which shall be used to support the implementation of clean production and the promotion of its technologies in the key areas, key industries, and key projects determined in the national plan for the promotion of clean production, as well as projects for implementing clean production in ecologically fragile areas. The specific measures for the use of funds from the central budget to support the promotion of clean production shall be formulated by the finance department and the development and reform department of the State Council in conjunction with relevant departments.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall coordinate the funds arranged by local finance for the promotion of clean production, and shall guide social funds to support key clean production projects.  
In the small and medium-sized enterprise development fund established according to the provisions of the State, an appropriate amount shall be arranged as necessary to support small and medium-sized enterprises in implementing clean production.

<a id="art-963"></a>第九百六十三条　国务院发展改革部门会同国务院生态环境、工业和信息化、科学技术、住房城乡建设、农业农村、林业草原等有关部门定期发布清洁生产技术、工艺、设备和产品导向目录。  
县级以上地方人民政府应当指导和支持清洁生产技术、工艺、设备和产品的研究开发，以及清洁生产技术示范和推广应用。

**Article 963** The development and reform department of the State Council shall, in conjunction with the ecological and environmental, industrial and information technology, science and technology, housing and urban-rural development, agriculture and rural affairs, forestry and grassland, and other relevant departments of the State Council, periodically publish a guiding catalogue of clean production technologies, processes, equipment, and products.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall guide and support the research and development of clean production technologies, processes, equipment, and products, as well as the demonstration and promotion and application of clean production technologies.

<a id="art-964"></a>第九百六十四条　省级以上人民政府发展改革、生态环境、工业和信息化部门会同有关部门，组织编制重点行业或者地区的清洁生产指南，指导实施清洁生产。

**Article 964** The development and reform, ecological and environmental, and industrial and information technology departments of people's governments at or above the provincial level shall, in conjunction with relevant departments, organize the formulation of clean production guidelines for key industries or regions to guide the implementation of clean production.

<a id="art-965"></a>第九百六十五条　企业在进行技术改造过程中，应当采取以下清洁生产措施：  
（一）采用无毒无害或者低毒低害的原料，替代毒性大、危害严重的原料；  
（二）采用资源利用率高、污染物和温室气体产生量少的工艺和设备，替代资源利用率低、污染物和温室气体产生量多的工艺和设备；  
（三）对生产过程中产生的废弃物进行综合利用；  
（四）采用能够达到国家或者地方规定的污染物排放标准和污染物排放总量控制指标的污染防治技术。

**Article 965** In the process of technological transformation, an enterprise shall adopt the following clean production measures:  
(1) adopting non-toxic, harmless or low-toxicity, low-harm raw materials to replace raw materials with high toxicity and serious hazards;  
(2) adopting processes and equipment with high resource utilization rates and low generation of pollutants and greenhouse gases to replace processes and equipment with low resource utilization rates and high generation of pollutants and greenhouse gases;  
(3) comprehensively utilizing wastes generated during the production process;  
(4) adopting pollution prevention and control technologies that can meet the pollutant discharge standards and the total pollutant discharge control targets prescribed by the State or local authorities.

<a id="art-966"></a>第九百六十六条　企业应当对生产和服务过程中的资源消耗以及废弃物的产生情况进行监测，并根据需要对生产和服务实施清洁生产审核。  
国家加强清洁生产审核结果应用，将其作为用水定额等差异化政策制定和实施的重要依据。  
清洁生产审核的具体办法，由国务院发展改革、生态环境部门会同有关部门制定。

**Article 966** An enterprise shall monitor the resource consumption and waste generation during its production and service processes, and shall conduct clean production audits on its production and services as necessary.  
The State shall strengthen the application of the results of clean production audits, and shall use them as an important basis for the formulation and implementation of differentiated policies such as water consumption quotas.  
The specific measures for clean production audits shall be formulated by the development and reform and ecological and environmental departments of the State Council in conjunction with relevant departments.

<a id="art-967"></a>第九百六十七条　有下列情形之一的企业，应当实施强制性清洁生产审核：  
（一）污染物排放超过国家或者地方规定的排放标准，或者虽未超过国家或者地方规定的排放标准，但超过重点污染物排放总量控制指标的；  
（二）使用有毒有害原料进行生产或者在生产中排放有毒有害物质的；  
（三）国家规定应当实施强制性清洁生产审核的其他情形。  
污染物排放超过国家或者地方规定的排放标准的企业，应当按照本法的规定承担法律责任。  
实施强制性清洁生产审核的企业，应当将审核结果向所在地县级以上地方人民政府发展改革、生态环境部门报告，并采取便于公众知晓的方式公开，接受公众监督，但涉及商业秘密的除外。  
县级以上地方人民政府有关部门应当对企业实施强制性清洁生产审核的情况进行监督，必要时可以组织对企业实施清洁生产的效果进行评估验收，所需费用纳入同级政府预算。承担评估验收工作的部门或者单位不得向被评估验收企业收取费用。

**Article 967** An enterprise that falls under any of the following circumstances shall undergo a mandatory clean production audit:  
(1) the discharge of pollutants exceeds the discharge standards prescribed by the State or local authorities, or although not exceeding the discharge standards prescribed by the State or local authorities, exceeds the total discharge control targets for key pollutants;  
(2) using toxic or hazardous raw materials for production or discharging toxic or hazardous substances during production;  
(3) other circumstances under which the State prescribes that a mandatory clean production audit shall be conducted.  
An enterprise whose pollutant discharge exceeds the discharge standards prescribed by the State or local authorities shall bear legal liability in accordance with the provisions of this Law.  
An enterprise that undergoes a mandatory clean production audit shall report the audit results to the development and reform and ecological and environmental departments of the local people's government at or above the county level, and shall disclose them in a manner convenient for the public to know, subject to public supervision, except where trade secrets are involved.  
The relevant departments of local people's governments at or above the county level shall supervise the implementation of mandatory clean production audits by enterprises, and may, when necessary, organize evaluation and acceptance of the effects of clean production implementation by enterprises, with the required expenses included in the budget of the same level of government. The department or unit undertaking the evaluation and acceptance work shall not charge fees to the enterprises being evaluated and accepted.

<a id="art-968"></a>第九百六十八条　[本法第九百六十七条](#art-967)第一款规定以外的企业可以按照国家规定，自愿与发展改革、生态环境、工业和信息化部门签订节约资源、减少污染物排放的协议。发展改革、生态环境、工业和信息化部门应当采取便于公众知晓的方式，公布企业的名称和节约资源、防治污染的成果。
