**Article 968** Enterprises other than those specified in paragraph 1 of Article 967 of this Law may, in accordance with the provisions of the State, voluntarily enter into agreements with the development and reform, ecological and environmental, and industrial and information technology departments on saving resources and reducing pollutant emissions. The development and reform, ecological and environmental, and industrial and information technology departments shall, in a manner convenient for the public to know, publish the names of the enterprises and their achievements in resource conservation and pollution prevention and control.

<a id="art-969"></a>第九百六十九条　生产大型机电设备、机动运输工具以及国务院工业和信息化主管部门指定的其他产品的企业，应当按照国务院标准化主管部门或者其授权机构制定的技术规范，在产品的主体构件上注明材料成分的标准牌号。

**Article 969** An enterprise producing large-scale mechanical and electrical equipment, motor transport vehicles, and other products designated by the competent department of industrial and information technology of the State Council shall, in accordance with the technical specifications formulated by the standardization department of the State Council or its authorized body, indicate the standard grades of material composition on the main components of the products.

<a id="art-970"></a>第九百七十条　国务院和省、自治区、直辖市人民政府的有关部门，应当组织和支持建立清洁生产信息系统和技术咨询服务体系。  
国家鼓励提供清洁生产咨询、审核等服务的机构开展清洁生产知识宣传和技术培训。

**Article 970** The relevant departments of the State Council and people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government shall organize and support the establishment of a clean production information system and a technical consulting service system.  
The State shall encourage institutions providing services such as clean production consulting and auditing to carry out publicity of clean production knowledge and technical training.

<a id="art-971"></a>第九百七十一条　企业用于清洁生产审核和培训的费用，可以列入企业经营成本。

**Article 971** The expenses incurred by an enterprise for clean production audits and training may be included in the enterprise's operating costs.

<a id="art-972"></a>第九百七十二条　国家鼓励产品的生产经营者开展绿色设计，推动绿色设计应用。  
电器电子、机动车等产品的设计，应当考虑其在生命周期中对人体健康和生态环境的影响，优先选择无毒无害、易于降解或者便于回收利用的方案，按照国家规定加强有害物质源头减量和替代。

**Article 972** The State shall encourage producers and operators of products to carry out green design and promote the application of green design.  
The design of products such as electrical and electronic products, motor vehicles, etc., shall take into account their impact on human health and the ecological environment during their life cycle, shall prioritize the selection of solutions that are non-toxic, harmless, easy to degrade, or convenient for recycling, and shall, in accordance with the provisions of the State, strengthen the source reduction and substitution of hazardous substances.

<a id="art-973"></a>第九百七十三条　国家鼓励产品的生产经营者使用绿色包装、减量包装。  
国务院标准化主管部门应当根据国家经济和技术条件、固体废物污染防治状况以及产品的技术要求，组织制定有关标准，防止过度包装造成资源浪费和环境污染。  
生产经营者对产品的包装应当合理，遵守限制商品过度包装的强制性标准，减少包装性废弃物的产生，不得进行过度包装。包装的材质、结构和成本应当与内装产品的质量、规格和成本相适应。  
县级以上地方人民政府市场监督管理等有关部门应当在各自职责范围内，加强对过度包装的监督管理。

**Article 973** The State shall encourage producers and operators of products to use green packaging and reduced packaging.  
The standardization department of the State Council shall, in light of the national economic and technical conditions, the situation of solid waste pollution prevention and control, and the technical requirements of products, organize the formulation of relevant standards to prevent excessive packaging from causing resource waste and environmental pollution.  
Producers and operators shall package their products properly, comply with mandatory standards on restricting excessive packaging of commodities, reduce the generation of packaging waste, and shall not engage in excessive packaging. The material, structure, and cost of packaging shall be compatible with the quality, specifications, and cost of the packaged products.  
The market regulation and other relevant departments of local people's governments at or above the county level shall, within their respective functions and duties, strengthen the supervision and management of excessive packaging.

<a id="art-974"></a>第九百七十四条　电子商务、快递、外卖等行业应当减少包装物的使用，优先采用可重复使用、易回收利用的包装物，并积极回收利用。县级以上地方人民政府商务、邮政管理等有关部门应当加强监督管理。

**Article 974** Industries such as e-commerce, express delivery, and takeout shall reduce the use of packaging materials, give priority to packaging materials that are reusable and easy to recycle, and actively recycle and reuse them. The commerce, postal administration, and other relevant departments of local people's governments at or above the county level shall strengthen supervision and management.

#### 第三节　废弃物循环利用

<a id="art-975"></a>第九百七十五条　国家推进废弃物循环利用体系建设，促进生产生活各领域废弃物精细管理、有效回收、高效利用，提升废弃物循环利用水平。

**Article 975** The State shall promote the establishment of a waste recycling system, promote the refined management, effective recovery, and efficient utilization of waste in all areas of production and daily life, and raise the level of waste recycling.

<a id="art-976"></a>第九百七十六条　企业应当采用先进适用的回收技术、工艺和设备，对生产过程中产生的余热、余压、废水、废液等进行综合利用。  
企业应当按照国家规定，对生产过程中产生的粉煤灰、煤矸石、磷石膏、赤泥、尾矿、废石、废料、废气等工业废弃物进行综合利用。

**Article 976** An enterprise shall adopt advanced and applicable recovery technologies, processes, and equipment to comprehensively utilize waste heat, waste pressure, wastewater, waste liquid, etc. generated during the production process.  
An enterprise shall, in accordance with the provisions of the State, comprehensively utilize industrial wastes such as fly ash, coal gangue, phosphogypsum, red mud, tailings, waste rock, waste materials, and waste gas generated during the production process.

<a id="art-977"></a>第九百七十七条　生产、进口、销售依法列入强制回收名录的产品和包装物的企业，应当按照国家规定对该产品和包装物进行回收。对其中可以利用的，由企业负责利用；对因不具备技术经济条件而不适合利用的，由企业负责无害化处置。  
对前款规定的废弃产品或者包装物，生产者委托销售者或者其他组织进行回收，或者委托废弃物利用、处置企业进行利用、处置的，受托方应当依照有关法律、行政法规的规定和合同的约定负责回收或者利用、处置。  
强制回收的产品和包装物的名录及管理办法，由国务院发展改革部门会同有关部门制定。

**Article 977** An enterprise that produces, imports, or sells products and packaging materials that are included in the mandatory recycling list according to law shall, in accordance with the provisions of the State, recycle such products and packaging materials. For those that are usable, the enterprise shall be responsible for utilizing them; for those that are not suitable for utilization due to lack of technical and economic conditions, the enterprise shall be responsible for disposing of them in a harmless manner.  
For the waste products or packaging materials specified in the preceding paragraph, if the producer entrusts a seller or other organization to recycle them, or entrusts a waste utilization or disposal enterprise to utilize or dispose of them, the entrusted party shall be responsible for recycling, utilizing, or disposing of them in accordance with the provisions of relevant laws and administrative regulations and the terms of the contract.  
The list and management measures for products and packaging materials subject to mandatory recycling shall be formulated by the development and reform department of the State Council in conjunction with relevant departments.

<a id="art-978"></a>第九百七十八条　电器电子、机动车、铅蓄电池、动力电池等产品的生产者应当按照国家规定，以自建或者委托等方式建立与产品销售量相匹配的废旧产品回收体系，并向社会公开，履行回收和利用责任。

**Article 978** Producers of products such as electrical and electronic products, motor vehicles, lead-acid batteries, power batteries, etc. shall, in accordance with the provisions of the State, establish a waste product recycling system that matches the sales volume of the products through self-construction or entrustment, shall disclose it to the public, and shall fulfill their responsibilities for recycling and utilization.

<a id="art-979"></a>第九百七十九条　国家建立健全风电和光伏发电退役设备处理责任制度。  
从事风电、光伏发电建设运营的企业，应当自行或者委托具备条件的企业对退役风电机组叶片、光伏组件等进行循环利用或者无害化处置。  
国家推进数据中心、通信基站等新型基础设施领域废弃物循环利用。

**Article 979** The State shall establish and improve the responsibility system for the treatment of decommissioned equipment from wind power and photovoltaic power generation.  
Enterprises engaged in the construction and operation of wind power and photovoltaic power generation shall, by themselves or by entrusting qualified enterprises, recycle or dispose of decommissioned wind turbine blades, photovoltaic modules, etc. in a harmless manner.  
The State shall promote the recycling of waste in new infrastructure areas such as data centers and communication base stations.

<a id="art-980"></a>第九百八十条　国家支持生产经营者建立产业废弃物交换信息系统，促进企业交流产业废弃物信息。  
企业对生产过程中产生的废弃物不具备综合利用条件的，应当提供给具备条件的生产经营者进行综合利用。

**Article 980** The State shall support producers and operators in establishing an industrial waste exchange information system to promote the exchange of industrial waste information among enterprises.  
If an enterprise does not have the conditions for comprehensive utilization of wastes generated during its production process, it shall provide such wastes to producers and operators that have the conditions for comprehensive utilization.

<a id="art-981"></a>第九百八十一条　国家鼓励、支持农业生产经营者和相关企业采用先进适用技术，对农作物秸秆、畜禽粪便、农产品加工业副产品、废农用薄膜等进行综合利用。

**Article 981** The State encourages and supports agricultural producers and operators as well as relevant enterprises to adopt advanced and applicable technologies for comprehensive utilization of crop straw, livestock and poultry manure, by-products from agricultural product processing, waste agricultural plastic films, and so on.

<a id="art-982"></a>第九百八十二条　国家鼓励采用先进适用技术、工艺、设备、材料和管理措施，推进建筑垃圾源头减量，建立健全建筑垃圾回收利用体系。  
县级以上地方人民政府应当推动建筑垃圾综合利用产品应用。

**Article 982** The State encourages the adoption of advanced and applicable technologies, processes, equipment, materials, and management measures to promote source reduction of construction waste and to establish a sound system for the recycling of construction waste.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level shall promote the application of products derived from comprehensive utilization of construction waste.

<a id="art-983"></a>第九百八十三条　国家加强生活垃圾分类收集、处理和资源化利用，推进生活垃圾减量化、资源化。  
县级以上地方人民政府有关部门应当统筹规划，合理布局回收、分拣、打包网点，促进生活垃圾回收利用。

**Article 983** The State strengthens the classified collection, treatment, and resource utilization of household waste, and advances the reduction and resource recovery of household waste.  
Relevant departments of local people's governments at or above the county level shall make overall plans and reasonably arrange collection, sorting, and baling stations to promote the recycling of household waste.

<a id="art-984"></a>第九百八十四条　国家推进生活垃圾分类网点和废旧物资回收网点融合建设，完善城乡废旧物资回收网络。  
地方人民政府应当按照国土空间规划，合理布局废旧物资回收网点和交易市场，支持废旧物资回收企业和其他组织开展废旧物资的收集、储存、运输及信息交流。

**Article 984** The State promotes the integrated construction of household waste sorting stations and waste material recycling stations, and improves the urban and rural waste material recycling network.  
Local people's governments shall, in accordance with the territorial spatial plan, rationally arrange waste material recycling stations and trading markets, and support waste material recycling enterprises and other organizations in collecting, storing, transporting, and exchanging information about waste materials.

<a id="art-985"></a>第九百八十五条　国家完善低值可回收物目录，促进废玻璃、废塑料、废旧纺织品等回收利用。

**Article 985** The State improves the catalog of low-value recyclables and promotes the recycling of waste glass, waste plastics, waste textiles, and others.

<a id="art-986"></a>第九百八十六条　国家加强废钢铁、废有色金属等高效利用，推动再生金属加工利用产业集聚化发展。  
国家鼓励企业提升废有色金属利用技术水平，支持从废弃电器电子产品等废弃物中提取稀贵金属技术的研究开发、推广应用。

**Article 986** The State strengthens the efficient utilization of waste iron and steel, waste non-ferrous metals, and the like, and promotes the clustered development of the recycling metal processing and utilization industry.  
The State encourages enterprises to improve the technical level of waste non-ferrous metal utilization, and supports the research, development, and promotion of technologies for extracting rare and precious metals from waste such as discarded electrical and electronic products.

<a id="art-987"></a>第九百八十七条　企业利用废弃物和从废弃物中回收原料生产产品，应当采用先进适用的技术、工艺和设备，提高回收利用率和加工利用水平。  
对废弃电器电子产品、报废机动车船和飞机、废轮胎、废铅蓄电池、废旧动力电池等特定产品进行拆解、处置或者再利用，应当符合本法和《中华人民共和国循环经济促进法》等有关法律法规的规定，禁止使用国家明令淘汰或者不符合生态环境保护要求的落后拆解加工技术、工艺和设备。

**Article 987** When an enterprise uses waste materials or recovers raw materials from waste to produce products, it shall adopt advanced and applicable technologies, processes, and equipment to improve the recycling rate and level of processing and utilization.  
The dismantling, disposal, or reuse of specific products such as discarded electrical and electronic products, end-of-life motor vehicles, ships and aircraft, waste tires, waste lead-acid batteries, and waste power batteries shall comply with this Law, the Circular Economy Promotion Law of the People's Republic of China, and other relevant laws and regulations. The use of backward dismantling and processing technologies, processes, and equipment that are expressly eliminated by the State or that do not meet the requirements of ecological and environmental protection is prohibited.

<a id="art-988"></a>第九百八十八条　国家对废弃电器电子产品等实行多渠道回收和集中处理制度。废弃电器电子产品等回收处理的具体管理办法由国务院制定。

**Article 988** The State applies a system of multi-channel recovery and centralized treatment for discarded electrical and electronic products, among others. The specific management measures for the recovery and treatment of discarded electrical and electronic products shall be formulated by the State Council.

<a id="art-989"></a>第九百八十九条　国家支持企业开展机动车零部件、工程机械、机床、文化办公设备等产品的再制造和轮胎翻新，推动风电、光伏发电、航空等领域高端装备再制造产业发展，鼓励再制造产品推广使用。  
再制造产品和翻新产品的质量应当符合国家规定的标准，并在显著位置标识为再制造产品或者翻新产品。

**Article 989** The State supports enterprises in carrying out remanufacturing of motor vehicle parts and components, construction machinery, machine tools, cultural and office equipment, and other products, and tire retreading; promotes the development of remanufacturing industries for high-end equipment in the fields of wind power, photovoltaic power generation, aviation, and others; and encourages the widespread use of remanufactured products.  
The quality of remanufactured and retreaded products shall meet the standards prescribed by the State, and such products shall be labeled conspicuously as remanufactured or retreaded products.

<a id="art-990"></a>第九百九十条　国家鼓励再生材料推广应用，建立健全再生材料标准和认证制度，支持机动车、电器电子等产品的生产者提高再生材料使用比例。

**Article 990** The State encourages the promotion and application of recycled materials, establishes and improves standards and certification systems for recycled materials, and supports producers of motor vehicles, electrical and electronic products, and others in increasing the proportion of recycled materials used.

<a id="art-991"></a>第九百九十一条　依法利用废弃物和从废弃物中回收原料生产产品的，按照国家规定享受税收等优惠。

**Article 991** Those who utilize waste materials or recover raw materials from waste to produce products according to law shall enjoy tax and other preferential treatments in accordance with State regulations.

#### 第四节　绿色消费

<a id="art-992"></a>第九百九十二条　国家建立健全绿色消费激励机制，扩大绿色低碳产品供给，加强绿色消费宣传教育，推广绿色低碳生活方式，推动消费模式绿色转型。

**Article 992** The State establishes and improves incentive mechanisms for green consumption, expands the supply of green and low-carbon products, strengthens education and publicity on green consumption, promotes green and low-carbon lifestyles, and drives the green transformation of consumption patterns.

<a id="art-993"></a>第九百九十三条　国家实行有利于资源节约和合理利用的价格、税收等政策，引导单位和个人节约和合理使用水、电、气等资源性产品。  
县级以上人民政府应当采取有效措施，鼓励绿色低碳产品消费，按照国家规定支持消费品以旧换新。

**Article 993** The State implements pricing, taxation, and other policies that are conducive to resource conservation and rational utilization, and guides entities and individuals to conserve and rationally use water, electricity, gas, and other resource-based products.  
People's governments at or above the county level shall take effective measures to encourage the consumption of green and low-carbon products and, in accordance with State regulations, support the replacement of old consumer goods with new ones.

<a id="art-994"></a>第九百九十四条　国家建立健全政府绿色采购制度，鼓励、支持将再生材料和产品纳入政府绿色采购范围。

**Article 994** The State establishes and improves a government green procurement system, and encourages and supports the inclusion of recycled materials and products in the scope of government green procurement.

<a id="art-995"></a>第九百九十五条　国家鼓励、支持二手商品流通交易，推动二手商品交易市场和平台建设。二手商品流通管理办法由国务院商务部门会同有关部门制定。  
县级以上地方人民政府市场监督管理、商务等有关部门应当在各自职责范围内，加强对二手商品交易的监督管理。

**Article 995** The State encourages and supports the circulation and trading of second-hand goods, and promotes the construction of second-hand goods trading markets and platforms. The measures for the administration of the circulation of second-hand goods shall be formulated by the commerce department of the State Council together with relevant departments.  
The market regulation, commerce, and other relevant departments of local people's governments at or above the county level shall, within their respective functions and duties, strengthen the supervision and management of second-hand goods trading.

<a id="art-996"></a>第九百九十六条　国家鼓励、引导消费者使用绿色包装、减量包装。  
对列入强制回收名录的产品和包装物，消费者应当将废弃的产品和包装物交给生产者或者其委托回收的销售者、其他组织。

**Article 996** The State encourages and guides consumers to use green packaging and reduced packaging.  
For products and packaging materials listed in the mandatory recycling catalog, consumers shall deliver the discarded products and packaging materials to the producers or to the sellers or other organizations authorized by the producers for recycling.

<a id="art-997"></a>第九百九十七条　国家在保障产品安全和卫生的前提下，限制一次性用品的生产和销售。  
旅游、住宿等行业应当按照国家规定，不主动提供一次性用品。  
机关、企业事业单位、社会团体等在办公场所应当减少使用一次性用品。

**Article 997** The State, on the premise of ensuring product safety and hygiene, restricts the production and sale of disposable products.  
Industries such as tourism and accommodation shall, in accordance with State regulations, not proactively provide disposable products.  
Government organs, enterprises, public institutions, social organizations, and others shall reduce the use of disposable products in their office premises.

<a id="art-998"></a>第九百九十八条　国家依法禁止和限制生产、销售、使用不可降解塑料袋等一次性塑料制品，鼓励、引导减少使用和积极回收一次性塑料制品，推广应用可循环、易回收、可降解且无害的替代产品。  
商品零售场所开办单位、电子商务平台企业和快递企业、外卖企业应当按照国家规定，向商务、邮政管理等部门报告一次性塑料制品的使用、回收情况。

**Article 998** The State prohibits and restricts, in accordance with law, the production, sale, and use of non-degradable plastic bags and other disposable plastic products; encourages and guides the reduction of use and active recycling of disposable plastic products; and promotes the application of recyclable, easily recoverable, degradable, and harmless alternative products.  
Entities running commodity retail sites, e-commerce platform enterprises, express delivery enterprises, and food delivery enterprises shall, in accordance with State regulations, report to the commerce, postal administration, and other departments on the use and recycling of disposable plastic products.

<a id="art-999"></a>第九百九十九条　公民应当增强绿色消费意识，养成绿色消费习惯，积极购买和使用绿色低碳产品，主动减少使用一次性用品、一次性塑料制品，自觉抵制过度包装。

**Article 999** Citizens shall enhance their awareness of green consumption, cultivate green consumption habits, actively purchase and use green and low-carbon products, voluntarily reduce the use of disposable products and disposable plastic products, and conscientiously resist excessive packaging.

<a id="art-1000"></a>第一千条　企业应当按照国家规定实施绿色采购，建立绿色供应链，优先采购和使用节能、节水、节材等有利于保护生态环境的原材料、产品和服务，促进上下游企业协同绿色转型。

**Article 1000** An enterprise shall, in accordance with State regulations, implement green procurement, establish a green supply chain, give priority to the procurement and use of raw materials, products, and services that are conducive to the protection of the ecological environment, such as energy-saving, water-saving, and material-saving ones, and promote the coordinated green transformation of upstream and downstream enterprises.

<a id="art-1001"></a>第一千零一条　餐饮、娱乐、宾馆等服务性企业应当按照国家规定，采用节能、节水、节材等有利于保护生态环境的技术、设备和设施，提供绿色低碳产品，减少使用或者不使用浪费资源、污染环境的消费品。

**Article 1001** : Service enterprises such as catering, entertainment, and hotels shall, in accordance with state regulations, adopt technologies, equipment, and facilities that are conducive to protecting the ecological environment, such as energy-saving, water-saving, and material-saving measures, provide green and low-carbon products, and reduce or refrain from using consumer goods that waste resources and pollute the environment.

### 第三章　能源节约与绿色低碳转型

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-1002"></a>第一千零二条　能源绿色低碳发展应当坚持节约优先、集约高效、创新驱动、保障安全、有序转型的原则。

**Article 1002** : The green and low-carbon development of energy shall adhere to the principles of prioritizing conservation, intensive efficiency, innovation-driven growth, ensuring safety, and orderly transformation.

<a id="art-1003"></a>第一千零三条　国家完善能源开发利用政策，优化能源供应结构和消费结构，积极推动能源绿色低碳发展。

**Article 1003** : The State shall improve energy development and utilization policies, optimize the structure of energy supply and consumption, and actively promote the green and low-carbon development of energy.

<a id="art-1004"></a>第一千零四条　国家推动节约能源和提高能源利用效率，鼓励发展分布式能源和多能互补、多能联供综合能源服务，积极推广合同能源管理等市场化节约能源服务，通过完善阶梯价格、分时价格等制度，引导能源用户合理调整用能方式、时间、数量等，提高终端能源消费清洁化、低碳化、高效化、智能化水平。

**Article 1004** : The State shall promote energy conservation and improve energy utilization efficiency, encourage the development of distributed energy and comprehensive energy services featuring multi-energy complementarity and multi-energy co-supply, actively promote market-oriented energy conservation services such as energy performance contracting, and guide energy users to reasonably adjust their energy consumption methods, timing, and quantities by improving systems such as tiered pricing and time-of-use pricing, thereby enhancing the cleanliness, low-carbon nature, efficiency, and smart level of terminal energy consumption.

<a id="art-1005"></a>第一千零五条　国家鼓励、支持化石能源清洁高效开发利用、可再生能源开发利用、核能安全利用以及储能、节约能源等能源绿色低碳发展相关领域基础性、关键性和前沿性重大技术、装备及相关新材料的研究开发、推广应用和产业化发展。能源绿色低碳科技创新应当纳入国家科技发展和高技术产业发展相关规划的重点支持领域。  
国家支持先进信息技术在能源领域的应用，推动能源生产和供应的数字化、智能化发展，以及多种能源协同转换与集成互补。

**Article 1005** : The State shall encourage and support the research and development, promotion, application, and industrial development of fundamental, critical, and cutting-edge major technologies, equipment, and related new materials in fields related to green and low-carbon energy development, including clean and efficient development and utilization of fossil energy, development and utilization of renewable energy, safe utilization of nuclear energy, energy storage, and energy conservation. Innovations in green and low-carbon energy science and technology shall be included in the key support areas of relevant plans for national science and technology development and high-tech industry development.  
The State shall support the application of advanced information technology in the energy sector, promote the digital and intelligent development of energy production and supply, as well as the coordinated conversion and integrated complementarity of multiple energy sources.

<a id="art-1006"></a>第一千零六条　县级以上人民政府应当加强农业农村节能工作，增加对农业农村节能技术、节能产品推广应用的资金投入。  
国家鼓励、支持农村地区的可再生能源综合开发利用，因地制宜推广应用生物质能、太阳能和风能等可再生能源利用技术。

**Article 1006** : People's governments at or above the county level shall strengthen energy conservation work in agriculture and rural areas, and increase financial investment in the promotion and application of energy-saving technologies and energy-saving products in agriculture and rural areas.  
The State shall encourage and support the comprehensive development and utilization of renewable energy in rural areas, and promote and apply renewable energy utilization technologies such as biomass energy, solar energy, and wind energy in accordance with local conditions.

#### 第二节　能源节约

<a id="art-1007"></a>第一千零七条　国务院发展改革部门负责全国的节能监督管理工作。国务院有关部门在各自职责范围内负责节能监督管理工作，并接受国务院发展改革部门的指导。  
县级以上地方人民政府节能主管部门负责本行政区域的节能监督管理工作。县级以上地方人民政府有关部门在各自职责范围内负责节能监督管理工作，并接受同级节能主管部门的指导。

**Article 1007** : The development and reform department of the State Council shall be responsible for the supervision and management of energy conservation nationwide. Relevant departments of the State Council shall be responsible for the supervision and management of energy conservation within their respective scopes of duties and shall accept guidance from the development and reform department of the State Council.  
The energy conservation department of a local people's government at or above the county level shall be responsible for the supervision and management of energy conservation within its administrative region. Relevant departments of a local people's government at or above the county level shall be responsible for the supervision and management of energy conservation within their respective scopes of duties and shall accept guidance from the energy conservation department at the same level.

<a id="art-1008"></a>第一千零八条　国务院标准化主管部门和国务院有关部门依法组织制定并适时修订有关节能的国家标准、行业标准。

**Article 1008** : The standardization department of the State Council and relevant departments of the State Council shall, according to law, organize the formulation and timely revision of national standards and industry standards related to energy conservation.

<a id="art-1009"></a>第一千零九条　国家实行固定资产投资项目节能审查制度，对项目用能和碳排放情况开展综合评价。具体办法由国务院发展改革部门会同有关部门制定。

**Article 1009** : The State shall implement an energy conservation review system for fixed asset investment projects, and conduct comprehensive evaluations of the energy consumption and carbon emissions of the projects. Specific measures shall be formulated by the development and reform department of the State Council in conjunction with relevant departments.

<a id="art-1010"></a>第一千零一十条　县级以上人民政府节能主管部门应当会同有关部门加强对重点用能单位的节能管理。  
重点用能单位应当每年向节能主管部门报送能源利用状况报告。节能主管部门应当对重点用能单位报送的能源利用状况报告进行审查。  
重点用能单位的范围适用《中华人民共和国节约能源法》的有关规定。

**Article 1010** : The energy conservation department of a people's government at or above the county level shall, in conjunction with relevant departments, strengthen energy conservation management of key energy-consuming units.  
Key energy-consuming units shall submit annual reports on their energy utilization status to the energy conservation department. The energy conservation department shall review the reports on energy utilization status submitted by key energy-consuming units.  
The scope of key energy-consuming units shall be subject to the relevant provisions of the Energy Conservation Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-1011"></a>第一千零一十一条　国家对家用电器等使用面广、耗能量大的用能产品，实行能源效率标识管理。能源效率标识管理的产品目录和具体管理办法，由国务院发展改革部门会同国务院市场监督管理部门制定并公布。

**Article 1011** : The State shall implement energy efficiency labeling management for energy-consuming products such as household appliances that are widely used and consume large amounts of energy. The product catalog and specific management measures for energy efficiency labeling management shall be formulated and published by the development and reform department of the State Council in conjunction with the market supervision and administration department of the State Council.

#### 第三节　能源绿色低碳转型

<a id="art-1012"></a>第一千零一十二条　国家支持优先开发利用可再生能源，合理开发、清洁高效利用化石能源，推进非化石能源安全可靠有序替代化石能源，提高非化石能源消费比重。

**Article 1012** : The State shall support the priority development and utilization of renewable energy, rational development and clean and efficient utilization of fossil energy, promote the safe, reliable, and orderly substitution of fossil energy with non-fossil energy, and increase the proportion of non-fossil energy consumption.

<a id="art-1013"></a>第一千零一十三条　国务院能源主管部门和国务院有关部门依照本法和《中华人民共和国能源法》、《中华人民共和国可再生能源法》等法律的规定，负责能源绿色低碳转型有关工作。

**Article 1013** : The energy authority of the State Council and relevant departments of the State Council shall, in accordance with this Law, the Energy Law of the People's Republic of China, the Renewable Energy Law of the People's Republic of China, and other laws, be responsible for the work related to the green and low-carbon transformation of energy.

<a id="art-1014"></a>第一千零一十四条　国家建立健全煤炭清洁高效利用机制，支持洁净煤技术的研究开发、推广应用，积极有序推进散煤替代，推动煤炭清洁高效燃烧和转化，促进煤炭分质分级利用，提升煤炭清洁高效利用水平。  
国家鼓励发展煤电联营，支持煤炭开采和上下游产业一体化布局，有序推进煤炭深加工产业发展，淘汰落后用煤技术和设备，采用先进煤电技术降低供电煤耗，推进煤电、钢铁、建材、化工等重点用煤行业节能降碳改造。

**Article 1014** : The State shall establish and improve the mechanism for clean and efficient utilization of coal, support the research, development, promotion, and application of clean coal technology, actively and orderly promote the substitution of scattered coal, promote the clean and efficient combustion and conversion of coal, facilitate the graded and classified utilization of coal, and enhance the level of clean and efficient utilization of coal.  
The State shall encourage the development of coal-electricity integration, support the integrated layout of coal mining and upstream and downstream industries, orderly promote the development of the coal deep processing industry, eliminate backward coal-using technologies and equipment, adopt advanced coal-electricity technology to reduce coal consumption for power supply, and promote energy conservation and carbon reduction transformation in key coal-using industries such as coal power, steel, building materials, and chemicals.

<a id="art-1015"></a>第一千零一十五条　国家鼓励、支持煤炭绿色智能开采，因地制宜采用保水开采、充填开采等绿色开采技术，减少对地质地貌和生态环境的影响。  
煤矿企业应当按照国家规定提升煤炭洗选加工水平，提高原煤入选率。  
县级以上地方人民政府和煤矿企业应当加强资源综合开发和系统治理，统筹开发利用煤炭共伴生资源，开展煤层气开采利用，提高煤矸石、煤泥等清洁利用水平，科学合理利用关闭煤矿残存资源和地下空间。

**Article 1015** : The State shall encourage and support green and intelligent coal mining, adopt green mining technologies such as water-preserved mining and backfill mining in accordance with local conditions, and reduce the impact on geological landforms and the ecological environment.  
Coal mining enterprises shall, in accordance with state regulations, improve the level of coal washing and processing, and increase the rate of raw coal preparation.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level and coal mining enterprises shall strengthen comprehensive resource development and systematic management, coordinate the development and utilization of coal-associated resources, carry out the extraction and utilization of coalbed methane, improve the clean utilization level of coal gangue, coal slime, etc., and scientifically and rationally utilize residual resources and underground space of closed coal mines.

<a id="art-1016"></a>第一千零一十六条　国家鼓励石油、天然气绿色高效开发，支持石油、天然气勘探开发与可再生能源融合发展。  
国家鼓励采用先进、集约的石油加工转换方式。  
国家支持合理开发利用可替代石油、天然气的新型燃料和工业原料。

**Article 1016** : The State shall encourage the green and efficient development of oil and natural gas, and support the integrated development of oil and natural gas exploration and exploitation with renewable energy.  
The State shall encourage the adoption of advanced and intensive oil processing and conversion methods.  
The State shall support the rational development and utilization of new fuels and industrial raw materials that can replace oil and natural gas.

<a id="art-1017"></a>第一千零一十七条　国家推进风能、太阳能开发利用，坚持集中式与分布式并举，加快风电和光伏发电基地建设，支持分布式风电和光伏发电就近开发利用，合理有序开发海上风电，积极发展光热发电。  
国家鼓励合理开发利用生物质能，促进海洋能规模化开发利用，因地制宜发展地热能。  
国家统筹水电开发和生态保护，严格控制开发建设小型水电站。  
国家积极安全有序发展核电。

**Article 1017** : The State shall promote the development and utilization of wind energy and solar energy, adhere to the simultaneous development of centralized and distributed forms, accelerate the construction of wind power and photovoltaic power bases, support the local development and utilization of distributed wind power and photovoltaic power, rationally and orderly develop offshore wind power, and actively develop solar thermal power generation.  
The State shall encourage the rational development and utilization of biomass energy, promote the large-scale development and utilization of ocean energy, and develop geothermal energy in accordance with local conditions.  
The State shall coordinate hydropower development and ecological protection, and strictly control the development and construction of small hydropower stations.  
The State shall actively, safely, and orderly develop nuclear power.

<a id="art-1018"></a>第一千零一十八条　国务院能源主管部门会同有关部门制定非化石能源开发利用中长期发展目标，按年度监测非化石能源开发利用情况，并向社会公布。

**Article 1018** : The energy authority of the State Council shall, in conjunction with relevant departments, formulate medium- and long-term development targets for the development and utilization of non-fossil energy, monitor the development and utilization of non-fossil energy on an annual basis, and publish the results to the public.

<a id="art-1019"></a>第一千零一十九条　国务院能源主管部门会同有关部门制定并组织实施可再生能源在能源消费中的最低比重目标。

**Article 1019** : The energy authority of the State Council shall, in conjunction with relevant departments, formulate and organize the implementation of minimum proportion targets for renewable energy in energy consumption.

<a id="art-1020"></a>第一千零二十条　国家完善可再生能源电力消纳保障机制。供电企业、售电企业、相关电力用户和使用自备电厂供电的企业等应当按照国家规定，承担消纳可再生能源发电量的责任。

**Article 1020** : The State shall improve the guarantee mechanism for the consumption of renewable energy electricity. Power supply enterprises, power sales enterprises, relevant electricity users, and enterprises using self-owned power plants for power supply shall, in accordance with state regulations, bear the responsibility for consuming the electricity generated from renewable energy.

<a id="art-1021"></a>第一千零二十一条　国家建立健全绿色能源消费促进机制，实施可再生能源绿色电力证书等制度，鼓励能源用户优先使用可再生能源等清洁低碳能源。

**Article 1021** The State shall establish and improve a mechanism for promoting green energy consumption, implement systems such as renewable energy green electricity certificates, and encourage energy users to give priority to using renewable energy and other clean and low-carbon energy.

<a id="art-1022"></a>第一千零二十二条　国家加快构建新型电力系统，加强电源电网协同建设，优化电力跨区域通道布局，发挥特高压输电通道稳定安全可靠作用，推进电网基础设施智能化改造和智能微电网建设，提高电网对可再生能源的接纳、配置和调控能力。

**Article 1022** The State shall accelerate the construction of a new power system, strengthen the coordinated construction of power sources and grids, optimize the layout of cross-regional power transmission corridors, give full play to the stable, safe and reliable role of ultra-high voltage transmission corridors, promote the intelligent transformation of power grid infrastructure and the construction of smart microgrids, and enhance the grid's capacity to accommodate, allocate and regulate renewable energy.

<a id="art-1023"></a>第一千零二十三条　国家合理布局、积极有序开发建设抽水蓄能电站，推进新型储能高质量发展，发挥各类储能在电力系统中的调节作用。

**Article 1023** The State shall rationally plan, actively and orderly develop and construct pumped-storage hydropower stations, promote the high-quality development of new energy storage, and give full play to the regulatory role of various types of energy storage in the power system.

<a id="art-1024"></a>第一千零二十四条　国家积极有序推进氢能开发利用，促进氢能产业高质量发展。

**Article 1024** The State shall actively and orderly promote the development and utilization of hydrogen energy, and promote the high-quality development of the hydrogen energy industry.

### 第四章　应对气候变化

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-1025"></a>第一千零二十五条　国家坚持减缓与适应并重，采取有效措施积极应对气候变化。

**Article 1025** The State shall adhere to the principle of equal emphasis on mitigation and adaptation, and take effective measures to actively address climate change.

<a id="art-1026"></a>第一千零二十六条　国务院生态环境主管部门负责全国的应对气候变化工作。国务院发展改革部门负责全国碳达峰碳中和工作的管理协调。国务院其他有关部门在各自职责范围内，负责应对气候变化与碳达峰碳中和有关工作。  
县级以上地方人民政府及其有关部门应当加强本行政区域的应对气候变化与碳达峰碳中和工作，采取减缓和适应措施，控制和减少温室气体排放。

**Article 1026** The competent department of the State Council in charge of ecological environment shall be responsible for the national climate change response work. The development and reform department of the State Council shall be responsible for the management and coordination of the national carbon peak and carbon neutrality work. Other relevant departments of the State Council shall, within their respective functions, be responsible for work related to climate change response and carbon peak and carbon neutrality.  
Local people's governments at or above the county level and their relevant departments shall strengthen the work on climate change response and carbon peak and carbon neutrality within their administrative regions, take mitigation and adaptation measures, and control and reduce greenhouse gas emissions.

<a id="art-1027"></a>第一千零二十七条　国家建立健全国务院有关部门参与的决策协调机制，统一部署应对气候变化与碳达峰碳中和有关工作，协调解决相关重大问题，督促重要目标任务落实。

**Article 1027** The State shall establish and improve a decision-making and coordination mechanism with the participation of relevant departments of the State Council to make unified arrangements for work related to climate change response and carbon peak and carbon neutrality, coordinate and resolve relevant major issues, and supervise the implementation of important objectives and tasks.

<a id="art-1028"></a>第一千零二十八条　国家加强温室气体与气候系统监测，建立健全气候变化监测发布制度。

**Article 1028** The State shall strengthen the monitoring of greenhouse gases and the climate system, and establish and improve the system for monitoring and releasing climate change information.

<a id="art-1029"></a>第一千零二十九条　国家建立应对气候变化信息共享机制，推动相关资源、数据交流共享。

**Article 1029** The State shall establish a mechanism for sharing information on climate change response, and promote the exchange and sharing of relevant resources and data.

<a id="art-1030"></a>第一千零三十条　国家通过加强生态系统保护、修复和可持续管理，发挥生态系统服务功能，增强应对气候变化的能力。

**Article 1030** The State shall, through strengthening the protection, restoration and sustainable management of ecosystems, give full play to ecosystem service functions, and enhance the capacity to address climate change.

<a id="art-1031"></a>第一千零三十一条　国家强化应对气候变化基础研究和科技支撑，把应对气候变化作为国家基础研究和科技创新的重点领域，加强关键核心技术研究开发、推广应用。

**Article 1031** The State shall strengthen basic research and scientific and technological support for climate change response, take climate change response as a key area of national basic research and scientific and technological innovation, and strengthen research, development, promotion and application of key core technologies.

#### 第二节　减缓气候变化与碳达峰碳中和

<a id="art-1032"></a>第一千零三十二条　国家将碳达峰碳中和目标要求全面融入国家发展规划，通过碳达峰碳中和目标推动减缓气候变化。  
国家实施碳排放总量和强度控制制度，基于碳达峰碳中和目标科学合理确定碳排放指标。

**Article 1032** The State shall fully integrate the target requirements for carbon peak and carbon neutrality into national development plans, and promote climate change mitigation through the carbon peak and carbon neutrality targets.  
The State shall implement a system for controlling the total amount and intensity of carbon emissions, and scientifically and rationally determine carbon emission indicators based on the carbon peak and carbon neutrality targets.

<a id="art-1033"></a>第一千零三十三条　推进碳达峰碳中和应当坚持全国统筹、节约优先、双轮驱动、内外通畅、防范风险的原则。

**Article 1033** To promote carbon peak and carbon neutrality, the principles of national coordination, priority on conservation, dual-wheel drive, unimpeded domestic and international circulation, and risk prevention shall be adhered to.

<a id="art-1034"></a>第一千零三十四条　国务院发展改革部门会同有关部门编制国家碳达峰碳中和行动方案并组织实施。省、自治区、直辖市人民政府结合实际情况，制定本行政区域碳达峰碳中和行动方案并组织实施。

**Article 1034** The development and reform department of the State Council shall, together with relevant departments, formulate the national action plan for carbon peak and carbon neutrality and organize its implementation. The people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government shall, in light of their actual conditions, formulate action plans for carbon peak and carbon neutrality within their respective administrative regions and organize their implementation.

<a id="art-1035"></a>第一千零三十五条　国家建立健全碳排放统计核算制度。  
国务院统计部门负责组织开展全国碳排放统计核算工作。  
国务院生态环境、市场监督管理部门会同有关部门组织制定重点行业的企业碳排放核算标准和技术规范。国务院生态环境、统计部门会同有关部门建立国家温室气体排放因子数据库并定期更新。  
本法所称温室气体排放因子，是指单位生产或者消费活动量的温室气体排放的系数。

**Article 1035** The State shall establish and improve a system for statistical accounting of carbon emissions.  
The statistical department of the State Council shall be responsible for organizing the national statistical accounting of carbon emissions.  
The departments of ecological environment and market regulation of the State Council shall, together with relevant departments, organize the formulation of enterprise carbon emission accounting standards and technical specifications for key industries. The departments of ecological environment and statistics of the State Council shall, together with relevant departments, establish a national database of greenhouse gas emission factors and update it regularly.  
For the purpose of this Law, the term "greenhouse gas emission factor" means the coefficient of greenhouse gas emission per unit of production or consumption activity.

<a id="art-1036"></a>第一千零三十六条　国务院生态环境、市场监督管理、发展改革等有关部门在各自职责范围内开展产品碳足迹管理，制定产品碳足迹核算标准和技术规范，建立产品碳足迹核算、分级管理、标识认证和信息披露制度。  
本法所称碳足迹，是指以二氧化碳当量表示的温室气体排放量和温室气体清除量的总和。

**Article 1036** Relevant departments of the State Council, such as those of ecological environment, market regulation, and development and reform, shall, within their respective functions, carry out product carbon footprint management, formulate product carbon footprint accounting standards and technical specifications, and establish a system for product carbon footprint accounting, hierarchical management, labeling and certification, and information disclosure.  
For the purpose of this Law, the term "carbon footprint" means the sum of greenhouse gas emissions and greenhouse gas removals expressed in carbon dioxide equivalent.

<a id="art-1037"></a>第一千零三十七条　国家建立全国碳排放权交易市场。纳入全国碳排放权交易市场的温室气体重点排放单位，应当按照国家规定履行强制减排责任。  
碳排放权交易产品包括碳排放配额和经国务院批准的其他现货交易产品。国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门制定年度碳排放配额总量和分配方案，并组织实施。  
温室气体重点排放单位应当采取有效措施控制温室气体排放，按照国家规定和国务院生态环境主管部门制定的技术规范，开展温室气体统计核算，编制温室气体年度排放报告，报省级人民政府生态环境主管部门核查后，按照规定的期限足额清缴碳排放配额。温室气体重点排放单位应当对排放统计核算数据、年度排放报告的真实性、准确性和完整性负责。  
温室气体重点排放单位名录由省级人民政府生态环境主管部门会同有关部门，依据国务院生态环境主管部门确定的条件制定。

**Article 1037** The State shall establish a national carbon emission rights trading market. Key greenhouse gas emitting entities included in the national carbon emission rights trading market shall, in accordance with national regulations, fulfill the responsibility of mandatory emission reduction.  
Carbon emission rights trading products include carbon emission allowances and other spot trading products approved by the State Council. The competent department of the State Council in charge of ecological environment shall, together with relevant departments, formulate the total amount of annual carbon emission allowances and the allocation plan, and organize their implementation.  
Key greenhouse gas emitting entities shall take effective measures to control greenhouse gas emissions, carry out greenhouse gas statistical accounting, prepare annual greenhouse gas emission reports in accordance with national regulations and the technical specifications formulated by the competent department of the State Council in charge of ecological environment, and after verification by the competent department of ecological environment of the provincial people's government, pay off the carbon emission allowances in full within the prescribed time limit. Key greenhouse gas emitting entities shall be responsible for the truthfulness, accuracy and completeness of the emission statistical accounting data and the annual emission reports.  
The list of key greenhouse gas emitting entities shall be formulated by the competent department of ecological environment of the provincial people's government together with relevant departments, based on the conditions determined by the competent department of the State Council in charge of ecological environment.

<a id="art-1038"></a>第一千零三十八条　国家建立全国温室气体自愿减排交易市场，鼓励温室气体自愿减排交易。  
单位和个人可以按照国家规定，参与温室气体自愿减排交易。  
申请温室气体自愿减排交易的项目应当有利于降碳增汇，能够避免、减少温室气体排放，或者实现温室气体的清除。

**Article 1038** The State shall establish a national greenhouse gas voluntary emission reduction trading market, and encourage voluntary greenhouse gas emission reduction trading.  
Entities and individuals may, in accordance with national regulations, participate in greenhouse gas voluntary emission reduction trading.  
Projects applying for greenhouse gas voluntary emission reduction trading shall be conducive to carbon reduction and sink enhancement, capable of avoiding or reducing greenhouse gas emissions, or achieving greenhouse gas removal.

<a id="art-1039"></a>第一千零三十九条　国务院生态环境主管部门负责碳排放权交易、温室气体自愿减排交易及相关活动的监督管理。国务院有关部门在各自职责范围内，负责碳排放权交易、温室气体自愿减排交易及相关活动的监督管理。

**Article 1039** The competent department of the State Council in charge of ecological environment shall be responsible for the supervision and management of carbon emission rights trading, greenhouse gas voluntary emission reduction trading and related activities. Relevant departments of the State Council shall, within their respective functions, be responsible for the supervision and management of carbon emission rights trading, greenhouse gas voluntary emission reduction trading and related activities.

<a id="art-1040"></a>第一千零四十条　国家建立健全生态系统碳汇监测核算体系，开展森林、草原、湿地、海洋、土壤等碳储量本底调查和评估，加强生态的保护与修复，巩固和提升生态系统碳汇能力。

**Article 1040** The State shall establish and improve the monitoring and accounting system for ecosystem carbon sinks, carry out baseline surveys and assessments of carbon stocks in forests, grasslands, wetlands, oceans, soils, etc., strengthen ecological protection and restoration, and consolidate and enhance the capacity of ecosystem carbon sinks.

#### 第三节　适应气候变化

<a id="art-1041"></a>第一千零四十一条　国家制定实施适应气候变化战略，坚持预防为主、科学适应，采取有效措施降低气候变化不利影响和风险，建设气候适应型社会。  
省、自治区、直辖市人民政府结合实际情况，制定本行政区域适应气候变化行动方案，确定适应气候变化的重点领域、区域和措施，并组织实施。

**Article 1041** The State shall formulate and implement strategies for adapting to climate change, adhere to the principle of prevention first and scientific adaptation, and take effective measures to reduce the adverse impacts and risks of climate change, and build a climate-resilient society.  
The people's governments of provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government shall, based on actual conditions, formulate action plans for adapting to climate change within their respective administrative regions, identify key areas, regions, and measures for climate change adaptation, and organize their implementation.

<a id="art-1042"></a>第一千零四十二条　国家根据不同区域特点开展区域适应气候变化行动，提升重大战略区域适应气候变化的能力。

**Article 1042** The State shall, based on the characteristics of different regions, carry out regional climate change adaptation actions, and enhance the capacity of major strategic regions to adapt to climate change.

<a id="art-1043"></a>第一千零四十三条　编制国土空间规划应当充分考虑气候变化的影响和风险，加强对气候资源条件、气候变化影响和风险的评估。

**Article 1043** In formulating territorial spatial plans, full consideration shall be given to the impacts and risks of climate change, and the assessment of climatic resource conditions, climate change impacts and risks shall be strengthened.

<a id="art-1044"></a>第一千零四十四条　国务院有关部门在各自职责范围内，加强对水、陆地、海洋等生态系统，以及农业与粮食安全、健康与公共卫生、基础设施与重大工程、城市与人居环境、气候变化敏感产业、文化遗产等经济社会重点领域的气候变化影响和风险评估，提升重点领域适应气候变化的能力。

**Article 1044** The relevant departments of the State Council shall, within their respective functions and duties, strengthen the assessment of climate change impacts and risks on ecosystems such as water, land, and oceans, as well as on key economic and social sectors such as agriculture and food security, health and public health, infrastructure and major projects, cities and human settlements, climate-sensitive industries, and cultural heritage, and enhance the capacity of key sectors to adapt to climate change.

<a id="art-1045"></a>第一千零四十五条　国家建立健全气候变化影响下灾害综合风险监测预警和评估制度，提升多灾种、灾害链风险综合预警和评估能力。

**Article 1045** The State shall establish and improve a system for comprehensive disaster risk monitoring, early warning and assessment under the influence of climate change, and enhance the capacity for comprehensive early warning and assessment of multi-hazard and disaster chain risks.

<a id="art-1046"></a>第一千零四十六条　国家加强对极端天气气候事件引发的自然灾害综合治理，采取有效措施改善城乡防灾基础条件，优化重大基础设施空间布局，强化应急指挥处置、抢险队伍建设，加强应急装备物资储备，提升极端天气气候事件下综合救援和公众安全保障能力。

**Article 1046** The State shall strengthen comprehensive management of natural disasters caused by extreme weather and climate events, take effective measures to improve the basic conditions for disaster prevention in urban and rural areas, optimize the spatial layout of major infrastructure, strengthen emergency command and response and the building of rescue teams, enhance the stockpiling of emergency equipment and supplies, and improve the capacity for comprehensive rescue and public safety protection under extreme weather and climate events.

<a id="art-1047"></a>第一千零四十七条　国家鼓励开展气候变化健康风险和适应能力评估，加强气候敏感疾病的监测预警和防控，推进气候变化健康适应行动，提高气候变化和极端天气气候事件下公众健康适应水平。

**Article 1047** The State shall encourage the assessment of climate change health risks and adaptation capacity, strengthen the surveillance, early warning, prevention and control of climate-sensitive diseases, advance health adaptation actions to climate change, and improve the level of public health adaptation under climate change and extreme weather and climate events.

#### 第四节　国际合作

<a id="art-1048"></a>第一千零四十八条　国家坚持共同但有区别的责任原则、公平原则、各自能力原则，积极参与和引领全球气候治理，推动构建公平合理、合作共赢的全球气候治理体系。

**Article 1048** The State shall adhere to the principles of common but differentiated responsibilities, equity, and respective capabilities, actively participate in and lead global climate governance, and promote the building of a fair, reasonable, and win-win global climate governance system.

<a id="art-1049"></a>第一千零四十九条　国家根据国情、发展阶段和实际能力，承担应对气候变化领域相应的国际义务。  
国务院生态环境主管部门会同有关部门定期编制国家自主贡献、国家温室气体清单和履约报告。

**Article 1049** The State shall, in light of its national conditions, stage of development, and actual capabilities, undertake corresponding international obligations in the field of addressing climate change.  
The competent department of ecological environment of the State Council shall, together with the relevant departments, periodically prepare nationally determined contributions, national greenhouse gas inventories, and compliance reports.

<a id="art-1050"></a>第一千零五十条　国务院有关部门在各自职责范围内开展应对气候变化多边、双边国际交流与合作，推动应对气候变化领域规则和标准衔接互认。

**Article 1050** The relevant departments of the State Council shall, within their respective functions and duties, conduct multilateral and bilateral international exchanges and cooperation on addressing climate change, and promote the alignment and mutual recognition of rules and standards in the field of addressing climate change.

<a id="art-1051"></a>第一千零五十一条　国家加强应对气候变化领域的国际科学技术合作与交流，支持国内外优秀科学技术人才合作研究开发，鼓励企业、高等学校、科研机构开展可再生能源、储能、氢能、碳捕集利用与封存等技术交流。

**Article 1051** The State shall strengthen international scientific and technological cooperation and exchanges in the field of addressing climate change, support the cooperative research and development of outstanding scientific and technological talents at home and abroad, and encourage enterprises, higher education institutions, and scientific research institutions to conduct technical exchanges on renewable energy, energy storage, hydrogen energy, carbon capture, utilization and storage, and other technologies.

## 第五编　法律责任和附则

### 第一章　法律责任通则

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-1052"></a>第一千零五十二条　任何单位和个人有污染环境、破坏生态和违反绿色低碳义务的行为的，应当严格依法追究其法律责任。  
法律责任的确定，应当与污染环境、破坏生态和违反绿色低碳义务的行为的事实、性质、情节、危害后果相当。

**Article 1052** Where any entity or individual pollutes the environment, damages the ecosystem, or violates green and low-carbon obligations, its legal liability shall be strictly investigated according to law.  
The determination of legal liability shall be proportionate to the facts, nature, circumstances, and harmful consequences of the act of polluting the environment, damaging the ecosystem, or violating green and low-carbon obligations.

<a id="art-1053"></a>第一千零五十三条　实施污染环境、破坏生态等违法行为的，除有证据足以证明其没有过错外，应当承担行政责任。  
实施污染环境、破坏生态等行为，造成他人民事权益损害的，不论有无过错，应当承担民事责任。

**Article 1053** Any person who commits an illegal act such as polluting the environment or damaging the ecosystem shall bear administrative liability, unless there is sufficient evidence to prove that there is no fault.  
Any person who commits an act such as polluting the environment or damaging the ecosystem, causing damage to the civil rights and interests of others, shall bear civil liability, regardless of fault or no fault.

<a id="art-1054"></a>第一千零五十四条　造成环境污染、生态破坏等危害后果的违法行为在五年内被发现的，应当依法给予行政处罚；其他违法行为在二年内被发现的，应当依法给予行政处罚。上述期限自违法行为发生之日起计算，违法行为有连续或者继续状态的，从行为终了之日起计算。  
提起生态环境民事诉讼的诉讼时效期间为五年。诉讼时效期间自当事人知道或者应当知道受到损害以及责任人之日起计算。

**Article 1054** If an illegal act causing harmful consequences such as environmental pollution or ecological damage is discovered within five years, an administrative penalty shall be imposed according to law; if other illegal acts are discovered within two years, an administrative penalty shall be imposed according to law. The aforesaid period shall be calculated from the date on which the illegal act is committed; if the illegal act is continuous or ongoing, it shall be calculated from the date on which the act ends.  
The limitation of action for bringing an ecological and environmental civil action is five years. The limitation period shall be calculated from the date on which the party knows or should have known of the damage and the liable person.

<a id="art-1055"></a>第一千零五十五条　同一个违法行为违反多个生态环境法律规范应当给予罚款处罚的，按照罚款数额高的规定处罚。

**Article 1055** Where the same illegal act violates multiple ecological and environmental legal provisions and a fine penalty is to be imposed, the penalty shall be imposed in accordance with the provision specifying the higher fine amount.

<a id="art-1056"></a>第一千零五十六条　实施污染环境、破坏生态等违法行为，有恶意损害生态环境、拒不改正违法行为、造成严重危害后果、多次实施违法行为被处罚等法定情形的，依法从重处罚。  
实施污染环境、破坏生态等违法行为，有主动消除或者减轻危害后果、有效采取生态环境修复措施、及时缴纳赔偿金等法定情形的，依法从轻或者减轻处罚。违法行为轻微并及时改正，没有造成危害后果的，不予处罚；初次违法且危害后果轻微并及时改正的，可以不予处罚。

**Article 1056** Where a person commits an illegal act such as polluting the environment or damaging the ecosystem and there are statutory circumstances such as malicious damage to the ecological environment, refusal to correct the illegal act, causing serious harmful consequences, or having been punished for multiple illegal acts, a severe punishment shall be imposed according to law.  
Where a person commits an illegal act such as polluting the environment or damaging the ecosystem and there are statutory circumstances such as voluntarily eliminating or mitigating the harmful consequences, effectively taking measures to restore the ecological environment, or promptly paying compensation, a lighter or mitigated punishment shall be imposed according to law. If the illegal act is minor and is promptly corrected without causing harmful consequences, no punishment shall be imposed; if it is a first-time illegal act with minor harmful consequences and is promptly corrected, punishment may be waived.

<a id="art-1057"></a>第一千零五十七条　实施行政处罚，适用违法行为发生时的生态环境法律、法规、规章的规定。但是，作出行政处罚决定时，生态环境法律、法规、规章已被修改或者废止，且新的规定处罚较轻或者不认为是违法的，适用新的规定。

**Article 1057** In imposing an administrative penalty, the provisions of the ecological and environmental laws, regulations, and rules at the time of the illegal act shall apply. However, if, at the time of making the decision on administrative penalty, the ecological and environmental laws, regulations, and rules have been amended or abolished, and the new provisions impose a lighter penalty or do not deem the act illegal, the new provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-1058"></a>第一千零五十八条　因污染环境、破坏生态等行为应当承担民事责任、行政责任、刑事责任的，承担行政责任或者刑事责任不影响承担民事责任。财产不足以支付的，优先承担民事责任。  
污染环境、破坏生态等违法行为构成犯罪，人民法院判处拘役或者有期徒刑时，行政机关已经实施行政拘留的，应当依法折抵相应刑期；已经实施行政罚款的，应当依法折抵相应罚金。

**Article 1058** Where a person shall bear civil liability, administrative liability, or criminal liability for acts such as polluting the environment or damaging the ecosystem, bearing administrative liability or criminal liability shall not affect the bearing of civil liability. If the property is insufficient to cover all, civil liability shall be satisfied first.  
Where an illegal act such as polluting the environment or damaging the ecosystem constitutes a crime, and when the People's Court imposes criminal detention or fixed-term imprisonment, if an administrative organ has already imposed administrative detention, it shall be offset against the term of imprisonment according to law; if an administrative fine has already been imposed, it shall be offset against the corresponding fine according to law.

<a id="art-1059"></a>第一千零五十九条　由于不可抗力造成污染环境、破坏生态损害的，不承担赔偿责任；法律另有规定的除外。对海洋环境造成污染损害的，适用本条第三款规定。  
损害是由受害人故意造成的，有关责任人不承担赔偿责任；损害是由受害人重大过失造成的，可以减轻赔偿责任。  
完全属于下列情形之一，经过及时采取合理措施，仍然不能避免对海洋环境造成污染损害的，造成污染损害的有关责任人免予承担责任：  
（一）战争；  
（二）不可抗拒的自然灾害；  
（三）负责灯塔或者其他助航设备的主管部门，在履行职责时的疏忽，或者其他过失行为。

**Article 1059** If pollution of the environment or damage to the ecosystem is caused by force majeure, no compensation liability shall be borne, unless otherwise provided by law. If pollution damage is caused to the marine environment, the provisions of paragraph 3 of this Article shall apply.  
If the damage is caused intentionally by the victim, the relevant liable person shall not bear compensation liability; if the damage is caused by gross negligence of the victim, the compensation liability may be mitigated.  
If it falls entirely under any of the following circumstances and, after timely taking reasonable measures, pollution damage to the marine environment still cannot be avoided, the relevant liable person causing the pollution damage shall be exempted from liability:  
(1) War;  
(2) Irresistible natural disasters;  
(3) Negligence or other wrongful acts of the competent authority responsible for lighthouses or other navigation aids in the performance of its duties.

<a id="art-1060"></a>第一千零六十条　企业事业单位和其他生产经营者违法排放、倾倒、处置污染物、废弃物或者其他物质，受到罚款处罚，被责令改正的，依法作出处罚决定的部门或者机构应当组织复查，发现其继续实施该违法行为、拒不改正或者拒绝、阻挠复查的，可以自责令改正之日的次日起，按照原罚款数额按日连续处罚，并及时采取措施制止违法行为。  
前款规定的原罚款数额，在法定幅度内按照污染防治设施的运行成本、违法行为造成的直接损失或者违法所得等因素予以确定。  
地方性法规可以根据生态环境保护的实际需要，增加第一款规定的按日连续处罚的违法行为的种类。

**Article 1060** Where an enterprise, public institution, or other producer or business operator illegally discharges, dumps, or disposes of pollutants, wastes, or other substances, is subject to a fine penalty, and is ordered to make correction, the department or institution that made the penalty decision according to law shall organize a review. If it is found that the illegal act continues, or that the person refuses to make correction or refuses or obstructs the review, the department or institution may, from the day following the date of the order to make correction, impose a daily cumulative penalty based on the original fine amount, and shall promptly take measures to stop the illegal act.  
The original fine amount as mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be determined within the statutory range based on factors such as the operational cost of pollution prevention facilities, the direct losses caused by the illegal act, or the illegal gains.  
Local regulations may, in light of the actual needs of ecological and environmental protection, increase the types of illegal acts subject to daily cumulative penalties as specified in the first paragraph.

#### 第二节　责任主体

<a id="art-1061"></a>第一千零六十一条　地方人民政府不依法履行职责，致使本地区生态环境质量问题突出、生态环境状况恶化的，应当对其负责人依法给予处分或者处理，并可以向社会公开。

**Article 1061** Where a local people's government fails to perform its duties according to law, resulting in prominent eco-environmental quality problems and deterioration of the eco-environment in the region, the persons in charge thereof shall be given sanctions or dealt with according to law, and may be publicly disclosed.

<a id="art-1062"></a>第一千零六十二条　地方各级人民政府、负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构有下列情形之一的，对负有责任的领导人员和直接责任人员给予记过、记大过或者降级处分；造成严重后果的，给予撤职或者开除处分：  
（一）未依法作出行政许可或者办理批准文件；  
（二）对生态环境违法行为进行包庇；  
（三）依法应当作出责令停业、关闭的决定而未作出；  
（四）对未按照许可证规定排放污染物、采用逃避监管的方式排放污染物、造成环境污染或者生态破坏事故以及不落实生态保护措施造成生态破坏等行为，发现或者接到举报未及时依法查处；  
（五）违法查封、扣押企业事业单位和其他生产经营者的有关场所、船舶、设施、设备、工具、物品；  
（六）篡改、伪造或者指使篡改、伪造监测数据；  
（七）应当依法公开生态环境信息而未公开；  
（八）违法收取费用，或者将依法收取的费用截留、挤占或者挪作他用；  
（九）对应当移送有关机关处理的案件不依法移送；  
（十）法律法规规定的其他违法情形。

**Article 1062** Where local people's governments at all levels, departments, and institutions responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration are under any of the following circumstances, the leading persons with responsibility and the directly responsible persons shall be given a demerit, a major demerit, or a demotion sanction; if serious consequences are caused, they shall be given a removal from office or expulsion sanction:  
(1) failing to grant an administrative license or handle approval documents according to law;  
(2) covering up illegal eco-environmental acts;  
(3) failing to make a decision ordering suspension of business or closure as legally required;  
(4) upon discovery or receipt of a report, failing to timely investigate and handle according to law acts such as discharging pollutants not in accordance with permit provisions, discharging pollutants by means evading supervision, causing environmental pollution or ecological damage accidents, and failing to implement ecological protection measures resulting in ecological damage;  
(5) illegally sealing up or impounding relevant premises, vessels, installations, equipment, tools, or articles of enterprises, institutions, and other producers and business operators;  
(6) falsifying, forging, or instructing the falsification or forgery of monitoring data;  
(7) failing to disclose eco-environmental information that shall be disclosed according to law;  
(8) illegally collecting fees, or intercepting, misappropriating, or diverting fees collected according to law for other purposes;  
(9) failing to transfer according to law a case that shall be transferred to a relevant organ for handling;  
(10) other illegal circumstances as provided for by laws and regulations.

<a id="art-1063"></a>第一千零六十三条　规划编制机关未依法组织生态环境影响评价，或者组织生态环境影响评价时弄虚作假或者有失职行为，造成生态环境影响评价严重失实的；规划审批机关对依法应当编写有关生态环境影响的篇章或者说明而未编写的规划草案，依法应当附送生态环境影响报告书而未附送的专项规划草案，违法予以批准的，对负有责任的领导人员和直接责任人员依法给予处分。  
生态环境主管部门在生态环境影响报告书、报告表、登记表的审批、审核、备案等环节违法收取费用，索取、收受财物，或者谋取其他不正当利益的，应当责令退还或者予以没收；对负有责任的领导人员和直接责任人员依法给予处分。

**Article 1063** Where a planning compilation authority fails to organize an ecological environment impact assessment according to law, or commits fraud or dereliction of duty when organizing such assessment, causing the assessment result to be seriously untrue; or where a planning approval authority illegally approves a draft plan that, according to law, should have included a chapter or explanation on ecological environment impact but did not, or a special plan draft that, according to law, should have had an ecological environment impact report attached but did not, the leading persons with responsibility and the directly responsible persons shall be given sanctions according to law.  
Where an ecological environment competent department illegally collects fees, extorts or accepts property, or seeks other illegitimate benefits in the examination and approval, review, or filing of ecological environment impact reports, report forms, or registration forms, it shall be ordered to refund or confiscate such fees or property; and the leading persons with responsibility and the directly responsible persons shall be given sanctions according to law.

<a id="art-1064"></a>第一千零六十四条　企业事业单位、其他生产经营者和个人污染环境、破坏生态或者有本法规定的其他违法情形的，应当依法承担法律责任；造成环境污染、生态破坏事故或者有其他法定情形的，企业事业单位、其他生产经营者的法定代表人、主要负责人、直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员应当依法承担法律责任。

**Article 1064** Enterprises, institutions, other producers and business operators, and individuals that pollute the environment, damage the ecology, or commit other illegal acts as provided for in this Law shall bear legal liability according to law. Where an environmental pollution or ecological damage accident is caused or other statutory circumstances exist, the legal representative, principal responsible person, directly responsible supervisor, and other directly responsible persons of the enterprise, institution, or other producer or business operator shall bear legal liability according to law.

<a id="art-1065"></a>第一千零六十五条　企业事业单位、其他生产经营者和个人造成环境污染、生态破坏的，应当依法承担消除污染、修复受损生态环境的责任；无法消除或者修复的，实施替代性治理或者修复措施。  
违法行为人变更的，由承继其权利义务的主体负责治理或者修复。

**Article 1065** Enterprises, institutions, other producers and business operators, and individuals that cause environmental pollution or ecological damage shall bear the responsibility according to law for eliminating pollution and restoring the damaged ecological environment; if elimination or restoration is impossible, they shall implement alternative treatment or restoration measures.  
If the person who committed the illegal act changes, the entity that succeeds to its rights and obligations shall be responsible for the treatment or restoration.

<a id="art-1066"></a>第一千零六十六条　土壤污染及其相关的地下水污染责任人无法认定，土地使用权人未依照本法规定履行污染风险管控和修复义务，造成他人民事权益损害的，应当依法承担民事侵权责任。  
违反本法规定，销售的机动车船、非道路移动机械、发动机不符合污染物排放标准的，销售者应当负责修理、更换、退货；给购买者造成损失的，销售者应当赔偿损失。

**Article 1066** Where the person responsible for soil pollution and related groundwater pollution cannot be identified, and the land use right holder fails to perform the obligations of pollution risk control and restoration according to the provisions of this Law, causing damage to the civil rights and interests of others, the land use right holder shall bear civil tort liability according to law.  
Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, the motor vehicles, vessels, non-road mobile machinery, or engines sold do not meet the pollutant emission standards, the seller shall be responsible for repair, replacement, or return of goods; if losses are caused to the buyer, the seller shall compensate for the losses.

<a id="art-1067"></a>第一千零六十七条　受委托从事生态环境服务活动的生态环境技术服务机构违反本法规定，对造成的环境污染、生态破坏等负有责任的，除依照本法及有关法律法规的规定予以处罚外，还应当与造成环境污染、生态破坏等的委托人承担连带责任。

**Article 1067** Where an ecological environment technical service institution entrusted to engage in ecological environment service activities violates the provisions of this Law and bears responsibility for the environmental pollution or ecological damage caused, in addition to being punished in accordance with the provisions of this Law and relevant laws and regulations, it shall also bear joint and several liability with the client that caused the environmental pollution or ecological damage.

<a id="art-1068"></a>第一千零六十八条　获准倾倒海洋废弃物的单位和产生工业固体废物的单位违反本法规定，未尽到核实、监督等义务委托他人实施废弃物海洋倾倒作业或者运输、利用、处置工业固体废物的，除依照本法及有关法律法规的规定予以处罚外，还应当与造成环境污染、生态破坏的受托人承担连带责任。  
违反本法规定，将中华人民共和国境外的固体废物、放射性废物和被放射性污染的物品输入境内的，承运人对固体废物、放射性废物和被放射性污染的物品的退运、处置，与进口者承担连带责任。

**Article 1068** Where a unit permitted to dump marine wastes or a unit generating industrial solid waste violates the provisions of this Law, fails to fulfill obligations such as verification and supervision, and entrusts another person to carry out marine waste dumping operations or to transport, utilize, or dispose of industrial solid waste, in addition to being punished in accordance with the provisions of this Law and relevant laws and regulations, it shall bear joint and several liability with the trustee that caused environmental pollution or ecological damage.  
Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, solid waste, radioactive waste, or articles contaminated by radioactivity from outside the People's Republic of China are brought into the territory, the carrier shall bear joint and several liability with the importer for the return and disposal of such solid waste, radioactive waste, or articles contaminated by radioactivity.

<a id="art-1069"></a>第一千零六十九条　企业事业单位有本法规定的违法情形，其负有责任的领导人员和直接责任人员属于公职人员的，依法给予处分。

**Article 1069** Where an enterprise or institution has an illegal circumstance as provided for in this Law, and the leading persons with responsibility and the directly responsible persons thereof are public officials, they shall be given sanctions according to law.

<a id="art-1070"></a>第一千零七十条　因污染环境、破坏生态等造成他人民事权益损害应当承担民事责任，本法未作规定的，依照《中华人民共和国民法典》的规定承担民事责任。

**Article 1070** Where civil liability shall be borne for damage caused to the civil rights and interests of others by environmental pollution, ecological damage, or otherwise, and this Law provides no provisions, civil liability shall be borne in accordance with the provisions of the Civil Code of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-1071"></a>第一千零七十一条　违反本法规定，构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 1071** Whoever violates the provisions of this Law and constitutes a crime shall be investigated for criminal liability according to law.

#### 第三节　责任追究

<a id="art-1072"></a>第一千零七十二条　负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构应当按照职责分工及时查处生态环境违法案件，依法追究法律责任。  
依法应当作出行政处罚而未作出的，上级人民政府负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门可以直接作出行政处罚的决定。

**Article 1072** Departments and institutions responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration shall, in accordance with the division of their duties, promptly investigate and handle illegal eco-environmental cases and pursue legal liability according to law.  
Where an administrative penalty should be imposed according to law but has not been imposed, the department responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration of the people's government at the higher level may directly make a decision on the administrative penalty.

<a id="art-1073"></a>第一千零七十三条　违反法律规定，污染环境、破坏生态等给国家造成损失的，由设区的市级以上地方人民政府或者其指定的部门、机构与责任人按照有关规定进行磋商，要求其承担生态环境损害赔偿责任；磋商未达成一致的，可以向人民法院提起诉讼。  
前款规定的地方人民政府、部门、机构不进行磋商，或者磋商未达成一致且不提起诉讼的，人民检察院可以对责任人依法向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 1073** Where, in violation of legal provisions, environmental pollution, ecological damage, or other acts cause losses to the State, the local people's government at or above the level of a city divided into districts, or the department or institution designated by it, shall consult with the person responsible in accordance with relevant provisions and require that person to bear compensation liability for ecological environment damage; if no agreement is reached through consultation, it may file a lawsuit with the People's Court.  
If the local people's government, department, or institution as provided for in the preceding paragraph fails to conduct consultation, or fails to reach an agreement through consultation and does not file a lawsuit, the People's Procuratorate may file a lawsuit against the person responsible with the People's Court according to law.

<a id="art-1074"></a>第一千零七十四条　污染海洋环境、破坏海洋生态给国家造成损失的，由依照本法规定行使海洋生态环境监督管理权的部门对责任人提出损害赔偿要求，可以向人民法院提起诉讼。  
责任人不承担损害赔偿责任，前款规定的部门不提起诉讼的，人民检察院可以依法向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 1074** Where pollution of the marine environment or damage to the marine ecology causes losses to the State, the department exercising the power of marine ecological environment supervision and administration according to this Law shall make a claim for damages against the person responsible, and may file a lawsuit with the People's Court.  
If the person responsible does not bear compensation liability for damages, and the department provided for in the preceding paragraph does not file a lawsuit, the People's Procuratorate may file a lawsuit with the People's Court according to law.

<a id="art-1075"></a>第一千零七十五条　违反法律规定，污染环境、破坏生态等损害社会公共利益的，人民检察院或者符合本法规定的社会组织可以依法向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 1075** Where, in violation of legal provisions, environmental pollution, ecological damage, or other acts harm the public interest, the People's Procuratorate or a social organization that meets the requirements of this Law may file a lawsuit with the People's Court according to law.

<a id="art-1076"></a>第一千零七十六条　人民检察院在履行职责中发现地方人民政府或者负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构违法行使职权或者不作为，给国家造成损失或者损害社会公共利益的，可以依法提起诉讼。

**Article 1076** Where, in performing its duties, the People's Procuratorate discovers that a local people's government or a department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration has exercised its powers illegally or failed to act, causing losses to the State or harming the public interest, it may file a lawsuit according to law.

<a id="art-1077"></a>第一千零七十七条　污染环境、破坏生态等侵害他人合法权益的，可以由当事人协商解决，也可以向负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构申请调解处理；调解不成的，可以向人民法院提起诉讼。当事人也可以直接向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 1077** Where environmental pollution, ecological damage, or other acts infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of others, the parties may resolve the matter through negotiation, or may apply to the department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration for mediation; if mediation fails, they may file a lawsuit with the People's Court. The parties may also directly file a lawsuit with the People's Court.

<a id="art-1078"></a>第一千零七十八条　国家鼓励排放噪声、油烟、异味的单位和公共场所管理者等与受到侵害的单位和个人友好协商，通过调整生产经营、施工作业时间等，采取预防、补救措施，支付补偿金、异地安置等方式，妥善解决纠纷。

**Article 1078** The State encourages units that discharge noise, cooking fumes, or odors, and managers of public places, among others, to amicably negotiate with the harmed units and individuals, and to properly resolve disputes through measures such as adjusting production and business operations or construction work hours, taking preventive and remedial measures, paying compensation, or relocating to another site.

<a id="art-1079"></a>第一千零七十九条　对正在实施或者即将实施的污染环境、破坏生态等行为，不及时制止将使当事人合法权益或者生态环境受到难以弥补的损害重大风险的，可以依照《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的有关规定，向人民法院申请采取禁止令保全措施，责令立即停止污染环境、破坏生态等行为。

**Article 1079** For acts of environmental pollution, ecological damage, or otherwise that are being or about to be committed, if failure to stop them promptly will subject the lawful rights and interests of a party or the ecological environment to substantial risk of irreparable damage, an application may be made to the People's Court, in accordance with the relevant provisions of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, for the adoption of preservation measures by injunction, ordering the immediate cessation of such acts of environmental pollution or ecological damage.

<a id="art-1080"></a>第一千零八十条　因污染环境、破坏生态等造成他人损害的，行为人应当就法律规定的不承担责任或者减轻责任的情形，及其行为与损害之间不存在因果关系依法承担举证责任。

**Article 1080** Where damage is caused to others by environmental pollution, ecological damage, or otherwise, the actor shall bear the burden of adducing evidence according to law with respect to the circumstances of non-liability or mitigation of liability as provided for by law, and the absence of a causal relationship between the actor's conduct and the damage.

<a id="art-1081"></a>第一千零八十一条　依据[本法第一千零七十三条](#art-1073)、第一千零七十五条规定向人民法院提起诉讼的机关和组织，应当提供以下证明：  
（一）行为人实施了污染环境、破坏生态等行为；  
（二）行为违反法律规定；  
（三）生态环境损害情况；  
（四）生态环境损失费用、生态环境修复费用及其他费用；  
（五）其他应当证明的事项。  
依据[本法第一千零七十四条](#art-1074)规定提起诉讼的，应当提供前款第一项、第三项至第五项规定的证明。

**Article 1081** : The organ or organization that files a lawsuit with the People's Court pursuant to Articles 1073 and 1075 of this Law shall provide the following evidence:  
(1) the act of the perpetrator polluting the environment or damaging the ecosystem;  
(2) the act violates the provisions of the law;  
(3) the circumstances of ecological and environmental damage;  
(4) the costs of ecological and environmental losses, the costs of ecological and environmental restoration, and other costs;  
(5) other matters that shall be evidenced.  
Where a lawsuit is filed pursuant to Article 1074 of this Law, the evidence specified in items (1), (3) to (5) of the preceding paragraph shall be provided.

<a id="art-1082"></a>第一千零八十二条　负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构以及人民检察院等，可以依法支持因污染环境、破坏生态受到损害的当事人向人民法院提起诉讼。  
国家鼓励法律服务机构和律师为前款规定的当事人提供法律援助。

**Article 1082** : The departments and institutions responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration, as well as the People's Procuratorate, may support, according to law, the party that has suffered damage due to pollution of the environment or damage to the ecosystem in filing a lawsuit with the People's Court.  
The State encourages legal service institutions and lawyers to provide legal aid to the party specified in the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-1083"></a>第一千零八十三条　相关部门在受理举报、监督检查或者在查处案件等工作中，发现违法线索或者案件属于负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构职责的，应当及时移送。  
负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构，以及人民法院、人民检察院在依法查处生态环境违法行为或者办理相关案件过程中，发现存在涉嫌犯罪行为的，应当移送具有侦查、调查职权的机关。

**Article 1083** : Where, in the course of handling reports, supervision and inspection, or investigating and dealing with cases, the relevant department discovers that a clue of an illegal act or a case falls within the functions and duties of a department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration, it shall transfer the matter in a timely manner.  
Where, in the process of investigating and dealing with ecological and environmental illegal acts or handling related cases according to law, a department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration, as well as the People's Court or the People's Procuratorate, discovers the existence of an act suspected of being a crime, it shall transfer the matter to the organ with the power of investigation or inquiry.

<a id="art-1084"></a>第一千零八十四条　上级人民政府及其生态环境主管部门应当加强对下级人民政府及其有关部门、机构生态环境保护工作的监督。发现有关工作人员有违法行为，依法应当给予处分的，应当移送其任免机关、单位或者监察机关依法处理。

**Article 1084** : The people's government at a higher level and its competent department of ecology and environment shall strengthen supervision over the ecological and environmental protection work of the people's government at a lower level and its relevant departments and institutions. Where it is discovered that a relevant staff member has committed an illegal act and shall be given a sanction according to law, the matter shall be transferred to the appointing and removing organ, unit, or the supervisory organ for handling according to law.

### 第二章　法律责任分则

#### 第一节　违反生态环境监测管理规定

<a id="art-1085"></a>第一千零八十五条　违反本法第一编第四章等规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1085** : Where the provisions of Chapter IV of Part I of this Law are violated, the provisions of this Section shall apply to impose administrative penalties.

<a id="art-1086"></a>第一千零八十六条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，可以责令限制生产、停产整治：  
（一）未对所排放的污染物、养殖尾水、土壤污染状况自行监测，未制定自行监测方案，未建立管理台账，未保存原始监测记录和管理台账，以及未如实报告；  
（二）未安装、使用、维护、正常运行污染物排放自动监测、监控设备，未与主管部门的监控设备联网，未保存原始监测记录，以及未如实报告；  
（三）未对排放有毒有害污染物的排污口和周边环境进行监测，以及未公开有毒有害污染物信息。  
违反本法规定，生活垃圾处理单位未安装、使用污染物排放监测设备，实时监测污染物的排放情况并公开污染排放数据的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处五十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1086** : Where any provision of this Law is violated and any of the following circumstances exists, the department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration shall, according to its division of duties, order correction and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, it may order restricted production or suspension of operations for remediation:  
(1) failure to conduct self-monitoring of the pollutants discharged, aquaculture tail water, or soil pollution status, failure to formulate a self-monitoring plan, failure to establish management ledgers, failure to preserve original monitoring records and management ledgers, and failure to report truthfully;  
(2) failure to install, use, maintain, or operate normally automatic pollutant discharge monitoring and surveillance equipment, failure to connect with the competent department's surveillance equipment, failure to preserve original monitoring records, and failure to report truthfully;  
(3) failure to monitor the discharge outlets of toxic and hazardous pollutants and the surrounding environment, and failure to disclose information on toxic and hazardous pollutants.
