Where a provision of this Law is violated and a domestic waste treatment unit fails to install or use pollutant discharge monitoring equipment, conduct real-time monitoring of pollutant discharge, and disclose pollutant discharge data, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order correction and impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1087"></a>第一千零八十七条　违反本法规定，企业事业单位和其他生产经营者开展生态环境监测使用不符合法律、行政法规规定和国家标准的监测设施、设备，或者未遵守监测规范导致监测数据失真的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，责令限制生产、停产整治。

**Article 1087** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and an enterprise, public institution, or other producer or business operator uses monitoring facilities or equipment that do not comply with the provisions of laws, administrative regulations, and national standards when conducting ecological and environmental monitoring, or fails to comply with monitoring specifications, thereby causing monitoring data to be distorted, the department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration shall, according to its division of duties, order correction and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, it shall order restricted production or suspension of operations for remediation.

<a id="art-1088"></a>第一千零八十八条　违反本法规定，企业事业单位和其他生产经营者对生态环境监测数据弄虚作假，或者指使其委托的生态环境监测机构对监测数据弄虚作假的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，并对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，责令限制生产、停产整治。

**Article 1088** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and an enterprise, public institution, or other producer or business operator falsifies ecological and environmental monitoring data, or instructs the ecological and environmental monitoring agency it entrusts to falsify monitoring data, the department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration shall, according to its division of duties, order correction, impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan, and impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons; if the circumstances are serious, it shall order restricted production or suspension of operations for remediation.

<a id="art-1089"></a>第一千零八十九条　违反本法规定，生态环境监测机构不具备相应的设施、设备、技术人员、技术能力、管理能力，或者不依法备案的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业。  
违反本法规定，从事放射性污染监测的生态环境监测机构未取得许可证或者未按照许可证规定开展监测业务的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1089** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and an ecological and environmental monitoring agency fails to have the corresponding facilities, equipment, technical personnel, technical capability, or management capability, or fails to file records according to law, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order correction, confiscate illegal gains, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, and ordered to suspend business.  
Where a provision of this Law is violated and an ecological and environmental monitoring agency engaged in monitoring of radioactive pollution fails to obtain a license or conducts monitoring business not in accordance with the provisions of the license, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order correction, confiscate illegal gains, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, and ordered to suspend business and close down.

<a id="art-1090"></a>第一千零九十条　违反本法规定，生态环境监测机构使用不符合法律、行政法规规定和国家标准的监测设施、设备，或者未遵守监测规范导致监测数据失真的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，没收违法所得，并处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，有许可证的吊销许可证，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1090** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and an ecological and environmental monitoring agency uses monitoring facilities or equipment that do not comply with the provisions of laws, administrative regulations, and national standards, or fails to comply with monitoring specifications, thereby causing monitoring data to be distorted, the department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration shall, according to its division of duties, order correction, confiscate illegal gains, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, the license shall be revoked if any, and the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, ordering suspension of business and closure.

<a id="art-1091"></a>第一千零九十一条　违反本法规定，生态环境监测机构对监测数据弄虚作假的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工没收违法所得，并处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，并处五十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款，有许可证的吊销许可证，禁止从事监测业务，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。  
有前款规定情形的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处一万元以上五万元以下的罚款，五年内禁止从事前款规定的业务；情节严重的，十年内禁止从事前款规定的业务。

**Article 1091** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and an ecological and environmental monitoring agency falsifies monitoring data, the department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration shall, according to its division of duties, confiscate illegal gains, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan shall be additionally imposed, the license shall be revoked if any, the agency shall be prohibited from engaging in monitoring business, and the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, ordering suspension of business and closure.  
Where the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph exist, the department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration shall, according to its division of duties, impose a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons, and prohibit them from engaging in the business specified in the preceding paragraph for five years; if the circumstances are serious, prohibit them from engaging in the business specified in the preceding paragraph for ten years.

<a id="art-1092"></a>第一千零九十二条　违反本法规定，生产、进口、销售不符合法律、行政法规规定和国家标准的监测设施、设备的，由市场监督管理部门、海关按照职责分工责令停止生产、进口、销售，没收违法所得和违法生产、进口、销售的设施、设备，并处违法生产、进口、销售的设施、设备货值金额一倍以上五倍以下的罚款，货值金额不足五万元的，以五万元计算；情节严重的，吊销许可证。

**Article 1092** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and monitoring facilities or equipment that do not comply with the provisions of laws, administrative regulations, and national standards are produced, imported, or sold, the market supervision and administration department and the customs office shall, according to their division of duties, order cessation of production, import, or sale, confiscate illegal gains and the illegally produced, imported, or sold facilities and equipment, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the value of the illegally produced, imported, or sold facilities and equipment; if the value is less than 50,000 yuan, it shall be calculated as 50,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, the license shall be revoked.

<a id="art-1093"></a>第一千零九十三条　违反本法规定，单位和个人以侵占、损毁或者擅自移动、改变等方式干扰、破坏生态环境监测设施、设备或者指使干扰、破坏生态环境监测设施、设备的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；构成违反治安管理行为的，由公安机关依法予以处罚。

**Article 1093** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and a unit or individual interferes with or damages ecological and environmental monitoring facilities or equipment by means of encroachment, destruction, or unauthorized removal or alteration, or instructs others to interfere with or damage ecological and environmental monitoring facilities or equipment, the department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration shall, according to its division of duties, order correction and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the act constitutes a violation of public security administration, the public security organ shall impose a penalty according to law.

#### 第二节　违反生态环境影响评价管理规定

<a id="art-1094"></a>第一千零九十四条　违反本法第一编第五章等规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1094** : Where the provisions of Chapter V of Part I of this Law are violated, the provisions of this Section shall apply to impose administrative penalties.

<a id="art-1095"></a>第一千零九十五条　违反本法规定，建设单位未依法报批，或者未依照本法有关规定重新报批或者报请重新审核生态环境影响报告书、报告表，建设项目生态环境影响报告书、报告表未经批准或者未经原审批部门重新审核同意，擅自开工建设的，由生态环境主管部门、海警机构按照职责分工责令其停止建设，并可以责令恢复原状，报批、审核类型为生态环境影响报告书的，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，情节严重的，处一百万元以上一千万元以下的罚款；报批、审核类型为生态环境影响报告表的，处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款，情节严重的，处五十万元以上五百万元以下的罚款。  
建设单位有前款规定情形，被责令停止建设或者恢复原状，拒不执行的，由公安机关或者海警机构按照职责分工对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处十日以上十五日以下拘留；情节较轻的，处五日以上十日以下拘留。  
违反本法规定，建设单位未依法将建设项目生态环境影响登记表报生态环境主管部门备案的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，可以处五万元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，核设施选址、建造、运行、退役和核技术、铀（钍）矿、伴生放射性矿开发利用中有第一款至第三款规定的违法情形的，适用第一款至第三款的规定予以处罚。

**Article 1095** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and a construction entity fails to submit a report for approval according to law, or fails to resubmit a report for approval or submit a report for reexamination in accordance with the relevant provisions of this Law, or the ecological and environmental impact report or statement of the construction project has not been approved or has not been re-approved by the original approving department, and the entity starts construction without authorization, the competent department of ecology and environment or the coast guard agency shall, according to their division of duties, order the entity to stop construction and may order restoration to the original state; if the type submitted for approval or reexamination is an ecological and environmental impact report, a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed, and if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 1,000,000 yuan but not more than 10,000,000 yuan; if the type submitted for approval or reexamination is an ecological and environmental impact statement, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan shall be imposed, and if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 5,000,000 yuan.  
Where a construction entity has the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph, is ordered to stop construction or restore to the original state, and refuses to execute, the public security organ or the coast guard agency shall, according to their division of duties, impose detention of not less than ten days but not more than fifteen days on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons; if the circumstances are relatively minor, detention of not less than five days but not more than ten days shall be imposed.  
Where a provision of this Law is violated and a construction entity fails to file an ecological and environmental impact registration form of the construction project with the competent department of ecology and environment for the record according to law, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order correction and may impose a fine of not more than 50,000 yuan.  
Where a provision of this Law is violated and, in the siting, construction, operation, and decommissioning of nuclear facilities and in the development and utilization of nuclear technology, uranium (thorium) mines, or associated radioactive mines, there are illegal circumstances specified in the first to third paragraphs, the provisions of the first to third paragraphs shall apply to impose penalties.

<a id="art-1096"></a>第一千零九十六条　违反本法规定，建设项目生态环境影响报告书、报告表存在基础资料明显不实，内容存在重大缺陷、遗漏或者虚假，生态环境影响评价结论不正确或者不合理等严重质量问题的，由生态环境主管部门、海警机构按照职责分工对建设单位处五十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款，并对建设单位的法定代表人、主要负责人、直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，责令限制生产、停产整治。  
建设单位自行编制生态环境影响报告书、报告表有前款规定的违法情形的，对编制主持人和主要编制人员处一万元以上五万元以下的罚款，五年内禁止从事生态环境影响报告书、报告表编制工作；情节严重的，十年内禁止从事生态环境影响报告书、报告表编制工作。

**Article 1096** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and an ecological and environmental impact report or statement of a construction project has serious quality problems such as obviously untrue basic data, major defects, omissions or falsification in content, or incorrect or unreasonable ecological and environmental impact assessment conclusions, the competent department of ecology and environment or the coast guard agency shall, according to their division of duties, impose a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan on the construction entity, and impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan on the legal representative, principal responsible person, directly responsible person in charge, and other directly liable persons of the construction entity; if the circumstances are serious, restricted production or suspension of operations for remediation shall be ordered.  
Where the construction entity compiles the ecological and environmental impact report or statement by itself and has the illegal circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan shall be imposed on the person in charge of compilation and principal compilers, and they shall be prohibited from engaging in the compilation of ecological and environmental impact reports or statements for five years; if the circumstances are serious, they shall be prohibited from engaging in such compilation for ten years.

<a id="art-1097"></a>第一千零九十七条　违反本法规定，接受委托为建设单位编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、报告表的技术单位违反国家有关生态环境影响评价标准和技术规范等规定，致使其编制的报告书、报告表存在基础资料明显不实，内容存在重大缺陷、遗漏或者虚假，生态环境影响评价结论不正确或者不合理等严重后果的，由生态环境主管部门、海警机构按照职责分工没收违法所得，并处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，并处五十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款，禁止从事生态环境影响报告书、报告表编制工作，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业。  
技术单位有前款规定的违法情形的，主持编制的主要人员五年内禁止从事生态环境影响报告书、报告表编制工作；情节严重的，十年内禁止从事生态环境影响报告书、报告表编制工作。

**Article 1097** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and a technical unit entrusted to compile an ecological and environmental impact report or statement for a construction entity violates the national standards for ecological and environmental impact assessment and technical specifications, resulting in the compiled report or statement having serious consequences such as obviously untrue basic data, major defects, omissions or falsification in content, or incorrect or unreasonable ecological and environmental impact assessment conclusions, the competent department of ecology and environment or the coast guard agency shall, according to their division of duties, confiscate illegal gains and concurrently impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan shall be additionally imposed, the unit shall be prohibited from engaging in the compilation of ecological and environmental impact reports or statements, and the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, ordering suspension of business.  
Where a technical unit has the illegal circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph, the principal person in charge of compilation shall be prohibited from engaging in the compilation of ecological and environmental impact reports or statements for five years; if the circumstances are serious, the prohibition shall be for ten years.

<a id="art-1098"></a>第一千零九十八条　违反本法规定，接受委托为建设单位编制建设项目生态环境影响报告书、报告表的技术单位和人员，与负责审批的部门存在利益关系的，或者该技术单位未依法报生态环境主管部门备案的，由生态环境主管部门、海警机构按照职责分工责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业。

**Article 1098** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and a technical unit or person entrusted to compile an ecological and environmental impact report or statement for a construction entity has an interest relationship with the department responsible for approval, or the technical unit fails to file with the competent department of ecology and environment for the record according to law, the competent department of ecology and environment or the coast guard agency shall, according to their division of duties, order correction and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, ordering suspension of business.

<a id="art-1099"></a>第一千零九十九条　违反本法规定，在建设项目建设、运行过程中，未实施生态环境影响报告书、报告表及其审批部门审批意见中提出的生态环境保护对策措施的，由生态环境主管部门、海警机构按照职责分工对建设单位处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，处五十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款。本法另有规定的，适用其规定。

**Article 1099** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and, during the construction or operation of a construction project, the ecological and environmental protection countermeasures and measures proposed in the ecological and environmental impact report or statement and in the approval opinion of the approving department are not implemented, the competent department of ecology and environment or the coast guard agency shall, according to their division of duties, impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan on the construction entity; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan shall be imposed. If this Law otherwise provides, the relevant provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-1100"></a>第一千一百条　违反本法规定，生态环境保护设施未与主体工程同时设计、同时施工、同时投产使用，或者生态环境保护设施未建成、未达到规定要求即投入生产、使用的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，拒不改正的，处一百万元以上二百万元以下的罚款；对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；造成重大环境污染、生态破坏的，责令其停止生产、使用，或者报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1100** : Where a provision of this Law is violated and the ecological and environmental protection facilities are not designed, constructed, and put into operation and use simultaneously with the main project, or the ecological and environmental protection facilities are not completed or do not meet the prescribed requirements and are put into production or use, the department or institution responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and administration shall, according to its division of duties, order correction and impose a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan; if it refuses to correct, a fine of not less than 1,000,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan shall be imposed; and a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons; if serious environmental pollution or ecological damage is caused, the entity shall be ordered to stop production or use, or the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, ordering suspension of business and closure.

#### 第三节　违反排污许可管理规定

<a id="art-1101"></a>第一千一百零一条　违反本法第二编第一分编第二章等规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1101** : Where the provisions of Chapter 2, Division 1, Part 2 of this Law, among others, are violated, the provisions of this Section shall apply to impose an administrative penalty.

<a id="art-1102"></a>第一千一百零二条　违反本法规定，未依法取得排污许可证排放污染物的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，并可以责令限制生产、停产整治；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。其中，污染物为工业噪声的，罚款数额为二万元以上二十万元以下。拒不停止排放污染物的，由生态环境主管部门将案件移送公安机关，由公安机关对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处十日以上十五日以下拘留；情节较轻的，处五日以上十日以下拘留。

**Article 1102** : Where, in violation of this Law, a pollutant is discharged without obtaining a pollutant discharge permit according to law, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan, and may order production restriction or suspension of production for remediation; if the circumstances are serious, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, and an order to suspend business or close down shall be issued. Where the pollutant is industrial noise, the amount of the fine shall be not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan. Where the discharge of pollutants is refused to be stopped, the competent department of ecological environment shall transfer the case to the public security organ, and the public security organ shall impose detention of not less than ten days but not more than fifteen days on the directly responsible主管人员 and other directly responsible persons; if the circumstances are relatively minor, detention of not less than five days but not more than ten days shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1103"></a>第一千一百零三条　违反本法规定，未按照排污许可证载明的污染物排放种类，或者超过载明的许可排放浓度、许可排放量、许可排放限值排放污染物的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，并可以责令限制生产、停产整治；情节严重的，吊销排污许可证，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。其中，污染物为工业噪声的，罚款数额为二万元以上二十万元以下。

**Article 1103** : Where, in violation of this Law, a pollutant is discharged not in accordance with the types of pollutant discharge specified in the pollutant discharge permit, or exceeds the permitted discharge concentration, permitted discharge amount, or permitted discharge limit specified therein, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan, and may order production restriction or suspension of production for remediation; if the circumstances are serious, the pollutant discharge permit shall be revoked, and the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, and an order to suspend business or close down shall be issued. Where the pollutant is industrial noise, the amount of the fine shall be not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1104"></a>第一千一百零四条　违反本法规定，未按照排污许可证的规定运行和维护污染防治设施的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处二万元以上五万元以下的罚款；致使污染防治设施不能正常发挥作用，但不属于以逃避监管方式排放污染物的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，责令限制生产、停产整治。  
违反本法规定，擅自拆除污染防治设施的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，责令限制生产、停产整治；情节严重的，吊销排污许可证，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1104** : Where, in violation of this Law, pollution prevention and control facilities are not operated and maintained in accordance with the provisions of the pollutant discharge permit, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan; if such failure causes the pollution prevention and control facilities to fail to function normally, but does not constitute discharging pollutants by means of evading supervision, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, production restriction or suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered.  
Where, in violation of this Law, pollution prevention and control facilities are dismantled without authorization, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan; if rectification is refused, production restriction or suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered; if the circumstances are serious, the pollutant discharge permit shall be revoked, and the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, and an order to suspend business or close down shall be issued.

<a id="art-1105"></a>第一千一百零五条　违反本法规定，以欺骗、贿赂等不正当手段申请取得排污许可证的，由生态环境主管部门依法撤销其排污许可证，处二十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1105** : Where, in violation of this Law, a pollutant discharge permit is obtained by applying through fraudulent, bribery, or other illegitimate means, the competent department of ecological environment shall revoke the pollutant discharge permit according to law and impose a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1106"></a>第一千一百零六条　违反本法规定，需要填报排污登记表的企业事业单位和其他生产经营者未依法填报排污信息的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，可以处五万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1106** : Where, in violation of this Law, enterprises, institutions, and other producers or operators that are required to fill in a pollutant discharge registration form fail to fill in pollutant discharge information according to law, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and may impose a fine of not more than 50,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1107"></a>第一千一百零七条　违反本法规定，超过污染物排放标准或者超过重点污染物排放总量控制指标排放污染物的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，可以责令限制生产、停产整治；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。其中，污染物为工业噪声的，罚款数额为二万元以上二十万元以下。

**Article 1107** : Where, in violation of this Law, a pollutant is discharged exceeding the pollutant discharge standard or exceeding the total emission control indicator for key pollutants, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan, and may order production restriction or suspension of production for remediation; if the circumstances are serious, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, and an order to suspend business or close down shall be issued. Where the pollutant is industrial noise, the amount of the fine shall be not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1108"></a>第一千一百零八条　违反本法规定，通过暗管、渗井、渗坑、灌注、裂隙、溶洞、篡改或者伪造监测数据、以逃避现场检查为目的的临时停产、非紧急情况下开启应急排放通道、不正常运行污染防治设施等逃避监管的方式排放污染物的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，责令限制生产、停产整治，并将案件移送公安机关，由公安机关对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五日以上十日以下拘留；情节严重的，吊销排污许可证，报经有批准权的地方人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭，并由公安机关对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处十日以上十五日以下拘留。

**Article 1108** : Where, in violation of this Law, a pollutant is discharged through concealed pipes, seepage wells, seepage pits, injection, fissures, karst caves, tampering with or falsifying monitoring data, temporary shutdown for the purpose of evading on-site inspection, opening emergency discharge channels under non-emergency circumstances, abnormal operation of pollution prevention and control facilities, or other means of evading supervision, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification, impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan, order production restriction or suspension of production for remediation, and transfer the case to the public security organ, which shall impose detention of not less than five days but not more than ten days on the directly responsible主管人员 and other directly responsible persons; if the circumstances are serious, the pollutant discharge permit shall be revoked, and the matter shall be reported to the local people's government with the power of approval for approval, and an order to suspend business or close down shall be issued, and the public security organ shall impose detention of not less than ten days but not more than fifteen days on the directly responsible主管人员 and other directly responsible persons.

<a id="art-1109"></a>第一千一百零九条　违反本法和其他法律法规、国务院生态环境主管部门的规定、排污许可证规定设置排污口的，除本条第二款规定外，由生态环境主管部门责令限期拆除，逾期不拆除的，强制拆除，所需费用由违法者承担，处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款，责令限制生产、停产整治。  
违反本法规定，在饮用水水源保护区内设置排污口的，或者在自然保护地水体等具有特殊经济文化价值的水体、生态保护红线区域及其他需要特别保护的区域新设排污口的，由县级以上地方人民政府责令限期拆除，逾期不拆除的，强制拆除，所需费用由违法者承担，由生态环境主管部门处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，处五十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，责令限制生产、停产整治。  
违反本法规定，设置入海排污口未备案的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1109** : Where, in violation of this Law and other laws and regulations, the provisions of the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council, or the provisions of the pollutant discharge permit, a pollutant discharge outlet is set up, except as provided in the second paragraph of this Article, the competent department of ecological environment shall order its removal within a time limit; if it is not removed within the time limit, compulsory removal shall be carried out, with the cost borne by the violator, and a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan shall be imposed; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan shall be imposed, and production restriction or suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a pollutant discharge outlet is set up in a drinking water source protection zone, or a new pollutant discharge outlet is set up in a water body of a nature reserve or other water bodies with special economic or cultural value, an ecological protection red line area, or other areas requiring special protection, the local people's government at or above the county level shall order its removal within a time limit; if it is not removed within the time limit, compulsory removal shall be carried out, with the cost borne by the violator, and the competent department of ecological environment shall impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed, and production restriction or suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a sea-entering pollutant discharge outlet is set up without filing, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1110"></a>第一千一百一十条　违反本法规定，未向社会公开或者不如实公开污染物排放信息、温室气体排放信息等生态环境信息的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，责令限制生产、停产整治，并公告上述生态环境信息。

**Article 1110** : Where, in violation of this Law, ecological environment information such as pollutant discharge information, greenhouse gas emission information, etc., is not disclosed to the public or is not truthfully disclosed, the department or institution with the responsibility of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management shall, in accordance with the division of duties, order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if rectification is refused, production restriction or suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered, and the aforementioned ecological environment information shall be publicly announced.

<a id="art-1111"></a>第一千一百一十一条　违反本法规定，建设单位未将污染防治所需资金列入工程造价的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，责令停产整治。

**Article 1111** : Where, in violation of this Law, the construction entity fails to include the funds required for pollution prevention and control in the project cost, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan; if rectification is refused, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed, and suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered.

<a id="art-1112"></a>第一千一百一十二条　违反本法规定，将中华人民共和国境外的固体废物、放射性废物和被放射性污染的物品输入境内的，由海关责令退运该固体废物、放射性废物和被放射性污染的物品，处五十万元以上五百万元以下的罚款。已经非法入境的，由省级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门依法向海关提出处理意见，由海关按照前述规定予以处理；已经造成环境污染的，由省级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门责令进口者消除污染。  
违反本法规定，将中华人民共和国境外废弃物运进中华人民共和国管辖海域倾倒的，由海警机构责令改正，处五十万元以上五百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1112** : Where, in violation of this Law, solid waste, radioactive waste, or articles contaminated with radioactivity from outside the territory of the People's Republic of China are brought into the territory, the Customs shall order the return of such solid waste, radioactive waste, or articles contaminated with radioactivity, and impose a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 5,000,000 yuan. If they have already entered the territory illegally, the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government at or above the provincial level shall, according to law, submit its handling opinion to the Customs, and the Customs shall handle the matter in accordance with the preceding provisions; if environmental pollution has already been caused, the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government at or above the provincial level shall order the importer to eliminate the pollution.  
Where, in violation of this Law, waste from outside the territory of the People's Republic of China is transported into the sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China for dumping, the maritime police agency shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 5,000,000 yuan.

#### 第四节　违反大气污染防治规定

<a id="art-1113"></a>第一千一百一十三条　违反本法第二编第二分编规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1113** : Where the provisions of Division 2, Part 2 of this Law are violated, the provisions of this Section shall apply to impose an administrative penalty.

<a id="art-1114"></a>第一千一百一十四条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由能源主管部门责令改正，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）煤矿未配套建设煤炭洗选设施；  
（二）燃煤电厂和其他燃煤单位未配套建设除尘、脱硫、脱硝等装置或者未采取技术改造等其他控制大气污染物排放的措施；  
（三）生产过程中排放粉尘、硫化物和氮氧化物的钢铁、建材、有色金属、石油、化工等企业未配套建设除尘、脱硫、脱硝等装置或者未采取技术改造等其他控制大气污染物排放的措施。  
违反本法规定，开采含放射性和砷等有毒有害物质超过规定标准的煤炭的，由县级以上人民政府按照国务院规定的权限责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1114** : Where, in violation of this Law, any of the following circumstances exists, the competent energy department shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan; if rectification is refused, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with the power of approval for approval, and an order to suspend business or close down shall be issued:  
(1) A coal mine fails to construct supporting coal washing and dressing facilities;  
(2) A coal-fired power plant or other coal-burning unit fails to construct supporting dust removal, desulfurization, denitrification and other devices, or fails to adopt technical renovation or other measures to control the discharge of air pollutants;  
(3) An enterprise such as steel, building materials, non-ferrous metals, petroleum, or chemical engineering that emits dust, sulfides, and nitrogen oxides during the production process fails to construct supporting dust removal, desulfurization, denitrification and other devices, or fails to adopt technical renovation or other measures to control the discharge of air pollutants.  
Where, in violation of this Law, coal containing toxic and hazardous substances such as radioactive substances and arsenic exceeding the prescribed standards is mined, the people's government at or above the county level shall, in accordance with the authority prescribed by the State Council, order suspension of business or close down.

<a id="art-1115"></a>第一千一百一十五条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由市场监督管理部门责令改正，没收原材料、产品和违法所得，并处货值金额一倍以上五倍以下的罚款：  
（一）销售不符合质量标准的煤炭、石油焦；  
（二）在禁燃区内销售高污染燃料；  
（三）生产、销售挥发性有机物含量不符合质量标准或者要求的原辅材料和产品；  
（四）生产、销售不符合标准的机动车船和非道路移动机械用燃料、发动机油、氮氧化物还原剂、燃料和润滑油添加剂以及其他添加剂。

**Article 1115** : Where, in violation of this Law, any of the following circumstances exists, the market supervision and administration department shall order rectification, confiscate the raw materials, products, and illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the value of the goods:  
(1) Selling coal or petroleum coke that does not meet the quality standards;  
(2) Selling high-pollution fuel within a prohibited burning area;  
(3) Producing or selling raw and auxiliary materials or products whose volatile organic compound content does not meet the quality standards or requirements;  
(4) Producing or selling fuels, engine oils, nitrogen oxide reducing agents, fuel and lubricant additives, and other additives for motor vehicles, vessels, and non-road mobile machinery that do not meet the standards.

<a id="art-1116"></a>第一千一百一十六条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由海关责令退运，没收违法所得，并处货值金额一倍以上五倍以下的罚款；无法退运的，没收原材料和产品：  
（一）进口不符合质量标准的煤炭、石油焦；  
（二）进口挥发性有机物含量不符合质量标准或者要求的原辅材料和产品；  
（三）进口不符合标准的机动车船和非道路移动机械用燃料、发动机油、氮氧化物还原剂、燃料和润滑油添加剂以及其他添加剂。

**Article 1116** : Where, in violation of this Law, any of the following circumstances exists, the Customs shall order the return of the goods, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the value of the goods; if the goods cannot be returned, the raw materials and products shall be confiscated:  
(1) Importing coal or petroleum coke that does not meet the quality standards;  
(2) Importing raw and auxiliary materials or products whose volatile organic compound content does not meet the quality standards or requirements;  
(3) Importing fuels, engine oils, nitrogen oxide reducing agents, fuel and lubricant additives, and other additives for motor vehicles, vessels, and non-road mobile machinery that do not meet the standards.

<a id="art-1117"></a>第一千一百一十七条　违反本法规定，单位燃用不符合质量标准的煤炭或者使用不符合质量标准的石油焦的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处货值金额一倍以上五倍以下的罚款。

**Article 1117** : Where, in violation of this Law, a unit burns coal that does not meet the quality standards or uses petroleum coke that does not meet the quality standards, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the value of the goods.

<a id="art-1118"></a>第一千一百一十八条　违反本法规定，船舶使用不符合标准或者要求的燃油的，由海事管理机构、渔业渔政主管部门按照职责分工处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1118** : Where, in violation of this Law, a vessel uses fuel oil that does not meet the standards or requirements, the maritime administrative agency or the competent department of fisheries and fishery administration shall, in accordance with the division of duties, impose a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1119"></a>第一千一百一十九条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门没收燃用高污染燃料的设施、燃煤供热锅炉并组织拆除，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款：  
（一）在禁燃区内新建、扩建燃用高污染燃料的设施；  
（二）在禁燃区内未停止燃用高污染燃料；  
（三）在城市集中供热管网覆盖地区新建、扩建分散燃煤供热锅炉；  
（四）未在要求期限内拆除已建成的不能达标排放的燃煤供热锅炉。  
违反本法规定，生产、进口、销售不符合规定标准或者要求的锅炉的，由市场监督管理部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；使用不符合规定标准或者要求的锅炉的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1119** : Where, in violation of this Law, any of the following circumstances exists, the competent department of ecological environment shall confiscate the facilities using high-pollution fuel and the coal-fired heating boilers and organize their dismantling, and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan:  
(1) Constructing or expanding facilities using high-pollution fuel within a prohibited burning area;  
(2) Failing to stop using high-pollution fuel within a prohibited burning area;  
(3) Constructing or expanding decentralized coal-fired heating boilers in areas covered by urban centralized heating pipe networks;  
(4) Failing to dismantle existing coal-fired heating boilers that cannot meet the discharge standards within the required time limit.  
Where, in violation of this Law, boilers that do not meet the prescribed standards or requirements are produced, imported, or sold, the market supervision and administration department shall order rectification, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; where boilers that do not meet the prescribed standards or requirements are used, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1120"></a>第一千一百二十条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，责令停产整治：  
（一）工业涂装企业等未使用低挥发性有机物含量的原辅材料和产品；  
（二）生产和使用产生含挥发性有机物原辅材料和产品，未在密闭空间或者设备中进行并安装、使用污染防治设施或者未采取减少废气排放措施；  
（三）石油、化工以及其他生产和使用有机溶剂的企业，未采取措施对管道、设备进行日常维护、维修，或者对泄漏的物料未及时收集处理；  
（四）储油库、加油站和油罐车等，未按照国家规定安装并正常使用油气回收装置；  
（五）钢铁、建材、有色金属、石油、化工、制药、矿产开采企业和其他工业生产企业，未采取集中收集处理、密闭、围挡、遮盖、清扫、洒水等措施，控制、减少粉尘和气态污染物的排放；  
（六）工业生产、垃圾填埋或者其他活动产生的可燃性气体未回收利用，不具备回收利用条件未进行污染防治处理；  
（七）可燃性气体回收利用装置不能正常作业，未及时修复或者更新；  
（八）在可燃性气体回收利用装置不能正常作业期间排放可燃性气体，未采取控制大气污染物排放措施。  
违反本法规定，原油成品油码头、原油成品油运输船舶、铁路罐车未按照国家规定安装并正常使用油气回收装置的，由生态环境、交通运输、海事管理等部门按照职责分工，依照前款规定予以处罚。

**Article 1120** : Where, in violation of this Law, any of the following circumstances exists, the competent department of ecological environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if rectification is refused, suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered:  
(1) An industrial painting enterprise, etc., fails to use raw and auxiliary materials or products with low volatile organic compound content;  
(2) Raw and auxiliary materials or products that generate volatile organic compounds are produced or used without being carried out in a closed space or equipment and without installing and using pollution prevention and control facilities or taking measures to reduce waste gas emissions;  
(3) Enterprises such as petroleum, chemical engineering, and other enterprises that produce and use organic solvents fail to take measures for daily maintenance and repair of pipes and equipment, or fail to collect and treat leaked materials in a timely manner;  
(4) Oil storage depots, gas stations, tanker trucks, etc., fail to install and properly use oil and gas recovery devices in accordance with state regulations;  
(5) Enterprises such as steel, building materials, non-ferrous metals, petroleum, chemical engineering, pharmaceutical, mining, and other industrial production enterprises fail to take measures such as centralized collection and treatment, enclosure, shielding, covering, sweeping, and water spraying to control and reduce the emission of dust and gaseous pollutants;  
(6) Combustible gases generated from industrial production, landfill, or other activities are not recovered or utilized, and where recovery and utilization conditions are not available, no pollution prevention and control treatment is carried out;  
(7) A combustible gas recovery and utilization device fails to operate normally and is not repaired or replaced in a timely manner;  
(8) Combustible gases are discharged during the period when the combustible gas recovery and utilization device cannot operate normally, without taking measures to control the discharge of air pollutants.  
Where, in violation of this Law, crude oil and refined oil terminals, crude oil and refined oil transport vessels, or railway tank cars fail to install and properly use oil and gas recovery devices in accordance with state regulations, the competent departments of ecological environment, transportation, maritime administration, etc., shall, in accordance with the division of duties, impose penalties in accordance with the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-1121"></a>第一千一百二十一条　违反本法规定，生产不符合大气污染物排放标准的机动车船、非道路移动机械、发动机的，由省级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处货值金额三倍以下的罚款，没收销毁无法达到污染物排放标准的机动车船、非道路移动机械、发动机；拒不改正的，责令限制生产、停产整治，并由国务院有关生产主管部门按照职责分工责令停止生产该车（船、机）型。  
违反本法规定，机动车、非道路移动机械、发动机生产企业对发动机、排放控制系统弄虚作假、以次充好，冒充排放检验合格产品出厂销售的，由省级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门责令停产整治，没收违法所得，并处货值金额一倍以上三倍以下的罚款，没收销毁无法达到污染物排放标准的机动车、非道路移动机械、发动机，并由国务院有关生产主管部门按照职责分工责令停止生产该车（机）型。

**Article 1121** : Where, in violation of this Law, a motor vehicle or vessel, non-road mobile machinery, or engine is produced which does not meet the air pollutant emission standards, the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government at or above the provincial level shall order correction, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not more than three times the value of the goods, and confiscate and destroy the motor vehicles or vessels, non-road mobile machinery, or engines that cannot meet the pollutant emission standards; if the violator refuses to correct, the department shall order restricted production and suspension of operations for remediation, and the relevant competent production department of the State Council shall, according to its division of responsibilities, order the cessation of production of that vehicle (vessel, machinery) model.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a manufacturer of motor vehicles, non-road mobile machinery, or engines practices fraud in respect of engines or emission control systems, passes off inferior products as quality products, and markets products falsely claiming to have passed emission tests, the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government at or above the provincial level shall order suspension of operations for remediation, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than three times the value of the goods, and confiscate and destroy the motor vehicles, non-road mobile machinery, or engines that cannot meet the pollutant emission standards, and the relevant competent production department of the State Council shall, according to its division of responsibilities, order the cessation of production of that vehicle (machinery) model.

<a id="art-1122"></a>第一千一百二十二条　违反本法规定，进口、销售超过污染物排放标准的机动车船、非道路移动机械、发动机的，由市场监督管理部门、海关按照职责分工没收违法所得，并处货值金额三倍以下的罚款，没收销毁无法达到污染物排放标准的机动车船、非道路移动机械、发动机。

**Article 1122** : Where, in violation of this Law, motor vehicles or vessels, non-road mobile machinery, or engines exceeding the pollutant emission standards are imported or marketed, the market regulatory authority and the Customs shall, according to their respective responsibilities, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not more than three times the value of the goods, and confiscate and destroy the motor vehicles or vessels, non-road mobile machinery, or engines that cannot meet the pollutant emission standards.

<a id="art-1123"></a>第一千一百二十三条　违反本法规定，机动车、非道路移动机械生产、进口企业未按照规定向社会公开其生产、进口机动车车型、非道路移动机械机型的排放检验信息或者污染控制技术信息的，由省级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门责令改正，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，机动车、非道路移动机械生产、进口企业未按照规定向社会公开其生产、进口机动车车型、非道路移动机械机型的有关维修技术信息的，由省级以上人民政府交通运输主管部门责令改正，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1123** : Where, in violation of this Law, a motor vehicle or non-road mobile machinery manufacturer or importer fails to publicly disclose to the society the emission test information or pollutant control technical information of the motor vehicle models or non-road mobile machinery models it produces or imports as required, the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government at or above the provincial level shall order correction and impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a motor vehicle or non-road mobile machinery manufacturer or importer fails to publicly disclose to the society relevant maintenance technical information of the motor vehicle models or non-road mobile machinery models it produces or imports as required, the competent department of transport of the people's government at or above the provincial level shall order correction and impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1124"></a>第一千一百二十四条　违反本法规定，以临时更换、篡改、屏蔽、伪造排放控制系统等弄虚作假的方式通过机动车船、非道路移动机械排放检验或者破坏、拆除、篡改、屏蔽、伪造机动车船、非道路移动机械排放控制系统的，由生态环境主管部门、海事管理机构、渔业渔政主管部门按照职责分工责令改正，对所有人处每辆（艘）五千元的罚款，对维修单位处每辆（艘）五千元的罚款。

**Article 1124** : Where, in violation of this Law, a party passes the emission test of a motor vehicle or vessel or non-road mobile machinery by fraudulent means such as temporary replacement, tampering, shielding, or counterfeiting the emission control system, or damages, removes, tampers with, shields, or counterfeits the emission control system of a motor vehicle or vessel or non-road mobile machinery, the competent department of ecological environment, the maritime administration, or the competent department of fisheries and fisheries administration shall, according to their respective responsibilities, order correction, and impose a fine of 5,000 yuan per vehicle (vessel) on the owner, and a fine of 5,000 yuan per vehicle (vessel) on the maintenance entity.

<a id="art-1125"></a>第一千一百二十五条　违反本法规定，生产、进口或者销售能够篡改、屏蔽、伪造机动车船、非道路移动机械、发动机排放检验数据的装置或者为虚假排放检验提供条件的检验设备的，由生态环境主管部门、市场监督管理部门、海关按照职责分工没收违法所得，没收销毁违法装置和检验设备，并处货值金额一倍以上五倍以下的罚款，货值金额不足五万元的，以五万元计算；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1125** : Where, in violation of this Law, a party produces, imports, or markets a device capable of tampering with, shielding, or counterfeiting the emission test data of a motor vehicle or vessel, non-road mobile machinery, or engine, or provides testing equipment that facilitates false emission tests, the competent department of ecological environment, the market regulatory authority, and the Customs shall, according to their respective responsibilities, confiscate the illegal gains, confiscate and destroy the illegal device and testing equipment, and impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the value of the goods; if the value of the goods is less than 50,000 yuan, the calculation shall be based on 50,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with approval authority for approval to order suspension of business and closure.

<a id="art-1126"></a>第一千一百二十六条　违反本法规定，机动车驾驶人驾驶排放检验不合格的机动车上道路行驶的，由公安机关交通管理部门依法予以处罚。

**Article 1126** : Where, in violation of this Law, a motor vehicle driver drives a motor vehicle that has failed the emission test on the road, the traffic management department of the public security organ shall impose a penalty according to law.

<a id="art-1127"></a>第一千一百二十七条　违反本法规定，使用排放不合格的非道路移动机械、铁路内燃机车、船舶，或者在用重型汽车、船舶、非道路移动机械未加装、更换符合要求的排放控制系统或者采取其他有效措施的，由生态环境、海事管理、渔业渔政等部门按照职责分工责令改正，处每辆（艘）五千元的罚款。  
违反本法规定，在禁止使用高排放非道路移动机械的区域使用高排放非道路移动机械的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处每辆五千元的罚款。

**Article 1127** : Where, in violation of this Law, a party uses non-road mobile machinery, railway diesel locomotives, or vessels with substandard emissions, or fails to install or replace compliant emission control systems or take other effective measures for heavy-duty vehicles, vessels, or non-road mobile machinery in use, the departments of ecological environment, maritime administration, fisheries and fisheries administration, among others, shall, according to their respective responsibilities, order correction and impose a fine of 5,000 yuan per vehicle (vessel).  
Where, in violation of this Law, a party uses high-emission non-road mobile machinery in an area where the use of such machinery is prohibited, the competent department of ecological environment shall order correction and impose a fine of 5,000 yuan per vehicle.

<a id="art-1128"></a>第一千一百二十八条　违反本法规定，施工单位有下列情形之一的，由住房城乡建设等部门按照职责分工责令改正，处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，责令停工整治：  
（一）施工工地未设置硬质围挡，或者未采取覆盖、分段作业、择时施工、洒水抑尘、冲洗地面和车辆等有效防尘降尘措施；  
（二）建筑土方未及时清运，或者在场地内堆存时未采用密闭式防尘网遮盖。  
违反本法规定，建设单位未对暂时不能开工的建设用地的裸露地面进行覆盖，或者未对超过三个月不能开工的建设用地的裸露地面进行绿化、铺装或者遮盖的，由住房城乡建设等部门按照职责分工依照前款规定予以处罚。

**Article 1128** : Where, in violation of this Law, a construction entity falls under any of the following circumstances, the department of housing and urban-rural development, among others, shall, according to its responsibilities, order correction and impose a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan; if the entity refuses to correct, it shall order suspension of work for remediation:  
(1) The construction site is not provided with hard enclosure, or effective dust prevention and suppression measures such as covering, phased operation, scheduled construction, water spraying for dust suppression, and washing of ground and vehicles are not taken;  
(2) Excavated earthwork is not promptly removed, or when stockpiled on site, it is not covered with a sealed dust-proof net.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a development entity fails to cover the exposed ground of construction land that cannot be commenced temporarily, or fails to green, pave, or cover the exposed ground of construction land that cannot be commenced for more than three months, the department of housing and urban-rural development, among others, shall, according to its responsibilities, impose a penalty in accordance with the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-1129"></a>第一千一百二十九条　违反本法规定，运输煤炭、垃圾、渣土、砂石、土方、灰浆等散装、流体物料的车辆和船舶，未采取密闭或者其他措施防止物料遗撒，或者未按照规定路线行驶的，由县级以上地方人民政府确定的监督管理部门、海事管理机构按照职责分工责令改正，处二千元以上二万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，车辆不得上道路行驶。

**Article 1129** : Where, in violation of this Law, a vehicle or vessel transporting bulk or fluid materials such as coal, garbage, muck, sand, gravel, earth, slurry, etc., fails to take sealing or other measures to prevent spillage of materials, or fails to travel along the prescribed route, the supervisory and administrative department designated by the local people's government at or above the county level or the maritime administration shall, according to their respective responsibilities, order correction and impose a fine of not less than 2,000 yuan but not more than 20,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, the vehicle shall not be allowed to travel on the road.

<a id="art-1130"></a>第一千一百三十条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境等部门按照职责分工责令改正，处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，责令停工整治或者停业整治：  
（一）装卸物料未采取密闭或者喷淋等方式防治扬尘污染；  
（二）未密闭贮存煤炭、煤矸石、煤渣、煤灰、水泥、石灰、石膏、砂土、铁矿石等易产生扬尘的物料；  
（三）对不能密闭贮存的易产生扬尘的物料，未设置不低于堆放物高度的严密围挡，或者未采取有效覆盖措施防治扬尘污染；  
（四）单位存放煤炭、煤矸石、煤渣、煤灰等物料，未采取防燃措施；  
（五）码头、矿山、填埋场和消纳场未实施分区作业并采取有效措施防治扬尘污染；  
（六）排放有毒有害大气污染物名录中所列有毒有害大气污染物的企业事业单位，未建设环境风险预警体系、排查环境安全隐患并采取有效措施防范环境风险；  
（七）向大气排放持久性有机污染物的企业事业单位和其他生产经营者以及废弃物焚烧设施的运营单位，未采取有利于减少持久性有机污染物排放的技术方法和工艺，配备有效的净化装置；  
（八）未采取有效措施防止排放恶臭气体。

**Article 1130** : Where, in violation of this Law, any of the following circumstances occurs, the departments of ecological environment, among others, shall, according to their respective responsibilities, order correction and impose a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, it shall order suspension of work for remediation or suspension of business for remediation:  
(1) When loading or unloading materials, sealing or spraying methods are not adopted to prevent dust pollution;  
(2) Materials prone to generating dust such as coal, coal gangue, coal slag, coal ash, cement, lime, gypsum, sand, iron ore, etc., are not stored in a sealed manner;  
(3) For materials prone to generating dust that cannot be stored in a sealed manner, a tight enclosure not lower than the height of the stockpile is not set up, or effective covering measures are not taken to prevent dust pollution;  
(4) An entity stores coal, coal gangue, coal slag, coal ash, and other materials without taking fire prevention measures;  
(5) Docks, mines, landfills, and receiving sites do not carry out partitioned operations or take effective measures to prevent dust pollution;  
(6) Enterprises or institutions discharging toxic and hazardous air pollutants listed in the catalogue of toxic and hazardous air pollutants fail to establish an environmental risk early warning system, investigate environmental safety hazards, and take effective measures to prevent environmental risks;  
(7) Enterprises, institutions, or other production or business operators that discharge persistent organic pollutants into the atmosphere, or operators of waste incineration facilities, fail to adopt technical methods and processes that help reduce the emission of persistent organic pollutants or to equip with effective purification devices;  
(8) Effective measures are not taken to prevent the emission of malodorous gases.

<a id="art-1131"></a>第一千一百三十一条　违反本法规定，在人口集中地区对树木、花草喷洒剧毒、高毒农药，或者在禁止焚烧的区域和时段露天焚烧秸秆、落叶等产生烟尘污染的物质的，由县级以上地方人民政府确定的监督管理部门责令改正，可以处五百元以上二千元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，生产、使用国家明令禁止生产、使用的农药，被责令改正，拒不改正的，由农业农村主管部门依照有关法律法规的规定予以处罚，并将案件移送公安机关，由公安机关对其直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处十日以上十五日以下拘留；情节较轻的，处五日以上十日以下拘留。  
违反本法规定，在人口集中地区和其他依法需要特殊保护的区域内，焚烧沥青、油毡、橡胶、塑料、皮革、垃圾以及其他产生有毒有害烟尘和恶臭气体的物质的，由县级人民政府确定的监督管理部门责令改正，对单位处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款，对个人处五百元以上二千元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，在禁止燃放的时段和区域内燃放烟花爆竹的，由县级以上地方人民政府确定的监督管理部门依法予以处罚。

**Article 1131** : Where, in violation of this Law, a party sprays highly toxic or acutely toxic pesticides on trees, flowers, or grass in densely populated areas, or openly burns substances that produce smoke and dust pollution such as straw or fallen leaves in areas or during periods where burning is prohibited, the supervisory and administrative department designated by the local people's government at or above the county level shall order correction and may impose a fine of not less than 500 yuan but not more than 2,000 yuan.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a party produces or uses pesticides explicitly prohibited by the state, and, after being ordered to correct, refuses to do so, the competent department of agriculture and rural affairs shall impose a penalty in accordance with the provisions of relevant laws and regulations, and shall transfer the case to the public security organ; the public security organ shall impose detention of not less than 10 days but not more than 15 days on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons; if the circumstances are relatively minor, detention of not less than 5 days but not more than 10 days shall be imposed.  
Where, in violation of this Law, within densely populated areas or other areas requiring special protection according to law, a party burns asphalt, oil felt, rubber, plastic, leather, garbage, or other substances that produce toxic, hazardous smoke or malodorous gases, the supervisory and administrative department designated by the people's government at the county level shall order correction, and impose a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan on the entity, and a fine of not less than 500 yuan but not more than 2,000 yuan on the individual.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a party sets off fireworks or firecrackers during prohibited periods or in prohibited areas, the supervisory and administrative department designated by the local people's government at or above the county level shall impose a penalty according to law.

<a id="art-1132"></a>第一千一百三十二条　违反本法规定，排放油烟的餐饮服务业经营者未安装油烟净化等污染防治设施，未保持正常使用油烟净化等污染防治设施，或者未采取其他油烟净化措施，致使超过排放标准排放油烟污染物的，由县级以上地方人民政府确定的监督管理部门责令改正，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，责令停业整治。  
违反本法规定，在居民住宅楼、未配套设立专用烟道的商住综合楼、商住综合楼内与居住层相邻的商业楼层内新建、改建、扩建产生油烟、异味、废气的餐饮服务项目的，由县级以上地方人民政府确定的监督管理部门责令改正；拒不改正的，责令关闭，并处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，在当地人民政府禁止的时段和区域内露天烧烤食品或者为露天烧烤食品提供场地的，由县级以上地方人民政府确定的监督管理部门责令改正，没收烧烤工具和违法所得，并处五百元以上五千元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处五千元以上二万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1132** : Where, in violation of this Law, a catering service operator that emits cooking fumes fails to install pollution prevention facilities such as fume purification devices, fails to maintain the normal operation of such facilities, or fails to take other fume purification measures, resulting in the discharge of cooking fume pollutants exceeding the emission standards, the supervisory and administrative department designated by the local people's government at or above the county level shall order correction and impose a fine of not less than 5,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, it shall order suspension of business for remediation.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a party constructs, renovates, or expands a catering service project that produces cooking fumes, odors, or waste gas in a residential building, a commercial-residential mixed-use building without dedicated flues, or in commercial floors adjacent to residential floors within a commercial-residential mixed-use building, the supervisory and administrative department designated by the local people's government at or above the county level shall order correction; if the party refuses to correct, it shall order closure and impose a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a party barbecues food in the open air or provides a venue for open-air barbecuing during periods or in areas prohibited by the local people's government, the supervisory and administrative department designated by the local people's government at or above the county level shall order correction, confiscate the barbecue tools and illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than 500 yuan but not more than 5,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, a fine of not less than 5,000 yuan but not more than 20,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1133"></a>第一千一百三十三条　违反本法规定，从事服装干洗和机动车维修等服务活动，未设置异味和废气处理装置等污染防治设施并保持正常使用的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处二千元以上二万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，责令停业整治。

**Article 1133** : Where, in violation of this Law, a party engaged in service activities such as garment dry cleaning and motor vehicle repair fails to install pollution prevention facilities such as odor and waste gas treatment devices and maintain their normal use, the competent department of ecological environment shall order correction and impose a fine of not less than 2,000 yuan but not more than 20,000 yuan; if the party refuses to correct, it shall order suspension of business for remediation.

<a id="art-1134"></a>第一千一百三十四条　违反本法规定，擅自向社会发布重污染天气预报预警信息，构成违反治安管理行为的，由公安机关依法予以处罚。  
违反本法规定，拒不执行停止工地土石方作业或者建筑物拆除施工等重污染天气应对措施的，由县级以上地方人民政府确定的监督管理部门处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1134** : Where, in violation of this Law, a party, without authorization, releases heavy pollution weather forecast or warning information to the public, constituting a violation of public security administration, the public security organ shall impose a penalty according to law.  
Where, in violation of this Law, a party refuses to implement heavy pollution weather response measures such as suspending earthwork or building demolition at construction sites, the supervisory and administrative department designated by the local people's government at or above the county level shall impose a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan.

#### 第五节　违反水污染防治规定

<a id="art-1135"></a>第一千一百三十五条　违反本法第二编第三分编规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1135** : Where a party violates the provisions of Part Two, Division Three of this Law, the provisions of this Section shall apply for administrative penalties.

<a id="art-1136"></a>第一千一百三十六条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，采取治理措施消除污染，处以罚款，可以责令采取限制生产、停产整治等措施；拒不采取治理措施的，可以指定有治理能力的单位代为治理，所需费用由违法者承担；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）向水体排放油类、酸液、碱液；  
（二）向水体排放剧毒废液，或者将含有汞、镉、砷、铬、铅、氰化物、黄磷等的可溶性剧毒废渣向水体排放、倾倒或者直接埋入地下；  
（三）在水体清洗装贮过油类、有毒有害污染物的车辆或者容器；  
（四）违反国家规定或者标准，向水体排放含热废水或者含病原体的污水；  
（五）向水体排放、倾倒工业废渣、城镇垃圾或者其他废弃物，或者向江河湖泊、运河、渠道、水库及其最高水位线以下的滩地、岸坡以及法律法规规定的其他地点倾倒、堆放、贮存固体废物或者其他污染物。  
有前款第三项、第四项情形之一的，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；有前款第一项、第五项情形之一的，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；有前款第二项情形的，处二十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1136** : Where, in violation of this Law, any of the following circumstances occurs, the competent department of ecological environment shall order cessation of the illegal act, take control measures to eliminate pollution, impose a fine, and may order measures such as restricted production and suspension of operations for remediation; if the party refuses to take control measures, the department may designate a unit with the capability to carry out the remediation on its behalf, with the costs borne by the violator; if the circumstances are serious, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with approval authority for approval to order suspension of business and closure:  
(1) Discharging oils, acid solutions, or alkali solutions into waters;  
(2) Discharging highly toxic waste liquids into waters, or discharging, dumping, or directly burying soluble highly toxic waste residues containing mercury, cadmium, arsenic, chromium, lead, cyanide, yellow phosphorus, etc., into waters;  
(3) Cleaning vehicles or containers that have stored oils or toxic or hazardous pollutants in waters;  
(4) Discharging thermal wastewater or wastewater containing pathogens into waters in violation of state regulations or standards;  
(5) Discharging or dumping industrial waste residues, municipal garbage, or other waste into waters, or dumping, piling up, or storing solid waste or other pollutants into rivers, lakes, canals, channels, reservoirs, and their beaches and bank slopes below the highest water level, or other locations prescribed by laws and regulations.  
Where any of the circumstances specified in items (3) or (4) of the preceding paragraph occurs, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed; where any of the circumstances specified in items (1) or (5) of the preceding paragraph occurs, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed; where the circumstance specified in item (2) of the preceding paragraph occurs, a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1137"></a>第一千一百三十七条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，采取治理措施消除污染，处以罚款，可以责令采取限制生产、停产整治等措施；拒不采取治理措施的，指定有治理能力的单位代为治理，所需费用由违法者承担；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）未采取防渗漏等措施，未建设地下水水质监测井进行监测；  
（二）加油站、采油厂等的地下油罐未使用双层罐或者采取建造防渗池等其他有效措施，或者未进行防渗漏监测；  
（三）利用无防渗漏措施的沟渠、坑塘等输送或者贮存含有毒有害污染物的废水、含病原体的污水或者其他废弃物；  
（四）未按照规定采取防护性措施。  
有前款第一项、第二项情形之一的，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；有前款第三项、第四项情形之一的，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1137** : Where, in violation of this Law, any of the following circumstances occurs, the competent department of ecological environment shall order cessation of the illegal act, take control measures to eliminate pollution, impose a fine, and may order measures such as restricted production and suspension of operations for remediation; if the party refuses to take control measures, the department shall designate a unit with the capability to carry out the remediation on its behalf, with the costs borne by the violator; if the circumstances are serious, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with approval authority for approval to order suspension of business and closure:  
(1) Failure to take anti-leakage measures or to construct groundwater quality monitoring wells for monitoring;  
(2) Underground oil tanks at gas stations, oil extraction plants, etc., are not double-walled or other effective measures such as constructing anti-leakage pools are not taken, or anti-leakage monitoring is not conducted;  
(3) Using ditches, pits, or ponds without anti-leakage measures to transport or store wastewater containing toxic or hazardous pollutants, wastewater containing pathogens, or other waste;  
(4) Failure to take protective measures as required.  
Where any of the circumstances specified in items (1) or (2) of the preceding paragraph occurs, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed; where any of the circumstances specified in items (3) or (4) of the preceding paragraph occurs, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1138"></a>第一千一百三十八条　违反本法规定，在泉域保护范围以及岩溶强发育、存在较多落水洞和岩溶漏斗的区域内，新建、改建、扩建造成地下水污染的建设项目的，由生态环境主管部门处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款，并报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令拆除或者关闭。

**Article 1138** : Where, in violation of this Law, a construction project that causes groundwater pollution is constructed, renovated, or expanded within the protection scope of a spring area or in an area with strong karst development, numerous sinkholes, and karst funnels, the competent department of ecological environment shall impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan, and shall report to the people's government with approval authority for approval to order demolition or closure.

<a id="art-1139"></a>第一千一百三十九条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处以罚款，可以责令采取限制生产、停产整治等措施；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）未采取有效措施，致使在处理安全生产事故过程中产生的可能严重污染水体的消防废物直接排入水体；  
（二）稀释排放含有毒有害水污染物的工业废水，但不属于以逃避监管方式排放污染物；  
（三）向农田灌溉渠道排放工业废水或者医疗污水；  
（四）向农田灌溉渠道排放城镇污水以及未综合利用的水产养殖尾水、农产品加工废水，致使其下游最近的灌溉取水点的水质不符合农田灌溉用水水质标准。  
有前款第三项、第四项情形之一的，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；有前款第一项、第二项情形之一的，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1139** : Where, in violation of this Law, any of the following circumstances occurs, the competent department of ecological environment shall order correction, impose a fine, and may order measures such as restricted production and suspension of operations for remediation; if the circumstances are serious, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with approval authority for approval to order suspension of business and closure:  
(1) Failure to take effective measures, resulting in the direct discharge into waters of fire-fighting waste generated during the handling of production safety accidents that may seriously pollute waters;  
(2) Diluting and discharging industrial wastewater containing toxic or hazardous water pollutants, but which does not constitute discharging pollutants by means of evading supervision;  
(3) Discharging industrial wastewater or medical wastewater into farmland irrigation channels;  
(4) Discharging urban wastewater and aquaculture tail water not utilized comprehensively, or agro-product processing wastewater into farmland irrigation channels, causing the water quality at the nearest irrigation intake downstream to fail to meet the water quality standards for farmland irrigation.  
Where any of the circumstances specified in items (3) or (4) of the preceding paragraph occurs, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed; where any of the circumstances specified in items (1) or (2) of the preceding paragraph occurs, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1140"></a>第一千一百四十条　违反本法规定，向污水集中处理设施排放不符合处理工艺要求的工业废水的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正或者责令限制生产、停产整治，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1140** : Where, in violation of this Law, industrial wastewater that does not meet the requirements of the treatment process is discharged into a centralized wastewater treatment facility, the competent department of ecological environment shall order correction or order restricted production and suspension of operations for remediation, and impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, the matter shall be reported to the people's government with approval authority for approval to order suspension of business and closure.

<a id="art-1141"></a>第一千一百四十一条　违反本法规定，建设不符合国家产业政策的小型造纸、制革、印染、染料、炼焦、炼硫、炼砷、炼汞、炼油、电镀、农药、石棉、水泥、玻璃、钢铁、火电以及其他严重污染水环境的生产项目的，由所在地设区的市、县级人民政府责令关闭。

**Article 1141** 第一千一百四十一条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, constructs small-scale papermaking, leather tanning, printing and dyeing, dyestuff, coking, sulfur refining, arsenic refining, mercury refining, oil refining, electroplating, pesticide, asbestos, cement, glass, iron and steel, thermal power, or other production projects that do not comply with the national industrial policy and that seriously pollute the water environment, shall be ordered to close down by the people's government of the city divided into districts or the county people's government at the place where the project is located.

<a id="art-1142"></a>第一千一百四十二条　违反本法规定，船舶未配备相应的防污设备和器材，或者未持有合法有效的防治水域环境污染的证书与文书的，由海事管理机构、渔业渔政主管部门按照职责分工责令限期改正，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款；逾期不改正的，责令船舶临时停航。  
违反本法规定，船舶进行涉及污染物、压载水等排放及操作，未遵守操作规程，未实施监测、监控，或者未如实记录并保存的，由海事管理机构、渔业渔政主管部门按照职责分工责令改正，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1142** 第一千一百四十二条 Any ship that, in violation of this Law, fails to be equipped with corresponding anti-pollution equipment and facilities, or fails to hold legally valid certificates and documents for the prevention and control of water environment pollution, shall be ordered to make corrections within a specified time limit by the maritime administrative agency or the fishery and fishery administration department according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined not less than 5,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan; if correction is not made within the specified time limit, the ship shall be ordered to suspend navigation temporarily.  
  
Any ship that, in violation of this Law, engages in the discharge or operation involving pollutants or ballast water, fails to comply with operational procedures, fails to implement monitoring and supervision, or fails to truthfully record and preserve the relevant information, shall be ordered to make corrections by the maritime administrative agency or the fishery and fishery administration department according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined not less than 5,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1143"></a>第一千一百四十三条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由海事管理机构、渔业渔政主管部门按照职责分工责令停止违法行为，处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款；造成水污染的，责令限期采取治理措施消除污染，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；逾期不采取治理措施的，指定有治理能力的单位代为治理，所需费用由船舶承担：  
（一）船舶向水体倾倒垃圾或者排放船舶的残油、废油、含油污水、生活污水；  
（二）船舶装载运输油类或者有毒有害货物未采取防止溢流和渗漏的措施；  
（三）船舶及有关作业单位从事有污染风险的作业活动，未采取污染防治措施；  
（四）船舶进行散装液体污染危害性货物的过驳作业，未编制方案或者未按照规定报经批准。  
违反本法规定，采取冲滩方式进行船舶拆解作业的，由海事管理机构、渔业渔政主管部门按照职责分工责令停止违法行为，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，进入中华人民共和国内河的国际航线船舶，排放不符合规定的船舶压载水的，由海事管理机构、渔业渔政主管部门按照职责分工责令停止违法行为，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1143** 第一千一百四十三条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to cease the illegal act by the maritime administrative agency or the fishery and fishery administration department according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan; if water pollution is caused, the person shall be ordered to take control measures within a specified time limit to eliminate the pollution, and shall be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the control measures are not taken within the specified time limit, a unit with the ability to control shall be designated to carry out the control on behalf of the violator, and the expenses incurred shall be borne by the ship:  
(1) Dumping garbage into waters or discharging residual oil, waste oil, oily wastewater, or domestic sewage from a ship;  
(2) Loading and transporting oil or toxic and hazardous goods without taking measures to prevent overflow and leakage;  
(3) Engaging in operational activities with pollution risks without taking pollution prevention and control measures;  
(4) Conducting lightering operations of bulk liquid pollution-hazardous cargo without preparing a plan or without reporting for approval according to regulations.  
  
Any person who, in violation of this Law, carries out ship scrapping operations by beaching shall be ordered to cease the illegal act by the maritime administrative agency or the fishery and fishery administration department according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan.  
  
Any international voyage ship entering the inland waters of the People's Republic of China that, in violation of this Law, discharges ship ballast water that does not comply with the regulations shall be ordered to cease the illegal act by the maritime administrative agency or the fishery and fishery administration department according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1144"></a>第一千一百四十四条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由海事管理机构、渔业渔政主管部门按照职责分工责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款：  
（一）港口、码头、装卸站、船舶修造厂未按照规定配备或者有效运行船舶污染物、废弃物接收设施；  
（二）从事船舶污染物、废弃物接收和船舱清洗作业活动，不具备相应接收处理能力。

**Article 1144** 第一千一百四十四条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to make corrections by the maritime administrative agency or the fishery and fishery administration department according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan:  
(1) A port, wharf, loading and unloading station, or shipbuilding and repairing yard fails to be equipped with or effectively operate facilities for receiving ship pollutants and wastes according to regulations;  
(2) Engaging in the reception of ship pollutants and wastes or tank cleaning operations without having the corresponding reception and treatment capacity.

<a id="art-1145"></a>第一千一百四十五条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款，并报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令拆除或者关闭：  
（一）在饮用水水源一级保护区内新建、改建、扩建与供水设施和保护水源无关的建设项目；  
（二）在饮用水水源二级保护区内新建、改建、扩建排放污染物的建设项目；  
（三）在饮用水水源准保护区内新建、扩建对水体污染严重的建设项目，或者改建建设项目增加排污量。  
违反本法规定，在饮用水水源一级保护区内从事网箱养殖或者组织进行旅游、垂钓或者其他可能污染饮用水水体的活动的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款。个人在饮用水水源一级保护区内游泳、垂钓或者从事其他可能污染饮用水水体的活动的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，可以处五百元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，饮用水供水单位供水水质不符合国家规定标准的，由供水主管部门责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，可以责令停业整顿。

**Article 1145** 第一千一百四十五条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to cease the illegal act by the competent department of ecological environment, shall be fined not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan, and shall be ordered to demolish or close down upon approval by the people's government with the approval authority:  
(1) Constructing, rebuilding, or expanding a construction project unrelated to water supply facilities and the protection of the water source in a first-class protection zone of a drinking water source;  
(2) Constructing, rebuilding, or expanding a construction project that discharges pollutants in a second-class protection zone of a drinking water source;  
(3) Constructing or expanding a construction project that seriously pollutes the water body in a quasi-protection zone of a drinking water source, or rebuilding a construction project to increase the amount of pollutant discharge.  
  
Any person who, in violation of this Law, engages in cage culture or organizes tourism, angling, or other activities that may pollute the drinking water body in a first-class protection zone of a drinking water source shall be ordered to cease the illegal act by the competent department of ecological environment, and shall be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan. An individual who swims, angles, or engages in other activities that may pollute the drinking water body in a first-class protection zone of a drinking water source shall be ordered to cease the illegal act by the competent department of ecological environment, and may be fined not more than 500 yuan.  
  
Any drinking water supply unit that, in violation of this Law, supplies water with quality that does not meet the national standards shall be ordered to make corrections by the competent water supply department, and shall be fined not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, it may be ordered to suspend operations for rectification upon approval by the people's government with the approval authority.

#### 第六节　违反海洋污染防治规定

<a id="art-1146"></a>第一千一百四十六条　违反本法第二编第四分编规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1146** 第一千一百四十六条 Any violation of the provisions of the Fourth Subsection of Part II of this Law shall be subject to administrative penalties under the provisions of this Section.

<a id="art-1147"></a>第一千一百四十七条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处以罚款，可以责令采取限制生产、停产整治等措施；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）向海域排放油类、酸液、碱液；  
（二）向海域排放海洋油气装备的残油、废油；  
（三）向海域排放剧毒废液；  
（四）向海域排放钻井使用的油基泥浆和其他有毒复合泥浆；  
（五）向海域排放未达标的含油污水和油性混合物；  
（六）向海域处置含油的工业固体废物；  
（七）违反国家规定或者标准，向海域排放含热废水，含病原体的医疗污水、生活污水和工业废水，含不易降解的有机物、重金属的废水；  
（八）在岸滩弃置、堆放、处理固体废物。  
有前款除第三项以外情形之一的，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；有前款第三项情形的，处二十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款。  
个人擅自在岸滩弃置、堆放和处理生活垃圾的，按次处一百元以上一千元以下的罚款。

**Article 1147** 第一千一百四十七条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to make corrections by the departments and agencies with the functions and duties of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management according to their respective functions and duties, shall be fined, and may be ordered to take measures such as restricting production or suspending production for remediation; if the circumstances are serious, the person shall be ordered to suspend operations or close down upon approval by the people's government with the approval authority:  
(1) Discharging oil, acid, or alkaline liquid into the sea;
