(2) Discharging residual oil or waste oil from offshore oil and gas equipment into the sea;  
(3) Discharging highly toxic waste liquid into the sea;  
(4) Discharging oil-based mud and other toxic composite mud used in drilling into the sea;  
(5) Discharging oily wastewater or oily mixtures that do not meet the standards into the sea;  
(6) Disposing of oil-containing industrial solid waste into the sea;  
(7) Discharging, in violation of national regulations or standards, heated wastewater, medical wastewater, domestic sewage, or industrial wastewater containing pathogens, or wastewater containing non-biodegradable organic substances or heavy metals into the sea;  
(8) Discarding, piling, or treating solid waste on the shore or beach.  
  
For any of the circumstances in the preceding paragraph except item (3), a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (3) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan shall be imposed.  
  
If an individual, without authorization, discards, piles, or treats domestic waste on the shore or beach, a fine of not less than 100 yuan but not more than 1,000 yuan shall be imposed per instance.

<a id="art-1148"></a>第一千一百四十八条　违反本法规定，从事海水养殖活动有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）违反养殖规模、养殖密度规定；  
（二）违反投饵、投肥、投饲、投药规定。

**Article 1148** 第一千一百四十八条 Any person engaged in mariculture activities who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to make corrections by the departments and agencies with the functions and duties of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined not less than 5,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, the person shall be fined not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan, and shall be ordered to suspend operations or close down upon approval by the people's government with the approval authority:  
(1) Violating the regulations on the scale and density of mariculture;  
(2) Violating the regulations on bait, fertilizer, feed, or drug application.

<a id="art-1149"></a>第一千一百四十九条　违反本法规定，在依法划定的自然保护地、重要渔业水域及其他需要特别保护的区域建设污染环境、破坏生态的海岸工程和海洋工程建设项目或者从事其他活动，或者在沿海陆域建设不符合国家产业政策的生产项目的，由县级以上人民政府按照管理权限责令关闭。

**Article 1149** 第一千一百四十九条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, constructs a coastal engineering or marine engineering construction project that pollutes the environment or damages the ecology, or engages in other activities in legally designated natural reserves, important fishery waters, or other areas requiring special protection, or constructs a production project that does not comply with the national industrial policy in the coastal land area, shall be ordered to close down by the people's government at or above the county level according to the management authority.

<a id="art-1150"></a>第一千一百五十条　违反本法规定，海岸工程和海洋工程建设项目有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令停止违法行为、消除危害，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）使用含超标放射性物质或者易溶出有毒有害物质的材料；  
（二）造成领海基点及其周边环境的侵蚀、淤积或者损害；  
（三）危及领海基点稳定。

**Article 1150** 第一千一百五十条 Any coastal engineering or marine engineering construction project that, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to cease the illegal act and eliminate the hazard by the departments and agencies with the functions and duties of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, the project shall be ordered to suspend operations or close down upon approval by the people's government with the approval authority:  
(1) Using materials containing radioactive substances exceeding the standard or toxic and hazardous substances that are easily leachable;  
(2) Causing erosion, silting, or damage to the baseline points of the territorial sea and their surrounding environment;  
(3) Endangering the stability of the baseline points of the territorial sea.

<a id="art-1151"></a>第一千一百五十一条　违反本法规定进行海洋油气勘探开发活动，造成海洋环境污染的，由海警机构责令改正，给予警告，并处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1151** 第一千一百五十一条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, carries out offshore oil and gas exploration and development activities and causes marine environmental pollution shall be ordered to make corrections and be given a warning by the maritime police agency, and shall also be fined not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1152"></a>第一千一百五十二条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由国务院生态环境主管部门及其海域派出机构、海事管理机构或者海警机构按照职责分工责令改正，处以罚款，暂扣或者吊销倾倒许可证，必要时可以扣押船舶；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）未取得倾倒许可证向海洋倾倒废弃物；  
（二）未按照倾倒许可证的规定倾倒废弃物；  
（三）未安装使用符合要求的海洋倾倒在线监控设备并与监管系统联网；  
（四）未按照规定报告倾倒情况；  
（五）倾倒废弃物的船舶驶出港口未报告；  
（六）获准倾倒废弃物的单位未依照本法规定委托实施废弃物海洋倾倒作业或者未监督实施；  
（七）海上焚烧废弃物。  
有前款第五项情形的，对违法船舶的所有人、经营人或者管理人处三千元以上三万元以下的罚款，对船长、责任船员或者其他责任人员处五百元以上五千元以下的罚款；有前款第四项情形的，按次处五千元以上二万元以下的罚款；有前款第三项情形的，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；有前款第六项情形的，处三万元以上三十万元以下的罚款；有前款第一项情形的，处二十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款；有前款第七项情形的，处五十万元以上五百万元以下的罚款。有前款第一项、第七项情形之一，二年内受到行政处罚三次以上的，三年内不得从事废弃物海洋倾倒活动；有前款第二项情形的，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，被吊销倾倒许可证的，三年内不得从事废弃物海洋倾倒活动。  
违反本法规定，以欺骗、贿赂等不正当手段申请取得倾倒许可证的，由国务院生态环境主管部门及其海域派出机构依法撤销倾倒许可证，并处二十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；三年内不得再次申请倾倒许可证。

**Article 1152** 第一千一百五十二条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to make corrections by the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council and its regional sea agencies, the maritime administrative agency, or the maritime police agency according to their respective functions and duties, shall be fined, the dumping permit shall be temporarily seized or revoked, and if necessary, the ship may be detained; if the circumstances are serious, the person shall be ordered to suspend operations or close down upon approval by the people's government with the approval authority:  
(1) Dumping waste into the sea without obtaining a dumping permit;  
(2) Dumping waste not in accordance with the provisions of the dumping permit;  
(3) Failing to install and use online monitoring equipment for marine dumping that meets requirements and connect it with the supervision system;  
(4) Failing to report dumping circumstances according to regulations;  
(5) A ship that dumps waste departing from port without reporting;  
(6) A unit that has been approved to dump waste failing to entrust or supervise the implementation of marine waste dumping operations in accordance with this Law;  
(7) Incinerating waste at sea.  
  
For the circumstance in item (5) of the preceding paragraph, the owner, operator, or manager of the illegal ship shall be fined not less than 3,000 yuan but not more than 30,000 yuan, and the captain, responsible crew members, or other responsible persons shall be fined not less than 500 yuan but not more than 5,000 yuan; for the circumstance in item (4) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 5,000 yuan but not more than 20,000 yuan shall be imposed per instance; for the circumstance in item (3) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (6) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 30,000 yuan but not more than 300,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (1) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (7) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 5,000,000 yuan shall be imposed. If any of the circumstances in items (1) or (7) of the preceding paragraph occurs and the person has been subject to administrative penalties three or more times within two years, the person shall not engage in marine waste dumping activities for three years; for the circumstance in item (2) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed, and if the dumping permit is revoked, the person shall not engage in marine waste dumping activities for three years.  
  
Any person who, in violation of this Law, obtains a dumping permit by fraudulent or bribery means or other improper means shall have the dumping permit revoked according to law by the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council or its regional sea agencies, and shall be fined not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; and the person shall not apply for a dumping permit again within three years.

<a id="art-1153"></a>第一千一百五十三条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处以罚款：  
（一）船舶向海域排放船舶垃圾、生活污水、含油污水、含有毒有害物质污水、废气等污染物、废弃物及其他有害物质；  
（二）向海洋排放不符合规定的船舶压载水和沉积物；  
（三）载运具有污染危害性货物的船舶未经批准进出港口或者装卸作业；  
（四）船舶进行散装液体污染危害性货物的过驳作业，未编制作业方案或者未按照规定报经批准；  
（五）装卸油类及有毒有害货物的作业，船岸双方未遵守安全防污操作规程。  
有前款第二项至第五项情形之一的，处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款；有前款第一项情形的，处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1153** 第一千一百五十三条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to make corrections by the departments and agencies with the functions and duties of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined:  
(1) A ship discharging pollutants, wastes, or other harmful substances such as ship garbage, domestic sewage, oily wastewater, wastewater containing toxic and hazardous substances, or exhaust gas into the sea;  
(2) Discharging ship ballast water and sediment that do not comply with regulations into the sea;  
(3) A ship carrying pollution-hazardous goods entering or leaving a port or loading or unloading without approval;  
(4) A ship conducting lightering operations of bulk liquid pollution-hazardous goods without preparing an operational plan or without reporting for approval according to regulations;  
(5) During the loading and unloading of oil and toxic and hazardous goods, the ship and shore parties failing to comply with safety and anti-pollution operational procedures.  
  
For any of the circumstances in items (2) to (5) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (1) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1154"></a>第一千一百五十四条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处以罚款：  
（一）船舶的材料、结构、配备的防污设备和器材不符合国家防治船舶污染海洋环境规定或者未经检验合格；  
（二）船舶未持有防治海洋环境污染的证书、文书；  
（三）船舶进行涉及船舶污染物、压载水和沉积物等排放及操作，未按照规定监测、监控，或者未如实记录和保存；  
（四）未在船上备有有害材料清单，未在船舶建造、营运和维修过程中持续更新有害材料清单，或者未在船舶拆解前将有害材料清单提供给从事船舶拆解的单位；  
（五）船舶采取措施提高能效水平未达到有关规定；  
（六）进入船舶污染物排放控制区的船舶不符合船舶污染物排放相关控制要求；  
（七）具备岸电使用条件的船舶靠港，不按照国家规定使用岸电；  
（八）具备岸电供应能力的港口经营人、岸电供电企业未按照国家规定为具备岸电使用条件的船舶提供岸电。  
有前款第四项情形的，处二万元以下的罚款；有前款第一项、第二项情形之一的，处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款，拒不改正的，责令船舶临时停航；有前款第三项、第五项情形之一的，处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款；有前款第六项情形的，处三万元以上三十万元以下的罚款；有前款第七项、第八项情形之一的，处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款，情节严重的，处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1154** 第一千一百五十四条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to make corrections by the departments and agencies with the functions and duties of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined:  
(1) The materials, structure, or anti-pollution equipment and facilities of a ship do not comply with the national regulations on the prevention and control of marine environment pollution from ships, or have not passed inspection;  
(2) A ship does not hold certificates or documents for the prevention and control of marine environment pollution;  
(3) A ship, in the discharge or operation involving ship pollutants, ballast water, and sediment, fails to conduct monitoring and surveillance according to regulations, or fails to truthfully record and preserve the relevant information;  
(4) A ship fails to carry a list of hazardous materials on board, fails to continuously update the list of hazardous materials during the ship's construction, operation, and repair, or fails to provide the list of hazardous materials to the unit engaged in ship scrapping before scrapping;  
(5) A ship taking measures to improve energy efficiency fails to meet the relevant requirements;  
(6) A ship entering a ship pollutant emission control area does not comply with the relevant control requirements for ship pollutant emissions;  
(7) A ship with shore power connection conditions, when berthing, fails to use shore power in accordance with national regulations;  
(8) A port operator or shore power supply enterprise with the capacity to supply shore power fails to provide shore power to ships with shore power connection conditions in accordance with national regulations.  
  
For the circumstance in item (4) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not more than 20,000 yuan shall be imposed; for any of the circumstances in items (1) or (2) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan shall be imposed, and if correction is refused, the ship shall be ordered to suspend navigation temporarily; for any of the circumstances in items (3) or (5) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (6) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 30,000 yuan but not more than 300,000 yuan shall be imposed; for any of the circumstances in items (7) or (8) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan shall be imposed, and if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1155"></a>第一千一百五十五条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处以罚款：  
（一）从事船舶污染物、废弃物接收和船舶清舱、洗舱作业活动，不具备相应接收处理能力；  
（二）港口、码头、装卸站、船舶修造拆解单位未按照规定配备或者有效运行船舶污染物、废弃物接收设施；  
（三）从事船舶拆解、旧船改装、打捞和其他水上、水下施工作业，造成海洋环境污染损害；  
（四）采取冲滩方式在海岸进行船舶拆解作业。  
有前款第一项、第二项情形之一的，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；有前款第三项情形的，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；有前款第四项情形的，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1155** 第一千一百五十五条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to make corrections by the departments and agencies with the functions and duties of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined:  
(1) Engaging in the reception of ship pollutants and wastes, or ship tank cleaning and hold cleaning operations without having the corresponding reception and treatment capacity;  
(2) A port, wharf, loading and unloading station, or shipbuilding, repairing, and scrapping unit fails to be equipped with or effectively operate facilities for receiving ship pollutants and wastes according to regulations;  
(3) Engaging in ship scrapping, old ship conversion, salvage, or other above-water or underwater construction operations, causing marine environmental pollution damage;  
(4) Carrying out ship scrapping operations on the coast by beaching.  
  
For any of the circumstances in items (1) or (2) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (3) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (4) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1156"></a>第一千一百五十六条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处以罚款：  
（一）未报或者谎报船舶载运污染危害性货物事先申报事项；  
（二）托运人未将托运的污染危害性货物的正式名称、污染危害性以及应当采取的防护措施如实告知承运人；  
（三）托运人交付承运人的污染危害性货物的单证、包装、标志、数量限制等不符合对所交付货物的有关规定；  
（四）托运人在托运的普通货物中夹带污染危害性货物或者将污染危害性货物谎报为普通货物；  
（五）需要船舶载运污染危害性不明的货物，未按照规定事先进行评估。  
有前款第一项情形的，处一万元以上五万元以下的罚款；有前款第三项、第五项情形之一的，处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款；有前款第二项情形的，处五万元以上十万元以下的罚款；有前款第四项情形的，处十万元以上二十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1156** 第一千一百五十六条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to make corrections by the departments and agencies with the functions and duties of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management according to their respective functions and duties, and shall be fined:  
(1) Failing to declare or making false declarations regarding the advance declaration matters for a ship carrying pollution-hazardous goods;  
(2) The shipper failing to truthfully inform the carrier of the proper name, pollution hazard, and the protective measures to be taken for the pollution-hazardous goods being shipped;  
(3) The documents, packaging, marks, quantity restrictions, etc., of the pollution-hazardous goods delivered by the shipper to the carrier do not comply with the relevant regulations for the delivered goods;  
(4) The shipper mixing pollution-hazardous goods into ordinary goods being shipped, or falsely declaring pollution-hazardous goods as ordinary goods;  
(5) For goods with unknown pollution hazard that need to be carried by ship, failing to conduct an assessment in advance according to regulations.  
  
For the circumstance in item (1) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan shall be imposed; for any of the circumstances in items (3) or (5) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (2) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan shall be imposed; for the circumstance in item (4) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1157"></a>第一千一百五十七条　违反本法规定，未按照国家规定缴纳倾倒费的，由国务院生态环境主管部门及其海域派出机构责令限期缴纳；逾期拒不缴纳的，处应缴纳倾倒费数额一倍以上三倍以下的罚款，并可以报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1157** 第一千一百五十七条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, fails to pay the dumping fee in accordance with national regulations shall be ordered to pay within a specified time limit by the competent department of ecological environment of the State Council or its regional sea agencies; if the person refuses to pay upon expiration of the time limit, the person shall be fined not less than one time but not more than three times the amount of the dumping fee payable, and may be ordered to suspend operations or close down upon approval by the people's government with the approval authority.

#### 第七节　违反土壤污染防治规定

<a id="art-1158"></a>第一千一百五十八条　违反本法第二编第五分编规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1158** 第一千一百五十八条 Any violation of the provisions of the Fifth Subsection of Part II of this Law shall be subject to administrative penalties under the provisions of this Section.

<a id="art-1159"></a>第一千一百五十九条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由地方人民政府负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门按照职责分工责令改正，处以罚款；拒不改正的，责令限制生产、停产整治：  
（一）土壤污染重点监管单位未建立并实施土壤、地下水污染隐患排查整治制度；  
（二）土壤污染重点监管单位拆除设施、设备或者建筑物、构筑物未制定并实施土壤污染防治工作方案；  
（三）拆除设施、设备或者建筑物、构筑物未采取土壤污染防治措施；  
（四）尾矿库运营、管理单位未按照规定采取有效措施防止土壤污染，未针对风险隐患采取相应措施，或者需要重点监管的尾矿库的运营、管理单位未进行土壤污染状况监测或者定期评估；  
（五）建设和运行污水集中处理设施、固体废物处置设施，未依照法律法规和相关标准的要求采取有效措施防止土壤污染。  
有前款情形之一的，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；有前款第二项至第五项情形之一，造成严重后果的，处二十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1159** 第一千一百五十九条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts shall be ordered to make corrections and be fined by the departments with the functions and duties of ecological and environmental protection supervision and management of the local people's government according to their respective functions and duties; if correction is refused, the person shall be ordered to restrict production or suspend production for remediation:  
(1) A key unit for soil pollution supervision fails to establish and implement a system for investigating and rectifying soil and groundwater pollution hazards;  
(2) A key unit for soil pollution supervision fails to formulate and implement a work plan for soil pollution prevention and control when dismantling facilities, equipment, or buildings or structures;  
(3) When dismantling facilities, equipment, or buildings or structures, soil pollution prevention and control measures are not taken;  
(4) The operation or management unit of a tailings pond fails to take effective measures to prevent soil pollution according to regulations, fails to take corresponding measures against risks and hazards, or the operation or management unit of a tailings pond that requires key supervision fails to monitor soil pollution conditions or conduct periodic assessments;  
(5) In constructing and operating centralized sewage treatment facilities or solid waste disposal facilities, effective measures to prevent soil pollution are not taken in accordance with the requirements of laws, regulations, and relevant standards.  
  
For any of the circumstances in the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed; for any of the circumstances in items (2) to (5) of the preceding paragraph that causes serious consequences, a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1160"></a>第一千一百六十条　违反本法规定，向农用地排放重金属或者其他有毒有害物质含量超标的污水、污泥，以及可能造成土壤污染的清淤底泥、尾矿、矿渣等的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，并处五十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款，并将案件移送公安机关，由公安机关对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处五日以上十五日以下拘留。

**Article 1160** 第一千一百六十条 Any person who, in violation of this Law, discharges into agricultural land sewage or sludge containing heavy metals or other toxic and hazardous substances in excess of the standards, or dredged sediment, tailings, slag, or other substances that may cause soil pollution, shall be ordered to make corrections by the competent department of ecological environment, the illegal gains shall be confiscated, and the person shall also be fined not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, the person shall also be fined not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than 2,000,000 yuan, and the case shall be transferred to the public security organ, which shall impose detention of not less than five days but not more than fifteen days on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons.

<a id="art-1161"></a>第一千一百六十一条　违反本法规定，农业投入品生产者、销售者、使用者未及时回收农药、肥料等农业投入品的包装废弃物或者农用薄膜，或者未将农药包装废弃物交由专门的机构或者组织进行无害化处理的，由农业农村主管部门责令改正，处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款；农业投入品使用者为个人的，可以处二百元以上二千元以下的罚款。

**Article 1161** Where any producer, seller or user of agricultural inputs, in violation of this Law, fails to promptly recycle packaging waste of agricultural inputs such as pesticides and fertilizers or agricultural films, or fails to deliver pesticide packaging waste to specialized institutions or organizations for harmless treatment, the competent department of agriculture and rural affairs shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 10,000 yuan but not more than RMB 100,000 yuan; if the user of agricultural inputs is an individual, a fine of not less than RMB 200 yuan but not more than RMB 2,000 yuan may be imposed.

<a id="art-1162"></a>第一千一百六十二条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，并处五十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处五千元以上二万元以下的罚款：  
（一）在土壤中使用重金属含量超标的降阻产品；  
（二）未单独收集、存放开发建设过程中剥离的表土；  
（三）实施风险管控、修复活动未对产生的污染物采取处理、处置措施，或者对土壤、周边环境造成新的污染；  
（四）转运的污染土壤属于危险废物的，修复施工单位未依法进行处置；  
（五）转运污染土壤，未将运输时间、方式、线路和污染土壤数量、去向、最终处置措施等提前报所在地和接受地生态环境主管部门；  
（六）在永久基本农田集中区域新建可能造成土壤污染的建设项目；  
（七）未达到土壤污染风险评估报告确定的风险管控、修复目标或者未按照规定进行土壤污染状况调查和土壤污染风险评估、风险管控、修复、风险管控效果评估、修复效果评估的建设用地地块，开工建设与风险管控、修复无关的项目。

**Article 1162** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification, confiscate illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 500,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than RMB 500,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed concurrently; the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be fined not less than RMB 5,000 yuan but not more than RMB 20,000 yuan:  
(1) Using resistance reduction products with heavy metal content exceeding the standard in soil;  
(2) Failing to separately collect and store topsoil stripped during development and construction;  
(3) Failing to take measures to treat or dispose of pollutants generated during risk control or remediation activities, or causing new pollution to soil or the surrounding environment;  
(4) Where the transported contaminated soil is hazardous waste, the remediation construction unit fails to dispose of it according to law;  
(5) Failing to report in advance the transportation time, method, route, quantity, destination and final disposal measures of contaminated soil to the competent department of ecology and environment of the location and the receiving place;  
(6) Constructing a construction project that may cause soil pollution in the concentrated area of permanent basic farmland;  
(7) Commencing construction of a project unrelated to risk control and remediation on a construction land plot that has not achieved the risk control and remediation objectives determined in the soil pollution risk assessment report, or has not conducted soil pollution status investigation, soil pollution risk assessment, risk control, remediation, effectiveness evaluation of risk control, or effectiveness evaluation of remediation as required.

<a id="art-1163"></a>第一千一百六十三条　违反本法规定，将重金属或者其他有毒有害物质含量超标的工业固体废物、生活垃圾或者污染土壤用于土地复垦的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1163** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, uses industrial solid waste, domestic waste or contaminated soil with heavy metal or other toxic and hazardous substance content exceeding the standard for land reclamation, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification, confiscate illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1164"></a>第一千一百六十四条　违反本法规定，土壤污染责任人或者土地使用权人未按照要求实施后期管理的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门按照职责分工责令改正，处一万元以上五万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1164** Where the soil pollution liability entity or land use right holder, in violation of this Law, fails to carry out post-management as required, the department responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and management shall, according to its duties, order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 10,000 yuan but not more than RMB 50,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 yuan but not more than RMB 500,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1165"></a>第一千一百六十五条　违反本法规定，土壤污染责任人或者土地使用权人有下列情形之一的，由地方人民政府负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门按照职责分工责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，并委托他人代为履行，所需费用由土壤污染责任人或者土地使用权人承担；对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处五千元以上二万元以下的罚款：  
（一）对土壤污染状况普查、详查和监测、现场检查表明有土壤污染风险的建设用地地块，用途变更为居住、公共管理与公共服务用地的地块等，未进行土壤污染状况调查；  
（二）土壤污染重点监管单位生产经营用地的用途变更或者在土地使用权收回、转让前，未进行土壤污染状况调查；  
（三）对土壤污染状况调查报告评审表明污染物含量超过土壤污染风险管控标准的建设用地地块等，未进行土壤污染风险评估；  
（四）对安全利用类和严格管控类农用地地块，建设用地土壤污染风险管控和修复名录中的地块等，未采取相应风险管控措施；  
（五）未按照规定实施修复；  
（六）风险管控、修复活动完成后，未另行委托有关单位对风险管控效果、修复效果进行评估。  
土壤污染责任人或者土地使用权人有前款第四项、第五项情形之一，情节严重的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门将案件移送公安机关，由公安机关对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处五日以上十五日以下拘留。

**Article 1165** Where the soil pollution liability entity or land use right holder, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts, the department responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and management of the local people's government shall, according to its duties, order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 20,000 yuan but not more than RMB 200,000 yuan; if it refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed, and the performance shall be entrusted to another person, with the necessary expenses borne by the soil pollution liability entity or land use right holder; the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be fined not less than RMB 5,000 yuan but not more than RMB 20,000 yuan:  
(1) Failing to conduct a soil pollution status investigation for construction land plots that, based on soil pollution status census, detailed survey, monitoring and on-site inspection, show risk of soil pollution, and plots whose use is changed to residential, public administration or public service land;  
(2) Failing to conduct a soil pollution status investigation before the change of use of production and operation land of key soil pollution monitoring entities or before the withdrawal or transfer of land use rights;  
(3) Failing to conduct soil pollution risk assessment for construction land plots, etc., whose soil pollution status investigation report evaluation shows that pollutant content exceeds the soil pollution risk control standard;  
(4) Failing to take corresponding risk control measures for agricultural land plots in safe utilization and strict control categories, plots in the soil pollution risk control and remediation list of construction land, etc.;  
(5) Failing to carry out remediation as required;  
(6) Failing to entrust another relevant entity to evaluate the effectiveness of risk control and remediation after the completion of risk control and remediation activities.  
If the soil pollution liability entity or land use right holder commits any of the circumstances in item (4) or (5) of the preceding paragraph and the circumstances are serious, the department responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and management shall transfer the case to the public security organ, which shall impose detention of not less than five days but not more than fifteen days on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons.

<a id="art-1166"></a>第一千一百六十六条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门、农业农村主管部门、林业草原主管部门按照职责分工责令改正；拒不改正的，处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款：  
（一）土壤污染重点监管单位未按年度报告有毒有害物质排放情况；  
（二）土壤污染重点监管单位未将贮存有毒有害物质的地下储罐信息报生态环境主管部门；  
（三）土地使用权人未依法报送土壤污染调查报告；  
（四）土壤污染责任人、土地使用权人未依法报送土壤污染风险评估报告；  
（五）土壤污染责任人未依法报告风险管控措施；  
（六）土壤污染责任人、土地使用权人未依法将修复方案、效果评估报告备案。

**Article 1166** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts, the competent department of ecology and environment, the competent department of agriculture and rural affairs, or the competent department of forestry and grassland shall, according to their respective duties, order rectification; if it refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 20,000 yuan but not more than RMB 100,000 yuan shall be imposed:  
(1) A key soil pollution monitoring entity fails to report the discharge of toxic and hazardous substances on an annual basis;  
(2) A key soil pollution monitoring entity fails to report the information on underground storage tanks storing toxic and hazardous substances to the competent department of ecology and environment;  
(3) The land use right holder fails to submit a soil pollution investigation report according to law;  
(4) The soil pollution liability entity or land use right holder fails to submit a soil pollution risk assessment report according to law;  
(5) The soil pollution liability entity fails to report risk control measures according to law;  
(6) The soil pollution liability entity or land use right holder fails to file the remediation plan and effectiveness evaluation report according to law.

<a id="art-1167"></a>第一千一百六十七条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门、工业和信息化主管部门按照职责分工责令改正；拒不改正的，处一万元以上五万元以下的罚款：  
（一）土壤污染重点监管单位未将土壤污染防治工作方案备案；  
（二）土地使用权人未将土壤污染状况调查报告备案。

**Article 1167** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts, the competent department of ecology and environment or the competent department of industry and information technology shall, according to their respective duties, order rectification; if it refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 10,000 yuan but not more than RMB 50,000 yuan shall be imposed:  
(1) A key soil pollution monitoring entity fails to file a soil pollution prevention and control work plan;  
(2) The land use right holder fails to file a soil pollution status investigation report.

#### 第八节　违反固体废物污染防治规定

<a id="art-1168"></a>第一千一百六十八条　违反本法第二编第六分编规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1168** Where any entity violates the provisions of Part Two, Chapter Six of this Law, the provisions of this Section shall apply to impose administrative penalties.

<a id="art-1169"></a>第一千一百六十九条　违反本法规定，擅自倾倒、堆放、丢弃、抛撒、遗撒、焚烧固体废物的，由生态环境主管部门、城镇排水主管部门、环境卫生主管部门按照职责分工责令停止违法行为，采取治理措施消除污染，没收违法所得，并处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，责令限制生产、停产整治；拒不采取治理措施的，指定有治理能力的单位代为治理，所需费用由违法者承担。  
前款规定的固体废物为危险废物、城镇污水处理设施产生的污泥和处理后的污泥的，处二十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款；对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款；造成严重后果的，处二百万元以上五百万元以下的罚款。  
个人擅自倾倒、堆放、丢弃、抛撒、遗撒、焚烧个人产生的生活垃圾、建筑垃圾的，由环境卫生主管部门责令改正，处一百元以上一千元以下的罚款。

**Article 1169** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, dumps, piles up, discards, scatters, lets fall, or burns solid waste without authorization, the competent department of ecology and environment, the competent department of urban drainage, or the competent department of environmental sanitation shall, according to their respective duties, order cessation of the illegal act, take control measures to eliminate pollution, confiscate illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, order restricted production or suspension of production for remediation; if it refuses to take control measures, designate a capable entity to take control on its behalf, with the necessary expenses borne by the violator.  
If the solid waste specified in the preceding paragraph is hazardous waste or sludge from urban sewage treatment facilities or treated sludge, a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 yuan but not more than RMB 2,000,000 yuan shall be imposed; the directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be fined not less than RMB 20,000 yuan but not more than RMB 100,000 yuan; if serious consequences are caused, a fine of not less than RMB 2,000,000 yuan but not more than RMB 5,000,000 yuan shall be imposed.  
If an individual dumps, piles up, discards, scatters, lets fall, or burns domestic waste or construction waste generated by himself/herself without authorization, the competent department of environmental sanitation shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 100 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1170"></a>第一千一百七十条　违反本法规定，未采取防范措施，造成工业固体废物扬散、流失、渗漏或者其他环境污染的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。工业固体废物为危险废物的，罚款数额为二十万元以上二百万元以下。

**Article 1170** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, fails to take preventive measures, causing industrial solid waste to scatter, run off, leak, or cause other environmental pollution, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, submit to the people's government with approval authority for approval, and order suspension of business or closure. If the industrial solid waste is hazardous waste, the amount of fine shall be not less than RMB 200,000 yuan but not more than RMB 2,000,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1171"></a>第一千一百七十一条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处以罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）在生态保护红线区域、永久基本农田集中区域和其他需要特别保护的区域内，建设工业固体废物、建筑垃圾、危险废物集中贮存、利用、处置的设施、场所和生活垃圾填埋场；  
（二）转移固体废物出省、自治区、直辖市行政区域贮存、处置未经批准；  
（三）转移固体废物出省、自治区、直辖市行政区域利用，未提前报送信息；  
（四）产生工业固体废物的单位违反本法规定委托他人运输、利用、处置工业固体废物；  
（五）贮存工业固体废物未采取符合国家生态环境保护标准的防护措施；  
（六）单位和其他生产经营者违反固体废物管理其他要求，污染环境、破坏生态。  
有前款第三项情形的，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；有前款除第三项以外情形之一的，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。对前款第六项情形的处罚，有关法律、行政法规另有规定的，依照其规定。  
违反本法规定，产生工业固体废物的单位未建立工业固体废物管理台账并如实记录的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正；拒不改正的，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1171** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification, confiscate illegal gains, and impose a fine; if the circumstances are serious, submit to the people's government with approval authority for approval, and order suspension of business or closure:  
(1) Constructing facilities and sites for centralized storage, utilization or disposal of industrial solid waste, construction waste, or hazardous waste, or domestic waste landfill sites, within ecological protection red line areas, concentrated areas of permanent basic farmland, or other areas requiring special protection;  
(2) Transferring solid waste out of the administrative region of a province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government for storage or disposal without approval;  
(3) Transferring solid waste out of the administrative region of a province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government for utilization without prior reporting of information;  
(4) A unit that generates industrial solid waste, in violation of this Law, entrusts another person to transport, utilize or dispose of industrial solid waste;  
(5) Failing to take protective measures that meet national ecological and environmental protection standards when storing industrial solid waste;  
(6) A unit or other business operator violates other requirements for solid waste management, polluting the environment or damaging the ecology.  
In the case of the circumstance in item (3) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 yuan but not more than RMB 500,000 yuan shall be imposed; in the case of any circumstance other than item (3) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed. Where punishment for the circumstance in item (6) of the preceding paragraph is otherwise provided by relevant laws or administrative regulations, such provisions shall apply.  
Where any entity generating industrial solid waste, in violation of this Law, fails to establish an industrial solid waste management ledger and keep true records, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification; if it refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 yuan but not more than RMB 200,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1172"></a>第一千一百七十二条　违反本法规定，城镇污水集中处理设施的运营单位或者污泥无害化处理处置单位对污泥流向、用途、用量等未进行跟踪、记录，或者处理处置后的污泥不符合国家标准的，由城镇排水主管部门责令改正，给予警告；造成严重后果的，并处十万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，指定有治理能力的单位代为治理，所需费用由违法者承担。

**Article 1172** Where the operator of a centralized urban sewage treatment facility or a sludge harmless treatment and disposal unit fails to track and record the flow, use, and quantity of sludge, or the sludge after treatment and disposal fails to meet national standards, in violation of this Law, the competent department of urban drainage shall order rectification and give a warning; if serious consequences are caused, a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 200,000 yuan shall be imposed concurrently; if it refuses to rectify, designate a capable entity to take control on its behalf, with the necessary expenses borne by the violator.

<a id="art-1173"></a>第一千一百七十三条　违反本法规定，尾矿、煤矸石、废石等矿业固体废物贮存设施停止使用后，未按照国家有关生态环境保护等规定进行封场的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1173** Where, in violation of this Law, after the cessation of use of a storage facility for mining solid wastes such as tailings, coal gangue, and waste rock, the facility is not closed in accordance with the provisions of the state on ecological and environmental protection, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1174"></a>第一千一百七十四条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由环境卫生主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处以罚款：  
（一）擅自关闭、闲置或者拆除生活垃圾处理设施、场所；  
（二）产生、收集厨余垃圾的单位和其他生产经营者未将厨余垃圾交由具备相应资质条件的单位进行无害化处理；  
（三）工程施工单位未按照规定对工程施工过程中产生的建筑垃圾进行利用或者处置；  
（四）工程施工单位未编制建筑垃圾处理方案并报备案，或者未及时清运工程施工过程中产生的建筑垃圾。  
单位有前款情形之一的，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；个人有前款第二项情形的，处一百元以上五百元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，未在指定的地点分类投放生活垃圾的，由环境卫生主管部门责令改正；拒不改正的，对单位处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款，对个人依法处以罚款。

**Article 1174** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts, the competent department of environmental sanitation shall order rectification, confiscate illegal gains, and impose a fine:  
(1) Closing, leaving idle, or demolishing domestic waste treatment facilities or sites without authorization;  
(2) A unit or other business operator that generates or collects kitchen waste fails to deliver the kitchen waste to a qualified entity for harmless treatment;  
(3) A construction unit fails to utilize or dispose of construction waste generated during the construction process as required;  
(4) A construction unit fails to prepare a construction waste treatment plan and file it, or fails to promptly remove construction waste generated during the construction process.  
If a unit commits any of the circumstances in the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed; if an individual commits the circumstance in item (2) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 100 yuan but not more than RMB 500 yuan shall be imposed.  
Where any entity, in violation of this Law, fails to sort and deposit domestic waste at the designated location, the competent department of environmental sanitation shall order rectification; if it refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 yuan but not more than RMB 500,000 yuan shall be imposed on the unit, and a fine according to law shall be imposed on the individual.

<a id="art-1175"></a>第一千一百七十五条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处以罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）未设置危险废物识别标志；  
（二）未制定危险废物管理计划，或者未申报危险废物有关资料；  
（三）未建立危险废物管理台账并如实记录；  
（四）未按照国家规定和生态环境保护标准贮存、利用、处置危险废物，或者将危险废物混入非危险废物中贮存；  
（五）将危险废物提供或者委托给无许可证的单位或者其他生产经营者从事相关活动；  
（六）未经安全性处置，混合收集、贮存、运输、处置性质不相容的危险废物；  
（七）未填写、运行危险废物转移联单；  
（八）未经批准擅自转移危险废物；  
（九）将危险废物与旅客在同一运输工具上载运；  
（十）未经过消除污染处理，将收集、贮存、运输、利用、处置危险废物的场所、设施、设备和容器、包装物及其他物品转作他用；  
（十一）未制定危险废物意外事故防范措施和应急预案。  
有前款第一项、第二项、第三项、第四项、第六项、第七项、第九项、第十项、第十一项情形之一的，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款，拒不改正的，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；有前款第五项、第八项情形之一的，处二十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1175** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification, confiscate illegal gains, and impose a fine; if the circumstances are serious, submit to the people's government with approval authority for approval, and order suspension of business or closure:  
(1) Failing to set up hazardous waste identification signs;  
(2) Failing to formulate a hazardous waste management plan, or failing to declare relevant information on hazardous waste;  
(3) Failing to establish a hazardous waste management ledger and keep true records;  
(4) Failing to store, utilize or dispose of hazardous waste in accordance with national regulations and ecological and environmental protection standards, or mixing hazardous waste with non-hazardous waste for storage;  
(5) Providing or entrusting hazardous waste to an unlicensed unit or other business operator to engage in relevant activities;  
(6) Mixing, collecting, storing, transporting or disposing of incompatible hazardous waste without safety treatment;  
(7) Failing to fill in or run a hazardous waste transfer manifest;  
(8) Transferring hazardous waste without authorization or approval;  
(9) Transporting hazardous waste and passengers on the same means of transport;  
(10) Without decontamination treatment, converting places, facilities, equipment, containers, packaging and other articles used for collecting, storing, transporting, utilizing or disposing of hazardous waste to other uses;  
(11) Failing to formulate preventive measures and emergency plans for hazardous waste accidents.  
In the case of any of the circumstances in items (1), (2), (3), (4), (6), (7), (9), (10) or (11) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 yuan but not more than RMB 200,000 yuan shall be imposed; if the violator refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan shall be imposed; in the case of any of the circumstances in items (5) or (8) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 yuan but not more than RMB 2,000,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1176"></a>第一千一百七十六条　生产、销售和使用有毒有害物质超过国家标准的建筑材料和装修材料的，依照《中华人民共和国产品质量法》和有关民事、刑事法律的规定追究法律责任。

**Article 1176** Where any entity produces, sells or uses building materials and decoration materials whose toxic and hazardous substances exceed national standards, legal liability shall be investigated in accordance with the Product Quality Law of the People's Republic of China and relevant civil and criminal laws.

<a id="art-1177"></a>第一千一百七十七条　违反本法规定，危险废物产生者未按照规定处置其产生的危险废物，被责令改正后拒不改正的，由生态环境主管部门组织代为处置，处置费用由危险废物产生者承担，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1177** Where a hazardous waste generator, in violation of this Law, fails to dispose of the hazardous waste generated by it as required, and after being ordered to rectify, refuses to do so, the competent department of ecology and environment shall arrange for disposal on its behalf, with the disposal expenses borne by the hazardous waste generator, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan.

<a id="art-1178"></a>第一千一百七十八条　违反本法规定，企业事业单位无许可证从事收集、贮存、利用、处置危险废物经营活动的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，对收集、贮存危险废物经营活动并处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，对利用、处置危险废物经营活动并处一百万元以上五百万元以下的罚款，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭；对法定代表人、主要负责人、直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，企业事业单位未按照许可证规定从事收集、贮存、利用、处置危险废物经营活动的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，限制生产、停产整治，对收集、贮存危险废物经营活动并处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款，对利用、处置危险废物经营活动并处五十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款；对法定代表人、主要负责人、直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭，由发证机关吊销许可证。  
其他生产经营者有前两款违法情形的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，没收违法所得，并处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，并处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1178** Where any enterprise or public institution, in violation of this Law, engages in the business of collecting, storing, utilizing or disposing of hazardous waste without a license, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification, confiscate illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan concurrently for the business of collecting or storing hazardous waste, and a fine of not less than RMB 1,000,000 yuan but not more than RMB 5,000,000 yuan concurrently for the business of utilizing or disposing of hazardous waste; and submit to the people's government with approval authority for approval to order suspension of business or closure; the legal representative, principal responsible person, directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be fined not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 1,000,000 yuan.  
Where any enterprise or public institution, in violation of this Law, engages in the business of collecting, storing, utilizing or disposing of hazardous waste in violation of the provisions of the license, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification, confiscate illegal gains, restrict production or suspend production for remediation, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 500,000 yuan concurrently for the business of collecting or storing hazardous waste, and a fine of not less than RMB 500,000 yuan but not more than RMB 2,000,000 yuan concurrently for the business of utilizing or disposing of hazardous waste; the legal representative, principal responsible person, directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons shall be fined not less than RMB 50,000 yuan but not more than RMB 500,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, submit to the people's government with approval authority for approval to order suspension of business or closure, and the license-issuing authority shall revoke the license.  
Where any other business operator commits any of the illegal circumstances in the preceding two paragraphs, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification, confiscate illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 20,000 yuan but not more than RMB 100,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 yuan but not more than RMB 500,000 yuan shall be imposed concurrently.

<a id="art-1179"></a>第一千一百七十九条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由公安机关对法定代表人、主要负责人、直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处十日以上十五日以下拘留；情节较轻的，处五日以上十日以下拘留：  
（一）擅自倾倒、堆放、丢弃、抛撒、遗撒、焚烧固体废物，造成严重后果；  
（二）未采取防范措施，造成危险废物扬散、流失、渗漏或者其他严重后果；  
（三）未经批准擅自转移危险废物；  
（四）在生态保护红线区域、永久基本农田集中区域和其他需要特别保护的区域内，建设工业固体废物、建筑垃圾、危险废物集中贮存、利用、处置的设施、场所和生活垃圾填埋场；  
（五）无许可证或者未按照许可证规定从事收集、贮存、利用、处置危险废物经营活动；  
（六）将危险废物提供或者委托给无许可证的单位或者个人从事相关活动。

**Article 1179** Where any entity, in violation of this Law, commits any of the following acts, the public security organ shall impose detention of not less than 10 days but not more than 15 days on the legal representative, principal responsible person, directly responsible person in charge and other directly liable persons; if the circumstances are relatively minor, detention of not less than 5 days but not more than 10 days shall be imposed:  
(1) Dumping, piling up, discarding, scattering, letting fall, or burning solid waste without authorization, causing serious consequences;  
(2) Failing to take preventive measures, causing hazardous waste to scatter, run off, leak, or cause other serious consequences;  
(3) Transferring hazardous waste without authorization or approval;  
(4) Constructing facilities and sites for centralized storage, utilization or disposal of industrial solid waste, construction waste, or hazardous waste, or domestic waste landfill sites, within ecological protection red line areas, concentrated areas of permanent basic farmland, or other areas requiring special protection;  
(5) Engaging in the business of collecting, storing, utilizing or disposing of hazardous waste without a license or in violation of the provisions of a license;  
(6) Providing or entrusting hazardous waste to an unlicensed unit or individual to engage in relevant activities.

<a id="art-1180"></a>第一千一百八十条　执法过程中查获的固体废物无法确定责任人或者无法退运的，由所在地县级以上地方人民政府组织处理。

**Article 1180** Where the responsible person of solid waste seized in law enforcement cannot be determined or the solid waste cannot be shipped back, the local people's government at or above the county level shall organize its disposal.

#### 第九节　违反噪声污染防治规定

<a id="art-1181"></a>第一千一百八十一条　违反本法第二编第七分编规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1181** : Where the provisions of Part 7 of Book II of this Law are violated, the provisions of this Section shall apply for imposing administrative penalties.

<a id="art-1182"></a>第一千一百八十二条　违反本法规定，生产、进口、销售超过噪声限值的产品的，由市场监督管理部门、海关按照职责分工责令改正，没收违法所得，并处货值金额一倍以上五倍以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1182** : Where a product that exceeds the noise limit values is produced, imported, or sold in violation of the provisions of this Law, the market regulatory department or the Customs shall, according to their respective functions and duties, order rectification, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the value of the goods. If the circumstances are serious, upon approval by the people's government with the authority to approve, the violator shall be ordered to suspend business or close down.

<a id="art-1183"></a>第一千一百八十三条　违反本法规定，建设单位建设噪声敏感建筑物不符合民用建筑隔声设计标准要求的，由住房城乡建设主管部门责令改正，处建设工程合同价款百分之二以上百分之四以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，建设单位在噪声敏感建筑物禁止建设区域新建与航空无关的噪声敏感建筑物的，由地方人民政府指定的部门责令停止违法行为，处建设工程合同价款百分之二以上百分之十以下的罚款，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令拆除。

**Article 1183** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a construction entity constructs noise-sensitive buildings that do not meet the requirements of the civil building acoustic design standards, the competent department of housing and urban-rural construction shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than 2% but not more than 4% of the construction project contract price.  
  
Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a construction entity constructs, in an area where the construction of noise-sensitive buildings is prohibited, a noise-sensitive building unrelated to aviation, the department designated by the local people's government shall order cessation of the illegal act, impose a fine of not less than 2% but not more than 10% of the construction project contract price, and, upon approval by the people's government with the authority to approve, order demolition.

<a id="art-1184"></a>第一千一百八十四条　违反本法规定，在噪声敏感建筑物集中区域新建排放噪声的工业企业的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。  
违反本法规定，在噪声敏感建筑物集中区域改建、扩建工业企业，未采取有效措施防止工业噪声污染的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1184** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, an industrial enterprise that emits noise is newly built in a concentrated area of noise-sensitive buildings, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order cessation of the illegal act, impose a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 but not more than RMB 500,000, and, upon approval by the people's government with the authority to approve, order suspension of business or closure.  
  
Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, an industrial enterprise is renovated or expanded in a concentrated area of noise-sensitive buildings without taking effective measures to prevent industrial noise pollution, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 but not more than RMB 500,000; if the violator refuses to rectify, upon approval by the people's government with the authority to approve, the violator shall be ordered to suspend business or close down.

<a id="art-1185"></a>第一千一百八十五条　违反本法规定，建设单位、施工单位有下列情形之一的，由工程所在地人民政府指定的部门责令改正，处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，可以责令暂停施工：  
（一）超过噪声排放标准排放建筑施工噪声；  
（二）未依法取得连续施工作业证明，在噪声敏感建筑物集中区域夜间进行产生噪声的建筑施工作业。

**Article 1185** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, any construction entity or construction contractor falls under any of the following circumstances, the department designated by the local people's government at the project location shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 10,000 but not more than RMB 100,000; if the violator refuses to rectify, it may order suspension of construction:  
(1) Discharging construction noise exceeding the noise emission standards;  
(2) Carrying out noise-generating construction operations at night in a concentrated area of noise-sensitive buildings without having obtained a certificate of continuous construction operations according to law.

<a id="art-1186"></a>第一千一百八十六条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由工程所在地人民政府指定的部门责令改正，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款：  
（一）施工单位未按照规定制定噪声污染防治实施方案，或者未采取有效措施减少振动、降低噪声；  
（二）建设单位未监督施工单位落实噪声污染防治实施方案；  
（三）建设单位、施工单位依法取得连续施工作业证明但未按照规定公告附近居民。

**Article 1186** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, any person falls under any of the following circumstances, the department designated by the local people's government at the project location shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 5,000 but not more than RMB 50,000; if the violator refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 200,000 shall be imposed:  
(1) The construction contractor fails to formulate an implementation plan for noise pollution prevention and control in accordance with the provisions, or fails to take effective measures to reduce vibration and noise;  
(2) The construction entity fails to supervise the construction contractor in implementing the implementation plan for noise pollution prevention and control;  
(3) The construction entity or construction contractor, having obtained the certificate of continuous construction operations according to law, fails to announce to nearby residents in accordance with the provisions.

<a id="art-1187"></a>第一千一百八十七条　违反本法规定，驾驶拆除或者损坏消声器、加装排气管等擅自改装的机动车轰鸣、疾驶，机动车运行时未按照规定使用声响装置，或者违反禁止机动车行驶和使用声响装置的路段和时间规定的，由公安机关交通管理部门给予警告，处二百元以上二千元以下的罚款、暂扣机动车驾驶证等处罚。  
违反本法规定，铁路机车车辆、城市轨道交通车辆、机动船舶等交通运输工具运行时未按照规定使用声响装置的，由交通运输主管部门、铁路监督管理部门、海事管理机构或者地方人民政府指定的城市轨道交通有关部门按照职责分工责令改正，处五千元以上一万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1187** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a person drives a motor vehicle that has been illegally modified, such as by removing or damaging the muffler or adding an exhaust pipe, causing it to roar or speed, fails to use a sound device as prescribed when the motor vehicle is running, or violates the provisions on roads and time periods where motor vehicle operation or use of sound devices is prohibited, the traffic management department of the public security organ shall give a warning and impose a fine of not less than RMB 200 but not more than RMB 2,000, and may also temporarily impound the motor vehicle driving license.  
  
Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, railway locomotives and rolling stock, urban rail transit vehicles, motor vessels, or other transportation vehicles fail to use sound devices as prescribed when running, the competent department of transport, the railway regulatory department, the maritime safety administration, or the relevant department of urban rail transit designated by the local people's government shall, according to their respective functions and duties, order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 5,000 but not more than RMB 10,000.

<a id="art-1188"></a>第一千一百八十八条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由交通运输主管部门、铁路监督管理部门、民用航空主管部门或者地方人民政府指定的城市道路、城市轨道交通有关部门按照职责分工责令改正，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款：  
（一）公路养护管理单位、城市道路养护维修单位、城市轨道交通运营单位、铁路运输企业未履行维护和保养义务，未保持减少振动、降低噪声设施正常运行；  
（二）在限制建设区域建设噪声敏感建筑物，建设单位未进行建筑隔声设计或者不符合民用建筑隔声设计标准要求；  
（三）民用机场管理机构、航空运输企业、通用航空企业未采取措施防止、减轻民用航空器噪声污染；  
（四）负有责任的企业事业单位未按照噪声污染综合治理方案的要求采取有效措施减轻噪声污染。

**Article 1188** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, any person falls under any of the following circumstances, the competent department of transport, the railway regulatory department, the competent department of civil aviation, or the relevant department of urban roads or urban rail transit designated by the local people's government shall, according to their respective functions and duties, order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 5,000 but not more than RMB 50,000; if the violator refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 200,000 shall be imposed:  
(1) The highway maintenance and management unit, urban road maintenance and repair unit, urban rail transit operation unit, or railway transport enterprise fails to fulfill the maintenance and repair obligations and fails to keep the vibration-reducing and noise-reducing facilities in normal operation;  
(2) In constructing noise-sensitive buildings in a restricted construction area, the construction entity fails to carry out building sound insulation design or does not meet the requirements of the civil building acoustic design standards;  
(3) The civil airport management organization, air transport enterprise, or general aviation enterprise fails to take measures to prevent or mitigate noise pollution from civil aircraft;  
(4) The responsible enterprises or institutions fail to take effective measures to mitigate noise pollution in accordance with the requirements of the comprehensive noise pollution control plan.

<a id="art-1189"></a>第一千一百八十九条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由地方人民政府指定的部门责令改正，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款，并可以报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业：  
（一）超过噪声排放标准排放社会生活噪声；  
（二）在商业经营活动中使用高音广播喇叭或者采用其他持续反复发出高噪声的方法进行广告宣传；  
（三）未对商业经营活动中产生的其他噪声采取有效措施造成噪声污染。

**Article 1189** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, any person falls under any of the following circumstances, the department designated by the local people's government shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 5,000 but not more than RMB 50,000; if the violator refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 200,000 shall be imposed, and the violator may be ordered to suspend business upon approval by the people's government with the authority to approve:  
(1) Discharging social life noise exceeding the noise emission standards;  
(2) Using loudspeakers in commercial business activities or adopting other methods of continuously and repeatedly emitting high noise for advertising publicity;  
(3) Failing to take effective measures to prevent other noise generated in commercial business activities, thereby causing noise pollution.

<a id="art-1190"></a>第一千一百九十条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由地方人民政府指定的部门说服教育，责令改正；拒不改正的，给予警告，对个人可以处二百元以上一千元以下的罚款，对单位可以处二千元以上二万元以下的罚款：  
（一）在噪声敏感建筑物集中区域使用高音广播喇叭；  
（二）在公共场所组织或者开展娱乐、健身等活动，未遵守公共场所管理者有关活动区域、时段、音量等规定，未采取有效措施造成噪声污染，或者违反规定使用音响器材产生过大音量；  
（三）对已竣工交付使用的建筑物进行室内装修活动，未在限定作业时间内进行，或者未采取有效措施造成噪声污染；  
（四）其他违反法律法规规定造成社会生活噪声污染。

**Article 1190** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, any person falls under any of the following circumstances, the department designated by the local people's government shall persuade and educate and order rectification; if the violator refuses to rectify, a warning shall be given, and a fine of not less than RMB 200 but not more than RMB 1,000 may be imposed on an individual, or a fine of not less than RMB 2,000 but not more than RMB 20,000 may be imposed on a unit:  
(1) Using a loudspeaker in a concentrated area of noise-sensitive buildings;  
(2) Organizing or engaging in recreational or fitness activities in public places, failing to comply with the provisions of the public place manager on activity areas, time periods, volume, etc., failing to take effective measures, thereby causing noise pollution, or using audio equipment in violation of the provisions, generating excessive volume;  
(3) Carrying out interior decoration activities in a building that has been completed and delivered for use, not within the prescribed working hours, or failing to take effective measures, thereby causing noise pollution;  
(4) Other acts in violation of laws and regulations that cause social life noise pollution.

<a id="art-1191"></a>第一千一百九十一条　违反本法规定，新建居民住房的房地产开发经营者未在销售场所公示住房可能受到噪声影响的情况以及采取或者拟采取的防治措施，或者未纳入买卖合同，未在买卖合同中明确住房的共用设施设备位置或者建筑隔声情况的，由住房城乡建设主管部门责令改正，处一万元以上五万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，责令暂停销售。  
违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由地方人民政府指定的部门责令改正，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款：  
（一）居民住宅区安装共用设施设备，设置不合理或者未采取减少振动、降低噪声的措施，不符合民用建筑隔声设计标准要求；  
（二）对已建成使用的居民住宅区共用设施设备，未进行维护管理，不符合民用建筑隔声设计标准要求。

**Article 1191** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, the developer or seller of newly-built residential housing fails to display at the sales site the situation of possible noise impact on the housing and the prevention and control measures taken or to be taken, or fails to include such information in the sales contract, or fails to specify in the sales contract the location of the common facilities and equipment of the housing or the building sound insulation conditions, the competent department of housing and urban-rural construction shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 10,000 but not more than RMB 50,000; if the violator refuses to rectify, the sale shall be suspended.  
  
Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, any person falls under any of the following circumstances, the department designated by the local people's government shall order rectification and impose a fine of not less than RMB 5,000 but not more than RMB 50,000; if the violator refuses to rectify, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 200,000 shall be imposed:  
(1) In a residential area, common facilities and equipment are installed in an unreasonable manner or without taking measures to reduce vibration and noise, failing to meet the requirements of the civil building acoustic design standards;  
(2) For common facilities and equipment in an existing residential area that has been put into use, maintenance and management are not carried out, and they fail to meet the requirements of the civil building acoustic design standards.

<a id="art-1192"></a>第一千一百九十二条　违反本法规定，产生社会生活噪声，经劝阻、调解和处理未能制止，持续干扰他人正常生活、工作和学习，或者有其他扰乱公共秩序、妨害社会管理等违反治安管理行为的，由公安机关依法给予治安管理处罚。

**Article 1192** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a person generates social life noise, and after being dissuaded, mediated, or subjected to other handling, fails to cease such noise, continuously interfering with others' normal life, work, and study, or commits other acts of disturbing public order or obstructing social management that violate public security administration, the public security organ shall impose a public security administration penalty according to law.

#### 第十节　违反放射性污染防治规定

<a id="art-1193"></a>第一千一百九十三条　违反本法第二编第八分编等规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1193** : Where the provisions of Part 8 of Book II of this Law or other relevant provisions are violated, the provisions of this Section shall apply for imposing administrative penalties.

<a id="art-1194"></a>第一千一百九十四条　违反本法规定，向环境排放禁止排放的放射性废气、废液，倾倒放射性固体废物的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，限期采取治理措施消除污染，处二十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款；逾期不采取治理措施的，指定有治理能力的单位代为治理，所需费用由违法者承担；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1194** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a person discharges radioactive waste gas or waste liquid that is prohibited from being discharged into the environment, or dumps radioactive solid waste, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order cessation of the illegal act, order the adoption of remediation measures within a specified period to eliminate pollution, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 but not more than RMB 2,000,000; if the violator fails to take remediation measures within the specified period, a unit with remediation capability shall be designated to carry out remediation on its behalf, with the costs borne by the violator; if the circumstances are serious, upon approval by the people's government with the authority to approve, the violator shall be ordered to suspend business or close down.

<a id="art-1195"></a>第一千一百九十五条　违反本法规定，在内河水域和海上处置放射性固体废物、放射性物质的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，限期采取治理措施消除污染，处五十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款；逾期不采取治理措施的，指定有治理能力的单位代为治理，所需费用由违法者承担。

**Article 1195** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a person disposes of radioactive solid waste or radioactive substances in inland waters or at sea, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order cessation of the illegal act, order the adoption of remediation measures within a specified period to eliminate pollution, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 500,000 but not more than RMB 2,000,000; if the violator fails to take remediation measures within the specified period, a unit with remediation capability shall be designated to carry out remediation on its behalf, with the costs borne by the violator.

<a id="art-1196"></a>第一千一百九十六条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门、核工业主管部门按照职责分工责令限期改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；逾期不改正的，责令限制生产、停产整治：  
（一）不按照规定设置放射性标识、标志、中文警示说明；  
（二）不按照规定建立健全安全保卫制度；  
（三）不按照规定单独存放放射性同位素；  
（四）不按照规定采取安全和防护措施；  
（五）未对其工作人员进行放射性安全教育、培训和采取有效的防护安全措施。

**Article 1196** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, any person falls under any of the following circumstances, the competent department of ecology and environment or the competent department of nuclear industry shall, according to their respective functions and duties, order rectification within a specified period and impose a fine of not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000; if the violator fails to rectify within the specified period, the violator shall be ordered to restrict production or suspend production for remediation:  
(1) Failing to set up radiation signs, warnings, and Chinese warning instructions in accordance with the provisions;  
(2) Failing to establish and improve security protection systems in accordance with the provisions;  
(3) Failing to store radioactive isotopes separately in accordance with the provisions;  
(4) Failing to take safety and protection measures in accordance with the provisions;  
(5) Failing to provide radiation safety education and training to its staff and take effective protection and safety measures.

<a id="art-1197"></a>第一千一百九十七条　违反本法规定，未经核设施安全许可，核设施营运单位擅自进行核设施的选址、建造、运行、退役等活动的，依照《中华人民共和国核安全法》的规定追究法律责任。

**Article 1197** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a nuclear facility operator, without obtaining a nuclear facility safety license, carries out activities such as the siting, construction, operation, or decommissioning of a nuclear facility, legal liability shall be investigated in accordance with the provisions of the Nuclear Safety Law of the People's Republic of China.

<a id="art-1198"></a>第一千一百九十八条　违反本法规定，生产、销售、使用、转让、进出口、贮存放射性同位素和射线装置以及装备有放射性同位素的仪表的，由生态环境主管部门、市场监督管理部门、海关按照职责分工责令停止违法行为，限期改正，没收违法所得，并处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；逾期不改正的，处违法所得一倍以上五倍以下的罚款，违法所得不足五十万元的，以五十万元计算，责令限制生产、停产整治或者吊销许可证。  
违反本法规定，在网络上销售放射性同位素和射线装置的，由市场监督管理部门责令停止违法行为，限期改正，没收违法所得，并处违法所得一倍以上五倍以下的罚款，违法所得不足五十万元的，以五十万元计算。

**Article 1198** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a person produces, sells, uses, transfers, imports, exports, or stores radioactive isotopes, radiation devices, or instruments equipped with radioactive isotopes, the competent department of ecology and environment, the market regulatory department, or the Customs shall, according to their respective functions and duties, order cessation of the illegal act, order rectification within a specified period, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000; if the violator fails to rectify within the specified period, a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the illegal gains shall be imposed, and if the illegal gains are less than RMB 500,000, it shall be calculated as RMB 500,000, and the violator shall be ordered to restrict production, suspend production for remediation, or have its license revoked.  
  
Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a person sells radioactive isotopes or radiation devices on the internet, the market regulatory department shall order cessation of the illegal act, order rectification within a specified period, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the illegal gains; if the illegal gains are less than RMB 500,000, it shall be calculated as RMB 500,000.

<a id="art-1199"></a>第一千一百九十九条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，限期改正，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；逾期不改正的，由原发证机关指定有处理能力的单位代为处理或者实施退役，费用由生产、销售、使用放射性同位素和射线装置的单位承担：  
（一）生产放射源的单位和其他生产经营者未按照规定回收和利用废旧放射源；  
（二）使用放射源的单位和其他生产经营者未按照规定交回、送交废旧放射源；  
（三）使用对人体健康和生态环境潜在危害程度较高的放射源的场所和生产放射性同位素的场所，以及终结运行后产生放射性污染的射线装置，未依法实施退役。

**Article 1199** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, any person falls under any of the following circumstances, the competent department of ecology and environment shall order cessation of the illegal act, order rectification within a specified period, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000; if the violator fails to rectify within the specified period, the original licensing authority shall designate a unit with processing capability to carry out disposal or decommissioning on its behalf, with the costs borne by the unit that produced, sold, or used the radioactive isotopes or radiation devices:  
(1) The unit producing radioactive sources or other producers or business operators fails to recover and reuse waste radioactive sources in accordance with the provisions;  
(2) The unit using radioactive sources or other producers or business operators fails to return or deliver waste radioactive sources in accordance with the provisions;  
(3) The premises using radioactive sources with a higher potential hazard to human health and the ecological environment, the premises producing radioactive isotopes, and the radiation devices that generate radioactive contamination after termination of operation fail to implement decommissioning according to law.

<a id="art-1200"></a>第一千二百条　违反本法规定，不按照规定报告放射源丢失、被盗情况的，由公安机关、卫生健康主管部门、生态环境主管部门按照职责分工责令限期改正，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，责令限制生产、停产整治。

**Article 1200** : Where, in violation of the provisions of this Law, a person fails to report the loss or theft of a radioactive source in accordance with the provisions, the public security organ, the competent department of health, or the competent department of ecology and environment shall, according to their respective functions and duties, order rectification within a specified period and impose a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000; if the circumstances are serious, the violator shall be ordered to restrict production or suspend production for remediation.

<a id="art-1201"></a>第一千二百零一条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，限期改正，处以罚款：  
（一）生产、使用放射性同位素和射线装置的单位和其他生产经营者未按照规定对产生的放射性废物进行收集、包装、贮存；  
（二）未建造尾矿库等设施，或者建造尾矿库等设施不符合放射性污染防治的要求，贮存、处置铀（钍）矿和伴生放射性矿的尾矿等放射性固体废物；  
（三）不按照规定处理或者贮存不得向环境排放的放射性废液；  
（四）不按照规定的方式排放放射性废液；  
（五）将放射性废物提供或者委托给无许可证的单位或者个人贮存、处理和处置。  
有前款第一项、第三项情形之一的，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；有前款第二项、第四项、第五项情形之一的，处十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1201** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, falls under any of the following circumstances, the competent department of ecological environment shall order the cessation of the illegal act, set a deadline for correction, and impose a fine:  
(1) entities that produce or use radioisotopes and radiation devices and other producers and business operators fail to collect, package, and store the radioactive waste generated according to the provisions;  
(2) failing to construct facilities such as tailings ponds, or constructing facilities such as tailings ponds that do not meet the requirements for prevention and control of radioactive pollution to store and dispose of radioactive solid waste such as tailings from uranium (thorium) mines and associated radioactive mines;  
(3) failing to treat or store radioactive liquid waste that shall not be discharged into the environment according to the provisions;  
(4) discharging radioactive liquid waste not in the manner prescribed;  
(5) providing or entrusting radioactive waste to entities or individuals without a license for storage, treatment, and disposal.  
For any of the circumstances under subparagraph (1) or (3) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000 shall be imposed; for any of the circumstances under subparagraph (2), (4), or (5) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 but not more than RMB 1,000,000 shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1202"></a>第一千二百零二条　违反本法规定，放射性固体废物产生者，不按照国家规定对其产生的放射性固体废物进行处理和处置的，由生态环境主管部门责令停止违法行为，限期改正，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；逾期不改正的，指定有处理能力的单位代为处理、处置，所需费用由放射性固体废物产生者承担，并处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1202** . Where a generator of radioactive solid waste, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to treat and dispose of the radioactive solid waste generated by it in accordance with the state provisions, the competent department of ecological environment shall order the cessation of the illegal act, set a deadline for correction, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 200,000; if correction is not made within the time limit, an entity with treatment capacity shall be designated to conduct treatment and disposal on behalf of the generator, and the expenses shall be borne by the generator of radioactive solid waste, and a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 but not more than RMB 1,000,000 shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1203"></a>第一千二百零三条　违反本法规定，未经许可擅自从事贮存、处理和处置放射性废物活动的，由省级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门没收违法所得，并处一百万元以上五百万元以下的罚款，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。  
违反本法规定，未按照许可证规定从事贮存、处理和处置放射性废物活动的，由省级以上人民政府生态环境主管部门责令限期改正，没收违法所得，并处五十万元以上五百万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，责令限制生产、停产整治或者吊销许可证。

**Article 1203** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, engages in storage, treatment, and disposal of radioactive waste without a license, the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government at or above the provincial level shall confiscate the illegal gains, impose a fine of not less than RMB 1,000,000 but not more than RMB 5,000,000, and, upon approval by the people's government with approval authority, order suspension of business and closure.  
Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, engages in storage, treatment, and disposal of radioactive waste not in accordance with the provisions of the license, the competent department of ecological environment of the people's government at or above the provincial level shall order correction within a time limit, confiscate the illegal gains, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 500,000 but not more than RMB 5,000,000; if the circumstances are serious, it shall order restricted production, suspension of production for remediation, or revoke the license.

#### 第十一节　违反化学物质污染风险管控、电磁辐射和光污染防治规定

<a id="art-1204"></a>第一千二百零四条　违反本法第二编第九分编规定，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1204** . Where the provisions of Part II, Division 9 of this Law are violated, the administrative penalties under this Section shall apply.

<a id="art-1205"></a>第一千二百零五条　违反本法规定，未按照重点管控新污染物禁止或者限制环境风险管控措施要求，生产或者进口重点管控新污染物清单中的化学物质，或者使用重点管控新污染物清单中化学物质生产产品的，由生态环境主管部门或者其他负有化学物质生产、进口、使用监督管理职责的部门按照职责分工责令改正，处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处五十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款，责令限制生产、停产整治；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1205** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, produces or imports chemical substances listed in the inventory of key controlled new pollutants without complying with the environmental risk control measures for prohibition or restriction of key controlled new pollutants, or uses chemical substances listed in the inventory of key controlled new pollutants to produce products, the competent department of ecological environment or other departments responsible for the supervision and management of production, import, and use of chemical substances shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, order correction and impose a fine of not less than RMB 100,000 but not more than RMB 500,000; if correction is refused, a fine of not less than RMB 500,000 but not more than RMB 1,000,000 shall be imposed, and restricted production, suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered; if the circumstances are serious, upon approval by the people's government with approval authority, suspension of business and closure shall be ordered.

<a id="art-1206"></a>第一千二百零六条　违反本法规定，不按照新化学物质环境管理登记证的要求生产、进口新化学物质的，由生态环境主管部门或者其他负有化学物质生产、进口监督管理职责的部门按照职责分工责令改正，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处一百万元以上二百万元以下的罚款，责令限制生产、停产整治；情节严重的，吊销新化学物质环境管理登记证书，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1206** .
