Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, produces or imports new chemical substances not in accordance with the requirements of the environmental management registration certificate for new chemical substances, the competent department of ecological environment or other departments responsible for the supervision and management of production and import of chemical substances shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, order correction and impose a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 but not more than RMB 1,000,000; if correction is refused, a fine of not less than RMB 1,000,000 but not more than RMB 2,000,000 shall be imposed, and restricted production, suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered; if the circumstances are serious, the environmental management registration certificate for new chemical substances shall be revoked, and upon approval by the people's government with approval authority, suspension of business and closure shall be ordered.

<a id="art-1207"></a>第一千二百零七条　违反本法规定，无新化学物质环境管理登记证，生产、进口新化学物质，或者使用无新化学物质环境管理登记证的企业事业单位生产、进口的新化学物质生产产品的，由生态环境主管部门或者其他负有化学物质生产、进口、使用监督管理职责的部门按照职责分工责令改正，处二十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，处一百万元以上二百万元以下的罚款，责令限制生产、停产整治；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1207** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, produces or imports new chemical substances without an environmental management registration certificate for new chemical substances, or uses new chemical substances produced or imported by enterprises and institutions without such certificate to produce products, the competent department of ecological environment or other departments responsible for the supervision and management of production, import, and use of chemical substances shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, order correction and impose a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 but not more than RMB 1,000,000; if correction is refused, a fine of not less than RMB 1,000,000 but not more than RMB 2,000,000 shall be imposed, and restricted production, suspension of production for remediation shall be ordered; if the circumstances are serious, upon approval by the people's government with approval authority, suspension of business and closure shall be ordered.

<a id="art-1208"></a>第一千二百零八条　违反本法规定，未实行排污许可管理的企业事业单位，有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，可以责令限制生产、停产整治：  
（一）超过电磁辐射排放标准排放电磁辐射；  
（二）未依法采取电磁辐射污染防治措施防止、减轻电磁辐射污染。

**Article 1208** . Where an enterprise or institution that is not subject to pollutant discharge permit management, in violation of the provisions of this Law, falls under any of the following circumstances, the departments and agencies responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and management shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, order correction and impose a fine of not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000; if correction is refused, restricted production or suspension of production for remediation may be ordered:  
(1) discharging electromagnetic radiation that exceeds the emission standards for electromagnetic radiation;  
(2) failing to take measures for prevention and control of electromagnetic radiation pollution to prevent or mitigate electromagnetic radiation pollution according to law.

<a id="art-1209"></a>第一千二百零九条　违反本法规定，生产、进口、销售超过电磁辐射限值的产品的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，没收违法所得，并处货值金额一倍以上五倍以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1209** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, produces, imports, or sells products that exceed the limits for electromagnetic radiation, the departments and agencies responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and management shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, order correction, confiscate the illegal gains, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the value of the goods; if the circumstances are serious, upon approval by the people's government with approval authority, suspension of business and closure shall be ordered.

<a id="art-1210"></a>第一千二百一十条　违反本法规定，广告屏、广告牌、灯箱、媒体立面墙、道路、体育场、施工场地等，未采取有效措施防止、减轻光污染，干扰他人正常生活、交通出行的，由住房城乡建设主管部门、生态环境主管部门、交通运输主管部门、公安机关交通管理部门按照职责分工责令改正；拒不改正的，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1210** . Where an advertising screen, billboard, light box, media facade wall, road, stadium, construction site, or the like, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to take effective measures to prevent or mitigate light pollution, thereby interfering with others' normal life or traffic travel, the competent department of housing and urban-rural construction, the competent department of ecological environment, the competent department of transport, and the traffic management department of the public security organ shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, order correction; if correction is refused, a fine of not less than RMB 5,000 but not more than RMB 50,000 shall be imposed.

#### 第十二节　违反生态保护管理规定

<a id="art-1211"></a>第一千二百一十一条　违反本法规定，违反国土空间规划，在禁止开垦的范围内从事土地开发利用活动，或者未经批准，擅自改变土地或者海域用途的，由自然资源、林业草原等部门按照职责分工，依照《中华人民共和国土地管理法》、《中华人民共和国海域使用管理法》、《中华人民共和国水法》等法律法规的规定予以处罚。

**Article 1211** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, violates the territorial space planning, engages in land development and utilization activities within prohibited reclamation areas, or, without approval, illegally changes the use of land or sea areas, the departments of natural resources, forestry and grassland, among others, shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, impose penalties according to the provisions of the Land Administration Law of the People's Republic of China, the Sea Area Use Management Law of the People's Republic of China, the Water Law of the People's Republic of China, and other laws and regulations.

<a id="art-1212"></a>第一千二百一十二条　违反本法规定，破坏森林、草原、湿地等重要生态系统，或者非法侵占、违法利用、违法占用江河湖泊水域或者岸线的，由林业草原、水行政、生态环境等部门按照职责分工，依照《中华人民共和国森林法》、《中华人民共和国草原法》、《中华人民共和国湿地保护法》等法律法规的规定予以处罚。

**Article 1212** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, damages important ecosystems such as forests, grasslands, and wetlands, or illegally encroaches upon, illegally uses, or illegally occupies waters or shorelines of rivers and lakes, the departments of forestry and grassland, water administration, ecological environment, among others, shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, impose penalties according to the provisions of the Forest Law of the People's Republic of China, the Grassland Law of the People's Republic of China, the Wetland Protection Law of the People's Republic of China, and other laws and regulations.

<a id="art-1213"></a>第一千二百一十三条　违反本法规定，擅自引进、释放或者丢弃外来物种的，由农业农村主管部门、林业草原主管部门、海关按照职责分工，依照《中华人民共和国生物安全法》、《中华人民共和国野生动物保护法》等法律法规的规定予以没收或者责令限期捕回、找回，并处以罚款。

**Article 1213** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, introduces, releases, or abandons alien species without authorization, the competent department of agriculture and rural affairs, the competent department of forestry and grassland, and the customs shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, confiscate or order the recapture or retrieval within a time limit according to the provisions of the Biosafety Law of the People's Republic of China, the Wildlife Protection Law of the People's Republic of China, and other laws and regulations, and concurrently impose a fine.

<a id="art-1214"></a>第一千二百一十四条　违反本法规定，在长江、黄河等重要流域非法侵占、违法利用、违法占用江河湖泊岸线，或者未经批准在水土流失严重、生态脆弱区域开展可能造成水土流失的生产建设活动等的，依照《中华人民共和国长江保护法》、《中华人民共和国黄河保护法》等法律法规的规定予以处罚。

**Article 1214** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, illegally encroaches upon, illegally uses, or illegally occupies shorelines of rivers and lakes in important river basins such as the Yangtze River and the Yellow River, or, without approval, conducts production and construction activities that may cause soil erosion in areas with severe soil erosion and fragile ecology, penalties shall be imposed according to the provisions of the Yangtze River Protection Law of the People's Republic of China, the Yellow River Protection Law of the People's Republic of China, and other laws and regulations.

<a id="art-1215"></a>第一千二百一十五条　违反本法规定，在自然保护地、青藏高原等重要区域，有破坏保护界线标志、损害野生动植物等情形的，依照《中华人民共和国国家公园法》、《中华人民共和国青藏高原生态保护法》等法律法规的规定予以处罚。

**Article 1215** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law, in important areas such as nature reserves and the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau, damages boundary markers of protected areas, harms wildlife and plants, or the like, penalties shall be imposed according to the provisions of the National Park Law of the People's Republic of China, the Qinghai-Tibet Plateau Ecological Protection Law of the People's Republic of China, and other laws and regulations.

<a id="art-1216"></a>第一千二百一十六条　违反本法有关海洋生态保护的规定，造成珊瑚礁等海洋生态系统破坏的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正、采取补救措施，处每平方米一千元以上一万元以下的罚款，有违法所得的，没收违法所得。

**Article 1216** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law concerning marine ecological protection, causes damage to marine ecosystems such as coral reefs, the departments and agencies responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and management shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, order correction and take remedial measures, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 1,000 but not more than RMB 10,000 per square meter; if there are illegal gains, the illegal gains shall be confiscated.

<a id="art-1217"></a>第一千二百一十七条　违反本法有关海洋生态保护的规定，有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处以罚款：  
（一）占用、损害自然岸线；  
（二）在严格保护岸线范围内开采海砂；  
（三）在严格保护岸线范围以外的其他区域开发利用海砂资源，未依法采取严格措施保护海洋生态；  
（四）海砂开采者未依法为载运海砂的船舶提供合法来源证明；  
（五）未持有合法来源证明载运海砂资源。  
有前款第一项情形的，处每米五百元以上一万元以下的罚款；有前款第二项情形的，处货值金额二倍以上二十倍以下的罚款，货值金额不足十万元的，处二十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款；有前款第三项、第四项、第五项情形之一的，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1217** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law concerning marine ecological protection, falls under any of the following circumstances, the departments and agencies responsible for ecological and environmental protection supervision and management shall, in accordance with their respective functions and duties, order correction and impose a fine:  
(1) occupying or damaging natural shorelines;  
(2) mining sea sand within the scope of strictly protected shorelines;  
(3) developing and utilizing sea sand resources in areas other than the scope of strictly protected shorelines, without taking strict measures to protect marine ecology according to law;  
(4) a sea sand miner fails to provide a legal source certificate for a vessel carrying sea sand according to law;  
(5) transporting sea sand resources without holding a legal source certificate.  
For the circumstance under subparagraph (1) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 500 but not more than RMB 10,000 per meter shall be imposed; for the circumstance under subparagraph (2) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than two times but not more than twenty times the value of the goods shall be imposed, and if the value of the goods is less than RMB 100,000, a fine of not less than RMB 200,000 but not more than RMB 2,000,000 shall be imposed; for any of the circumstances under subparagraph (3), (4), or (5) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000 shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1218"></a>第一千二百一十八条　违反本法和有关法律法规的规定，未经批准擅自取用地下水的，由水行政主管部门责令停止违法行为，限期采取补救措施，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；其中，在地下水禁止开采区、限制开采区范围内擅自取用地下水的，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1218** . Where anyone, in violation of the provisions of this Law and relevant laws and regulations, extracts groundwater without approval, the competent department of water administration shall order the cessation of the illegal act, set a time limit for taking remedial measures, and impose a fine of not less than RMB 20,000 but not more than RMB 200,000; if the extraction of groundwater is conducted within the scope of prohibited or restricted mining areas for groundwater, a fine of not less than RMB 50,000 but not more than RMB 500,000 shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1219"></a>第一千二百一十九条　违反本法有关生态保护义务，本节未规定行政处罚的，依照有关法律的规定予以处罚。

**Article 1219** . Where anyone violates the obligations concerning ecological protection under this Law, and this Section does not provide for administrative penalties, penalties shall be imposed in accordance with the provisions of relevant laws.

#### 第十三节　违反绿色低碳发展管理规定

<a id="art-1220"></a>第一千二百二十条　违反本法规定，生产大型机电设备、机动运输工具以及国务院工业和信息化主管部门指定的其他产品的企业未注明产品材料成分或者不如实注明的，由市场监督管理部门责令限期改正；拒不改正的，处五万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1220** . Where an enterprise that produces large mechanical and electrical equipment, motor transport vehicles, and other products designated by the competent department of industry and information technology of the State Council, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to indicate the material composition of the products or does not truthfully indicate it, the market supervision and administration department shall order correction within a time limit; if correction is refused, a fine of not more than RMB 50,000 shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1221"></a>第一千二百二十一条　违反本法规定，不实施强制性清洁生产审核或者在清洁生产审核中弄虚作假，或者实施强制性清洁生产审核的企业不报告或者不如实报告审核结果的，由发展改革部门、生态环境主管部门按照职责分工责令限期改正；拒不改正的，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，承担评估验收工作的部门或者单位及其工作人员向被评估验收企业收取费用，不如实评估验收或者在评估验收中弄虚作假，或者利用职务上的便利谋取利益的，对负有责任的领导人员和直接责任人员依法给予处分。

**Article 1221** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to conduct compulsory cleaner production audit, or practices fraud in such audit, or fails to report or truthfully report the audit results, the development and reform department and the competent department of ecological environment shall, according to their respective functions, order it to make corrections within a prescribed time limit; if it refuses to make corrections, a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan shall be imposed.  
Where any department or entity undertaking the evaluation and acceptance work or any of its staff members, in violation of the provisions of this Law, collects fees from the enterprise being evaluated and accepted, fails to evaluate and accept truthfully, practices fraud in evaluation and acceptance, or seeks personal gains by taking advantage of their positions, the responsible leaders and directly responsible persons shall be given sanctions according to law.

<a id="art-1222"></a>第一千二百二十二条　违反本法规定，生产、进口、销售或者使用国家有关目录、名录中禁止或者淘汰的设备、材料、产品，采用国家有关目录、名录中禁止或者淘汰的技术、工艺，或者将淘汰或者限期淘汰的设备、材料、产品转让给他人使用的，由县级以上人民政府指定的部门、海关按照职责分工责令改正，没收违法所得，并处货值金额一倍以上五倍以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1222** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, produces, imports, sells or uses any equipment, material or product that is prohibited or eliminated in the relevant national catalogs or lists, adopts any technology or process that is prohibited or eliminated in the relevant national catalogs or lists, or transfers to others for use any equipment, material or product that is eliminated or subject to elimination within a prescribed time limit, the department designated by the people's government at or above the county level or the customs shall, according to their respective functions, order it to make corrections, confiscate the illegal gains, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than five times the value of the goods; if the circumstances are serious, upon approval by the people's government with the power of approval, order it to suspend business or close down.

<a id="art-1223"></a>第一千二百二十三条　违反本法规定，电器电子、机动车、铅蓄电池、动力电池等产品的生产者未按照规定建立与产品销售量相匹配的废旧产品回收体系并向社会公开的，由工业和信息化、商务等部门按照职责分工责令改正；拒不改正的，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1223** : Where a producer of electrical and electronic products, motor vehicles, lead-acid batteries, power batteries, or other products, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to establish a waste product recycling system commensurate with the sales volume of its products and make it public to society, the competent departments of industry and information technology, commerce, and other departments shall, according to their respective functions, order it to make corrections; if it refuses to make corrections, a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1224"></a>第一千二百二十四条　违反本法规定，生产经营者未遵守限制商品过度包装的强制性标准的，由市场监督管理部门和有关部门按照职责分工责令改正；拒不改正的，处二千元以上二万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，处二万元以上十万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1224** : Where any producer or business operator, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to comply with the compulsory standards for restricting excessive packaging of commodities, the market supervision and administration department and relevant departments shall, according to their respective functions, order it to make corrections; if it refuses to make corrections, a fine of not less than 2,000 yuan but not more than 20,000 yuan shall be imposed; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1225"></a>第一千二百二十五条　违反本法规定，未遵守国家有关禁止、限制生产、销售不可降解塑料袋等一次性塑料制品的规定的，由市场监督管理部门责令停止生产、销售，没收违法生产、销售的产品，并处违法生产、销售产品货值金额一倍以上三倍以下的罚款；有违法所得的，没收违法所得；情节严重的，吊销许可证。  
违反本法规定，未遵守国家有关禁止、限制使用不可降解塑料袋等一次性塑料制品的规定的，由商务、邮政等部门按照职责分工责令改正，对企业事业单位处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款，对其他生产经营者处五百元以上五千元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，未按照国家规定报告塑料袋等一次性塑料制品的使用、回收情况的，依照前款有关规定予以处罚。

**Article 1225** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to comply with the state provisions on prohibiting or restricting the production and sale of non-degradable plastic bags and other disposable plastic products, the market supervision and administration department shall order it to stop production and sale, confiscate the illegally produced and sold products, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than one time but not more than three times the value of the illegally produced and sold products; if there are illegal gains, the illegal gains shall be confiscated; if the circumstances are serious, the license shall be revoked.  
Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to comply with the state provisions on prohibiting or restricting the use of non-degradable plastic bags and other disposable plastic products, the competent departments of commerce, postal services, and other departments shall, according to their respective functions, order it to make corrections, impose a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan on any enterprise or public institution, and impose a fine of not less than 500 yuan but not more than 5,000 yuan on any other producer or business operator.  
Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to report the use and recycling of plastic bags and other disposable plastic products in accordance with state provisions, it shall be punished in accordance with the relevant provisions of the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-1226"></a>第一千二百二十六条　违反本法规定，纳入全国碳排放权交易市场的温室气体重点排放单位未报送排放统计核算数据、年度排放报告的，由生态环境主管部门责令改正，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，可以责令停产整治。  
违反本法规定，纳入全国碳排放权交易市场的温室气体重点排放单位未按照规定的期限足额清缴碳排放配额的，由生态环境主管部门责令限期改正，处一百万元以上五百万元以下的罚款；限期内未完成清缴的，处未清缴的碳排放配额清缴时限前一个月市场交易平均成交价格五倍以上十倍以下的罚款，可以责令停产整治。

**Article 1226** : Where any key emitting entity of greenhouse gases included in the national carbon emission trading market, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to submit emission statistics and accounting data or annual emission reports, the competent department of ecological environment shall order it to make corrections and impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if it refuses to make corrections, it may be ordered to suspend production for remediation.  
Where any key emitting entity of greenhouse gases included in the national carbon emission trading market, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to pay its carbon emission allowances in full within the prescribed time limit, the competent department of ecological environment shall order it to make corrections within a prescribed time limit and impose a fine of not less than one million yuan but not more than five million yuan; if it fails to complete the payment within the time limit, a fine of not less than five times but not more than ten times the average transaction price of the allowances not paid in the market one month before the payment deadline shall be imposed, and it may be ordered to suspend production for remediation.

<a id="art-1227"></a>第一千二百二十七条　违反本法有关绿色低碳义务，本节未规定行政处罚的，依照《中华人民共和国循环经济促进法》、《中华人民共和国能源法》、《中华人民共和国节约能源法》、《中华人民共和国可再生能源法》等法律法规的规定予以处罚。

**Article 1227** : Where any entity violates the provisions of this Law on green and low-carbon obligations, for which no administrative penalty is provided in this Section, it shall be punished in accordance with the provisions of the Circular Economy Promotion Law of the People's Republic of China, the Energy Law of the People's Republic of China, the Energy Conservation Law of the People's Republic of China, the Renewable Energy Law of the People's Republic of China, and other laws and regulations.

#### 第十四节　其他违法行为

<a id="art-1228"></a>第一千二百二十八条　除本章第一节至第十三节外，违反本法规定的，适用本节规定予以行政处罚。

**Article 1228** : Where any entity violates the provisions of this Law other than those from Section 1 to Section 13 of this Chapter, the provisions of this Section shall apply for administrative penalty.

<a id="art-1229"></a>第一千二百二十九条　违反本法规定，以拖延、围堵、滞留执法人员等方式拒绝、阻挠监督检查和突发生态环境事件调查处理，或者在接受监督检查和调查处理时弄虚作假或者拒不提供必要资料的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处五万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员，处五千元以上五万元以下的罚款；构成违反治安管理行为的，由公安机关、海警机构按照职责分工依法予以处罚。

**Article 1229** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, refuses or obstructs supervision and inspection and investigation and handling of emergencies in ecological environment by means of delay, encirclement, or detention of law enforcement personnel, or practices fraud or refuses to provide necessary materials during supervision and inspection or investigation and handling, the department or institution with the duty of ecological environmental protection supervision and management shall, according to its functions, order it to make corrections, and impose a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; a fine of not less than 5,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan shall be imposed on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly responsible persons; if the act constitutes a violation of public security administration, the public security organ or the maritime police agency shall, according to their respective functions, impose penalties according to law.

<a id="art-1230"></a>第一千二百三十条　违反本法规定，从事机动车船排放检验、土壤污染调查和评估、污染防治设施维护或者运营、固体废物或者危险废物鉴别等活动的生态环境技术服务机构，未使用符合法律、行政法规规定和国家标准的设施、设备，或者未遵守有关技术规范导致数据失真的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，没收违法所得，并处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；拒不改正的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业。  
违反本法规定，前款所列机构在生态环境服务活动中弄虚作假、出具虚假报告的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工没收违法所得，并处十万元以上五十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，并处五十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款，有许可证的吊销许可证，禁止从事上述业务，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。  
有前款规定情形的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处一万元以上五万元以下的罚款，五年内禁止从事前款规定的业务；情节严重的，十年内禁止从事前款规定的业务。

**Article 1230** : Where an ecological environment technical service institution engaged in activities such as emission inspection of motor vehicles and vessels, soil pollution investigation and assessment, maintenance or operation of pollution prevention and control facilities, or identification of solid waste or hazardous waste, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to use facilities and equipment that comply with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations and national standards, or fails to comply with relevant technical specifications resulting in data distortion, the department or institution with the duty of ecological environmental protection supervision and management shall, according to its functions, order it to make corrections, confiscate the illegal gains, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan; if it refuses to make corrections, upon approval by the people's government with the power of approval, order it to suspend business.  
Where any institution specified in the preceding paragraph, in violation of the provisions of this Law, practices fraud or issues false reports in ecological environment services, the department or institution with the duty of ecological environmental protection supervision and management shall, according to its functions, confiscate the illegal gains, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than 100,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, concurrently impose a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than two million yuan, revoke the license if any, prohibit it from engaging in the aforesaid business, and upon approval by the people's government with the power of approval, order it to suspend business or close down.  
Where the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph occur, the department or institution with the duty of ecological environmental protection supervision and management shall, according to its functions, impose a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly responsible persons, and prohibit them from engaging in the business specified in the preceding paragraph for five years; if the circumstances are serious, prohibit them from engaging in the said business for ten years.

<a id="art-1231"></a>第一千二百三十一条　违反本法规定，有下列情形之一的，由生态环境主管部门、环境卫生主管部门按照职责分工责令改正，可以处十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭：  
（一）从事畜禽规模养殖未及时收集、贮存、清运、处理或者利用养殖过程中产生的畜禽粪污、尸体等废弃物；  
（二）畜禽养殖场利用未经无害化处理的厨余垃圾饲喂畜禽；  
（三）向农田灌溉渠道排放未综合利用的畜禽养殖粪污，致使其下游最近的灌溉取水点的水质不符合农田灌溉用水水质标准。

**Article 1231** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, has any of the following circumstances, the competent department of ecological environment and the competent department of environmental sanitation shall, according to their respective functions, order it to make corrections, and may impose a fine of not more than 100,000 yuan; if the circumstances are serious, upon approval by the people's government with the power of approval, order it to suspend business or close down:  
(1) engaging in large-scale livestock and poultry breeding without timely collection, storage, removal, treatment or utilization of livestock and poultry manure, carcasses and other wastes generated during the breeding process;  
(2) feeding livestock and poultry with kitchen waste that has not undergone harmless treatment in livestock and poultry farms; or  
(3) discharging livestock and poultry manure that has not been comprehensively utilized into farmland irrigation channels, causing the water quality at the nearest downstream irrigation intake point to fail to meet the water quality standards for farmland irrigation.

<a id="art-1232"></a>第一千二百三十二条　违反本法规定，经中华人民共和国过境转移危险废物，以及将放射性废物和被放射性污染的物品输入中华人民共和国境内或者经境内转移的，由海关、海事管理机构按照职责分工责令退运或者责令非法运输该危险废物的船舶退出中华人民共和国管辖海域，并处五十万元以上五百万元以下的罚款。  
违反本法规定，未事先取得国务院生态环境主管部门书面同意，经中华人民共和国管辖的其他海域转移危险废物的，由海事管理机构责令非法运输该危险废物的船舶退出中华人民共和国管辖海域，处五十万元以上五百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1232** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, transfers hazardous waste through the territory of the People's Republic of China in transit, or imports radioactive waste or articles contaminated by radioactive substances into the territory of the People's Republic of China or transfers them through the territory, the customs and the maritime administrative agency shall, according to their respective functions, order the return of the waste or order the vessel illegally transporting the hazardous waste to withdraw from the sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China, and concurrently impose a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than five million yuan.  
Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, transfers hazardous waste through other sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China without prior written consent from the competent department of ecological environment under the State Council, the maritime administrative agency shall order the vessel illegally transporting the hazardous waste to withdraw from the sea areas under the jurisdiction of the People's Republic of China, and impose a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than five million yuan.

<a id="art-1233"></a>第一千二百三十三条　违反本法规定，排放污染物的企业事业单位未建立污染防治责任制度的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正；拒不改正的，处二万元以上二十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，可以责令限制生产、停产整治。

**Article 1233** : Where any enterprise or public institution that discharges pollutants, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to establish a pollution prevention and control responsibility system, the department or institution with the duty of ecological environmental protection supervision and management shall, according to its functions, order it to make corrections; if it refuses to make corrections, a fine of not less than 20,000 yuan but not more than 200,000 yuan shall be imposed; if the circumstances are serious, it may be ordered to restrict production or suspend production for remediation.

<a id="art-1234"></a>第一千二百三十四条　违反本法规定，未按照生态环境准入清单的规定进行生产建设活动的，由生态环境主管部门、自然资源主管部门按照职责分工，责令停止违法行为，没收违法所得，限期拆除并恢复原状，所需费用由违法者承担，并处五十万元以上五百万元以下的罚款，对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处五万元以上十万元以下的罚款；情节严重的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。

**Article 1234** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, fails to carry out production and construction activities in accordance with the ecological environment access list, the competent department of ecological environment and the competent department of natural resources shall, according to their respective functions, order cessation of the illegal act, confiscate the illegal gains, order demolition and restoration to the original condition within a prescribed time limit (the costs shall be borne by the violator), and concurrently impose a fine of not less than 500,000 yuan but not more than five million yuan, and a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly responsible persons; if the circumstances are serious, upon approval by the people's government with the power of approval, order it to suspend business or close down.

<a id="art-1235"></a>第一千二百三十五条　违反本法规定，企业事业单位和其他生产经营者有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正；情节严重的，处一万元以上五万元以下的罚款：  
（一）未制定环境污染、生态破坏事故或者突发生态环境事件的应急预案；  
（二）应急预案未报备案；  
（三）未配备应急设备、器材。

**Article 1235** : Where any enterprise, public institution, or other producer or business operator, in violation of the provisions of this Law, has any of the following circumstances, the department or institution with the duty of ecological environmental protection supervision and management shall, according to its functions, order it to make corrections; if the circumstances are serious, a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 50,000 yuan shall be imposed:  
(1) failing to formulate an emergency response plan for environmental pollution, ecological damage accidents, or sudden ecological environment incidents;  
(2) failing to report the emergency response plan for recordation; or  
(3) failing to equip emergency equipment and apparatus.

<a id="art-1236"></a>第一千二百三十六条　违反本法规定，造成环境污染、生态破坏事故的，除依法承担赔偿责任外，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令限期采取治理措施，适用本条第二款、第三款的规定处以罚款；对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员可以处上一年度从本企业事业单位取得收入百分之五十以下的罚款；对造成重大或者特别重大环境污染、生态破坏事故的，报经有批准权的人民政府批准，责令停业、关闭。未按照要求采取治理措施的，指定有治理能力的单位代为治理，所需费用由违法者承担。  
对造成一般环境污染、生态破坏事故的，处三十万元以上一百万元以下的罚款；对造成较大环境污染、生态破坏事故的，处一百万元以上二百万元以下的罚款；对造成重大环境污染、生态破坏事故的，处二百万元以上一千万元以下的罚款；对造成特别重大环境污染、生态破坏事故的，处一千万元以上二千万元以下的罚款。  
发生环境污染、生态破坏事故，情节特别严重、影响特别恶劣的，负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构可以按照前款罚款数额的二倍以上五倍以下对负有责任的企业事业单位或者其他生产经营者处以罚款。

**Article 1236** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, causes an environmental pollution or ecological damage accident, in addition to bearing compensation liability according to law, the department or institution with the duty of ecological environmental protection supervision and management shall, according to its functions, order it to take remediation measures within a prescribed time limit, and impose a fine in accordance with the provisions of paragraphs 2 and 3 of this Article; a fine of not more than 50% of the income obtained from the enterprise or public institution in the previous year may be imposed on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly responsible persons; if a major or especially major environmental pollution or ecological damage accident is caused, upon approval by the people's government with the power of approval, order it to suspend business or close down. If it fails to take remediation measures as required, the department or institution shall designate a unit with remediation capabilities to carry out the remediation on its behalf, and the costs shall be borne by the violator.  
For causing a general environmental pollution or ecological damage accident, a fine of not less than 300,000 yuan but not more than one million yuan shall be imposed; for causing a relatively large environmental pollution or ecological damage accident, a fine of not less than one million yuan but not more than two million yuan shall be imposed; for causing a major environmental pollution or ecological damage accident, a fine of not less than two million yuan but not more than ten million yuan shall be imposed; for causing an especially major environmental pollution or ecological damage accident, a fine of not less than ten million yuan but not more than twenty million yuan shall be imposed.  
Where an environmental pollution or ecological damage accident occurs with particularly serious circumstances and particularly adverse impact, the department or institution with the duty of ecological environmental protection supervision and management may impose a fine of not less than two times but not more than five times the amount of the fine specified in the preceding paragraph on the responsible enterprise, public institution, or other producer or business operator.

<a id="art-1237"></a>第一千二百三十七条　违反本法规定，因发生事故或者其他突发性事件，造成或者可能造成环境污染、生态破坏事件，有下列情形之一的，由负有生态环境保护监督管理职责的部门、机构按照职责分工责令改正，处以罚款：  
（一）未及时通报或者报告；  
（二）未立即采取有效措施或者逃逸；  
（三）未采取必要应对措施，造成生态灾害危害扩大。  
有前款第一项情形的，处五万元以上五十万元以下的罚款，对直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员处一万元以上十万元以下的罚款，并可以暂扣或者吊销相关任职资格许可；有前款第二项、第三项情形之一的，处二十万元以上二百万元以下的罚款。

**Article 1237** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, causes or may cause an environmental pollution or ecological damage incident due to an accident or other emergency, and has any of the following circumstances, the department or institution with the duty of ecological environmental protection supervision and management shall, according to its functions, order it to make corrections and impose a fine:  
(1) failing to notify or report in a timely manner;  
(2) failing to take effective measures immediately or escaping; or  
(3) failing to take necessary response measures, resulting in the expansion of ecological disaster damage.  
For the circumstance specified in subparagraph (1) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 50,000 yuan but not more than 500,000 yuan shall be imposed, a fine of not less than 10,000 yuan but not more than 100,000 yuan shall be imposed on the directly responsible person in charge and other directly responsible persons, and the relevant qualification permits may be temporarily seized or revoked; for any of the circumstances specified in subparagraphs (2) and (3) of the preceding paragraph, a fine of not less than 200,000 yuan but not more than two million yuan shall be imposed.

<a id="art-1238"></a>第一千二百三十八条　违反本法规定，对举报人以解除、变更劳动合同、聘用合同或者以其他方式打击报复的，应当依照有关法律的规定承担责任。

**Article 1238** : Where any entity, in violation of the provisions of this Law, retaliates against a whistleblower by terminating or modifying the labor contract or employment contract, or by other means, it shall bear liability in accordance with the provisions of relevant laws.

### 第三章　附则

<a id="art-1239"></a>第一千二百三十九条　其他法律对生态保护、绿色低碳发展等生态环境保护相关领域有具体或者进一步规定的，适用其规定。

**Article 1239** : Where other laws have specific or further provisions on ecological protection, green and low-carbon development, and other fields related to ecological environmental protection, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-1240"></a>第一千二百四十条　国家根据实际需要，对生态环境监督管理体制作出调整的，依照其规定。

**Article 1240** : Where the State makes adjustments to the ecological environment supervision and management system based on actual needs, the provisions on such adjustments shall apply.

<a id="art-1241"></a>第一千二百四十一条　国务院、中央军事委员会可以依照本法规定的原则制定有关军队生态环境保护工作的具体管理办法。

**Article 1241** The State Council and the Central Military Commission may, in accordance with the principles set forth in this Law, formulate specific management measures concerning ecological and environmental protection work in the military.

<a id="art-1242"></a>第一千二百四十二条　本法自2026年8月15日起施行。《中华人民共和国环境保护法》、《中华人民共和国环境影响评价法》、《中华人民共和国海洋环境保护法》、《中华人民共和国大气污染防治法》、《中华人民共和国水污染防治法》、《中华人民共和国土壤污染防治法》、《中华人民共和国固体废物污染环境防治法》、《中华人民共和国噪声污染防治法》、《中华人民共和国放射性污染防治法》、《中华人民共和国清洁生产促进法》同时废止。

**Article 1242** This Law shall come into force on August 15, 2026. The Environmental Protection Law of the People's Republic of China, the Environmental Impact Assessment Law of the People's Republic of China, the Marine Environmental Protection Law of the People's Republic of China, the Law on the Prevention and Control of Atmospheric Pollution of the People's Republic of China, the Law on the Prevention and Control of Water Pollution of the People's Republic of China, the Law on the Prevention and Control of Soil Pollution of the People's Republic of China, the Law on the Prevention and Control of Environmental Pollution by Solid Wastes of the People's Republic of China, the Law on the Prevention and Control of Noise Pollution of the People's Republic of China, the Law on the Prevention and Control of Radioactive Pollution of the People's Republic of China, and the Cleaner Production Promotion Law of the People's Republic of China shall be abolished simultaneously.
